yāḻiṭip piṟavā iciyē yeṉkō!
80		tāḻiruṅ kūntal tiyāl! niṉṉiyeṉṟu
ulavāk kaṭṭuri palapā rāṭṭit
tayaṅkiṇark kōti taṉṉoṭu tarukki
vayaṅkiṇart tārōṉ makiḻntucel vuḻināḷ
vāroli kūntalip pēriyaṟ kiḻatti
85		maṟapparum kēṇmiyō ṭaṟappari cāramum
viruntu puṟantarūum peruntaṇ vāḻkkiyum
vēṟupaṭu tiruviṉ vīṟupeṟak kāṇa
urimic cuṟṟamō ṭorutaṉi puṇarkka
yāṇṭucila kaḻintaṉa iṟperuṅ kiḻamiyiṟ
90	kāṇṭaku ciṟappiṟ kaṇṇaki taṉakkeṉa.
veṇpā
		tūmap paṇikaḷoṉṟit tōyntā leṉavoruvār
kāmar maṉiviyeṉak kikalantu ? nāman
toliyāta iṉpamelān tuṉṉiṉār maṇmēl
niliyāmi kaṇṭavarpōl niṉṟu.
uri
1 - 7 		(uricāl .... taruvaṉarīṭṭa) kalattiṉum kāliṉum taruvaṉar īṭṭa - kalattāṉum kālā ṉum tantu īṭṭutalāl, arumporuḷ tarūum viruntiṉ tēem oruṅku tokkaṉṉa uṭip perum paṇṭam - peṟutaṟkariya poruḷit tarum putumiyuṭiya tēyaṅkaḷ oruṅku kūṭiyiruntāṟ pōlum perumiyuṭiya paṇṭaṅkaḷ, muḻaṅku kaṭal ñālam muḻu vatum variṉum - ārkali cūḻnta ñālamuḻutum orucēra variṉum, vaḻaṅkat tavāa vaḷattatu āki - vaḻaṅkat toliyāta vaḷattiṉi yuṭiyatāki, uricāl ciṟappiṉ aricu viḻi tiruviṉ paratar malinta payam keḻu mānakar - pukaḻaminta ciṟappiṉiyuṭiya aracarum virumpum celvattiyuṭiya paratar mikka payaṉ poruntiya periya pukār nakariṉkaṇ,
paratar - vaṇikar ; kaṭalōṭikaḷ eṉpatu arumpatavuri paratarāl mēṉmiyuṟṟa eṉṟumām. ñālam, ākupeyar. tēem - tēyam. pukāriṉkaṇ piṟanāṭṭu arumporuḷkaḷ vantu tokutali,
          1"	nīriṉ vanta nimirparip puraviyum
		kāliṉ vanta karuṅkaṟi mūṭiyum
		vaṭamalip piṟanta maṇiyum poṉṉum
		kuṭamalip piṟanta vāramu makilum
		teṉkaṭaṉ muttuṅ kuṇakaṭaṟ ṟukirum
		kaṅki vāriyuṅ kāvirip payaṉum
		īḻat tuṇavuṅ kāḻakat tākkamum
		ariyavum periyavum neḷiya vīṇṭi"
eṉpataṉāṉaṟika. kalam - marakkalam. kāl - vaṭṭi ; cākāṭu. taruvaṉar, eccamuṟṟu. īṭṭutalāl tokkaṉṉa paṇṭam vaḻaṅkat tavā vaḷattatāki aracu viḻi tiruviṉiyuṭiya paratar malinta nakar eṉka. 
8 - 11 	kulattiṟ kuṉṟāk koḻuṅkuṭic celvar - kula voḻukkattiṟ kuṉṟāta naṟkuṭiyiṉarākiya celvarkaṭku, attaku tiruviṉ aruntavam muṭittōr uttara kuruviṉ op pattōṉṟiya - aṅṅaṉam aṟattiṉ īṭṭiya poruḷālē talippaṭutāṉattic ceytōr eytum uttarakuruvi an nakarokkumpaṭi tōṉṟiya, kayamalark kaṇṇiyum kātal koḻunaṉum - periya malarpōlum kaṇṇiṉiyuṭi yāḷum avaḷāṟ kātalikkappaṭum koḻunaṉum,
kulavoḻukkamāvaṉa : 1"neṭunukattup pakalpōla, naṭuvu niṉṟa naṉṉeñciṉōr, vaṭuvañci vāymoḻintu, tamavum piṟavu moppa nāṭik, koḷvatūu mikikoḷātu koṭuppatūuṅ kuṟikoṭātu" vāṇikañ ceytal mutaliyaṉa. celvar eṉṟatu iruvar tantiyiyum. aruntavam - īṇṭut talippaṭu tāṉattiṉ mēṟṟu. muṭittōr eytum eṉa oru col varuvikka. uttara  kuru: - pōka pūmi yāṟaṉuḷ oṉṟu. talippaṭutāṉam iṉṉateṉpa taṉi,
2"	aṟatti ṉāṟṟiya varumperum poruḷip
puṟattuṟik kuṟṟamūṉ ṟaṟuttanaṟ ṟavarkkuk
koḷkeṉap paṇintu kuṟiyiran tavarvayiṉ
uḷḷamuvan tīva tuttama tāṉam"
eṉpataṉāṉum, pōka pūmiyiṉ iyalpiṉi,
3“	patiṉā ṟāṭṭik kumaraṉuñ ciṟanta
paṉṉī rāṭṭik kumariyu māki
otta marapiṉu motta vaṉpiṉum
kaṟpaka naṉmaram naṟpaya ṉutava
ākiya ceytavat taḷavu mavvaḻip
pōkam nukarvatu pōka pūmi”
eṉpataṉāṉum, pōka pūmiyiṉ vakiyā ṟaṇiyum,
1"	ātiyari vañcam nallari vañcam
ēmata vañcam iraṇa vañcam 
tēva kuruvam uttara kuruvameṉap
pōka pūmi yaṟuvakip paṭumē"
eṉpataṉāṉum aṟika. kaya - perumi ;
2"	taṭavum kayavum naḷiyum perumi"
eṉpatu tolkāppiyam. kayamalar - nīrppū eṉṉalumām. ivarkaḷ tōṉṟip pōkam nukartalāl pukār uttara kuruvi yotta teṉka. mānakarkkaṇ annakar uttara kuruvi yokkumpaṭi cel varkkut tōṉṟiya kaṇṇiyum koḻunaṉum eṉṟurikka.
12 - 13 	mayaṉ vitittaṉṉa maṇikkāl amaḷimici neṭunili māṭattu iṭinilattu iruntuḻi-- eḻunili māṭattu iṭi nilikkaṇṇē - mayaṉ nirumittu vittālotta aḻakiya kālkaḷiyuṭiya kaṭṭiliṉmītu iruntavaḷavil, mayaṉ - teyvat taccaṉ vitittal - maṉattāl nirumittal, maṇi - pavaḻam mutaliyavumām, neṭunili - eḻunili. iṭinilam - nāṉkām nilam. 
14 - 25 	(kaḻunīrāmpal ........ kātaliṟ ciṟantu) kaḻunīr āmpal muḻuneṟik kuvaḷi - kaḻunīrum cētāmpalum muḻuneṟiyākiya ceṅkaḻunīrum, arumpu poti aviḻnta curumpu imir tāmari - arumpu kaṭṭaviḻnta vaṇṭu olikkum tāmariyum ākiya, vayaṟ pū vācam aḷii - nīrppūkkaḷiṉ maṇattiṉik kalantuṇṭu, ayaṟ pū - avaṟṟiṉ vēṟāya kōṭṭuppū mutaliyavaṟṟuḷ, mētaku tāḻi viriyal veṇtōṭṭu - mēṉmi poruntiya tāḻiyiṉ virinta veḷḷiya tōṭṭa kattum, kōti mātavi caṇpakap potumpar - caṇpakak kāviluḷḷa mālipōlum mātavip pūviṉiṭattum, tātu tērntu uṇṭu - tātiṉi ārāyntu uṇṭu pōntu, mātar vāḷ mukattup purikuḻal aḷakattup pukal ēkkaṟṟut tiritaru curum poṭu - oḷḷiya mukattiṉiyuṭiya mātaruṭiya purinta kuḻaṟciyiyuṭiya kūntalil uṇṭākiya kalavi maṇam peṟutaṟku ēkkaṟṟup pukutaṟku vaḻikāṇāmaṟ cuḻalu kiṉṟa curum pōṭum, cevvi pārttu - cevviyaṟintu, mālit tāmattu maṇi nirittu vakutta kōlac cāḷarak kuṟuṅkaṇ nuḻintu - maṇik kōviyālē oḻuṅkupaṭa nirittu vakutta aḻakiṉi yuṭiya cāḷarattiṉ kuṟiya puḻikaḷāl nuḻintu, vaṇṭoṭu pukka maṇavāyt teṉṟal kaṇṭu - vaṇṭōṭum pukunta maṇattiṉiyuṭiya teṉṟalik kaṇṭu, makiḻvu eytik kātaliṟ ciṟantu - makiḻcciyuṟṟuk kalaviyi virumpik kātal mikutalāl,
muḻuneṟi - itaḻoṭiyāta muḻup pū. kuvaḷi-īṇṭuc ceṅ kaḻunīr ; 1kuvaḷik kūmpaviḻ muḻuneṟi" eṉpatum, ataṉuriyum nōkkuka. cētāmpal ēṉiya pōṉṟu pakalil malarum pūvaṉṟāyiṉum avi viriyuṅ kālattu itu kuvitaliṉmiyiṉ oruṅku kūṟiṉār. potumpar - marac ceṟivu. uṇṭu eṉpataṉi muṉṉuṅ kūṭṭi, aḷii uṇṭu, tērntuṇṭu eṉka. mātavi - kurukkatti. caṇpakattōṭu malartaliṉ tērntuṇṭu eṉṟār, caṇpakam vaṇṭuṇā malar maramākaliṉ vāṇmukattu mātar eṉavum, aḷakattu ēkkaṟṟu eṉavum, pukaṟkut tiritaru eṉavum, maṇittāmattu eṉavum, māli nirittu eṉavum māṟuka.  cevvi - ivar makiḻuñ cevvi. māli - oḻuṅku. curumpōṭum vaṇṭōṭum pukka veṉka. maṇavāyt teṉṟal - maṇattit taṉṉiṭattēyuṭiya teṉṟal; vāy - iṭam.
aḷii uṇṭu tērntuṇṭu ēkkaṟṟut tiritaru curumpōṭum vaṇṭōṭum pukka teṉṟal eṉka.
26 - 27	virimalar vāḷiyoṭu vēṉil vīṟṟirukkum niri nili māṭattu aramiyam ēṟi - aṅṅaṉam kātal mikutalāl avarkaḷ niritta nilikaḷiyuṭiya māṭattiṉ iṭi
nilattu niṉṟum maṇattiṉiyuṭiya malarkkaṇiyōṭē kāmaṉ vīṟṟirukkum mēṉilamākiya nilā muṟṟattiṉ mēl ēṟi, vēṉil - kāmaṉ : ākupeyar. aramiyam - nilā muṟṟam.  
28 - 31 	(curumpuṇak kiṭanta ..... kāṭci pōla) curumpuṇak kiṭanta naṟumpūñ cēkki - curumpukaḷ uṇṇum paṭi parappiya naṟiya pūkkaḷi yuṭiya cēkkikkaṇṇē, mutir kaṭal ñālam muḻuvatum viḷakkum katir oruṅku irunta kāṭci pōla - muṟṟiya kaṭaliyuṭiya ñālam muḻuvatiyum viḷakkum
ñāyiṟum tiṅkaḷum cēra irunta kāṭcipōla iruntu, karumpum valliyum peruntōḷ eḻuti - karumpu valli eṉ paṉavaṟṟip periya tōḷilē yeḻuti,
curumpukaḷuṇṭaṟkup parappi vittāṟpōlak kiṭanta naṟum pūñ cēkki yeṉalumām, mutirtal - cūḻtalumām. katir - veṅkatirākiya ñāyiṟum taṇkatirākiya tiṅkaḷum. oruṅkiruttal, ilporuḷuvamam. pōla iruntu eṉa oru col varuvikka. karumpum valliyum cantaṉak kuḻampāl eḻutappaṭuvaṉa.
(aṭi. karumpiyum valliyiyum tōḷil eḻuti yeṉavē toyyi loṉṟiyum mulimē leḻuti eṉpatāyiṟṟu.)
32 - 37 vaṇṭu vāy tiṟappa neṭunilā virinta veṇtōṭṭu malliki viriyal māliyoṭu - vaṇṭukaḷ puriyi nekiḻkka neṭiya nilavippōla virinta veḷḷiya itaḻiyuṭiya mallikiyiṉ malarcciyiyuṭiya māliyōṭē, kaḻunīrp piṇiyal muḻuneṟi piṟaḻa - itaḻoṭiyātu kaṭṭiṉa kaḻunīrp piṇiyalum kulintu aliya, tārum māliyum mayaṅkik kiyaṟṟu - mārpiliṭṭa ivviru vaki māliyum mayaṅkap paṭṭu iruvarum ceyalaṟṟa puṇarcci yiṟutikkaṇ, tīrāk kātal tirumukam nōkki-nīṅkāta kātaliyuṭiya tirumakaḷip pōlvāḷuṭiya mukatti nōkki, kōvalaṉ kūṟum ōr kuṟiyāk kaṭṭuri - muṉṉark karutāta poruḷ potinta uriyik kōvalaṉ kūṟāniṟpaṉ:
nilā - oḷiyumām. tārum māliyum mayaṅki eṉpataṉāṟ puṇarcci kūṟiṟṟu ; iṭakkaraṭakku. kiyaṟutal - kalaviyāl avacamuṟutal. kātalāl eṉa virittalumām. tiru - tiruppōl vāḷ. ōr, aci. kuṟiyā - taṉ aṟivu mutaliyavaṟṟik karutāta eṉṟumām. kaṭṭuri - nalam pārāṭṭal eṉṉum poruḷ potinta uri.  
38 - 41	kuḻavit tiṅkaḷ imiyavar ētta aḻakoṭu muṭitta arumittu āyiṉum-piṟimatiyāṉatu imiyavar ēttā niṟka iṟivaṉ taṉ muṭikku aḻaku ceytal kāraṇattāṟ cūṭiya arumi yuṭiyatāyiṉum, uritiṉ niṉṉōṭu uṭaṉ piṟappu uṇmiyiṉ periyōṉ taruka tirunutal ākeṉa - atu niṉṉōṭu uṭaṉpiṟappākaliṉ niṉakku urit tātalāl apperiyōṉ ataṉi niṉakkut tirunutalākat tarak kaṭavaṉ;
tiṅkaṭ kuḻaviyeṉṟatu 1"colliya valla piṟa vavaṇ variṉum" eṉṉum marapiyaṟ puṟaṉaṭiyāṉ muṭiyumeṉpar aṭiyārkku nallār ;
1"	piḷḷi kuḻavi kaṉṟē pōtteṉak
	koḷḷavu mamiyu mōraṟi vuyirkkē " 
eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, ōraṟivuyirkkuk koḷḷavum amiyu meṉṉum ummiyi eccappaṭuttup piṟavaḻiyuṅ koḷḷappaṭum eṉa muṭippar pērāciriyar. imiyava rēttal 2"appiṟi patiṉeṇ kaṇaṉu mēttavum paṭumē  eṉpataṉāṉumaṟika. uritu - urittu. periyōṉ - mātēvaṉ; iṟivaṉ. tirunutalāka veṉṟu taruka eṉka. piṟi tiruvoṭu pāṟkaṭaliṭip piṟattaliṉ ivaḷit tirumakaḷāka matittu iṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟiṉāṉ. 
(aṭi. 	taruka veṉṟatu cūṭiṉa piṟi iraṇṭu kaliyātaliṉ, ataṉi eṇṇāṭṭiṅkaḷākkit tarukaveṉpatu karuttu; eṉṉi ? 3‘mākkaṭa ṉaṭuvaṇ eṇṇāṭ pakkattup, pacuveṇ ṭiṅkaḷ tōṉṟi yāṅkuk, katuppayal viḷaṅkuñ ciṟunutal’ eṉṟārākaliṉ. itaṉāṉ mēṟkūṟukiṉṟa karumpiyum vaccirattiyum avvav vuṟuppukkaṭ kēṟpat tirutti īkka aḷikka eṉpatāyiṟṟu ; eṉṟatu, karumpiṟku niṟaṉum, vaccirattiṟku nērmiyum uṇṭākki yeṉṟavāṟu.  
42 - 45 	aṭiyār muṉiyakattu amar mēmpaṭunarkkup paṭi vaḻaṅkuvatu ōr paṇpu uṇṭu ākaliṉ - pakivar muṉiyiṭattē pōriṉi mēmpaṭuppār cilarkkup paṭikkalam vaḻaṅkuva tōr muṟimi aracark kuṇṭātaliṉ, uruvi lāḷaṉ oru peruṅ karuppuvil iru karum puruvamāka īkka - aṉaṅkaṉ pōr ceytaṟku eṭutta periya karumpuvil oṉṟiyum niṉakkuk kariya iru puruvamākat tiruttit tarakkaṭavaṉ;
‘oru peruṅ karuppuvil iru karum puruvam eṉṟatu cēma villiyuṅ kūṭṭi’ eṉa aṭiyārkku nallār kūṟiṉar ; piṉ, vēloṉṟu kaṇṇiraṇṭā īttatu eṉa varutaliṉ, itaṉiyum avvāṟu kōṭal poruntum. maṟṟum avar, ‘muṉivarākiya muraṭ pakiyi aḻittaṟku valvillē vēṇṭutaliṉ, oru peruṅ karuppuvil leṉṟār’ eṉṟu kūṟiyatum īṇṭikkup poruntuva taṉṟu ; kāmavēḷ taṉṉuṭaṉ ceyyum pōril avaṟku veṉṟi taruvāḷākak koṇṭu iṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟiṉāṉeṉka.  
46 - 48 	mūvā maruntiṉ muṉṉart tōṉṟaliṉ - mūvāmikku ētuvākiya amiḻtiṟku nī muṟpiṟattalāl, tēvar kōmāṉ - tēvarkkaracaṉākiya intiraṉ, teyvak kāvaṟ paṭi - acurari yaḻittuc curarik kāttaṟkeṭutta vaccirap paṭiyi, niṉakku aḷikka ataṉ iṭi niṉakku iṭiyeṉa - ataṉ iṭi niṉakku iṭiyākaveṉat tiruttit tarak kaṭavaṉ ;
mūvā eṉa mūppiṉi yoḻittal kūṟavē iṟappiṉi yoḻittalum kūṟiṟṟām. nī amutākaliṉ niṉakku muṉṉar vacciram piṟattalāl eṉiṉumamiyum. vacciram irutalic cūlamāy naṭuvu piṭiyā yiruttaliṉ ataṉ iṭiyi makaḷir iṭikku uvamaṅ kūṟuvar. āka veṉa oru col varuvikka.  
49 - 52 	aṟumuka oruvaṉ ōr peṟumuṟi iṉṟiyum - āṟu tirumukattiyuṭiya oppillāṉākiya murukaṉ ivvāṟu ōr urimiyiṉṟiyum, iṟumuṟi kāṇum iyalpiṉiṉ aṉṟē - yāṉ tuṉpuṟu muṟimiyit taṉ kaṇṇāṟ kāṇṭal kāra ṇattāṉaṉṟē, am cuṭar neṭuvēl oṉṟu - taṉ kiyiluḷḷa aḻakiya cuṭariyuṭiya neṭiya vēloṉṟiyum, niṉ mukattuc ceṅkaṭi maḻikkaṇ iraṇṭā īttatu - niṉ mukattilē civanta kaṭiyiyuṭiya kuḷircci poruntiya kaṇ iraṇṭumāmpaṭi īttatu ;
peṟumuṟiyiṉṟiyum - urimiyiṉṟiyum ; taṉakkup peṟuṅ kūṟu oṉṟilli yākavum eṉiṉu mamiyum. iṟumuṟi - varuntu muṟimi ; cākkāṭumām ; itu,
1"	koḷḷum poruḷila rāyiṉum vampalar tuḷḷunark kāṇmār toṭarntuyir vavvaliṉ "
eṉṟāṟ pōlvatu. iraṇṭā īttatu - iraṇṭāka nirumittut tantatu ;
2"	ōkkiya murukaṉ vivē lōriraṇ ṭaṉiya kaṇṇāḷ "
eṉpatu kāṇka. īttatu, toḻiṟ peyar. oruvaṉ īttatu kāṇum iyalpiṉiṉ eṉa muṭikka. ivaṟṟuṭ kaṭpulaṉāy eḻutappaṭum uṟuppukkaḷip pārāṭṭi, iṉik kaṭpulaṉāyum ākātumāy eḻu tappaṭātaṉapārāṭṭuvāṉ. 
53 - 61 	(māyirum pīli .... oruvāvāyiṉa) māyirum pīli maṇiniṟa maññi niṉ cāyaṟku iṭintu taṇkāṉ aṭi yavum - kariya periya pīliyiyuṭiya nīla maṇi pōlum niṟattiyuṭiya mayilkaḷ niṉ cāyaṟkut tōṟṟut taṇṇiya kāṭṭiṭattē pōy oṭuṅkāniṟkavum, aṉṉam naṉṉutal meṉṉaṭikku aḻintu naṉṉīrp paṇṇi naḷimalarc ceṟiyavum - nalla neṟṟiyi yuṭiyāy, aṉṉaṅkaḷ niṉ meṉmi yuṭiya naṭikkut tōṟṟu nalla nīriyuṭiya vayalkaḷiṟ ceṟinta malariṭiyē pukku maṟiyavum, aḷiya tāmē ciṟu pacuṅkiḷiyē - ciṟiya paciya kiḷikaḷ tām aḷikkattakkaṉa ; kuḻalum yāḻum amiḻtum kuḻitta niṉ maḻalik kiḷavikku varuntiṉa vākiyum - avi kuḻaliṉiciyiyum yāḻiṉiciyiyum amiḻtattiyum kalantu kuḻittālotta niṉatu mutirāta maḻalic coṟkut tōṟṟaṉavākiyum, maṭa naṭi mātu niṉ malark kiyiṉ nīṅkātu uṭaṉ uṟivu marīi oruvā vāyiṉa- maṭappattiyuṭiya naṭiyiṉiyuṭiya mātē, niṉṉi vaḻipaṭṭu ac colliṉik kaṟkak karuti niṉatu malar pōluṅ kiyiṉiṉṟum nīṅkāvāy niṉṉuṭaṉē veṟupput tōṉṟāmal uṟitaliyum poruntip piriyāvāyiṉa ākalāṉ.
māyiru eṉa yakara uṭampaṭu mey peṟṟatu. 1"kiḷantavalla" eṉṉum puṟaṉaṭiyāṉ amiyum. maṇi - nīla maṇi. taṇ kāṉ - iḻintakāṭu. naḷi - ceṟivu. ceṟital - oṭuṅkutal, kuḻitta - kuḻittā lotta ; ilporuḷuvamam; kuḻivitta eṉiṉumamiyum ; varuttiya eṉṟapaṭi. 
naṉṉutal, mātu, maññi aṭiyavum aṉṉam ceṟiyavum, kiḷi varuntiṉavākiyum nīṅkātu marīi oruvāvāyiṉa ; ākaliṉ avi aḷikkattakkaṉa ; eṉa viṉi muṭipu koḷka.  
62 - 64 	naṟumalark kōti - naṟiya malari yaṇinta kōtiyē, niṉ nalam pārāṭṭunar - niṉṉip puṉintu aḻaku ceyyum makaḷir, maṟuil maṅkala aṇiyē aṉṟiyum - kuṟṟamaṟṟa  niṉatu puṉiyāta aḻakirukkavum aḵtaṉṟi, piṟitu aṇi aṇiyap peṟṟati evaṉkol - vēṟu cila aṇikaḷi aṇinta taṉāṟ peṟṟatu yātō ;
nalam pārāṭṭunar - oppaṉi ceyvār, maṅkalavaṇi - iyaṟ kiyaḻaku; māṅkaliya meṉpārumuḷar. aṇiya - aṇitalāl.
2"	umiḻcuṭark kalaṉkaḷ naṅki yuruviṉi maṟippa tōrār"
eṉpatu oppu nōkkaṟpālatu.  
65 - 66 	pal iruṅ kūntaṟ ciṉmalar aṉṟiyum - palavakittākiya kariya kūntalil maṅkalamākac cila malarip peyta laṉṟiyum, el aviḻ māliyoṭu eṉ uṟṟaṉar kol - oḷiyuṭiya aviḻkiṉṟa māliyip puṉitaṟku am māliyōṭu avarkaḷ eṉṉa uṟavuṭiyārkaḷō ;  
67 - 68 	nāṉam nal akil naṟumpuki aṉṟiyum - avar, kūntaṟku nāṟṟamuṭiya nalla akiṟpukiyi ūṭṭutalaṉṟiyum, māṉmatac cāntoṭu vantati evaṉkol - māṉmatac cānti aṇitaṟku ataṉōṭu tamakkuḷatākiya kaṇṇōṭṭam yātō ;
 nāṉam - neyyumām. māṉmatam - kattūri, vantati - poruntiya kaṇṇōṭṭam ; vanta uṟavu eṉalumām.  
69 - 70 	tirumulit taṭattiṭit toyyil aṉṟiyum - avar, mulittaṭattiṉmēl toyyi leḻututa laṉṟiyum, oru kāḻ muttamoṭu uṟṟati evaṉkol - taṉi muttu vaṭat tip pūṭṭuvataṟku ataṉōṭu avarkkuṇṭāṉa urimi yātō ;
tiru - mulimēṟ ṟōṉṟum vīṟṟut teyvam eṉpar; 1"āmaṇaṅku kuṭiyirun tañcuṇaṅku parantaṉavē" eṉṟār cintā maṇiyilum. taṭam - parappu. kāḻ - vaṭam. muttak kāḻ eṉa māṟuka.  
71 - 72 	tiṅkaḷ muttu arumpavum - matipōlum mukattil muttup pōlum viyar tōṉṟavum, ciṟuku iṭi varuntavum- nuṇṇiya iṭi ociyavum, iṅku ivi aṇintaṉar - iv vuḻi ivaṟṟi aṇintārākaliṉ, eṉ uṟṟaṉarkol - avarkaḷ eṉṉa pittēṟiṉārkaḷō ;
tiṅkaḷ muttu eṉpaṉa kātalum nalaṉum nilikkaḷaṉākat tōṉṟiya 2paṇpuvamat toki ; kuṟippiṉāṟ poruḷ uṇara niṉṟaṉa ; ākupeya reṉpārumuḷar.
maṅkalavaṇiyum ciṉmalarum akiṟpukiyum toyyilumē pāramāy arumpavum varuntavum, piṟitaṇiyum māliyum cāntum muttum aṇintār eṉa muṟkūṟiyavaṟṟik karutik kūṟiyavāṟu.  
73 - 74 	mācu aṟu poṉṉē - kaṭku iṉimiyāṟ kuṟṟa maṟṟa poṉṉi yoppāy, valampuri muttē - ūṟṟiṉ iṉ pattāl valampuri yīṉṟa mutti yoppāy, kācu aṟu viriyē - uyirppiṉ iṉimiyāṟ kuṟṟamaṟṟa viriyi yoppāy, karumpē - cuviyiṉimiyiṟ karumpiyoppāy, tēṉē - iṉiya moḻiyiyuṭimiyāl tēṉi yoppāy,
valampurimut teṉṟataṉāl marapiṉ tūymiyuṅ kūṟiṉāṉ : 1"valampuri muttiṟ kulampuri piṟappum" eṉpatu kāṇka. tēṉ - yāḻiṉ narampiṟku ākupeya reṉṉalumām. 2"narampārttaṉṉa iṉkuraṟ ṟokuti" eṉṟār nakkīraṉārum. oḷiyum ūṟum nāṟṟa mum cuviyum ōciyumākiya aimpulaṉuṅ kūṟi nalam pārāṭṭiṉāṉ ;
3“	kaṇṭukēṭ ṭuṇṭuyirt tuṟṟaṟiyu mimpulaṉum
oṇṭoṭi kaṇṇē yuḷa”
eṉṉum vaḷḷuvar vāymoḻiyuṅ kāṇka.  
75 - 76 	arumpeṟal pāvāy - peṟutaṟkariya pāviyē, ār uyir maruntē - ariya uyiri nilipeṟac ceyyum maruntē, peruṅkuṭi vāṇikaṉ perumaṭamakaḷē - peruṅ kuṭip piṟanta vaṇikaṉuṭiya perumi poruntiya maṭappatti yuṭiya putalviyē,
kāṭciyiṉ uyir mayakkuṟutaliṟ kolliyiṟ pāvāy eṉṟum, avvāṟu aḻiyumuyiri imippiṟ ṟarutaliṉ āruyir maruntē eṉṟuṅ kūṟiṉāṉ. uyir maruntu - miruta cañcīviṉi eṉpar.  
77 - 81 	maliyiṭip piṟavā maṇiyē eṉkō - maliyiṟ piṟavāta maṇiyē eṉpēṉō, aliyiṭip piṟavā amiḻtē eṉkō - kaṭaliṟ piṟavāta amiḻtē eṉpēṉō, yāḻiṭip piṟavā iciyē eṉkō - yāḻiṟ piṟavāta iciyē eṉ pēṉō, tāḻ iruṅ kūntal tiyāl niṉṉi eṉṟu - nīṇṭa kariya kūntaliyuṭiya tiyalē niṉṉi eṉṟu, ulavāk kaṭ ṭuri pala pārāṭṭi - toliyāta kaṭṭuri palavaṟṟāl nalam pārāṭṭi,
ali, ākupeyar. tiyāl niṉṉi eṉkō eṉkō eṉkō eṉṟu pārāṭṭi yeṉka.
(aṭi.. 	maliyiṭip piṟakku maṇi kuḻiyāmiyiṉ atiṟ piṟavā maṇiyē yeṉpēṉō, aliyiṭip piṟakkum amirtiṟku ivvaṭiviṉmiyiṉ ataṉiṟ piṟavāta amutē yeṉpēṉō, yāḻ kaṭkiṉṉā tākaliṉ ataṉiṭip piṟavāta iciyē yeṉpēṉō veṉat teritaru tēṟṟavuvami yeṉṉum alaṅkāramum, mali kaṭal eṉa virōtamum, piṉvarunili yeṉṉum alaṅkāramum pulappaṭuttiṉā reṉa vuṇarka.)  
82 - 83 	tayaṅku iṇark kōti taṉṉoṭu tarukki - viḷaṅkukiṉṟa pūṅ kottukkaḷālākiya kōtiyi yuṭiyāḷōṭum iṉpattil mikku, vayaṅku iṇart tārōṉ makiḻntu cel vuḻi nāḷ - viḷaṅkukiṉṟa iṇart tāriṉi yuṭiyōṉ makiḻntu cellā niṉṟa nāṭkaḷil orunāḷ,
tayaṅkal, vayaṅkal iraṇṭum viḷakkameṉṉum poruḷaṉa; iṅṅaṉam varuvaṉavaṟṟip pariyāyavalaṅkāra meṉpar aṭiyārkku nallār.  
84 - 90 	vār oli kūntaṟ pēriyaṟ kiḻatti - nīṇṭa taḻitta kūntaliyuṭiya irunitik kiḻavaṉ maṉikkiḻatti, maṟapparuṅ kēṇmiyōṭu - maṟattalariya cuṟṟantaḻālōṭē, aṟap paricāramum - aṟaneṟiyāḷari ōmpalum, viruntu puṟan tarūum peruntaṇ vāḻkki - viruntiṉarip pēṇutalu mākiya ivaṟṟuṭaṉ kūṭiya perumiyuṭiya ilvāḻkkiyi, vēṟupaṭutiruviṉ vīṟupeṟak kāṇa - nāṉāvitamāṉa celvat tōṭē naṭattik kivantu uyarcci peṟutalik kāṇa
vēṇṭi, urimic cuṟṟamōṭu oru taṉi puṇarkka - aṭimit tira ḷōṭē vēṟāka irukkac ceyya, yāṇṭu cila kaḻintaṉa iṟ peruṅ kiḻamiyiṉ kāṇtaku ciṟappiṉ kaṇṇaki taṉakkeṉ - kāṇattakka ciṟappiṉiyuṭiya kaṇṇakikkup perumiyuṭiya illaṟatti naṭattum urimippāṭṭuṭaṉ cila āṇṭukaḷ kaḻintaṉa.
vāroli kūntal - kaṇṇakiyi eṉṟumām. ‘kūntali yuṭiya pēriyaṟkiḻattiyeṉiṉu mamiyum’ eṉa aṭiyārkku nal lār kūṟutaliṉ, ‘kūntalip pēriyaṟ kiḻatti’ eṉap piṟar pāṭaṅ koṇṭami poruntuvataṉṟu.
‘pēriṟ kiḻatti’ eṉpatum, ‘aṟapparikāram’ eṉpatum arum patavuri yāciriyar koṇṭa pāṭam. aṟapparikāram - tuṟan tārip pūcittu aṟattip parikarittal eṉpar. maṟappariya eṉṟa aṭiyip piṟavaṟṟōṭuṅ kūṭṭuka. aṟapparikāram eṉpatil aṟavōrk kaḷittalum antaṇarōmpalum tuṟavōrkketirta lum aṭaṅkum; piṉ, 1kolikkaḷak kātiyiṟ kaṇṇaki kūṟumāṟu aṟika. puṟantarūum - puṟantaralum eṉka; puṟantaral - pēṇutal. ēṉi mūṉṟu niliyiṉariyum vaṟiyar mutalāṉāriyum vēṇṭuvaṉa tantu purakkum aṉpum aruḷumuṭimiyāl peruntaṇ vāḻkki yeṉṟu peyar kūṟiṉār. eṉ, aci.
celvarkkut tōṉṟiya kaṇṇiyum koḻunaṉum teṉṟalik kaṇṭu makiḻntu ciṟantu ēṟik kāṭcipōlac cēkkiyiliruntu eḻuti mayaṅkik kiyaṟṟu mukanōkkik kaṭṭuri kūṟukiṉṟavaṉ pala pārāṭṭi makiḻntu celvuḻi orunāḷ pēriyaṟ kiḻatti oru taṉi puṇarkkak kaṇṇakitaṉakkup peruṅ kiḻamiyōṭu āṇṭu cila kaḻintaṉa eṉak kūṭṭuka.
ellāvaṭiyum nēraṭiyāl vantu muṭitaliṉ, itu nilimaṇṭila vāciriyappā. 


veṇpāvuri
 (tūma ........... pōṉiṉṟu.) maṇmēl niliyāmi kaṇṭa varpōl niṉṟu - puviyiṉkaṇ poruḷ mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ nili yāmiyik kaṇṭu avi uḷḷapoḻutē aṉittiṉpamum tuyt taṟku virital pōl niṉṟu, kāmar maṉiviyeṉak kikalantu - kāmaṉum iratiyumpōlak kātalāl oruvar oruvariṟ kalantu, tūmap paṇikaḷ oṉṟit tōyntāleṉa oruvār - ciṉatti yuṭiya pāmpukaḷ oṉṟupaṭṭut taḻuviṉāṟpōla viṭṭu nīṅkā rāy, nāmam toliyāta iṉpamelām tuṉṉiṉār - aḻaku keṭāta iṉpatti yellām tuyttaṉar (kōvalaṉum kaṇṇaki yum) eṉka. 
maṉiyaṟampaṭutta kāti muṟṟiṟṟu.
3. araṅkēṟṟu kāti  

(kāvirippūm paṭṭiṉattilē kaṇikiyar kulattiṟ ṟōṉṟiya mātavi yeṉpāḷ āṭal pāṭal aḻaku eṉṉum mūṉṟilum ciṟantu viḷaṅkutaṟkēṟṟavaḷāy iruntamiyiṉ, avaḷi aintām āṇṭil taṇṭiyam piṭippittu, ēḻāṇṭu iyaṟṟuvittup paṉṉī rāṇṭeytiyapiṉ aracaṟku avi yaraṅkēṟik kāṭṭali virumpi, āṭalācāṉ, iciyāciriyaṉ, iyaṟṟamiḻp pulavaṉ, taṇṇumi mutalvaṉ, kuḻalōṉ, yāḻāciriyaṉ eṉpōr oruṅku kūṭi, takka nilattilē ciṟpanūṉ muṟippaṭi iyaṟṟap paṭṭatum, viḷakkukaḷ ēṟṟiyum eḻiṉikaḷ vakuttum vitāṉam kaṭṭiyum muttumāli nāṟṟiyum puṉiyappaṭṭatumākiya araṅkiṉ kaṇ, māṟṟaracariṉ kuṭikkāmpu koṇṭu navamaṇi patittiyaṟṟiyatum, aracaṉ kōyilil vaḻipāṭu ceytu iruttappeṟṟatumākiya talik kōliṉi nalla nāḷilē poṟkuṭattēnti vanta puṇṇiya naṉ ṉīrāl maṇṇiya piṉpu māli yaṇintu araca yāṉiyiṉ kiyiṟ koṭuttaṉar. piṉpu avarkaḷ avviyāṉiyuṭaṉ aracaṉum aim peruṅkuḻuvum uṭaṉvara valamāka vantu vītiyilē tēriṉmici niṉṟa pāṭuvāṉ kiyil ataṉik koṭuppittu, nakariyi valam vantu araṅkiṟ pukuntu etirmukamāka ataṉi vittaṉar. vikka, mātaviyāṉavaḷ araṅkilē valakkāli muṉvittu ēṟi, valat tūṇip porunti, aracaṉ mutalāyiṉār aviyil amarnta piṉ kuyiluvak karuvikaḷellām kūṭi niṉṟicikka maṅkalamākiya pālip paṇṇip pāṭi, tēciyum vaṭukumākiya akakkūttu puṟakkūttukkaḷi nūṉmuṟi vaḻuvātu poṉṉā ṉiyaṉṟa pūṅkoṭi naṭippatupōl aviṉayan tōṉṟa āṭik kāṭṭiṉāḷ. ataṉāl avaḷ aracaṉatu paccimāliyum talikkōṟ paṭṭamum peṟṟatuṭaṉ, talivarici yāka āyiratteṇkaḻañcu poṉ pariyam peṟṟāḷ. am māliyi ōr kūṉi kiyiṟkoṭuttu ‘im māli āyiratteṇ kaḻañcu poṉ peṟuvatu ; ivvaḷavu poṉ koṭuttu itaṉip peṟuvōr mātavikku maṇamakaṉāvar’ eṉak kūṟi, nakara nampiyar ulā varum vīti yil niṟkac ceyya, kōvalaṉ am māliyi vāṅkik kūṉiyuṭaṉ mātavi maṉiyi aṭintu avaḷi aṇinta aṉṟē mayaṅki, taṉ maṉiviyiyum maṉiyiyum maṟantu, mātaviyi orupoḻutum viṭṭu nīṅkā viruppuṭiyaṉāyiṉaṉ. (ikkātiyil ici nāṭakaṅ kaḷiṉ iyalpukaḷum, avaṟṟiṟku aṅkamāṉavikaḷum virivākak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa)  
		teyva mālvarit tirumuṉi yaruḷa
eytiya cāpattu intira ciṟuvaṉoṭu
talikkōl tāṉattuc cāpam nīṅkiya
malipparuñ ciṟappiṉ vāṉavar makaḷir
5		ciṟappiṟ kuṉṟāc ceykiyoṭu poruntiya
piṟappiṟ kuṉṟāp peruntōḷ maṭanti
tātaviḻ purikuḻal mātavi taṉṉi
āṭalum pāṭalum aḻakum eṉṟik
kūṟiya mūṉṟi ṉoṉṟukuṟi paṭāmal
10		ēḻāṇ ṭiyaṟṟiyōr īrā ṟāṇṭil
cūḻkaḻal maṉṉaṟkuk kāṭṭal vēṇṭi
iruvakik kūttiṉ ilakkaṇam aṟintu
palavakik kūttum vilakkiṉiṟ puṇarttup
patiṉō rāṭalum pāṭṭuṅ koṭṭum
15		vitimāṇ koḷkiyiṉ viḷaṅka aṟintāṅku
āṭalum pāṭalum pāṇiyun tūkkum
kūṭiya neṟiyiṉa koḷuttuṅ kālip
piṇṭiyum piṇiyalum eḻiṟkiyun toḻiṟkiyuṅ
koṇṭa vakiyintu kūttuvaru kālik
20		kūṭi ceytaki vārattuk kaḷitalum
vārañ ceytaki kūṭiyiṟ kaḷitalum
piṇṭi ceytaki āṭaliṟ kaḷitalum
āṭal ceytaki piṇṭiyiṟ kaḷitalum
kuraviyum variyum viravala celutti
25		āṭaṟ kaminta ācāṉ taṉṉoṭum
yāḻuṅ kuḻaluñ cīrum miṭaṟun
tāḻkural taṇṇumi āṭalo ṭivaṟṟiṉ
icinta pāṭal iciyuṭaṉ paṭuttu
varikkum āṭaṟkum uripporuḷ iyakkit
30		tēcikat tiruviṉ ōci kaṭippiṭittut
tēcikat tiruviṉ ōci yellām
āciṉ ṟuṇarnta aṟiviṉa ṉākik
kaviyatu kuṟippum āṭal tokutiyum
pakutip pāṭaluṅ koḷuttuṅ kāli
35		vaciyaṟu kēḷvi vakuttaṉaṉ virikkum
aciyā marapiṉ iciyoṉ ṟāṉum
imiḻkaṭal varippil tamiḻakam aṟiyat
tamiḻmuḻu taṟinta taṉmiya ṉāki
vēttiyal potuviyal eṉṟiru tiṟattiṉ
40		nāṭṭiya naṉṉūl naṉku kaṭippiṭittu
iciyōṉ vakkirit tiṭṭati yuṇarntāṅku
aciyā marapi ṉatupaṭa vittu
māṟṟōr ceyta vacimoḻi yaṟintu
nāttoli villā naṉṉūṟ pulavaṉum
45		āṭal pāṭal iciyē tamiḻē
paṇṇē pāṇi tūkkē muṭamē
tēcikam eṉṟivi āci ṉuṇarntu
kūṭi nilattik kuṟiviṉṟu mikuttāṅku
vāra nilatti vāṅkupu vāṅki
50		vāṅkiya vārattu yāḻum kuḻalum
ēṅkiya miṭaṟum icivaṉa kēṭpak
kūrukirk karaṇaṅ kuṟiyaṟintu cērtti
ākkalum aṭakkalum mīttiṟam paṭāmic
cittirak karaṇañ citiviṉṟu celuttum
55		attaku taṇṇumi aruntoḻiṉ mutalvaṉuñ
colliya iyalpiṉiṟ cittira vañcaṉi
pulliya aṟintu puṇarppōṉ paṇpiṉ
varttaṉi nāṉkum mayalaṟap peytāṅku
ēṟṟiya kuraliḷi eṉṟiru narampiṉ
60		oppak kēṭkum uṇarviṉa ṉākip
paṇṇami muḻaviṉ kaṇṇeṟi yaṟintu
taṇṇumi mutalvaṉ taṉṉoṭum porunti
vaṇṇap paṭṭaṭi yāḻmēl vittāṅku
iciyōṉ pāṭiya iciyi ṉiyaṟki
65		vantatu vaḷarttu varuvatu oṟṟi
iṉpuṟa iyakki icipaṭa vittu
vāra nilattik kēṭiṉṟu vaḷarttāṅku
īra nilattiṉ eḻutteḻut tāka
vaḻuviṉṟu icikkuṅ kuḻalōṉ ṟāṉum
70		īrēḻ toṭutta cemmuṟik kēḷviyiṉ
orēḻ pāli niṟuttal vēṇṭi
vaṇmiyiṟ kiṭanta tāra pākamum
meṉmiyiṟ kiṭanta kuraliṉ pākamum
meykkiḷi narampiṟ kikkiḷi koḷḷak
75		kikkiḷi yoḻinta pākamum poṟpuṭit
taḷarāt tāram viḷarikku īttuk
kiḷivaḻip paṭṭaṉa ḷāṅkē kiḷiyun
taṉkiḷi aḻivukaṇ ṭavaḷvayiṟ cēra
ēṉi makaḷiruṅ kiḷivaḻic cēra
80		mēlatu uḻiyiḷi kīḻatu kikkiḷi
vampuṟu marapiṟ cempāli yāyatu
iṟuti yāti yāka āṅkavi
peṟumuṟi vanta peṟṟiyiṉ nīṅkātu
paṭumali cevvaḻi pakararum pāliyeṉak
85		kuralkura lākat taṟkiḻami tirintapiṉ
muṉṉataṉ vakiya muṟimiyil tirintāṅku
iḷimuta lākiya etirpaṭu kiḻamiyuṅ
kōṭi viḷari mēṟcem pāliyeṉa
nīṭik kiṭanta kēḷvik kiṭakkiyiṉ
90		iṇinaram puṭiyaṉa aṇivuṟak koṇṭāṅku
yāḻmēṟ pāli iṭamuṟi meliyak
kuḻaṉmēṟ kōṭi valamuṟi meliya
valivum melivuñ camaṉu mellām
poliyak kōtta pulami yōṉuṭaṉ
95		eṇṇiya nūlōr iyalpiṉiṉ vaḻāatu
maṇṇakam oruvaḻivakuttaṉar koṇṭu
puṇṇiya neṭuvarip pōkiya neṭuṅkaḻik
kaṇṇiṭi orucāṇ vaḷarntatu koṇṭu
nūṉeṟi marapiṉ araṅkam aḷakkuṅ
100		kōlaḷa virupattu nālvira lāka
eḻukō lakalat teṇkōl nīḷat
torukōl uyarat tuṟappiṉa tāki
uttarap palakiyō ṭāṅkiṉ palaki
vitta iṭinilam nāṟkō lāka
105		ēṟṟa vāyil iraṇṭuṭaṉ poliyat
tōṟṟiya araṅkil toḻutaṉa rēttap
pūtari yeḻuti mēṉili vittut
tūṇniḻaṟ puṟappaṭa māṇviḷak keṭuttāṅku
orumuka eḻiṉiyum porumuka eḻiṉiyuṅ
110		karantuvara leḻiṉiyum purintuṭaṉ vakuttāṅku
ōviya vitāṉattu uripeṟu nittilattu
mālit tāmam vaḷiyuṭaṉ nāṟṟi
viruntupaṭak kiṭanta aruntoḻil araṅkattup
pērici maṉṉar peyarpuṟat teṭutta
115		cīriyal veṇkuṭik kāmpunaṉi koṇṭu
kaṇṇiṭi navamaṇi yoḻukki maṇṇiya
nāvalam polantakaṭ ṭiṭinilam pōkki
kāval veṇkuṭi maṉṉavaṉ kōyil
intira ciṟuvaṉ cayanta ṉākeṉa
120		vantaṉi ceytu vaḻipaṭu talikkōl
puṇṇiya naṉṉīr poṟkuṭat tēnti
maṇṇiya piṉṉar māli yaṇintu
nalantaru nāḷāṟ polampūṇ ōṭi
aracuvāt taṭakkiyiṟ paraciṉar koṇṭu
125		muraceḻun tiyampap palliya mārppa
aricoṭu paṭṭa aimperuṅ kuḻuvuṅ
tērvalañ ceytu kavikik koṭuppa
ūrvalañ ceytu pukuntumuṉ vittāṅku
iyalpiṉiṉ vaḻāa irukki muṟimiyiṉ
130		kuyiluva mākkaḷ neṟippaṭa niṟpa
valakkāl muṉmitittu ēṟi araṅkattu
valattūṇ cērtal vaḻakkeṉap porunti
inneṟi vakiyāl iṭattūṇ cērnta
toṉṉeṟi iyaṟkit tōriya makaḷirum
135		cīriyal poliya nīrala nīṅka
vāram iraṇṭum variciyiṟ pāṭap
pāṭiya vārattu īṟṟiṉniṉ ṟicikkuṅ
kūṭiya kuyiluvak karuvika ḷellāṅ
kuḻalvaḻi niṉṟatu yāḻē yāḻvaḻit
140		taṇṇumi niṉṟatu takavē taṇṇumip
piṉvaḻi niṉṟatu muḻavē muḻavoṭu
kūṭiniṉ ṟicitta tāman tiriki
āman tirikiyō ṭantara miṉṟik
koṭṭiraṇ ṭuṭiyatōr maṇṭila mākak
145		kaṭṭiya maṇṭilam patiṉoṉṟu pōkki
vanta muṟiyiṉ vaḻimuṟi vaḻāmal
antarak koṭṭuṭaṉ aṭaṅkiya piṉṉar
mīttiṟam paṭāmi vakkāṇam vakuttup
pāṟpaṭa niṉṟa pālip paṇamēl
150		nāṉkiṉ orīiya naṉkaṉam aṟintu
mūṉṟaḷan toṉṟu koṭṭi ataṉi
aitumaṇ ṭilattāṟ kūṭi pōkki
vantavā ramvaḻi mayaṅkiya piṉṟi
āṟum nālum ammuṟi pōkkik
155		kūṟiya aintiṉ koḷki pōlap
piṉṉiyum ammuṟi pēriya piṉṟip
poṉṉiyal pūṅkoṭi purintuṭaṉ vakutteṉa
nāṭṭiya naṉṉūl naṉkukaṭip piṭittuk
kāṭṭiṉaḷ ātaliṟ kāval vēntaṉ
160		ilippūṅ kōti iyalpiṉiṉ vaḻāmit
talikkōl eytit taliyaraṅ kēṟi
vitimuṟik koḷkiyiṉ āyirat teṇkaḻañcu
orumuṟi yākap peṟṟaṉaḷ atuvē
nūṟupat taṭukki eṭṭukkaṭi niṟutta
165		vīṟuyar pacumpoṉ peṟuvatim māli
māli vāṅkunar cālunaṅ koṭikkeṉa
māṉamar nōkkiyōr kūṉikik koṭuttu
nakara nampiyar tiritaru maṟukiṟ
pakarvaṉar pōlvatōr pāṉmiyiṉ niṟutta
170		māmalar neṭuṅkaṇ mātavi māli
kōvalaṉ vāṅkik kūṉi taṉṉoṭu
maṇamaṉi pukku mātavitaṉṉoṭu
aṇivuṟu vikaliṉ ayantaṉaṉ mayaṅki
viṭuta laṟiyā viruppiṉ ṉāyiṉaṉ
175	vaṭunīṅku ciṟappiṉtaṉ maṉiyakam maṟanteṉ.
veṇpā
		eṇṇum eḻuttum iyalintum paṇṇāṉkum
paṇṇiṉṟa kūttup patiṉoṉṟum  maṇṇiṉmēṟ
pōkkiṉāḷ pūmpukārp poṟṟoṭi mātavitaṉ
vākkiṉāl āṭaraṅkiṉ vantu.
uri
kūttiyatu amiti
1 - 7 		teyva mālvarit tirumuṉi aruḷa - teyvat taṉmi yuṭiya periya maliyākiya potiyiliṉkaṇ uḷḷa akattiya muṉivaṉ aruḷ ceytalāl, eytiya cāpattu intira ciṟuva ṉoṭu - muṉpu avaṉāl eytiya cāpattiyuṭiya intiraṉ kumāraṉākiya cayantaṉōṭum, talikkōl tāṉattuc cāpam nīṅ kiya - nāṭaka araṅkiṉ kaṇṇē cāpam nīṅkap peṟṟa, malip paruñ ciṟappiṉ vāṉavar makaḷir ciṟappiṟ kuṉṟāc ceyki yoṭu poruntiya - māṟupaṭuttalariya ciṟappiṉi yuṭiya aram piyaril variciyiṟ kuṉṟāta nāṭakat toḻi loṭu porun tiya uruppaciyākiya am mātavi marapil vanta, piṟappiṟ kuṉ ṟāp peruntōḷ maṭanti - piṟappiṟ kuṉṟuta lillāta periya tōḷiyuṭiya maṭantiyākiya, tātu aviḻ purikuḻal mātavi taṉṉi - tātuviriyum pūkkaḷi yaṇinta kaṭi kuḻaṉṟu curuṇṭa kūntaliyuṭiya mātaviyi,
muṉivaruṭ ciṟantōṉākaliṉ akattiyaṉit tirumuṉi eṉṟār. aruḷa - cāpamiṭṭaruḷa veṉṟumām. talikkōṟṟāṉam - nāṭaka varaṅku. intira ciṟuvaṉoṭu cāpam nīṅkiya uruppaci yeṉka. ‘ceykiyoṭu poruntiya’ eṉpataṉpiṉ, uruppaciyākiya am mātavi marapilvanta eṉa viritturikka. ‘cāpa nīṅkiya’ eṉpataṉ piṉ aṅṅaṉam viritturittu, vāṉavar makaḷir eṉpataṟkut taḷiyilār eṉṟu poruḷ kūṟuvar arumpatavuriyāciriyar. cayantaṉum, uruppaciyum cāpam peṟṟatum, atiṉīṅkiyatumākiya varalāṟu piṉṉark   1kaṭalāṭu kātiyāṉum,  aṭiyārkku nallār eṭuttuk kāṭṭiya paḻiya mēṟkōḷāṉum, viḷakka
muṟum; āṇṭuk kāṇka. aruḷāl eṉpatūum pāṭam. 
8 - 9 	āṭalum pāṭalum aḻakum eṉṟu ik kūṟiya mūṉṟiṉ oṉṟu kuṟipaṭāmal - kūttum pāṭṭum aḻakumeṉṟu collappaṭṭa im mūṉṟiṉuḷ oṉṟum kuṟivupaṭāmal, ivi kuṟivu paṭāmikkuk kāraṇamuṭiyaḷākaliṉ eṉka..
10 - 11 	ēḻāṇṭu iyaṟṟi ōr īrāṟu āṇṭil cūḻkaḻal maṉṉaṟkuk kāṭṭal vēṇṭi - aiyāṇṭil taṇṭiyam piṭip pittu ēḻāṇṭu iyaṟṟuvittup paṉṉīrāṇṭil vīrakkaḻal cūḻnta kāliṉiyuṭiya aracaṟku avaṉatu aviyaraṅkēṟik kāṭṭali virumpi, taṇṭiyam - kōl. maṉṉaṉ - cōḻaṉ karikāṟperuvaḷat tāṉ eṉpar arumpatavuriyāciriyarum aṭiyārkku nallārum. īṇṭuk karikālaṉ eṉṟu peyar kūṟappaṭāmiyāṉum,
2" ceruveṅ kātaliṉ tirumā vaḷavaṉ
puṇṇiyat ticimukam pōkiya vannāḷ"
eṉa intiraviḻavūreṭutta kātiyiṉum,
3"	maṉṉaṉ karikāl vaḷavaṉnīṅ kiyanāḷ 
innakar pōlvatōr iyalpiṉa tāki"
eṉa maṇimēkali yuḷḷum karikālaṉ vaṭaticikkaṭ paṭiyeṭuttuc ceṉṟami kūṟappaṭ ṭiruttalaṉṟi, nikaḻkālattil vittu avaṉ yāṇṭuṅ kūṟappaṭāmiyāṉum, maturik kāṇṭat tiṟutik kaṭṭuriyil pāṇṭiyaṉ neṭuñceḻiyaṉiyum, vañcik kāṇṭat tiṟutik kaṭṭuriyil cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉiyum kiḷantōtum aṭikaḷ pukārk kāṇṭat tiṟutik kaṭṭuriyil cōḻaṉoruvaṉiyum peyar kuṟittuk kūṟāmiyāṉum karikālaṉ appoḻutiruntā ṉeṉṟu tuṇital cālāteṉka.
paratacēṉāpatiyār kūṟiya, piṉvarum veṇpākkaḷ īṇṭu aṟiyaṟpālaṉa     :
"	paṇṇiyamvit tāṉimukaṉ pātam paṇintunāṭ
puṇṇiya vōri pukaṉṟaṉakoṇ-ṭeṇṇiyē
vaṇṭirukkuṅ kūntaṉ maṭavarali aiyāṇṭil
taṇṭiyañcēr vippatē cālpu"
"	vaṭṭaṇiyun tūciyu maṇṭalamum paṇṇamiya
eṭṭuṭa ṉīriraṇṭāṇ ṭeytiyapiṉ-kaṭṭaḷiya
kītak kuṟippum alaṅkāra muṅkiḷarac
cōtit taraṅkēṟac cūḻ"
"	naṉṉar viruppuṭiyōḷ naṟkuṇamu mikkuyarntōḷ
coṉṉakulat tālaminta toṉmiyaḷāyp-paṉṉiraṇṭāṇ
ṭēyntataṟpiṉ āṭaluṭaṉ pāṭalaḻa kimmūṉṟum
vāyntavaraṅ kēṟṟal vaḻakku."
(āṭalāciriyaṉ amiti)
12 - 25 	iruvakik kūttiṉ ilakkaṇam aṟintu - akakkūttum puṟakkūttumākiya iruvakik kūttiṉilak kaṇaṅkaḷiyum aṟintu, palavakik kūttum vilakkiṉiṟ puṇarttu - avaṟṟiṉ pakutikaḷākiya pala kūttukkaḷiyum vilakkuṟuppuk kaḷuṭaṉ puṇarkka vallaṉāy, patiṉōr āṭalum pāṭṭum koṭṭum - alliyam mutaṟ koṭukoṭṭi yīṟāyk kiṭanta teyva virutti yākiya patiṉoru kūttukkaḷiyum akkūttuk kaḷukkuriya pāṭṭukkaḷiyum akkūttukkaḷiṉ vikaṟpaṅkaḷuk kellām aminta vākkiyaṅkaḷiṉ kūṟukaḷiyum, vitimāṇ koḷkiyiṉ viḷaṅka aṟintu āṅku - vitittal māṭcimip paṭṭa nūliṉvaḻiyē viḷaṅka vaṟintu, āṭalum pāṭalum pāṇiyum tūkkum kūṭiya neṟiyiṉa koḷut tuṅkāli - āṭalum pāṭalum tāḷaṅkaḷum tāḷaṅkaḷiṉ vaḻi varum tūkkukkaḷum tammiṟ kūṭiya neṟiyiṉavāka nikaḻttumiṭattu, piṇṭiyum piṇiyalum eḻiṟkiyum toḻiṟkiyum koṇṭavaki aṟintu - piṇṭi piṇiyal eḻiṟki toḻiṟki eṉṟu collappaṭṭa nāṉkiṉiyum koḷḷutaṟkuriya vakiyiṉi aṟintu, kūttu varukāli - iruvakik kūttukkaḷum nikaḻu miṭattu, kūṭi ceyta ki vārattuk kaḷitalum vāram ceyta ki kūṭiyiṟ kaḷitalum-kūṭik katiyākac ceyta ki vārak katiyuṭ pukāmalum vārak katiyākac ceyta ki kūṭik katiyuṭ pukāmalum kaḷitalum, piṇṭi ceyta ki āṭaliṟ kaḷitalum āṭal ceyta ki piṇṭiyiṟ kaḷitalum - āṭal nikaḻuṅkāl avinayam nikaḻāmalum avinayam nikaḻuṅkāl āṭal nikaḻāmalum kaḷitalum pēṇi, kuraviyum variyum viravala celutti, kuravik kūttum varikkūttum tammil viravātapaṭi celutti, āṭaṟku aminta ācāṉ taṉṉoṭum - ivvāṟu āṭavum āṭṭuvikkavum valla āṭalāciriyaṉōṭum;
arumpata vuriyāciriyarum aṭiyārkkunallārum iruvakik kūttu eṉpataṟku iruvakippaṭṭa akakkūttu eṉṟum, palavakik kūttu eṉpataṟkup palavakippaṭṭa puṟa naṭaṅkaḷ eṉṟum poruḷ kūṟiṉar. avaruḷ muṉṉavar, “iruvakik kūttāvaṉa; tēci, mārkkam eṉa vivi; ‘mārkkameṉpatu, vaṭukiṉ peyarē’ ‘eṉṟum, piṉṉavar, ''ivvakik kūttāvaṉa; vacik kūttu, pukaḻkkūttu; vēttiyal, potuviyal; varikkūttu; varic cāntik kūttu; cāntik kūttu, vinōtak kūttu; āriyam, tamiḻ; iyalpuk kūttu, tēcik kūttu; eṉap palavakiya" eṉṟum, "īṇṭu iruvakik kūttāvaṉa; cāntiyum, vinōtamum" eṉṟum viḷakkam kūṟiṉar. akakkūttellām tēci, mārkkam eṉa aṭaṅkāmiyāṉum, vaci, vari, vinōtam mutaliyaṉa akakkūttuḷ aṭaṅkāmiyāṉum, iruvakik kūttu eṉpataṟku akamum puṟamumākiya iruvakik kūttu eṉṟalē poruttamākum. iraṇṭiraṇṭāka eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyavi yellām vevvēṟiyalpu paṟṟi vēṟu vēṟu peyarāṉ vaḻaṅkappaṭuvaṉa vaṉṟi, vevvēṟu kūttukkaḷalla. ivvāṟṟāl aviyellāvaṟṟiyum akam, puṟam eṉṟātal, vēttiyal, potuviyal eṉṟātal, cānti, vinōtam eṉṟātal pākupaṭuttal poruntum.
vēttiyal - aracark kāṭuvatu. potuviyal-ēṉiyōrk kāṭuvatu. vēttiyali akameṉṟum, potuviyalip puṟameṉṟum kūṟuvārumuḷar.
"	cāntik kūttē taliva ṉiṉpam
ēntiniṉ ṟāṭiya vīriru naṭamavi
cokka meyyē yavinaya nāṭakam
eṉṟip pāṟpaṭūu meṉmaṉār pulavar";
eṉpataṉāl, cāntikkūttu nālvakippaṭum; avaṟṟuḷ, cokkam eṉpatu cutta niruttam; āvatu tāḷa layatti ātāramāka vuṭiyatu. meykkūttu eṉpatu meyttoḻiṟ kūttu eṉṟum, atu tēci, vaṭuku, ciṅkaḷam eṉa mūvakippaṭum eṉṟum kūṟuvar. iḵtu uḷḷak kuṟippi ātāramākak koṇṭatu pōlum. avinayak kūttu eṉpatu kati taḻuvātu pāṭṭiṉ poruḷukkuk ki kāṭṭi vallapañ ceyyuṅ kūttu. nāṭakam eṉpatu kati taḻuvi varuṅkūttu. in nāṉkiṉum iṟutikkaṇ niṉṟa nāṭakam ciṟantatātal ōrntuṇarka. ivi yāvum nāyakaṉ cāntamāka āṭiya kūttākaliṉ cāntik kūtteṉappaṭum eṉpar.
vinōtak kūttu eṉpatil kuravi, kalinaṭam, kuṭakkūttu, karaṇam, nōkku, tōṟpāvi eṉpaṉa aṭaṅkumeṉpar. avaṟṟuḷ,
 kuravi yeṉpatu kāmamum veṉṟiyum poruḷāka kuravic ceyyuḷ pāṭṭāka eḻuvarēṉum eṇmarēṉum oṉpatiṉ marēṉum kipiṇintāṭuvatu.
kalinaṭam eṉpatu kaḻāyk kūttu.
kuṭakkūt teṉpatu patiṉōrāṭaluḷ oṉṟu.
karaṇamāvatu paṭintavāṭal.
nōkkeṉpatu pāramum nuṇmiyum māyamum mutalāyiṉa vaṟṟiyuṭiyatu.
tōṟpāvi yeṉpatu tōlāṟpāvi ceytu āṭṭuvippatu, iṉṉum nakittiṟac cuviyuṭiya vitūṭakak kūttiṉōṭu ēḻeṉpārum, veṟiyāṭṭu mutalākat teyvamēṟi yāṭuṅ kūttiṉik kūṭṭi ēḻeṉpāru muḷar. vitūṭakak kūttu - vacik kūttu; atu vēttiyal, potuviyal eṉa iraṇṭu vakippaṭu meṉpar.
 12. 		iruvakik kūttiṉ ilakkaṇaṅkaḷāvaṉa; "aṟuvaki niliyum aivakip pātamum, īreṇ vakiya aṅkak kiriyiyum varuttaṉi nāṉkum niruttakki muppatum, attaku toḻila vāku meṉpa" eṉṟōtappaṭṭaṉa.
13. 		palavakik kūttum eṉṟatu mēlē kūṟappaṭṭa akakkūttu mutaliyavaṟṟuṭaṉ veṉṟik kūttum, vacikkūttu mākum. "palvaki yeṉpatu pakaruṅkāli, veṉṟi vaciyē vinōta mākum" eṉpatoru cūttiraṅkāṭṭuvar. avaṟṟuḷ, "māṟṟā ṉoṭukkamum maṉṉa ṉuyarcciyum. mēṟpaṭak kūṟum veṉṟik kūttē" eṉavum, "palvaki yuruvamum paḻittuk kāṭṭa, valla ṉātal vaciyeṉap paṭumē" eṉavum veṉṟi, vacik kūttukaṭku ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟuvar.
vilakkiṉiṉ - vilakkuṟuppōṭu; vēṟṟumi mayakkam. vilakkuṟuppu eṉṟa coṟkup poruḷ, vēntu vilakku, paṭi vilakku, ūrvilakku eṉṉum vilakkukkaḷākiya pāṭṭukkaḷukku uṟuppāy varuvatu eṉṟum, talivaṉ celuttukiṉṟa katiyi vilakkiyum ak katiyi naṭāttiyum muṉpu ceyta katikkē uṟuppākuvatu eṉṟum kūṟuvar;
“	vilakkuṟup peṉpatu virikkuṅ kālip
poruḷum yōṉiyum viruttiyuñ cantiyum
cuviyuñ cātiyuṅ kuṟippuñ cattuvamum
avinayañ collē colvaki vaṇṇamum
variyuñ cētamum uḷappaṭat tokii
iciya veṇṇi ṉīrē ḻuṟuppē"
eṉṉuñ cūttirattāl, vilakkuṟuppukkaḷ patiṉāṉku vakiyiṉa vātal peṟappaṭum.
ippatiṉāṉkaṉuḷ poruḷ, cāti, yōṉi, virutti eṉṉum nāṉkum oruvakiyum, cuvi, kuṟippu, cattuvam, avinayam eṉṉum nāṉkum oruvakiyum, col, colvaki, vaṇṇam, vari eṉṉum nāṉkum oruvakiyum, canti. cētam eṉṉum iraṇṭum oruvakiyumākap pākupaṭum. 
avaṟṟuḷ, poruḷ nāṉku vakippaṭum; avi - aṟam, poruḷ, iṉpam, vīṭu eṉpaṉa. ivi nāṭakattiṟ kūṭiyum kuṟintum varumiṭattu nāṭakam, pirakaraṇap pirakaraṇam, pirakaraṇam, aṅkam eṉap peyar vēṟupaṭum. aṟamutal nāṉkum amintatu mutalatum, aṟam poruḷ iṉpam amintatu iraṇṭāvatum, aṟam poruḷ amintatu mūṉṟāvatum, aṟamoṉṟum amintatu nāṉkāvatum ām; ivi nāṉkum nāṭakamē.ivitām muṟiyē antaṇar mutaliya cātikaḷākavum kūṟappaṭum.
yōṉiyāvatu poruḷ tōṉṟumiṭam. uḷḷōṉ talivaṉāka uḷḷatōr poruṇmēṟ ceytalum. illōṉ talivaṉāka uḷḷatōr poruṇmēṟ ceytalum, uḷḷōṉ talivaṉāka illatōr poruṇmēṟ ceytalum, illōṉ talivaṉāka illatōr poruṇmēṟ ceytalum eṉa atu nāṉku vakip paṭum; eṉṉi?
	uḷḷōṟ kuḷḷatum illōṟ kuḷḷatum
uḷḷōṟ killatum illōṟ killatum 
eḷḷā turittal yōṉi yākum,
eṉṟārākaliṉ.
viruttiyāvatu nāṭakattiṉ iyalpu allatu taṉmi. cāttuvati, ārapaṭi, kiciki, pārati eṉa virutti nālvakippaṭum. avaṟṟuḷ, cāttuvati yeṉpatu aṟam poruḷākat teyvamāṉiṭar talivarāka varuvatu. ārapaṭi yeṉpatu poruḷ poruḷāka vīrarākiya māṉiṭar talivarāka varuvatu. kiciki yeṉpatu kāmam poruḷākak kāmukarākiya makkaḷ talivarāka varuvatu. pāratiyāvatu kūttaṉ talivaṉāka naṭaṉ naṭi poruḷākak kāṭṭiyum urittum varuvatu. cuvi oṉpatu vakippaṭum; avi - vīram, accam, iḻippu, viyappu, kāmam, avalam, vekuḷi, naki, camaṉili eṉpaṉa. vēmpeṉṉum poruḷum nāveṉṉum poṟiyum kūṭiyavaḻi nāvāl uṇarappaṭum kippuc cuvi pōlac cuvikkappaṭum poruḷum ataṉic cuvikkum poṟiyuṅ kūṭiyavaḻip piṟakkum poṟiyuṇarvu cuviyeṉappaṭum.
kuṟippāvatu ac cuvi yuṇarvu maṉattup paṭṭavaḻi uḷḷattē nikaḻum kuṟippākum.
cattuvamāvatu uḷḷattu nikaḻuṅ kuṟippiṉuk kēṟpa uṭam piṉkaṇ nikaḻum vēṟupāṭu. itu viṟal eṉavum paṭum. meym mayir cilirttal, kaṇṇīr vārtal, 1naṭukkamaṭuttal, viyarttal. tēṟṟam, kaḷittal, viḻittal, vetumpal, cākkāṭu, kuraṟcitivu eṉac cattuvam pattu vakippaṭum eṉpar. vaṭanūlār cattuvatti eṇvakippaṭuttuc ciṟitu vēṟupaṭa vurippar. cuvip poruḷ, poṟiyuṇarvu, kuṟippu, viṟal eṉṉum nāṉkuṅ kūṭiyapoḻutu cuvi yeṉṉum meyppāṭu tōṉṟum.
oṉpāṉ cuvikaṭkuriya viṟalkaḷ avvac cuvi yavinayaṅkaḷ eṉavum paṭum. avaṟṟip piṉvaruñ cūttiraṅkaḷāl aṟika.
"vīraccuvi yavinayam viḷampuṅkāli, murintapuruvamuñ civanta kaṇṇum, piṭitta vāḷuṅ kaṭitta veyiṟum, maṭitta vutaṭuñ curuṭṭiya nutalum, tiṇṇeṉa vuṟṟa collum pakivari, eṇṇal cellā vikaḻcciyum piṟavum. naṇṇu meṉpa naṉkuṇarntōrē."
acca vavinayam āyuṅ kāli, oṭuṅkiya vuṭampum naṭuṅkiya niliyum, malaṅkiya kaṇṇuṅ kalaṅkiya vuḷaṉum, karantuvara luṭimiyuṅ kiyetir maṟuttalum, paranta nōkkamu micipaṇ piṉavē."
“iḻippi ṉavinayam iyampuṅ kāli, iṭuṅkiya kaṇṇu meyiṟupuṟam pōtalum, oṭuṅkiya mukamu muñaṟṟāk kālum, cōrnta yākkiyuñ coṉṉiram pāmiyum, nērntaṉa veṉpa neṟiyaṟintōrē."
aṟputa vavinayam aṟivarak kiḷappiṉ, coṟcōr vuṭiyatu cōrnta kiyatu, meymmayir kuḷirppatu viyattaka vuṭiyatu, eytiya timittalum viḻittalu mikavāteṉ, ṟiyamil pulava raṟintaṉa reṉpa."
''kāma vavinayaṅ karutuṅ kālit, tūvuḷ ḷuṟutta vaṭivun toḻilum, kāriki kalanta kaṭikkaṇuṅ kaviṉpeṟu, mūraṉ muṟuval ciṟunilā varumpalum, malarnta mukaṉu mirantameṉ kiḷaviyum, kalantaṉa piṟavuṅ kaṭippiṭit taṉarē"
"avalat tavinayam aṟivarak kiḷappiṉ, kavaliyoṭu puṇarnta  kaṇṇīr māriyum, vāṭiya nīrmiyum varuntiya celavum, pīṭaḻiyiṭumpiyum pitaṟṟiya collum, niṟiki yaḻitalum nīrmiyil kiḷaviyum, poṟiyiṉ ṟākalum puṇarttiṉar pulavar."
vekuḷiccuvi yavinaya" tti uṇarttuñ cūttiram citintu viṭṭatu. ''kipiciyā vāymaṭiyāk kaṇcivavā veytuyirā, meykuliyā vērā vekuṇṭeḻuntāṉ" eṉṉum taṇṭiyalaṅ kāra mēṟkōḷāl ataṉiyaṟika.
nakiyi ṉavinayam nāṭṭuṅ kāli, mikipaṭu nakiyatu piṟarnakiyuṭiyatu, kōṭṭiya mukattatu..... viṭṭumuri puruvamoṭu vilāvuṟuppuṭiyatu, ceyvatu piṟitāy vēṟucē tippateṉ, ṟiyamil pulavaraṟintaṉa reṉpa.
"nāṭṭuṅ kāli naṭuvunili yavinayam, kōṭpā ṭaṟiyāk koḷkiyu māṭciyum, aṟantaru neñcamum āṟiya viḻiyum, piṟaḻnta kāṭci nīṅkiya niliyum, kuṟippiṉ ṟākalun tuṇukka millāt, takimika vuṭimiyun taṇṇeṉa vuṭimiyum, aḷattaṟ karumiyu maṉpoṭu puṇartalum, kalakkamoṭu puṇarnta nōkkuṅ katirppum, vilakkā reṉpa vēṇṭumoḻip pulavar."
avinayam eṉpatu pāvakam. muṟkuṟitta cuvinili yavinayaṅka ḷaṉṟi vēṟu irupattu nāṉku avinayaṅkaḷ uḷa. avi; vekuṇṭōṉ avinayam, aiyamuṟṟōṉ avinayam, cōmpiṉōṉ avinayam, kaḷittōṉ avinayam, uvantōṉ avinayam, aḻukkā ṟuṭiyōṉ avinayam, iṉpamuṟṟōṉ avinayam, teyva muṟṟōṉ avinayam, ñaññiyuṟṟōṉ avinayam, uṭaṉ paṭṭōṉ avinayam, uṟaṅkiṉōṉ avinayam, tuyiluṇarn tōṉ avinayam, cettōṉ avinayam, maḻi peyyappaṭṭōṉ avinayam, paṉit talippaṭṭōṉ avinayam veyiṟṟalip paṭṭōṉ avinayam, nāṇamuṟṟōṉ avinayam, varutta muṟṟōṉ avinayam, kaṇṇō vuṟṟōṉ avinayam, tali nōvuṟṟōṉ avinayam, aḻaṟṟiṟampaṭṭōṉ avinayam, cītamuṟṟōṉ avinayam, veppamuṟṟōṉ. avinayam, nañcuṇṭōṉ avinayam eṉpaṉa. ivi yaṉittiṟkum aṭiyārkku nallār kāṭṭiya nūṟpākkaḷ varumāṟu;
''vekuṇṭōṉ avinayam viḷampuṅ kāli, maṭitta vāyu malarnta mārpun, tuṭitta puruvamuñ cuṭṭiya viralum, kaṉṟiṉa uḷḷamoṭu kipuṭit tiṭutalum, aṉṉa nōkkamō ṭāyntaṉar koḷalē";
"poyyil kāṭcip pulavōr āynta, aiya muṟṟōṉ avinayam urippiṉ, vāṭiya uṟuppum mayaṅkiya nōkkamum, pīṭaḻi pulaṉum pēcā tiruttalum, piṟaḻnta ceykiyum vāṉṟici nōkkalum, aṟintaṉar piṟavum aṟintici ṉōrē." ;
"maṭiyiṉ avinayam vakukkuṅ kāli, noṭiyoṭu palakoṭ ṭāvimika vuṭimiyum, mūri nimirttalum muṉivoṭu puṇartaluṅ, kāraṇa miṉṟi yāḻntumaṭin tiruttalum, piṇiyu miṉṟic cōrnta celavō, ṭaṇitaru pulavar āyntaṉa reṉpa";
"kaḷittōṉ avinayam kaḻaṟuṅ kāli, oḷittavi oḷiyāṉ uritta liṉmiyum, kaviḻntuñ cōrntun tāḻntun taḷarntum, vīḻnta colloṭu miḻaṟṟic cāytalum, kaḷikik kavarnta kaṭikkaṇōk kuṭimiyum, pērici yāḷar pēṇiṉarkoḷalē" ;
"uvantōṉ avinayam urikkuṅ kāli, nivantiṉi tākiya kaṇmala ruṭimiyum, iṉiti ṉiyaṉṟa uḷḷa muṭimiyum muṉivi ṉakaṉṟa muṟuvaṉaki yuṭimiyum, irukkiyuñ cēṟaluṅ kāṉamum piṟavum, oruṅkuṭa ṉaminta kuṟippiṟ ṟaṉṟē" ;
"aḻukkā ṟuṭiyōṉ avinayam urippiṉ, iḻukkoṭu puṇarnta icipporu ḷuṭimiyuṅ, kūmpiya vāyuṅ kōṭiya uriyum, ōmpātu vitirkkum kivaki yuṭimiyum, āraṇaṅkākiya vekuḷi uṭimiyuṅ, kāraṇa miṉṟi melintamuka muṭimiyu, melivoṭu puṇarnta iṭumpiyu mēvarap, poliyu meṉpa poruntu moḻip pulavar" ;
"iṉpamoṭu puṇarntōṉ avinayam iyampiṉ, tuṉpam nīṅkit tuvartta yākkiyun, tayaṅkit tāḻnta perumakiḻ vuṭimiyum, mayaṅki vanta celavunaṉi yuṭimiyum, aḻakuḷ ḷuṟutta coṟpoli vuṭimiyum, eḻiloṭu puṇarnta naṟumala ruṭimiyum, kalaṅkaḷcērn takaṉṟa tōṇmār puṭimiyum, nalaṅkeḻu pulavar nāṭiṉa reṉpa" ;
1"teyva muṟṟōṉ avinayam ceppiṟ, kiviṭ. ṭeṟinta kalakka muṭimiyum, maṭitteyiṟu kuviya vāyttoḻi luṭimiyum, tuṭitta puruvamun tuḷaṅkiya niliyuñ, ceyya mukamuñ cērnta cerukkum, eytu meṉpa iyalpuṇarn tōrē";
2"ñaññi yuṟṟō ṉavinayam nāṭil, paṉmeṉ ṟiṟukiya nāvaḻi vuṭimiyum, nuri cērntu kūmpum vāyum nōkkiṉark, kurippōṉ pōla uṇarvi lāmiyum, viḻippōṉ pōla viḻiyā tiruttalum, viḻuttaka vuṭimiyum oḻukki lāmiyum vayaṅkiya tirumukam aḻuṅkalum piṟavum, mēviya teṉpa viḷaṅku moḻip pulavar" ;
"cintiyuṭam paṭṭōṉ avinayam teriyiṉ, munti yāyiṉum uṇarā nilimiyum, piṭitta kimēl aṭitta kaviṉum, muṭitta luṟāta karuma nilimiyuñ, colvatu yātum uṇarā nilimiyum, pullu meṉpa poruntumoḻip pulavar";
"tuñcā niṉṟōṉ avinayam tuṇiyiṉ, eñcutaliṉṟi iru puṭi maruṅku, malarntuṅ kaviḻntum varupaṭi yiyaṟṟiyu, malarntuyirp puṭiya āṟṟalu mākum" ;
"iṉṉuyi luṇarntōṉ avinayam iyampiṉ, oṉṟiya kuṟuṅ koṭṭāviyum uyirppun, tūṅkiya mukamun tuḷaṅkiya uṭampum, ōṅkiya tiripum oḻintavuṅ koḷalē" ;
"cettōṉ avinayam ceppuṅ kāli, attaka accamum aḻippum ākkalum, kaṭitta nirippaliṉ veṭittup poṭittup, pōntatuṇi vuṭimiyum valitta uṟuppum, melinta vakaṭu meṉmimika vuṭimiyum, veṇmaṇi tōṉṟak karumaṇi karattalum uṇmiyiṟ pulavar uṇarnta vāṟē";
"maḻi peyyap paṭṭōṉ avinayam vakukkiṉ, iḻitaka vuṭiya iyalpunaṉi yuṭimiyum, meykūr naṭukkamum piṇittalum paṭātti, meypūṇ ṭoṭukkiya mukattoṭu puṇarttalum, oḷippaṭu maṉṉi lulaṟiya kaṇṇum, viḷiyiṉun tuḷiyiṉu maṭinta cevi yuṭimiyuṅ, koṭukiviṭ ṭeṟinta kuḷirmika vuṭimiyum, naṭuṅku palloli yuṭimiyu muṭiyak kaṉavukaṇ ṭāṟṟā ṉeḻutalu muṇṭē" ;
"paṉittalip paṭṭōṉ avinayam pakariṉ, naṭukka muṭimiyum nakipaṭu nilimiyuñ, coṟṟaḷarn ticittalu maṟṟami lavatiyum, pōrvi viḻitalum puntinō vuṭimiyum, 1nīṟām viḻiyuñ cēṟu muṉitalum, iṉṉavi piṟavum icintaṉar koḷalē" ;
"uccip poḻutiṉ vantōṉ avinayam, ecca miṉṟi iyampuṅkālic, coriyā niṉṟa peruntuya ruḻantu, teriyā niṉṟa uṭam peri yeṉṉac, civanta kaṇṇum ayarnta nōkkamum, payanta teṉpa paṇpuṇarn tōrē" ;
"nāṇa muṟṟōṉ avinayam nāṭiṉ, iṟiñciya taliyum maṟinta ceykiyum, vāṭiya mukamuṅ kōṭiya uṭampuṅ, keṭṭa voḷiyuṅ kīḻkkaṇ ṇōkkamum, ōṭṭiṉa reṉpa uṇarntici ṉōrē";
"virutta muṟṟōṉ avinayam vakuppiṟ, porutta millāp puṉka ṇuṭimiyuñ, cōrnta yākkiyuñ cōrnta muṭiyuṅ kūrnta viyarvuṅ kuṟumpal luyāvum, vaṟṟiya vāyum vaṇaṅkiya uṟuppum, uṟṟa teṉpa uṇarntici ṉōrē" ;
"kaṇṇō vuṟṟōṉ avinayam kāṭṭi, ṉaṇṇiya kaṇṇīrt tuḷiviral teṟittalum, vaḷintapuru vattoṭu vāṭiya mukamum, veḷḷiṭi nōkkiṉ viḻitaru maccamun, teḷḷitiṟ pulavar  teḷintaṉar koḷalē" ;
"talinō vuṟṟōṉ avinayam cāṟṟiṉ, nilimi yiṉṟit taliyāṭ ṭuṭimiyuṅ, kōṭiya virukkiyun taḷarnta vēroṭu, peruvira liṭukkiya nutalum varunti, oṭuṅkiya kaṇṇoṭu piṟavun, tiruntu meṉpa cenneṟip pulavar" ;
"aḻaṟṟiṟam paṭṭōṉ avinayam urippiṉ, niḻaṟṟiṟam vēṇṭum neṟimiyiṉ viruppum, aḻalum veyiluñ cuṭarum añcalum, niḻalum nīruñ cēṟu muvattalum, paṉinī ruvappum pātirit toṭiyalum, nuṉivira līra maruneṟi yākkalum, pukka tuṉpōṭu pularnta yākkiyun, tokka teṉpa tuṇivaṟin tōrē" ;
"cīta muṟṟōṉ avinayam ceppiṉ, ōtiya paruvara luḷḷamō ṭuḻattalu, mīra mākiya pōrvi yuṟuttalu, māra veyiluḻan taḻalum vēṇṭalu, muraciyu muraṉṟu muyirttu murittalun, takkaṉa piṟavuñ cāṟṟiṉar pulavar" ;
"veppiṉ avinayam virikkuṅ kālit, tappil kaṭip piṭit taṉmiyun tākamum, eriyiṉaṉṉa vemmiyō ṭiyivum, veruvaru miyakkamum vempiya viḻiyum, nīruṇ vēṭkiyu nirampā valiyum, ōruṅ kāli uṇarntaṉar koḷalē" ; 
"koñciya moḻiyiṟ vāyiṟ paṉinuri kūmpalun, tañca māntar tammuka nōkkiyōr, iṉco liyampuvāṉ pōliyam pāmiyum, nañcuṇ ṭōṉṟaṉ avinayam eṉpa" ;
"colliya vaṉṟiyum varuvaṉa vuḷaveṉiṟ, pulluvaḻic cērttip poruntuvaḻip puṇarppa."
iṉi, col eṉṉum uṟuppu mūṉṟu vakippaṭum. avi; uṭcol, puṟaccol, ākāyac col eṉpaṉa. neñcoṭu kūṟal uṭcollum, kēṭpōrk kurittal puṟaccollum, tāṉē kūṟal ākāyaccollum ām.
colvaki  nāṉku vakippaṭum. avi; cuṇṇam, curitakam, vaṇṇam, varitakam eṉpaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ, cuṇṇam nāṉkaṭiyāṉ varuvatu; curitakam eṭṭaṭiyāṉ varuvatu; vaṇṇam patiṉāṟaṭiyāṉ varuvatu; varitakam muppattiraṇṭaṭiyāṉ varuvatu.
vaṇṇamāṉatu oru vakiyāṉ peruvaṇṇam, iṭi vaṇṇam, vaṉappu vaṇṇameṉa mūṉṟu vakippaṭumeṉṟum, avaṟṟuḷ, peruvaṇṇam āṟāyum, iṭivaṇṇam irupat toṉṟāyum, vaṉappuvaṇṇam nāṟpattoṉṟāyum varumeṉṟuṅ kūṟuvar. āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār1  irupatu vaṇṇaṅ kūṟiṉar. nūṟuvaṇṇaṅ kūṟiṉārumuḷar.
variyāvatu; varikkūttukkuriya pāṭal.
"varippāṭalāvatu; paṇṇum, tiṟamum, ceyalum, pāṇiyum oru neṟiyaṉṟi mayaṅkac collappaṭṭa eṭṭaṉiyalpum, āṟaṉiyalpum peṟṟut, taṉ mutalum iṟutiyuṅ keṭṭu, iyalpum muṭamumāka muṭintu, karutappaṭṭa cantiyum cārttum peṟṟum peṟātum varum" eṉṟum, "atutāṉ, teyvañ cuṭṭiyum makkaḷip paḻicciyum varum" eṉṟum kūṟuvar arumpata uriyāciriyar, varippāṭṭukkaḷ tiṇinili vari, kiṇinili vari eṉavum, mukamuṭi vari, mukamil vari, paṭippu vari eṉavum, avi palavākavum pāku pāṭeytum. avaṟṟiṉiyalpellām, piṉṉar, kāṉalvari yuḷḷum, vēṉiṟkātiyuḷḷum viḷaṅkaluṟum.
iṉi, canti yeṉpatu nāṭṭiyak kaṭṭuriyiṉ pirivukaḷ oṉṟōṭoṉṟu toṭarntu niṟkum nili. atu, mukam, piratimukam, karuppam, viḷivu, tuyttal eṉa aivakippaṭum. avaṟṟuḷ mukamā vatu eḻuvakippaṭṭa uḻaviṉāṟ camikkappaṭṭa pūmiyuḷ iṭṭa vittup paruvañ ceytu muḷittu muṭivatu pōlvatu; pirati mukamāvatu aṅṅaṉam muḷittal mutalāy ilitōṉṟi nāṟṟāy muṭivatu pōlvatu; karuppamāvatu an nāṟṟu mutalāyk karuviruntu perukit taṉṉuṭ poruḷ potintu karuppamuṟṟi niṟpatu pōlvatu; viḷivāvatu karuppa mutalāy virintu katir tiraṇṭiṭṭuk kāy tāḻntu muṟṟi viḷintu muṭivatu pōlvatu; tuyttalāvatu viḷiyappaṭṭa poruḷi aṟuttup pōriṭṭuk kaṭāviṭṭut tūṟṟip poli ceytu koṇṭupōy uṇṭu makiḻvatu pōlvatu. vittu, nāṟṟu, karu, viḷivu, tuyttal eṉpaṉa oṉṟiṉoṉṟu toṭarntu muṭitalpōla nāṭakattiṟ kuriya kati aintu pakutiyāy oṉṟiṉoṉṟu toṭarntu muṭiya vēṇṭum eṉṟapaṭi. ivvuṟuppu nāṭakattiṟ kiṉṟiyamiyāta toṉṟeṉka.
cētam eṉpatu ātik katiyi āriyam, tamiḻ eṉṉum iruvakik kūttiṟkēṟpac cētittiṭuvatu eṉpar;
   ‘	āriyat tamiḻeṉuñ cīrnaṭa miraṇṭiṉum
ātik katiyi yavaṟṟiṟ koppac
cētit tiṭuvatu cētameṉ ṟākum.'
eṉpatu kāṇka. 'vilakkiṉiṟ puṇarttu' eṉpataṉāṟ kuṟikkap paṭṭa vilakkuṟuppukkaḷ itukāṟum uṇarttappaṭṭaṉa 
14. 		patiṉōrāṭalāvaṉa; acurarik kolla amararāṭiya pati ṉoru kūttukkaḷ. avaṟṟi.       
  "kaṭiyamayi rāṇimarak kālvinti kantaṉ
kuṭituṭimā lalliyamaṟ kumpam - cuṭarviḻiyāṟ
paṭṭamataṉ pēṭutirup pāviyaraṉ pāṇṭaraṅkam
koṭṭiyivi kāṇpatiṉōr kūttu"
eṉpataṉālaṟika. ivi niṉṟāṭal, paṭintāṭal eṉa iruvakiyiṉa;
  "alliyaṅ koṭṭi kuṭikuṭam pāṇṭaraṅkam
malluṭa ṉiṉṟāṭa lāṟu"
  "	tuṭikaṭiyam pēṭu marakkālē pāvi
vaṭivuṭaṉ vīḻntāṭa lintu"
ikkūttu ovvoṉṟum evvek kāraṇampaṟṟi evvev viṭattu nikaḻttappaṭṭaṉaveṉpatu piṉṉark 1kaṭalāṭu kātiyāl viḷakkamām.
14. 		pāṭṭu eṉṟatu aka nāṭakaṅkaḷukkum, puṟanāṭakaṅ kaḷukku muriya urukkaḷi. aka nāṭakaṅkaḷukkuriya uruvāvaṉa; kanta mutal pirapantavuru vīṟāka irupatteṭṭum. ivaṟṟuḷ, kanta meṉpatu aṭivariyaṟi yuṭittāy oru tāḷattāṟ puṇarppatu; pirapanta meṉpatu aṭivariyaṟi yiṉṟip pala tāḷat tāṟ puṇarppatu. puṟanāṭakaṅkaḷukkuriya uruvāvaṉa; tēva pāṇi mutalāka araṅkoḻi ceyyu ḷīṟākac centuṟi vikaṟ paṅka ḷellāmeṉka.
14. 		koṭṭu - koṭṭappaṭum vācciyaṅkaḷ. avi kītāṅ kam, niruttāṅkam, upayāṅkam eṉpaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ, kītāṅkam kītattiṟku vācippatu; niruttāṅkam niruttattiṟku vācippatu; upayāṅkam iraṇṭiṟkum vācippatu. 
15. 	koḷki - nūl, āṅku, aci.. 
16. 		āṭal - muṟkūṟiya akakkūttiṉum puṟakkūttiṉumuḷḷa āṭal. kīṟṟu, kaṭicari mutalākiya tēcikkuriya kālkaḷ iru pattu nāṉkum, cuṟṟutal, eṟital mutalākiya vaṭukiṟkuriya kālkaḷ patiṉāṉkum, uṭaṟṟūkku mutalākiya uṭalavart taṉi oṉpatum akakkūttuk kuriyaṉa. "ciṅkaḷam iruvaki niliyiṉum eytum" eṉpārum, uṭalavart taṉiyic ciṅkaḷa meṉpārumuḷar. eṉavē, tamiḻ, vaṭuku, ciṅkaḷam eṉa āṭal mūvakippaṭum eṉka.
pāṭal - iṉpam, teḷivu, niṟi, oḷi, vaṉcol, iṟuti, mantam, uccam eṉṉum eṇvakip payaṉumuṭiya pāṭal .
pāṇi - tāḷam ; atu koṭṭu, aci, tūkku, aḷavu eṉa nāṉku niliyiṉiyuṭiyatu ; koṭṭāvatu amukkutal; aciyāvatu tākki yeḻutal ; tūkkāvatu tākkit tūkkutal ; aḷavāvatu tākkiṉa vōci nērē mūṉṟu māttiri peṟumaḷavum varutal. koṭṭu ari māttiri ; ataṟku vaṭivu, ka ; aci oru māttiri ; ataṟku vaṭivu, e ; tūkku iraṇṭu māttiri ; ataṟku vaṭivu, u ; aḷavu mūṉṟu māttiri ; ataṟku vaṭivu, ḵ eṉak koḷka. koṭṭu mutaliya vaṟṟiṉ vaṭivik kuṟikka, 'ka' mutaliya iṭukuṟiyāka niṟuttap peṟṟaṉa veṉka;
"	koṭṭum aciyun tūkkum aḷavum
oṭṭap puṇarppatu pāṇi yākum"
"	kakaraṅ koṭṭē ekaram aciyē
ukaran tūkkē aḷavē yāytam"
eṉpaṉa kāṇka.
 ari māttiriyuṭiya ēkatāḷam mutalākap patiṉāṟu māt tiriyuṭiya pārvati lōcaṉam īṟākac coṉṉa nāṟpattoru tāḷam puṟakkūttukkuriya veṉavum, āṟaṉ maṭṭam eṉpaṉavum, eṭṭaṉ maṭṭam eṉpaṉavum, tāḷavoriyal eṉpaṉavum,  taṉinili yoriyal eṉpaṉavum, oṉṟaṉ pāṇi mutalāka eṇkūttup pāṇi yīṟākak kiṭanta patiṉoru pāṇi vikaṟpaṅ kaḷum, mutalnaṭi, vāram mutalāyiṉavum akakkūttiṟkuriya veṉavum kūṟuvar.
16.		tūkku - ittāḷaṅkaḷiṉ vaḻivarum eḻuvakippaṭṭa tūkkukkaḷ ; avi centūkku, matalit tūkku, tuṇiput tūkku, kōyiṟṟūkku, nivapput tūkku, kaḻāṟṟūkku, neṭun tūkku eṉpaṉa ; eṉṉi? "orucīr centūk kirucīr matali, muccīr tuṇipu nāṟcīr kōyil, aiñcīr nivappē yaṟucīr kaḻālē, eḻucīr neṭuntūk keṉmaṉār pulavar" eṉṟā rākaliṉ.
 17. 		koḷuttutal - āṭṭuvittal. 
18. 		piṇṭi - oṉṟu ; īṇṭu oṟṟik kikku ākupeyar. piṇiyal - iṇital ; īṇṭu iraṭṭikki. piṇṭi, oṟṟikki, iṇiyā viṉikki eṉpaṉa oru poruḷaṉa; piṇiyal, iṇikki, iraṭṭikki eṉpaṉa oru poruḷaṉa. avinayakki iṅṅaṉam iruvakippaṭum.  piṇṭi - poruṭki yām ; piṇiyal - toḻiṟkiyām eṉka. avaṟṟuḷ oṟ ṟikki muppattu mūṉṟu vakippaṭum. avi: patāki-1, tiripatāki-2, kattariki-3, tūpam-4, arāḷam-5, iḷampiṟi-6, cukatuṇṭam-7, muṭṭi-8, kaṭakam-9, cūci-10, kamala kōcikam-11, kāṅkūlam-12, kapittam- 13, viṟpaṭi-14, kuṭaṅki-15, alāpattiram-16, pira maram-17, tāmpira cūṭam-18, pacācam-19, mukuḷam-20, piṇṭi-21, terinili-22, meynnili-23, uṉṉam- 24, maṇṭalam-25, caturam-26, māṉṟali-27, caṅku -28, vaṇṭu-29, ilati-30, kapōtam-31, makara mukam-32, valampuri-33 eṉpaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ,
patākiyāvatu nāṉku viralum tammuḷ oṭṭi nimirap peru viral kuñcittu niṟpatu.
tiripatākiyāvatu patākikkiyiṉ aṇiviral muṭakkuvatu.
kattarikiyāvatu tiripatākiyiṉ muṭaṅkiya aṇiviraṟ puṟatta tākiya naṭuviralic cuṭṭu viralōṭu porunta nimirppatu.
tūpamāvatu nimirnta naṭuviralum cuṭṭuviralum pātippaṭa vaḷiya niṟpatu.
arāḷamāvatu peruviral kuñcittuc cuṭṭuviraṉ muṭakki oḻinta viraṉ mūṉṟum nimirntu vaḷivatu.
iḷampiṟiyāvatu cuṭṭuviralum naṭuviralum aṇiviraliyum ciṟuviraliyum oṭṭi akam vaḷiya, vaḷitta peruviral avaṟṟi viṭṭu nīṅkuvatu ; "cuṭṭum pēṭum anāmiki ciṟuviral, oṭṭi yakam vaḷiya vocitta peruviral, viṭṭu nīṅkum vitiyiṟ ṟeṉpa."
cukatuṇṭam: "cukatuṇṭa meṉpatu toḻilpeṟak kiḷap piṉ, cuṭṭu viralum peruviral tāṉum, oṭṭi yukirnuṉi kavvi muṉvaḷin, tanāmiki muṭaṅkap pēṭṭoṭu ciṟuviral, tāṉmika nimirnta takutit teṉpa."
anāmiki - aṇiviral; pavuttiraviral. pēṭu - naṭuviral.
muṭṭi : "muṭṭi yeṉpatu moḻiyuṅ kālic, cuṭṭu naṭuviral anāmiki ciṟuviral, iṟuka muṭakki yivaṟṟiṉmicip peruviral, muṟukap piṭitta muṟimit teṉpa."
kaṭakam : "kaṭaka mukamē karutuṅ kālip, peruvira ṉuṉiyuñ cuṭṭuvira ṉuṉiyu, maruva vaḷintav vukir nuṉi kuvi, yoḻinta mūṉṟum vaḻivaḻi nimira, moḻintaṉa reṉpa muṭipaṟin tōrē."
cūci : "cūci yeṉpatu tuṇiyuṅ kāli, naṭuviral peruvira leṉṟivi tammi, laṭivuṭa ṉoṟṟic cuṭṭuvira ṉimira, voḻintaṉa vaḻivaḻi muṭaṅki niṟpa, moḻintaṉar mātō muṭipaṟintōrē."
patumakōcikam : "patuma kōcikam pakaruṅ kāli, yoppak kivaḷit tintu viralu, meyppaṭa vakaṉṟa vitiyiṟ ṟākum."
kāṅkūlamāvatu : "kāṅkū lammē karutuṅ kālic, cuṭṭum pēṭum peruviraṉ mūṉṟu, moṭṭimuṉ kuviya vanāmiki muṭaṅkic, ciṟuvira ṉimirnta ceykit tākum," "mukiḻkāṅ kūla muntuṟa moḻinta, kuvikāṅ kūlaṅ kuviviḻan tatuvē," "malarkāṅ kūla matumalarn tatuvē."
kapittamāvatu : cuṭṭuviraṉuṉiyum peruviraṉuṉiyum ukir nuṉi kuvippiṭitta oḻinta mūṉṟuviralum melleṉap piṭippatu.
viṟpiṭi : "viṟpiṭi yeṉpatu virikkuṅ kālic, cuṭṭoṭu pēṭi yanāmiki ciṟuvira, loṭṭi yakappāl vaḷiyap peruviral, viṭṭu nimirum vitiyiṟ ṟākum."
kuṭaṅkiyāvatu ellā viraluṅ kūṭṭi uṭkuḻippatu.
alāpattiramāvatu ciṟuviraṉ mutalākiya vintum vaḷintu maṟivatu.
piramaramāvatu anāmiki viralum naṭuviralum tammiṟ porunti valañcāyap peruviral naṭuvirali ṉuḷḷē cērac cuṭṭu viralum ciṟuviralum piṉpē vaḷintu niṟpatu.
tāmpiracūṭamāvatu naṭuviralum cuṭṭuviralum peruviralum tammil nuṉiyottuk kūṭi vaḷintu ciṟuviralum aṇiviralum muṭaṅki nimirvatu.
pacācamāvatu peruviralum cuṭṭuviralumaṉṟi oḻinta mūṉṟu viralum tammiṟ polintu niṟpateṉakkoḷka.
ap pacācantāṉ mūṉṟu vakippaṭum : akanilippacācam-cuṭṭuvira ṉuṉiyil peruvira lakappaṭuvatu.
mukanilippacācam-avviraṉ mukaṅkūṭi ukirviṭṭu niṟpatu.
ukirnilippacācam-cuṭṭuviralum peruviralum ukirnuṉi kuvi niṟpatu.
mukuḷamāvatu aintuviralun tammil talikuvintu uyarntu niṟpatu.
piṇṭiyeṉṟatu cuṭṭuviral pēṭiviral anāmiki ciṟuviral oṭṭi nekiḻa muṭaṅka avaṟṟiṉmēlē kuṟukkiṭam peruviral kaṭṭi vilaṅki viralvaḻi muṟiyoṟṟal.
teriniliyāvatu ellāviralum virintu kuñcittu niṟpatu.
meynniliyāvatu ciṟuviralum aṇiviralum naṭu viralum cuṭṭu viralum viṭṭunimirac cuṭṭuviraṉmēṟ peruviral cēravippatu.
uṉṉamāvatu ciṟuviralum peruviralum tammuṭkūṭa oḻinta mūṉṟu viralum viṭṭunimirvatu.
maṇṭalam : "maṇṭalameṉpatu mācaṟak kiḷappiṟ, pēṭu nuṉiyum peruvira ṉuṉiyuṅ, kūṭi vaḷintutam mukirnuṉi kuvi, yoḻinta mūṉṟu mokka vaḷivateṉa, moḻintaṉa reṉpa muḻu tuṇarn tōrē."
caturamāvatu cuṭṭu viralum naṭuviralum aṇiviralum tammuṭ cērntu iṟiñcap peruviral akam vara vittuc ciṟuviral piṉpē nimirntu niṟpatu.
māṉṟaliyāvatu peruviraluñ ciṟuviralu moḻinta mūṉṟun tammil ottu oṉṟi muṉṉē iṟiñci niṟpatu.
caṅkamāvatu peruviral nimira oḻinta nāṉkuviralum vaḷintu niṟpatu.
vaṇṭāvatu peruviralum aṇiviralum vaḷintu nuṉi yoṉṟic ciṟuviral nimirntu cuṭṭu viralum naṭuviralum nekiḻa vaḷintu niṟpatu.
ilatiyāvatu naṭuviralum cuṭṭuviralum kūṭinimirap peruviral avaṟṟiṉ kīḻvari cēra oḻinta iraṇṭu viralum vaḻimuṟi piṉṉē nimirntu niṟpatu.
kapōtameṉṟatu patākik kiyiṟ peruviral viṭṭu nimirvatu.
makaramukamāvatu peruviralum cuṭṭuviralum nimirntukūṭa oḻinta mūṉṟuviralun tammuḷoṉṟi ataṟku vēṟāy niṟpatu.
valampuriyāvatu ciṟuviralum peruviralu nimirntu cuṭṭu viraliṉ akam vaḷintu oḻinta viraṇṭum nimirntu iṟiñci niṟpatu.
iṉi, iraṭṭikki patiṉintu vakippaṭum. avi : añcali -1, puṭpāñcali-2, patumāñcali-3, kapōtam-4, kaṟ kaṭakam-5, cuvattikam-6, kaṭakāvaruttam-7, niṭatam-8, tōram-9, uṟcaṅkam-10, puṭpapuṭam-11, makaram-12, cayantam-13, apayavattam-14, varuttamāṉam-15. ivaṟṟuḷ, 
añcaliyāvatu : iraṇṭu kiyum patākiyāy akamoṉṟuvatu. 
puṭpāñcaliyāvatu iraṇṭu kiyum kuṭaṅkiyāy vantu oṉṟuvatu.
kapōtameṉṟatu iraṇṭu kiyum kapōtamākak kūṭṭuvatu.
kaṟkaṭaka meṉṟatu terinilik kiyiraṇṭum aṅkuli piṇintu varuvatu.
kavattika meṉṟatu maṇikkaṭṭiṟ poruntiya patākiyiraṇṭiyum maṇikkaṭṭi lēṟṟi vippatu.
kaṭakāvaruttamāvatu iraṇṭu kiyum kaṭakamāy maṇik kaṭṭukku ēṟa iyintu niṟpatu.
niṭatamāvatu muṭṭiyāka iraṇṭu kiyum camañceyvatu.
tōramāvatu iraṇṭu kiyum patākiyākki akampuṟa moṉṟi muṉtāḻntu niṟpatu.
uṟcaṅkamāvatu oruki piṟikkiyākak koṇṭu  oruki arāḷamākki iraṇṭu kiyum maṇikkaṭṭi lēṟṟi vippatu.
puṭpapuṭamāvatu kuṭaṅkiyiraṇṭum tammiṟ pakkaṅ kāṭṭi niṟpatu.
makarameṉṟatu kapōta miraṇṭu kiyum akampuṟa moṉṟa vippatu.
cayantam : (viṭupaṭṭatu)
apayavattamāvatu irukiyum cukatuṇṭamāka neñcuṟa nōkki nekiḻntu niṟpatu.
varuttamāṉamāvatu mukuḷak kiyiṟ kapōtak kiyi etiriṭṭuc cērppatu.
18. 		eḻiṟki - aḻakupeṟak kāṭṭuṅki. toḻiṟki - toḻil peṟak kāṭṭuṅ ki. poruṭki yeṉpatum varuvikkappaṭum ; āvatu, poruḷuṟak kāṭṭuṅki.
19. 		koṇṭavaki yaṟitalāvatu - piṇṭiyum piṇiyalum puṟakkūttukkuriya ki yeṉṟum, eḻiṟkiyum toḻiṟkiyum akakkūttukkuriya ki yeṉṟum aṟital.
20 - 21 	kūṭi, vāram eṉpaṉa cila tāḷavikaṟpaṅkaḷukkum, icip pāṭṭukkaḷiṉ iyakkaṅkaḷukkum, avinayak ki kaṭkum, piṟa cilavaṟṟiṟkum peyar ; īṇṭu avinayak kikaḷi yuṇartti niṉṟaṉa.
kūṭi - oṟṟikki ; iraṭṭi yoṟṟik kiyākiya kuvitta kiyiyum itu kuṟikkum. vāram - iraṭṭikki. akakkūttu nikaḻumiṭattu oṟṟiyiṟ ceyta kittoḻil iraṭṭiyiṟ pukāmalum, iraṭṭiyiṟ ceyta kittoḻil oṟṟiyiṟ pukāmalum kaḷital. iṉṉum, tēciyiṟ kittoḻil mārkkattup pukāmalum, mārkkattuk kittoḻil tēciyiṟ pukāmalum kaḷitaleṉṟumām ; oṟṟiyum, iraṭṭiyum tēcikkūṟākalāṉum iraṭṭiyum iraṭṭik kiraṭṭiyum vaṭukiṟ kūṟākalāṉumeṉka. 
22 - 23 	piṇṭi - poruṭki. āṭal - piṇiyal ; toḻiṟki. puṟakkūttil āṭal nikaḻumiṭattu avinayam nikaḻāmalum, avinayam nikaḻumiṭattu āṭal nikaḻāmalum kaḷital. 
24. 		kuravi - kuravikkūttu ; atu, kāmamum veṉṟiyum poruḷākak kuravic ceyyuḷ pāṭṭāka eḻuvarēṉum eṇmarēṉum oṉpatiṉmarēṉum kipiṇittāṭuvatu ; ataṉ iyalpiṉi, "kuravi yeṉpatu kūṟuṅ kālic, ceytōr ceyta kāmamum viṟalum, eyta vurikku miyalpiṟ ṟeṉpa" eṉpataṉāṉaṟika.
vari - varikkūttu ; aḵtu avaravar piṟanta nilattaṉmiyum piṟappiṟkēṟṟa toḻiṟ ṟaṉmiyum tōṉṟa naṭittal. itaṟku varicceyyuḷ pāṭṭākum. itaṉ iyalpiṉi, "variyeṉap paṭuvatu vakukkuṅ kālip, piṟanta nilaṉuñ ciṟanta toḻilum, aṟiyak kūṟi yāṟṟuḻi vaḻaṅkal" eṉpataṉāṉaṟika. varikkūttu oruvar perumpālum vēṟṟurut tāṅki naṭippatu.
it toṭarnilic ceyyuḷiṉkaṇ, mulli cārntu āycciyar kuraviyum, kuṟiñci cārntu kuṉṟak kuraviyum, neytal cārntu kāṉalvariyum, pāli cārntu vēṭṭuva variyum, marutañ cārntu ūr cūḻvariyum nikaḻntaṉavāka amintiruttal aṟiyaṟpāṟṟu.
varikkūttāṉatu kaṇkūṭuvari, kāṉalvari, uḷvari, puṟavari, kiḷarvari, tērccivari, kāṭcivari, eṭuttukkōḷvari eṉa eṇ vakippaṭum. ivaṟṟiṉiyalpellām vēṉiṟkātiyāl viḷakkamām. iṉi, "iv vari yeṉpataṉip palvarik kūtteṉpāru muḷar" eṉṟu kūṟi, aṭiyārkku nallār ataṟkeṭuttuk kāṭṭiya kaliveṇ pāṭṭoṉṟāl tamiḻakattē paṇṭināḷil vaḻaṅkiya ettaṉiyō vakiyāṉa kūttukkaḷum pāṭṭukkaḷum pulaṉā kiṉṟaṉa; acceyyuḷ piṉvaruvatu :
"	cintup piḻukki yuṭaṉcanti yōrmuli
konti kavuci kuṭappiḻukki-kantaṉpāṭ
ṭālaṅkāṭ ṭāṇṭi parumaṇa ṉellicci
cūlan tarunaṭṭan tūṇṭiluṭaṉ-cīlamikum
āṇṭi yamaṇpuṉavē ṭāḷatti kōppāḷi
pāṇṭip piḻukkiyuṭaṉ pāmpāṭṭi-mīṇṭa
kaṭavuṭ caṭivīra mākēcaṅ kāmaṉ
makiḻcintu vāmaṉa rūpam-vikaṭaneṭum
pattiraṅ koṟṟi palakivāḷ papparappeṇ
tattacam pāram takuṇiccaṅ-kattu
muṟiyīṇ ṭiruñcittu muṇṭita maṉṉap
paṟipaṇ ṭitaṉpuṭpa pāṇam-iṟiparavu
pattaṉ kuraviyē pappaṟi kāvataṉ
pittaṉoṭu māṇi perumpiḻukki-ettuṟiyum
ēttivaruṅ kaṭkaḷi yāṇṭu viḷiyāṭṭuk
kōtta paṟikkuṭumpu kōṟkūttu-mūtta
kiḻavaṉ kiḻaviyē kiḷḷup piṟāṇṭi
aḻakuṭiya paṇṇivika ṭāṅkan-tikaḻcempoṉ
ammaṉi pantu kaḻaṅkāṭa lālikkum
viṇṇakak kāḷi viṟaṟkonti-allāta
vāynta taṉivaṇṭu vāricci picciyuṭaṉ
cānta muṭiya caṭātāri-ēyntaviṭi
takkapiṭār nirttan taḷippāṭṭuc cāturaṅkan
tokka toḻilpuṉinta cōṇāṇṭu-mikka
maliyāḷi vētāḷi vāṇi kutiri
ciliyāṭu vēṭu civapput-taliyil
tiruviḷakkup picci tirukkuṉ ṟayiṟpeṇ
ṭiruṇmukattup pēti yiruḷaṉ-porumukattup
pallāṅ kuḻiyē pakaṭi pakavatiyāḷ
nallārtan tōḷvīccu naṟcāḻal-allāta
unti yavaliṭi yūrāḷi yōkiṉicci
kuntivarum pāraṉ kuṇalikkūt-tantiyampō
tāṭuṅ kaḷikoyyu muḷḷippū viyaṉukkup
pāṭumpāṭ ṭāṭum paṭupaḷḷi-nāṭaṟiyuṅ
kumpīṭu nāṭṭaṅ kuṇāṭṭaṅ kuṇāliyē
tuñcāta cummippūc cōṉaka-mañcari
ēṟṟa vuḻimi paṟimimuta leṉṟeṇṇik
kōttavarik kūttiṉ kulam."     
 i-iryāciriyaṉ amiti
26 -36 	yāḻum kuḻalum cīrum miṭaṟum tāḻkural taṇṇumi āṭaloṭu - yāḻppāṭalum vaṅkiyap pāṭalum tāḷak kūṟupāṭukaḷum miṭaṟṟup pāṭalum mantamākiya curat tiṉiyuṭiya taṇṇumiyum kūttukkaḷum vallaṉāy, ivaṟṟiṉ icinta pāṭal iciyuṭaṉ paṭuttu - ivaṟṟuṭaṉē cērac ceyta urukkaḷi ici koḷḷumpaṭiyum cuvi poruntumpaṭiyum puṇarkkavum vallaṉāy, varikkum āṭaṟkum uripporuḷ iyakki - centuṟi veṇṭuṟi eṉṉum iruvakippaṭṭa pāṭalkaḷukkum poruḷāṉa iyakkam nāṉkiṉiyum amittu, tēcikat tiruviṉ ōci kaṭippiṭittu - aḻakuṭiya tēcāntaraṅkaḷiṉ pāṭi kaḷiyum aṟintu, tēcikat tiruviṉ ōci yellām āciṉṟu uṇarnta aṟiviṉaṉ āki - antap pāṭikaḷ icipūṇum paṭiyiyum aṟintu. kaviyatu kuṟippum āṭaṟṟokutiyum pakutip pāṭalum koḷuttuṅ kāli - iyaṟpulavaṉ karuttum nāṭakap pulavaṉ īṭuvaravukaḷum avaṟṟuk kaṭitta pāṭalkaḷum tammiṟ cantippikku miṭattu, vaciaṟu kēḷvi vakuttaṉaṉ virikkum - kuṟṟan tīrnta nūlvaḻakkālē vakukkavum virikkavum vallaṉāyuḷḷa, aciyā marapiṉ iciyōṉtāṉum - taḷarāta iyalpiṉiyuṭiya icippulavaṉum ; 
 26.		yāḻ - pēriyāḻ, makarayāḻ, cakōṭayāḻ, ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻ eṉa yāḻ nālvakippaṭum. avaṟṟukku narampu muṟiyē irupattoṉṟum, pattoṉpatum, patiṉāṉkum, ēḻum ākum. eṉṉi?
"	oṉṟu mirupatu moṉpatum pattuṭaṉē niṉṟa patiṉāṉkum piṉṉēḻum-kuṉṟāta
nālvaki yāḻiṟku naṉṉarampu coṉmuṟiyē
mēlvaki nūlōr viti"
eṉpavākaliṉ. ōrēḻmēṟpattuṭaṉē eṉpatu pāṭamāyiṉ makara yāḻiṟku narampu patiṉēḻākum. innālvakiyaṉṟic ciṟupāṉmiyāṉ varum yāḻ piṟavu muḷa eṉpar. yāḻiṉ piṟa vilakkaṇaṅkaḷ piṉ kāṉalvari yuriyiṟ kūṟappaṭum. yāḻ- yāḻppāṭal eṉka.
kuḻal - kuḻaliṉpāṭal eṉka. kuḻal - vaṅkiyam ; atu mūṅkil, cantaṉam, veṇkalam, ceṅkāli, karuṅkāli yeṉṉum aintāṉum iyaṟṟappaṭum. ivaṟṟuḷ mūṅkilē mutaṉmiyāṉatu ; atu paṟṟiyē pullāṅkuḻal, vēyṅkuḻal, vaṅkiyam, vēṇu eṉṉum peyarkaḷ kuḻalukku vaḻaṅkuva vāyiṉa. mūṅkil uttamam, veṇkalam mattimam, ēṉiya atamam eṉpar. ikkālattuk karuṅkāli ceṅkāli cantaṉam ivaṟṟāṟ koḷḷappaṭum eṉavum, ivi koḷḷu miṭattu, uyarnta otta nilattiṟ peruka vaḷarntu nālu kāṟṟum mayaṅkiṉ nāta milliyātalāl, mayaṅkā nilattilē iḷamiyum neṭum pirāyamumiṉṟi oru puruṭāyup pukka periya maratti veṭṭi, oru puruṭākāramākac ceytu, ataṉi niḻalilē yiṭṭu āṟa vittu, tirukutal piḷattal pōḻntupaṭutal iṉmi yaṟintu, ōr yāṇṭu ceṉṟapiṉ vaṅkiyam ceyyappaṭum eṉavum, itaṉ nīḷam irupatu viralaḷavum, cuṟṟu nālari viralaḷavumām eṉavum, itu tuḷiyiṭumiṭattu nellariciyil oru pāti maraṉiṟuttik kaṭintu veṇkalattāl aṇicu paṇṇi iṭa mukatti yaṭittu valamukam veḷiyāka viṭappaṭum eṉavum, itilē tūpamukattiṉ iraṇṭu viralnīkki mutalvāyviṭṭu, immutal vāykku ēḻaṅkulamviṭṭu vaḷivāyiṉum iraṇṭu nīkki naṭuviṉiṉṟa oṉpatu viraliṉum eṭṭut tuḷiyiṭappaṭum eṉavum, tuḷikaḷiṉ iṭipparappu oru viralakalam koḷḷappaṭum eṉavum kūṟuvar. itaṉi vācikkumiṭattu, vaḷivāy cērnta tuḷiyi muttiri yeṉṟu nīkki, muṉṉiṉṟa ēḻu tuḷiyiṉum iṭakkiyiṉ iṭi mūṉṟuviralum, valakkiyiṉ peruvira loḻinta nāṉku viralum paṟṟi vācikkappaṭum. iv vaṅkiyattiṉ ēḻu tuḷikaḷilum carikamapatani eṉṉum ēḻeḻuttiṉiyum māttirippaṭuttit toḻil ceyya ivaṟṟuḷḷē ēḻiciyum piṟakkum ; ivi piṟantu ivaṟṟuḷḷē paṇkaḷ piṟakkum;
"	carika mapataniyeṉ ṟēḻeḻuttāṟ ṟāṉam
variparanta kaṇṇiṇāy vittut-terivariya
ēḻiciyun tōṉṟu mivaṟṟuḷē paṇpiṟakkum
cūḻmutalāñ cuttat tuḷi"
eṉpatu kāṇka.
cīr - akak kūttiṟkum puṟakkūttiṟku muriya iruvakit tāḷak kūṟupāṭukaḷ.
miṭaṟu - miṭaṟṟuppāṭal. itaṉic cārīravīṇi yeṉpar. puṟkalam eṉappaṭum uṭampu aimpūtaṅkaḷiṉ pariṇāmattāl ākumuṟimi aṭiyārkku nallārāl viritturikkap paṭṭuḷatu; aḵtu īṇṭikku vēṇṭappaṭuvatiṉṟu.
uṭampiṉaḷavu taṉkiyāl toṇṇūṟṟāṟu aṅkulam eṉavum, avaṟṟuḷ mēlē nāṟpattēḻari yaṅkulamum kīḻē nāṟpattēḻari yaṅkulamum viṭṭu naṭuniṉṟa ōraṅkulam mūlātāram eṉavum, mūlātāran toṭaṅki eḻuttiṉ nātam āḷattiyāyp piṉ ici yeṉṟum paṇṇeṉṟum peyarām eṉavum, neñcum miṭaṟum nākkum mūkkum aṇṇākkum utaṭum pallum taliyum eṉṉum peruntāṉam eṭṭiṉum eṭuttal paṭuttal nalital kampitam kuṭilam oli uruṭṭu tākku eṉṉuṅ kiriyika ḷeṭṭāṉum paṇṇip paṭuttalāṟ paṇṇeṉṟu peyarāyiṟṟu eṉavum kūṟuvar.
āḷatti yeṉpatu ikkālattu ālāpaṉam eṉa vaḻaṅkap peṟum. atu makara oṟṟuṭaṉ kūṭiya kuṟṟeḻuttālum, neṭṭeḻut tālum teṉṉā, teṉā, teṉṉā teṉā eṉṉum acikaḷ kūṭṭiyum ceyyappaṭum eṉavum, maṟṟum, ma, na, ta eṉṉum mūṉṟu oṟṟukkaḷuṭaṉ kūṭiya a i u e o eṉṉuṅ kuṟṟeḻut tintāṉum, ā ī ū ē ō eṉṉum neṭṭe.ḻut tintāṉum ceyyappaṭum eṉavum, ivvāḷattitāṉ accu, pāraṇi yeṉṟum, kāṭṭāḷatti,  paṇṇāḷatti yeṉṟum eḻuttu vēṟṟumi yāṟ peyareytum eṉavum, piṟavāṟum kūṟupa. 
 27. 		taṇṇumi - piṟakaruvikaṭkum upalakkaṇam. 
 28,		āṭal - akakkūttu, puṟakkūttu, patiṉōrāṭal eṉpaṉa. 
 29. 		urip poruḷ - iyakkam ; pāṭṭiṉ naṭi. atu mutaṉaṭi, vāram, kūṭi, tiraḷ eṉa nālvakippaṭum. avaṟṟuḷ, mutaṉaṭi mikat tāḻnta celaviṉi yuṭiyatu ; vāram colloḻukkamum ici yoḻukkamum uṭiyatu ; kūṭi coṟ ceṟivum icic ceṟivum uṭiyatu ; tiraḷ mika muṭukiya naṭiyiṉi yuṭiyatu.
iciyuṭaṉ paṭuttu iyakkik kaṭip piṭittu uṇarnta aṟi viṉaṉāki virikkum iciyōṉ eṉka. 
kaviñaṉ amiti
37 - 44 	imiḻ kaṭal varippil tamiḻakam aṟiya - olikkiṉṟa kaṭal cūḻnta puviyiṉkaṇṇē tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉar aṟiya, tamiḻ muḻutu aṟinta taṉmiyaṉ āki - muttamiḻum tuṟipōkak kaṟṟuṇarnta taṉmiyi yuṭiyaṉāki, vēttiyal potuviyal eṉṟu iru tiṟattiṉ - vēttiya leṉṟum potuviya leṉṟum kūṟappaṭum iraṇṭu kūṟupāṭṭiṉi yuṭiya, nāṭṭiya naṉṉūl naṉku kaṭippiṭitta - nāṭaka nūli naṉṟākap paṟṟikkoṇṭu, iciyōṉ vakkirit tiṭṭatit uṇarntu - icip pulavaṉ āḷatti vitta paṇṇīr miyi aṟintu, āṅku - aṟinta vaṇṇam, aciyā marapiṉ - taḷarāta muṟimiyālē, atu paṭavittu - avaṉ tāḷaniliyil eyta vitta niṟam taṉ kaviyilē tōṉṟa vikka vallaṉāy, māṟṟōr ceyta vacimoḻi aṟintu nāttolivu illā - muṉpakivar ceyta vaci moḻikaḷi yaṟintu avi tōṟṟātapaṭi vaci yillāta moḻikaḷāl nāṭakak kavi ceyyavalla keṭāta nāviṉiyuṭiya, naṉṉūṟ pulavaṉum - nalla nūli valla pulavaṉum ; 
37. 		tamiḻakam - vēṅkaṭam kumari tīmpuṉaṟ puvam eṉpaṉa vaṭakkum teṟkum kuṭakkum kuṇakkum elliyākavuṭiya vaṇpukaḻ mūvar taṇ poḻil varippu. 
41. 		vakkirittal - āḷatti ceytal. uṇarntu eṉpataṟku, mutalum muṟiyum muṭivum niṟiyum kuṟiyum kiḻamiyum valivum melivum camaṉum variyaṟiyum nīrmiyum eṉṉum patiṉoru pākupāṭṭiṉāṉum aṟintu eṉpar.
taṉmiyaṉākik kaṭippiṭittu uṇarntu vittu aṟintu tolivillāta pulavaṉum eṉka. 
taṇṇumi yāciriyaṉ amiti
45 - 55 	āṭal pāṭal iciyē tamiḻē - ellāk kūttukkaḷum ellāp pāṭṭukkaḷum ellā icikaḷum iyal ici nāṭaka meṉṉum mūvakit tamiḻkaḷum, paṇṇē pāṇi tūkkē muṭamē - ellāp paṇkaḷum iruvakit tāḷaṅkaḷum eḻuvakit tūkkukkaḷum ivaṟṟiṉ kuṟṟaṅkaḷum, tēcikam - iyaṟcol, tiricol, ticiccol, vaṭacol eṉṉum nālvakic col vaḻakkukkaḷum, eṉṟivi āciṉ uṇarntu - eṉṟu collappaṭṭa ivaṟṟi nuṇṇitiṉ uṇarntu, kūṭi nilattik kuṟiviṉṟi mikuttu - ōruruvi iraṭṭikkiraṭṭi cērtta viṭattu nekiḻātapaṭi nirampa niṟuttavum, āṅku vāra nilatti vāṅkupu vāṅki - avviṭattup peṟum iraṭṭiyip pākavuruvāṉavaḻi niṟkumāṉam niṟuttik kaḻiyumāṉaṅ kaḻikkavum vallaṉāy, vāṅkiya vārattu yāḻum kuḻalum ēṅkiya miṭaṟum icivaṉa kēṭpa - ippaṭi nikaḻnta urukkaḷil yāḻp pāṭalum kuḻaliṉ pāṭalum kaṇṭap pāṭalum icintu naṭakkiṉṟapaṭi kēṭpōr cevik koḷḷumāṟu, kūr ukirkkaraṇam kuṟi aṟintu cērtti - taṇṇumiyi viraliṉ ceykiyālē kuṟiyaṟintu cēravācikka vallaṉāy, ākkalum aṭakkalum - maṟṟik karuvi kaḷiṉ kuṟiyi nirapputalum mikutiyi aṭakkutalum, mīt tiṟam paṭāmi - ākkumiṭattum aṭakkumiṭattum iciyil intiran tōṉṟāmaṟ ceytalum, cittirak karaṇam citi viṉṟu celuttum - ivvaṉittum ceyyumiṭattuk kit toḻil aḻaku peṟac ceytu kāṭṭalum vallaṉāy, attaku taṇṇumi aruntoḻil mutalvaṉum - attaṉmi yuṭiya taṇṇumik karuviyiṉ ariya toḻiliyuṭiya āciriyaṉum; 
45. 		ici - narappaṭivāl urikkappaṭṭa patiṉōrāyirattut toḷḷāyirattut toṇṇūṟṟoṉṟākiya ātiyicikaḷ eṉpar. itaṟkup piramāṇamāka eṭuttuk kāṭṭap peṟṟatōr cūttiram aṭiyil varumāṟu citintu kāṇap paṭukiṉṟatu;
"	uyiruyir meyyaḷa vurittavim pāliṉum uṭaṟami ḻiyalici yēḻuṭaṉ pakuttu
mūvēḻ peytan........................
toṇṭu mīṇṭa paṉṉī rāyiraṅ
koṇṭaṉa riyaṟṟal koḷivallōr kaṭaṉē"
45.		tamiḻ eṉpataṟku, vaṭaveḻuttorīi vanta eḻuttāṉē uṟaḻntu kāṭṭappaṭṭa vākkiyak kūṟukaḷ eṉṟum urippar. 
46. 		paṇ - narappaṭivāl niṟantōṉṟap paṇṇap paṭāniṉṟa paṇṇum, paṇṇiyaṟ ṟiṟamum, tiṟamum, tiṟattiṟamu mām.
47.		muṭam - kuṟṟam.
47. 		ācu - nuṇmi. āciṉ ṟuṇarntu eṉṉum pāṭat tiṟkuk kuṟṟamiṉṟi yuṇarnteṉpatu poruḷākum.; 
51.	 ēṅkiya - olittaliyuṭiya.
52.	 kūrukir - kūriya nakam; viralukku ākupeyar. karaṇam-ceyki.
53. 		taṇṇumi eṉṟatu ēṉittōṟ karuvikaḷiyum aṭakki niṉṟatu. avaṟṟi,
"	pēriki paṭakam iṭakki uṭukki
cīrmiku mattaḷam calliki karaṭiki
timili kuṭamuḻāt takki kaṇappaṟi
tamarukam taṇṇumi tāvil taṭāri
antari muḻavoṭu cantira vaḷiyam
monti muracē kaṇviṭu tūmpu
nicāḷam tuṭumi ciṟupaṟi aṭakkam
mācil takuṇiccam viralēṟu pākam
tokka upāṅkam tuṭiperum paṟiyeṉa
mikka nūlōr viritturit taṉarē"
eṉṉuñ cūttirat tālaṟika. ivi akamuḻavu, akappuṟa muḻavu, puṟamuḻavu, puṟappuṟa muḻavu, paṇṇami muḻavu, nāṇ muḻavu, kāli muḻavu eṉa eḻuvakippaṭum eṉṟum, muṉ coṉṉa uttamamāṉa mattaḷam calliki iṭakki karaṭiki pēriki paṭakam kuṭamuḻā eṉpaṉa akamuḻavum, mattimamāṉa taṇṇumi takki takuṇiccam mutaliyaṉa akappuṟamuḻavum, atamak karuviyāṉa kaṇappaṟi muta liyaṉa puṟamuḻavum, muṉ kūṟappaṭāta neytaṟ paṟi mutaliyaṉa puṟappuṟa muḻavum, muracu nicāḷam tuṭumi timili yeṉṉum vīramuḻavu nāṉkum paṇṇami muḻavum, nāḻikip paṟiyāṉatu nāṇmuḻavum, tuṭi eṉpatu kāli muḻavum ākumeṉṟum kūṟuvar. aṭikaḷ taṇṇumi yoṉṟaṉiyē vitantōtutaliṉ atuvē ēṉik karuvikaṭ kellām mutalāmeṉpatu peṟṟām.     
kuḻalāciriyaṉ amiti
56 - 69 	colliya iyalpiṉ - nūlkaḷiṟ coṉṉa muṟimiyālē, cittira vañcaṉi pulliya aṟintu - cittirap puṇarppum vañcaṉip puṇarppum eṉṉum iru kūṟṟiṉiyum aṟintu, puṇarp
pōṉ paṇpiṉ - puṇarkkavalla pāṭalāciriyaṉi yotta aṟiviṉi yuṭiyaṉāki, varttaṉi nāṉkum mayal aṟap peytu - ārōkaṇa avarōkaṇaṅkaḷil viralkaḷi viṭṭup
piṭikkum varttaṉi nāṉkiṉālum nūṟṟu mūṉṟu paṇṇīrmi kaḷiyum tannili kuliyāmaṟ kāṭṭavallaṉāy, āṅku - avviṭattu, ēṟṟiya kural iḷi eṉṟu iru narampiṉ - patiṉāṟ kōviyiṉiṭattuk kural narampu iraṭṭikkavarum arum pāliyiyum, iḷi narampu iraṭṭikkavarum mēṟcem pāliyiyum, ivipōla allāta pālikaḷiyum, oppak kēṭkum uṇarviṉaṉ āki - ici nūl vaḻakkālē iṇi narampu toṭuttup pāṭum aṟiviṉiyumuṭiyaṉāy, paṇ ami muḻaviṉ kaṇṇeṟi aṟintu - paṇṇutalaminta muḻaviṉ kaṇṇeṟiyiṉi aṟintu, taṇṇumi mutalvaṉ taṉṉoṭum porunti - taṇṇumi mutalvaṉōṭum porunti, vaṇṇap paṭṭaṭi yāḻmēl vittu āṅku - niṟat tiṉiyuṭiya iḷiyeṉṉum narampiṉi yāḻmēl vittu, iciyōṉ pāṭiya iciyiṉ iyaṟki - ataṉvaḻiyē icikkāraṉ pāṭiya pāṭṭiṉiyalpi, vantatu vaḷarttu - pāṭukiṉṟa paṇ varavukaḷukkuc curam kuṟivu paṭāmi niṟutti, varuvatu oṟṟi - antap paṇṇukku ayal viravāmal nōkki iṉpuṟa iyakki - vaṇṇa mutalākak kāṭṭappaṭṭa pāṭaliyal vaḻakkellām cuvi porunta nirampak kāṭṭi, ici paṭa vittu - muṟkūṟiya mutalum muṟiyum mutalāṉa paṇṇilakkaṇam patiṉoṉṟiṉiyum nirampa vittu, vāra nilattik kēṭiṉṟu pārttu - vārappāṭali aḷavu nirampa niṟuttavallaṉāy, āṅku - pāṭaliṭattu, īranilattiṉ eḻuttu eḻuttāka - coṉṉīrmikaḷiṉ eḻuttukkaḷ citiyāmalē eḻutteḻuttāka icikkum, vaḻuviṉṟu kuḻalōṉ tāṉum - iccollappaṭṭa iyalpukaḷi ilakkaṇappaṭi vaḻuvāmal vācittukkāṭṭa valla kuḻalāciriyaṉum;
56. 		cittirap puṇarppāvatu icikoḷḷum eḻuttukkaḷiṉ mēlē valloṟṟu vantavaḻi melloṟṟup pōlap paṇṇīrmi niṟuttal. vañcaṉip puṇarppāvatu ici koḷḷā veḻuttukkaḷiṉ mēlē valloṟṟu vantavaḻi melloṟṟuppōla nekiḻttup puṇarttal.
58.		varttaṉi - kuḻalil viraluḷarntūtun tuḷikaḷ cuṭṭuviral mutalāka viṭṭup piṭippatu ārōkaṇam; ciṟu viral mutalāka viṭṭuppiṭippatu avarōkaṇam; iṅṅaṉam ēṟṟiyiṟakkalum, iṟakkiyēṟṟalum varttaṉi yeṉappaṭum.
59. 		‘ēṟṟiya kuraliḷi............uṇarviṉaṉāki' eṉpataṉ poruḷ piṉṉar yāḻāciriyaṉ amiti kūṟumiṭattu urikku muṟiyāl viḷakkamām.
63. paṭṭaṭi - aṭimaṇi; ellāp paṇṇiṟkum aṭi maṇiyātaliṉ iḷiyeṉṉum narampu, paṭṭaṭi yeṉap paṭṭatu. yāḻiṉ mēṟpaṇkaḷi iḷi muṟiyālē vittu eṉka. iḷi muṟiyāvatu caṭcak kiramam.  āṅku;aci.
65. oṟṟi - niṉintu.
67. 		vāra nilattik kēṭiṉṟu pārttu eṉpatu - mutaṉaṭi, vāram, kūṭi, tiraḷ eṉṟu collappaṭṭa iyakkam nāṉkiṉum mutal naṭi mikavum tāḻnta celaviṉi yuṭittākalāṉum, tiraḷmikamuṭukiya naṭiyiṉi yuṭittākalāṉum ivi tavirntum, iṭippaṭṭa vārap pāṭal colloḻukkamum iciyoḻukkamum uṭittākalāṉum, kūṭippāṭal coṟceṟivum icicceṟivum uṭittākalāṉum ciṟappunōkki, avviraṇṭiṉuḷḷum vārappāṭali aḷavu nirampa niṟuttavallaṉāy eṉka. eṉavē, kūṭippāṭalum amivatāyiṟṟu.
69.		icikkum eṉpati eḻutteḻuttāka icikkum eṉṟum, collappaṭṭa yāvaṟṟiyum vaḻuviṉṟicikkum eṉṟum pirittuk kūṭṭuka.
aṟintu mayalaṟappeytu uṇarviṉaṉāki aṟintu porunti vittu vaḷarttu oṟṟi iyakki vittup pārttu icikkuṅ kuḻa lōṉ eṉka.
yāḻāciriyaṉ amiti
ippakutiyiṉ uri naṉku viḷaṅkutaṟ poruṭṭu iciyip paṟṟiya cila koḷkikaḷi mutaṟkaṇ viḷakkutum;-
tamiḻil ici eṉpatu curam, irākam eṉṉum iru poruḷilum vaḻaṅkum. irākattiṟku iciyeṉṉum peyaraṉṟip paṇ eṉṟa peyarum uṇṭu. vaṭamoḻiyil curutiyeṉṟu collap paṭuvatu tamiḻil alaku eṉṟum, māttiri yeṉṟum vaḻaṅkum. maṟṟum, curam eṉpataṟku narampu eṉṟa peyarum, paṇ eṉpataṟku yāḻ eṉṟa peyarum tamiḻil vaḻaṅkum. ēḻicikaṭkum tamiḻilē kural, tuttam, kikkiḷi,  uḻi, iḷi, viḷari, tāram eṉpaṉa peyarkaḷām. ēḻu curaṅkaṭkum vaṭamoḻiyilē caṭcam, riṭapam kāntāram, mattimam, pañcamam, tivatam, niṭātam eṉpaṉa peyarkaḷām.
tamiḻil ēḻicikaṭkum irupattiraṇṭu alakukaḷ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa; avi kural mutaliyavaṟṟiṟku muṟiyē 4, 4, 3, 2, 4, 3, 2 ākum; itaṉi,
"	kuraltuttam nāṉku kiḷimūṉ ṟiraṇṭām  kuriyā uḻiyiḷi nāṉku - viriyā
viḷariyeṉiṉ mūṉṟiraṇṭu tārameṉac coṉṉār 
kaḷaricēr kaṇṇuṟ ṟavar"
eṉpataṉāṉaṟika.
vaṭamoḻiyilum ēḻu curaṅkaṭkum irupattiraṇṭu curutikaḷē kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa; avi caṭcam mutaliyavaṟṟiṟku muṟiyē 4, 3, 2, 4, 4, 3, 2 ākum.
inta ici allatu curaṅkaḷiṉ varicikkut tamiḻil kōvi yeṉṟum peyaruṇṭu. ivvarici yamippukkaḷ āyappāli, caturappāli, vaṭṭappāli, tirikōṇappāli eṉa nālvakip paṭukiṉṟaṉa, ivaṟṟiṉiṉṟum uṇṭāvatu irākam allatu paṇ ākum.
ēḻici yeṉappaṭum curaṅkaḷ ēḻiṉiyum māṟuntiṟat tiṉālēyē palavakip paṇkaḷ allatu irākaṅkaḷ uṇṭā kiṉṟaṉa; ākaliṉ curaṅkaḷippaṟṟiya ceytikaḷi mutalil naṉku terintu koḷḷutal vēṇṭum. kural mutalāya ēḻaṉuḷ, tārattil uḻiyum, uḻiyiṟ kuralum, kuralil iḷiyum, iḷiyil tuttamum, tuttattil viḷariyum, viḷariyiṟ kikkiḷiyum  piṟakkum; ivaṟṟuḷ mutaliṟ ṟōṉṟiyatu tāram.
"	tārattuṭ ṭōṉṟum uḻiyuḻi yuṭṭōṉṟum
ōruṅ kural kurali ṉuṭṭōṉṟic - cērumiḷi
yuṭṭōṉṟun tuttattuṭ ṭōṉṟum viḷariyuṭ
kikkiḷi tōṉṟum piṟappu"
eṉpatu kāṇka. tārattuḷ ataṟ kintāvatākiya uḻiyum, uḻiyuḷ ataṟ kintāvatākiya kuralum, immuṟiyē ēṉiyavum tōṉṟiṉa veṉpatu aṟiyaṟpālatu. ivvēḻicikaḷum vaṭṭap
pāli muṟiyil ōr irāci maṇṭalattiṉ paṉṉiraṇṭu irukki kaḷiyum iṭamākak koḷḷum eṉpatu piṉṉar āycciyar kuraviyuḷ viḷakkappaṭum.
ōr irākam allatu paṇṇiṉi uṇṭākkutaṟ poruṭṭu mutalilē toṭaṅkappeṟum curam kural eṉpatākum. ēḻu curaṅkaḷil etaṉiyum kuralāka niṟuttutal uṇṭu; aḵtāvatu kuralē kuralākavum, tuttam kuralākavum, kikkiḷi kuralākavum iṅṅaṉam toṭaṅkap peṟum eṉpatām.
curaṅkaḷik koṇṭu iciyi eḻupputaṟkuk karuviyākak koḷḷappaṭṭa eḻuttukkaḷ carikamapatani eṉṉum ēḻumām. ivveḻuttukkaḷ caṭcam mutaliya peyarkaḷiṉ mutaleḻuttukkaḷ eṉṟu koḷvaṉa poruntāmiyāl, ivi kuṟiyīṭāka amittuk koḷḷap peṟṟaṉavātal vēṇṭum. vaṭa nūṟṟuṟipōyacilarum iṅṅaṉam karutuvar.
"	ā ī ū ē ai ō oḷa veṉum 
ivvē ḻeḻuttum ēḻicik kuriya"
eṉat tivākaram kūṟutaliṉ, ā mutaliya neṭṭuyir ēḻaṉiyum karuviyākak koṇṭu paṇṭit tamiḻ makkaḷ ici pāṭiṉa rātalum vēṇṭum.
tamiḻilē irākaṅkaḷ paṇ eṉavum, tiṟam eṉavum iru vakip paṭum. "niṟinaram piṟṟē paṇṇeṉa lākum," "kuṟinaram piṟṟē tiṟameṉap paṭumē" eṉṉum tivākara nūṟpākkaḷāl ēḻunarampāṉum iyaṉṟatu paṇṇām eṉpatum, āṟu aintu nāṉku eṉak kuṟinta narampukaḷāṉ iyaṉṟaṉa tiṟamām eṉpatum peṟap paṭum. tiṟatti mūṉṟu vakippaṭuttup paṇṇiyaṟṟiṟam, tiṟam, tiṟattiṟam eṉa vaḻaṅkutalu muṇṭu. paṇṇum, tiṟamūṉṟum ākiya in nālvaki irākaṅkaḷiyum kuṟikkum vaṭamoḻip peyarkaḷ campūraṇam, cāṭavam, oḷaṭavam, caturttam eṉpaṉa. innāṉkum tamiḻil paṇ, tiṟam eṉa iraṇṭākak kūṟappaṭuta lōṭu, yāvum paṇṇeṉṟē kūṟappaṭutalum uṇṭu.
tamiḻil aintiṇikkumuriya paṇkaḷ kuṟiñciyāḻ, pāli yāḻ, mulliyāḻ, neytalyāḻ, marutayāḻ eṉpaṉa, ivaṟṟuḷ neytal yāḻukku viḷari eṉpatum peyar. itu tiṟaṉil yāḻ eṉap paṭutaliṉ, tiṟaṅkaḷuṭiya ēṉi nāṉkumē perumpaṇkaḷ eṉappaṭum; "yāma yāḻppeyar kuṟiñci yāḻum, cevvaḻi yāḻp peyar mulli yāḻum, pāli yāḻum maruta yāḻumeṉa, nālvaki yāḻum nāṟperum paṇṇē" eṉpatu tivākaram; itiliruntu kuṟiñci yāḻukku yāma yāḻ eṉṟa peyarum, mulli yāḻukkuc cevvaḻi yāḻ eṉṟa peyarum uṇṭeṉpatu pulaṉām. in nāṟ perum paṇṇum piṟakkumāṟu "tārat tuḻitōṉṟap pāli yāḻ taṇkural, orumuḻi tōṉṟak kuṟiñciyāḻ - nērē, iḷikuraliṟ ṟōṉṟa marutayāḻ tuttam, iḷiyiṟ piṟakkaneyta liyāḻ" eṉakkūṟappaṭṭuḷatu. itil neytal yāḻ eṉṟatu cevvaḻi yātal vēṇṭum. eṉavē tāram kuralāka uḻi ataṟkuk kiḷiyākat tōṉṟuvatu pāliyāḻ eṉavum, uḻikuralākak kural ataṟkuk kiḷiyākat tōṉṟuvatu kuṟiñciyāḻ eṉavum kural kuralāka iḷi ataṟkuk kiḷiyākat tōṉṟuvatu marutayāḻ eṉavum, iḷi kuralākat tuttam ataṟkuk kiḷiyākat tōṉṟuvatu cevvaḻi yāḻ eṉavum kūṟappaṭum eṉka. kiḷiyāvatu niṉṟa narampiṉiṉṟum tōṉṟum narampu; niṉṟa narampiṟku aintāvatu narampu. ivvāṟē niṉṟa narampiṟku nāṉkāvatu naṭpu narampeṉṟum, mūṉ ṟāvatum āṟāvatum paki narampeṉṟum, iraṇṭāvatum ēḻāvatum iṇi narampeṉṟum aṟika.
iṉi. muṟkuṟitta nāṉku paṇkaḷuḷ pāliyāḻiliruntu cempāli, paṭumalip pāli, cevvaḻip pāli, arumpāli, kōṭippāli, viḷarip pāli, mēṟcem pāli eṉṉum ēḻu pāli yicikaḷ piṟakkum; ivi piṟakkumāṟu mēl ik kāti yuḷḷum, āycciyar kuraviyuḷḷum viḷakkamām.
iṉi, pāli, kuṟiñci, marutam, cevvaḻi yeṉṉum nāṟ perumpaṇkaḷil ovvoṉṟum akanili, puṟanili, arukiyal, perukiyal eṉṉum iṉa vēṟu pāṭṭāl nannāṉku ākap perumpaṇkaḷ patiṉāṟākum. nāṟperum paṇkaḷuḷḷē pāli yāḻukku aintum, kuṟiñci yāḻukku eṭṭum, maruta yāḻukku nāṉkum, cevvaḻi yāḻukku nāṉkum āka irupattoru tiṟaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. "īriru paṇṇum eḻumūṉṟu tiṟaṉum" eṉpatu piṅkalam. tiṟam irupat toṉṟum akanili, puṟanili, arukiyal, perukiyal eṉṉum vēṟu pāṭṭāl eṇpattu nāṉku ākum. eṉavē, paṇ patiṉāṟum, tiṟam eṇpattu nāṉkum cērntu nūṟu eṉṉum tokiyiṉavākiṉṟaṉa. piṅkala nikaṇṭilē tiṟaṅkaḷiṉ vaki yāvaṟṟiṟkum peyar kūṟi muṭitta piṉ, "tārap paṇṭiṟam piyuḷ kāñci, paṭumali yivi nūṟṟu mūṉṟu tiṟattaṉa" eṉṟu kūṟap paṭṭiruttaliṉ, tārap paṇṭiṟam, piyuḷ kāñci, paṭumali eṉṉum mūṉṟu tiṟaṅkaḷum muṟkūṟiya nūṟṟuṭaṉ cērap paṇkaḷ nūṟṟu mūṉṟu eṉṉum tokipeṟum. paṇ nūṟṟumūṉṟeṉṟal peru vaḻakku. perum paṇkaḷiṉ vaki patiṉāṟaṉuṭ paṉṉiraṇṭukkum, tiṟaṅkaḷum, avaṟṟiṉ vakiyumākiya eṇpattu nāṉkiṟkum peyar piṅkala nikaṇṭiṟ kāṇka.
iṉi, tamiḻilē kural mutalākavum, vaṭamoḻiyilē caṭcam mutalākavum ēḻucuraṅkaḷum peyar kūṟappaṭutaliṉ, kuralum caṭcamum oṉṟeṉavum, tuttamum riṭapamum oṉṟeṉavum, ivvāṟē ēṉiyavum muṟiyē ovvoṉṟā meṉavum karututal kūṭum. āyiṉ, ivaṟṟukkuk kūṟappaṭum alakum oliyum piṟappiṭamum vēṟupaṭutaliṉ, ammuṟiyē ivviraṇṭum oṉṟeṉal amiyāmi peṟṟām. īṇṭu alakiṉi nōkkutum :-
kural- 4, tuttam- 4, kikkiḷi- 3, uḻi- 2, iḷi-4, viḷari-3, tāram - 2.
  caṭcam - 4, riṭapam - 3, kāntāram - 2, mattimam - 4, pañcamam - 4, tivatam - 3, niṭātam - 2.
ivaṟṟuḷ iraṇṭāvatum mūṉṟāvatum nāṉkāvatum māttiriyil ovvāmi kāṇka. caṭcam mutaliyavaṟṟiṟku iṅkē kāṭṭiya curutikaḷiṉ aḷavē cāraṅkatēvar iyaṟṟiya caṅkītaratṉākaram mutaliya nūṟkaḷ palavaṟṟilum kāṇap paṭutaliṉ, ammuṟiyē tamiḻukkum poruttamātal vēṇṭum. kural eṉpataṉic caṭcam eṉṟu koḷḷātu mattimam eṉak koḷḷiṉ iraṇṭilum curutiyaḷavukaḷ ottu viṭukiṉṟaṉa.
kural - 4, tuttam - 4, kikkiḷi -3, uḻi - 2, iḷi-4, viḷari - 3, tāram - 2.
ma - 4, pa - 4, ta - 3, ni - 2, ca - 4, ri - 3, ka - 2.
curukkaṅkaruti mattimam mutaliya peyarkaḷ ma mutaliya eḻuttuk kaḷāṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭaṉa. mēlē kāṭṭiyavaṟṟil iru tiṟattum alaku ottiruttal kāṇka. ivvāṟṟāl paṇṭit tamiḻ ici nūlōr vaṭanūṉ muṟiyilaminta mattimatti ārampa curamākak koṇṭaṉa reṉpatu pōtarum. eṉavē, tārattu uḻi tōṉṟum eṉṟa muṟippaṭi kāntārattil niṭātamum, niṭātattil mattimamum, mattimattil caṭcamum, caṭcattil pañcamamum, pañcamattil riṭapamum, riṭapattil tivatamum piṟakkum eṉak koḷḷa vēṇṭum. ivaṟṟi naṉku niṉivil vittuk koḷḷiṉ, piṉ kūṟappaṭum iciyiyalpukaḷ perumpālum mayakkaṟa viḷaṅkum. mika nuṭpamāṉa iciyiyalpukaḷi aṟiya viḻivōr ici nūṟkaḷik kaṟṟum, icippayiṟci ceytum aṟital vēṇṭum.
70 -94 	(īrēḻtoṭutta............pulami yōṉuṭaṉ)
70. 		īrēḻ toṭutta cemmuṟik kēḷviyiṉ - cevviya muṟiyē iraṇṭēḻākat toṭukkappaṭṭa āyap pāliyāy niṉṟa patiṉāṟkōviyil, 
71. 		ōrēḻ pāli niṟuttal vēṇṭi - cempāli paṭumalip pāli cevvaḻippāli arumpāli kōṭippāli viḷarip pāli mēṟcempāli yeṉappaṭṭa ēḻu pāliyiṉiyum iṇinarampu toṭuttu nirampa niṟuttik kāṭṭal kāraṇamāka, 
72.		vaṇmiyiṟ kiṭanta tārapākamum - ippāliyiṉ muṭivut tāṉamāy valinta nilimiyiṉiyuṭiya tāram peṟṟa iraṇṭalakil ōralakiyum, 
 73.		meṉmiyiṟ kiṭanta kuraliṉ pākamum - mutaṟṟāṉamāy meliviṉiṟkum kural narampupeṟṟa nālalakil iraṇṭalakiyum, 
74. 		tārattiṟkuk kikkiḷi iṭamuṟiyāl aintāvatākaliṉ kiḷi narampeṉṟār. kiḷi - aintāvatu narampu.
 74 - 75	meykkiḷi narampiṟ kikkiḷi koḷḷak kikkiḷi - tāranarampil antarak kōlilē kikkiḷiyāka niṟuttat tārantāṉ kikkiḷiyāyiṟṟu; 
75 - 77 	oḻinta pākamum poṟpuṭit taḷarāttāram viḷarikku īttuk kiḷivaḻip paṭṭaṉaḷ - taḷarāta aḻakuṭiya tāra narampil oḻinta ōralakiyum viḷarikkuttara avviḷari tutta narampāyiṟṟu; 
77 - 78 	āṅkē kiḷiyum taṉ kiḷi aḻivu kaṇṭu avaḷ vayiṟ cēra - ammuṟiyē iḷiyum taṉ kiḷiyākiya kuraliṉ aḻiviṉik kaṇṭu ataṉpāṟ cēravum, 
79.  		ēṉi makaḷirum kiḷivaḻic cēra - ēṉiya uḻi mutalāyiṉavum tattamakkuk kiḷiyāyiṉavaṟṟiṟ cēravum ivvāṟāya patiṉāṟ kōviyilē, 
80 - 81 	mēlatu uḻi yiḷi kīḻatu kikkiḷi vampuṟu marapiṟ cempāli āyatu - uḻimutalākak kikkiḷi yiṟutiyāka melivu nāṉkum camam ēḻum valivu mūṉṟumāyp putumiyuṟṟa muṟiyālē uḻi kuralākac cempāliyāyatu;
