98-101. 	mācu il kuravar malaraṭi toḻutēṉ kōcika māṇi kāṭṭu eṉak koṭuttu - kōcikaṉē kuṟṟamillāta eṉ irumutu kuravaratu malar pōṉṟa tiruvaṭikaḷi vaṇaṅkiṉēṉ eṉac colli im muṭaṅkali avariṭam kāṭṭuvāyāka veṉa avaṉiṭam koṭuttu, naṭukkam kaḷintu - eṉ pirivāl avarkkuṇṭāṉa naṭukkattiṉip pōkki, avar nal akam poruntiya-avaratu nalla uḷḷattiṟ poruntiya, iṭukkaṇ kaḷitaṟku īṇṭu eṉap pōkki - tuṉpattiṉi oḻippataṟku virintu celvāyāka eṉṟu avaṉippōkaviṭṭu;
īṇṭutal - virital eṉaccolli' eṉa oru col varuvikka. ‘mācil kuravar' eṉavum ‘avar nallakam' eṉavum kūṟiyatu eṉ tīviṉip poruṭṭāl avar tuṉpam nukara nērntataṉṟi avar tīviṉiyāḷar allar eṉpatu kuṟittaṟkeṉka. ‘naṭukkam kaḷintu' eṉpataṟku nī naṭuṅkum naṭukkatti pōkki eṉa urippar arumpata vuriyāciriyar.
102-103. 	mācu il kaṟpiṉ maṉiyoṭu irunta - kuṟṟamilāk kaṟpiṉiyuṭiya taṉ maṉiviyākiya kaṇṇakiyōṭu irunta, ācu il koḷki aṟavipāl aṇintu - tavaṟillāk kōṭ pāṭṭiṉiyuṭiya kavunti yaṭikaḷiṭam ceṉṟu cērntu ;
103-105. 	āṅku - avviṭattu, āṭu iyal koḷki antari kōlam pāṭum pāṇaril - veṟṟi poruntiya koḷkiyi yuṭiya turkkiyatu pōrkkōlattip pāṭum pāṇaruṭaṉ, pāṅkuṟac cērntu - urimiyuṟak kalantu ;
1"pāṇa rokkal" eṉpavākaliṉ, pāṇariṟ pāṅkuṟac cērnteṉṟār. āṭu - veṟṟi.
106. 		cenniṟam purinta ceṅkōṭṭu yāḻil - cevvitiṉ iyaṟṟappaṭṭa ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻil ;
ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻ - nālvaki yāḻiloṉṟu ; ēḻu narampiṉi yuṭiyatu. itu pāṇaratu yāḻ eṉka. 
107. 		tantiri karattoṭu tivavu uṟuttu yāttu - tantirikaram tivavu eṉṉum iraṇṭiṉiyum uṟutipeṟak kaṭṭi ;
tantirikaramāvatu narampu tuvakkutaṟkut takippoḻiya irucāṇ nālviral nīḷamākak kuṟukkiṭattuc cērttum aimpattōruṟuppu eṉpar. ik kālattu itu meṭṭu eṉa vaḻaṅkum. tivavu eṉpatu narampukaḷi valipeṟak kaṭṭum vārkkaṭṭu. 
108. 		oṟṟu uṟuppu uṭimiyiṉ paṟṟuvaḻic cērtti-ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻ oṟṟu, paṟṟu eṉṉum uṟuppukkaḷiyuṭit tākalāṉ oṟṟiṉip paṟṟiṭattiṟ cērtti ;
ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻiṉ uṟuppukkaḷ āṟu ; itaṉi, "ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻē cevvitiṟ ṟeriyiṉ, aṟuvaki yuṟuppiṟ ṟākumeṉpa" "avitām, kōṭē tivavē yoṟṟē....... tantirikaramē narampō ṭāṟē" eṉpavaṟṟāṉaṟika. oṟṟuṟuppu - narampiṉum pattariṉum tākkuvatōr karuvi yeṉpar. ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻiṉ paṇ moḻi narampu nāṉkoḻiya ēṉi mūṉṟum tāḷamum curutiyum kūṭṭuvaṉavākaliṉ, aviyē oṟṟum, paṟṟum eṉalāyiṉa; oṟṟoṉṟē koḷḷalumām. 
109. 		uḻi mutal kikkiḷi iṟuvāyk kaṭṭi - uḻi kuralākavum kikkiḷi tāramākavum narampukaḷi niṟutti ;
eḻuvakip pāliyuḷ arumpāli eṉṉum iciyip piṟappit teṉṟavāṟu. uḻi kuralāyatu arumpāli yeṉpataṉi āycciyar, kuraviyuḷ "kuṭamuta liṭamuṟi...vēṇṭiya peyarē" eṉpataṉ uriyālaṟika. itu kuṭamutaṟ pālit tirivu.
110-112.	varaṉmuṟi vanta mūvakit tāṉattu - nūlkaḷiṉ varalāṟṟu muṟimiyāl vanta mūvakit tāṉattālum, pāykalip pāvi pāṭaṟ pāṇi - pāyntu cellum kali yūrtiyi yuṭiya koṟṟaviyiṉ pāṭalākiya paṇṇi, ācāṉ tiṟattiṉ amivarakkēṭṭu - ācāṉ eṉṉum paṇṇiyaliṉ nālvakic cātiyākiya tiṟaṅkaḷuṭaṉ poruntutalvarac cevip pulattāṉaṟintu;
mūvakittāṉam - valivu melivu camaṉ eṉṉum mūvaki iyakkiṟkuriya iṭam; eḻuttu aci cīr eṉpārumuḷar. paṇ, paṇṇiyaṟ ṟiṟam, tiṟam, tiṟattiṟam eṉap paṇkaḷ nāṉku kūṟupaṭum; ivaṟṟuḷ paṇ eṉpatu ēḻu curamum amiyap peṟuvatu; ēṉiyaṉa muṟiyē ovvoru curam kuṟintu varuvaṉa; ivaṟṟik kuṟikkum vaṭamoḻip peyarkaḷ campūraṇam, cāṭavam, oḷaṭavam, caturttam eṉpaṉa. paṇ mutaliya nāṉkum paṇ, tiṟam eṉa iraṇṭākavum aṭakkik kūṟappaṭum. pāliyāḻ eṉṉum perum paṇṇiṉ tiṟam arākam, nērtiṟam, uṟuppu, kuṟuṅkali, ācāṉ eṉṉum aintumām. ivaṟṟuḷ ovvoṉṟum akanili, puṟanili, arukiyal. perukiyal eṉṉum nālvakic cātipaṟṟi nannāṉku ākum. ācāṉ eṉṉun tiṟattiṉ akanili kāntāramum, puṟanili cikaṇṭiyum, arukiyal tēcākkiriyum, perukiyal curutikāntāramum ām ; iṟutik kaṇṇatu cutta kāntāram eṉavum kūṟappaṭum. iv virākaṅkaḷic ceviyāl ōrtteṉka. 
 113. 		pāṭaṟ pāṇi aḷii avaroṭu - iṅṅaṉamākiya paṇkaḷi avarōṭu kalantu vācittu;  
114. 		kūṭaṟ kāvatam kūṟumiṉ nīr eṉa - ivviṭattiṉiṉṟu maturi ettuṇik kāvata tūrattiluḷḷatu; ataṉik kūṟumiṉ nīreṉṟu kōvalaṉ kēṭpa;  
115-118. 	kāḻ akil cāntam kamaḻ pūṅ kuṅkumam nāvik kuḻampu - vayiram paṟṟiya akiliṉ cāntum maṇaṅ kamaḻum kuṅkumap pūṅ kuḻampum puḻukuk kuḻampum, nalaṅkoḷ tēyvi māṉmatac cāntam - maṇamākiya naṉmi aminta cantaṉac cāntum kattūric cāntumākiya, maṇam kamaḻ teyvat tēmmeṉ koḻuñ cēṟu āṭi - it teyva maṇam vīcum iṉiya melliya vaḷaviya cēṟṟi yaḷintu;
kuḻampu eṉpataṉik kuṅkumattōṭuṅ kūṭṭuka. nāvi - puḻuku. tēm - iṉimi.
118-121. 	āṅkut tātu cēr kaḻunīr caṇpakak kōtiyoṭu - pūm poṭi poruntiya kaḻunīr malarum caṇpaka malarum eṉṉum ivaṟṟāṉāya māliyoṭu, mātavi malliki maṉi vaḷar mullip pōtu viritoṭiyal pū aṇi porunti - kurukkattiyum mallikiyum maṉikkaṇ vaḷarkkap peṟṟa mulliyumeṉṟa ivaṟṟiṉ virinta malarkaḷāl toṭutta māliyiyum uṭiya malar aṇikkaṇ porunti ;
āṅku - āci ; appoḻutu eṉṟalumām. pū aṇi - poliviṉiyuṭiya aṇi yeṉṟumām. mulli maṉiyil vaḷarvataṉi 1"illeḻu mulliyoṭu malliki mayaṅki" eṉpataṉālum aṟika.
122-126. 	aṭṭil pukiyum - aṭukkaḷikaḷil tōṉṟum tāḷippu mutaliya pukiyum, akal aṅkāṭi muṭṭāk kūviyar mōtakap pukiyum - akaṉṟa kaṭivītikkaṇ tam vāṇipam muṭṭup peṟāta appa vāṇikar appam cuṭukiṉṟamiyāṉ uṇṭākum pukiyum, mintarum makaḷirum māṭattu eṭutta antīm pukiyum - āṭavarum makaḷirum mēl māṭattu uṇṭākkiya aḻakiya iṉiya akiṟ pukiyum, ākutip pukiyum - vēḷvic cālikaḷil ōmam paṇṇutalāl eḻum pukiyum ākiya, palvēṟu pūm puki aḷii - palavāka vēṟupaṭṭa poliviṉiyuṭiya pukiyi aḷāvi ;
aṅkāṭi - kaṭivīti. kūviyar - appa vāṇikar. muṭṭā mōtakam eṉa iyittu muṭṭāmaṟ cuṭukiṉṟa mōtakam eṉalu mām. mintarum makaḷirum puki eṭuttal mayir, tukil, māli mutaliyavaṟṟiṟku maṇamūṭṭaṟkeṉka. ;  
126-129. 	velpōr viḷaṅku pūṇ mārpiṟ pāṇṭiyaṉ kōyiliṉ - veṉṟi kāṇum pōriṉiyum kalaṉ viḷaṅkum mārpiṉiyum uṭiya pāṇṭiyaṉatu kōyiliṉkaṇ tōṉṟum, aḷantu uṇarvu aṟiyā - aṟivāṉ aḷaviṭṭaṟiya voṇṇāta, ār uyir piṇikkum - ariya uḷḷattit takikkum, kalavik kūṭṭam kāṇvarat tōṉṟi - naṟumaṇak kalaviyiṉ tokuti veḷippaṭat tōṉṟi ;
viḷaṅku pūṇ - intiraṉāṟ pūṭṭappaṭṭa āramumām. kōyiliṟ kalavi, aṟiyāk kalavi, piṇikkum kalavi eṉka. tammi orukāl nukarnta uḷḷam piṉṉar vēṟoṉṟiṉi nukara virumpātu tammiyē virumpi niṟkumāṟu ceyyum kalavik kūṭṭam eṉpār āruyir piṇikkum kalavik kūṭṭam eṉṟār. 
130-132. 	pulavar cennāp poruntiya nivappiṉ - pulavarkaḷatu cevviya nāvāl pukaḻappaṭṭa ciṟappiṉiyuṭiya, potiyil teṉṟal pōlātu - potiyiṉ maliyil tōṉṟum teṉṟal taṉṉi ovvā vaṇṇam, īṅku maturit teṉṟal vantatu kāṇīr - inta maturiyiṉ teṉṟalāṉatu vantatu itaṉik kāṇpīr;
cennā - poy kūṟā nā. nivappu - uyarvu ; ciṟappu ; ciṟappiṉiyuṭiya maturit teṉṟal eṉka. potiyiṟkaṇ amiyāta maṇam palavaṟṟiyum it teṉṟal maturikkaṇ aḷintu varutaliṉ itaṟku ap potiyiṟ ṟeṉṟal ovvātāyiṟṟu. āṭi, porunti, aḷii, tōṉṟit teṉṟal vantatu eṉka.  
133-134. 	naṉi cēyttu aṉṟu avaṉ tiru mali mūtūr - ataṉāl ap pāṇṭiyaṉatu celva mikka maturi mikac cēymiyiluḷḷataṉṟu; taṉi nīr kaḻiyiṉum takikkunar il eṉa - mēlum taṉitta nīrmiyiṟ celliṉum vaḻikkaṇ taṭuppār oruvarum illi eṉṟu colla;
taṉi nīrkaḻiyiṉum eṉpataṟku nīvir taṉiyē celliṉum eṉṟalum poruntum. takikkunar - taṭuttu niṟutti āṟalippār.  
135-136. 	muṉṉāḷ muṟimiyiṉ irun tava mutalviyoṭu piṉṉiyum alliṭip peyarntaṉar - periya tavattiṉiuṭiya kavuntiyaṭikaḷuṭaṉē mutaṉāḷiṟ ceṉṟa muṟimi pōlap piṟṟi ñāṉṟum iraviṉkaṇ ceṉṟaṉar, peyarn tāṅku - cella,
137-140. 	arunteṟal kaṭavuḷ akaṉ peruṅ kōyilum - piṟarkku ariya aḻittaṟṟoḻili valla iṟivaṉatu akaṉṟa periya kōyiliṉiṭattum, perum peyar maṉṉavaṉ pēr icik kōyilum - periya pukaḻ vāynta pāṇṭiyaṉatu mikka pukaḻ aminta kōyiliṉiṭattum, pāl keḻu ciṟappiṉ palliyam ciṟanta kāli muracak kaṉikural ōtiyum - pakutippaṭṭa ciṟappiṉiyuṭiya kāli muracamākiya ciṟanta palliyattiṉ ceṟinta muḻakkoliyum ;
peyarntu - peyaraveṉat tirikka. teṟaṟ kaṭavuḷ - ālavāy iṟivaṉ. peyarntu eṉpataṉip palliyam eṉpataṉōṭu iyittup palliyam peyarntu olikkum oli eṉapporuḷ koṇṭu, peyartal - tāḷamaṟuta eṉavurippar aṭiyārkku nallār. kālimuracam - paḷḷiyeḻucci muracam. kāli muracap palliya vōtiyum eṉka.  
141. 		nāṉmaṟi antaṇar naviṉṟa ōtiyum - pārppār nāṉku maṟikaḷiyum payiṉṟōtum ōciyum ;
142. 		mātavar ōti malinta ōtiyum - iruṭikaḷ kāliyil mantiram ōtutalāṉ niṟinta ōciyum ; 
143-144. 	mīḷā veṉṟi vēntaṉ ciṟappoṭu - veṟṟi taṉṉiṉiṉṟum mīḷap peṟāta aracaṉāṟ peṟṟa ciṟappuṭaṉē, vāḷōr eṭutta nāḷ aṇi muḻavamum - vāḷvīrar tattam vīrattiṟku nāṭ kāliyil eṭutta muracu mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ aḻakiya ōciyum;
ciṟappoṭu eṭutta muḻavam eṉṟatu aracaṉpāl variciyākap peṟṟa muḻavam eṉṟapaṭi. nāḷ - kāli. nāḷ - kuṭi nāṭkōḷ mutaliyavumām. 
145. 		pōril koṇṭa poru kari muḻakkamum - pōrilē pakivari veṉṟu koṇṭuvanta pōr yāṉiyiṉ muḻakkamum;
146. 		vārikkoṇṭa vayakkari muḻakkamum - kāṭṭiṟ piṭittuk koṇṭuvanta valiya yāṉiyiṉ muḻakkamum. 
147.		paṇinilip puravi ālum ōtiyum - pantikaḷil niṟkum kutirikaḷ ālikkum ōciyum;
148. 		kiṇinilip porunar vikaṟip pāṇiyum - kiṇip paṟiyiyuṭiya maḷḷar maruta nilattu vikaṟikkaṇ koṭṭum oliyum ākiya ;
kiṇi - marutappaṟi. porunar - kūttarumām. pāṇi - pāṭṭi ṉoliyumām.
146-150. 	kārkkaṭal oliyil kali keḻu kūṭal ārppoli etirkoḷa ār añar nīṅki - makiḻcci mikka kūṭaṟkaṇ ārkkum olikaḷ kariya kaṭal oli pōṉṟu mikku olittu etir koḷḷutalāṉ muṉṉit tuṉpaṅkaḷellām nīṅki;
etirkoḷa - etirē vara ; etirkoṇṭaḻippatu pōlāka. palvaki olikaḷik kēṭṭalāṉum, patiyi yaṭintō meṉṉum makiḻcciyāṉum añar nīṅkiṉāreṉka. 
151-154. 	kuravamum vakuḷamum kōṅkamum vēṅkiyum - kurāvum makiḻum kōṅkum vēṅkiyum, maravamum nākamum tilakamum marutamum - veṇkaṭampum curapuṉṉiyum mañcāṭiyum marutamaramum, cēṭalum ceruntiyum ceṇpaka ōṅkalum - uccic celuntilum ceruntiyum ceṇpakamaramum, pāṭalam taṉṉoṭu paṉ malar virintu - ākiya ivi pātiriyuṭaṉē pala vaki malarkaḷum virintu;
kuravamalar mutaliyaṉavum paṉmalarum virintu eṉka. cēṭal - uccic celuntil eṉṉum marameṉpar. ōṅkal - maram; ākupeyar.
155-160. 	kurukum taḷavamum koḻuṅkoṭi mucuṇṭiyum- kurukkattiyum cemmulliyum vaḷaviya koṭiyiṉiyuṭiya mucuṭṭiyum, virimalar atiralum veṇkūtāḷamum - virinta pūkkaḷiyuṭiya mōci mallikiyum veḷḷi naṟun
tāḷiyum, kuṭacamum vetiramum koḻuṅkoṭip pakaṉṟiyum - veṭpāliyum mūṅkilum koḻuviya koṭiyākap poruntiya civatiyum, piṭavamum mayiliyum piṇaṅku aril maṇanta - kuṭṭip piṭavamum iruvāṭciyumāya ivi piṉṉiya piṇakkattuṭaṉ kalakkap peṟṟa, koṭuṅkari mēkalik kōvi yāṅkaṇummiṭintu cūḻpōkiya akaṉṟu ēntu alkul - kōviyākiya mēkali evviṭattum ceṟintu cuṟṟiya akaṉṟu uyarnta kariyākiya alkuliṉiyum;
vetiram - am cāriyi. pakaṉṟi - cīntilumām; peruṅkiyāl eṉṟumurippar. mayili - koṭimalliki yeṉpārumuḷar. koṭumi kūṟiṉār alkul eṉpataṟkup porunta. virimalar, koḻuṅkoṭi ivaṟṟi ēṟpuḻik kūṭṭuka.
paṉmalar virintu maṇanta mēkalikkōvi yāṅkaṇum cūḻ pōkiya akaṉṟēntiya koṭuṅkariyākiya alkul eṉak kūṭṭuka. kariyiṉ puṟavāyeṅkum kurava mutalākap paṉmalar virintavi mītuṭutta pūntukilākavum, akavāyeṅkum kuruku mutalākap piṇaṅkaril maṇantavi mēkaliyākavum uṭiya kariyākiya valkul eṉpar aṭiyārkku nallār.
161-163. 	vālukam kuviiya malarpūn turuttip pālpuṭik koṇṭu palmalar ōṅki etir etir viḷaṅkiya katir iḷa vaṉa muli - virinta poliviṉiyuṭiya āṟṟiṭik kuṟiyiṉkaṇ aṭippakkam akaṉṟu pala malarkaḷāl uyarcci peṟṟu oṉṟaṟkoṉṟu ottu viḷaṅkiya kuvitaliyuṭiya maṇaṟkuṉṟākiya viḷakkam poruntiya iḷiya aḻakiya muliyiṉiyum;
turuttikkaṇ puṭikkoṇṭu ōṅki viḷaṅkiya vāluka muli eṉka. etir - oppu; māṟupaṭalumām. pālpuṭik koḷḷalum paṉmalarōṅkalum etiretir viḷaṅkalum mulikkum poruntumāṟuṇarka. vilaṅkiya eṉap pāṭaṅkoṇṭu, oṉṟiyoṉṟu neruṅkiya veṉṟurippar arumpata vuriyāciriyar. 
164. 		kari niṉṟu utirtta kavir itaḻc cevvāy - karik kaṇ niṉṟu utirtta murukkamalariṉ itaḻākiya civanta vāyiṉiyum; 
165. 		aruvi mulli aṇi nakiyāṭṭi - aruvi nīroṭu vanta mulli malarākiya aḻakiya nakiyiṉiyu muṭiyāḷ; 
166. 		vilaṅku nimirntu oḻukiya karuṅkayal neṭuṅkaṇ - kuṟukkē maṟintum neṭuka ōṭiyum tirikiṉṟa periya kayal ākiya nīṇṭa kaṇkaḷiyum;
vilaṅku nimirtalum oḻukalum kaṇṇukkum poruntumā ṟuṇarka. 
167.		viri malar nīṅkā avir aṟaṟ kūntal - maṇam porun tiya malarkaḷ nīṅkap peṟātu viḷaṅkukiṉṟa aṟalākiya kūnta liṉiyum; 
168-170. 	ulaku purantu ūṭṭum uyar pēr oḻukkattu - ulakiṉip pala poruḷiyum viḷittu uṇpittuk kākkiṉṟa uyarnta periya oḻukkattiṉiyuṭiya, pulavar nāvil porun tiya pūṅkoṭi - pulavarkaḷuṭiya nāviṉkaṇ poruntiya tirumakaḷi oppāḷ, viyi eṉṟa poyyāk kulakkoṭi - viyi eṉṟu collappaṭukiṉṟa paruvam poyyāta pāṇṭiyar kulakkoṭiyāyuḷḷāḷ;
koṭuṅkariyākiya alkuliyum vālukamākiya muliyiṉiyum itaḻākiya civanta vāyiṉiyum kayalākiya kaṇṇiṉiyum aṟalākiya kūntaliṉiyum mulliyākiya nakiyiṉiyumuṭiyāḷ pūṅkoṭi kulakkoṭi eṉka. oḻukkattup poruntiya pūṅkoṭi eṉka. purantu ūṭṭum eṉṟa eccaṅkaḷuḷ vikuti pirittuk kūṭṭuka. iru pattāṟu paripāṭṭuk
kaḷālum piṟavaṟṟāṉum ciṟappikkap peṟṟa miyiṉ pulavar nāviṟ poruntiya eṉṟum, celvamaḷittaliṉ pūṅ koṭi eṉṟum, eññāṉṟum pāṇṭiyarkkuriyatākaliṉ poy yāk kulakkoṭi eṉṟum kūṟiṉār.
171-173. 	tiyaṟku uṟuvatu tāṉ aṟintaṉaḷ pōl-kaṇṇa kikku mēlvarum tuṉpattiṉit tāṉ muṉṉarē aṟintāḷ pōla, puṇṇiya naṟumalar āṭi pōrttu - tūymiyuṭiya naṟiya pūkkaḷākiya āṭiyāl taṉ meymmuḻutum pōrttu, kaṇṇiṟi neṭunīr karantaṉaḷ aṭakki - taṉkaṇ niṟinta mikka nīriṉi maṟittu uḷḷaṭakka;
puṇṇiya naṟumalar - taṉṉikkoṇṭu aruccippārkkup puṇṇiyaṅkaḷik koṭukkum naṟiya pū eṉṟurippārumuḷar.
kaṇṇiṟi neṭunīr eṉpataṟkut taṉṉiṭattu niṟinta nīr eṉavum kaṇkaḷil niṟinta nīr eṉavum poruḷ koḷka. aṭakki - aṭakka veṉat tirikka. karantaṉaḷ aṭakkutal ivar nīr mikuti kaṇṭu veruvātiruttaṟporuṭṭeṉka.
174-175. 	puṉal yāṟu aṉṟu itu pūmpuṉal yāṟu eṉa - iv viyāṟu nīr āṟu aṉṟu pūvāṟu eṉṟu pukaḻntu, aṉa naṭi mātarum aiyaṉum toḻutu - aṉṉam pōṉṟa naṭiyiṉi yuṭiya kaṇṇakiyum kōvalaṉum vaṇaṅki;
176-180. 	parimuka ampiyum karimuka ampiyum arimuka ampi yum aruntuṟi iyakkum - ariya tuṟiyilē celuttum kutirimuka ōṭamum yāṉimuka ōṭamum ciṅkamuka ōṭamumākiya ivaṟṟiṉēṟi, peruntuṟi maruṅkiṉ peyarātu - palarum cellum periya tuṟippakkattē cellātu, āṅkaṇ māta vattāṭṭiyoṭu marappuṇi pōki - ataṟku ayaliluḷḷa ciṟiya tuṟiyil kavuntiyaṭikaḷuṭaṉē marappuṇiyil ceṉṟu, tē malar naṟumpoḻil teṉkari eyti - tēṉ poruntiya malarkaḷiyuṭiya nalla cōli niṟinta teṟkuk kariyiṉi aṭintu;
aruntuṟi - ōṭakkōl nilittalariya tuṟi. ōṭattil pala rum ēṟuvārākalāṉ tāṅkaḷ mūvarum taṉiyē kaṭṭumarap puṇiyil cērntaṉar eṉka; eṉṉi? muṉṉar, "vampap paratti vaṟumoḻi yāḷaṉoṭu" cāpa muṟutaliṉ. 
181-183. 	vāṉavar uṟiyum maturi valaṅ koḷa - tēvarkaḷ uṟiyum maturi nakarattiṉi valaṅ koṇṭāl, tāṉ naṉi peritum takavu uṭittu eṉṟu - mikavum aṟamuṇṭām eṉṟu karuti, āṅku arumiḷi uṭutta akaḻi cūḻpōki - av viṭattē, aḻittaṟkariya kāvaṟkāṭu cūḻnta akaḻiyic cuṟṟip pōy;
naṉi peritu, oruporuṭ paṉmoḻi, tāṉ, aci, miḷi - kaṭṭuvēliyumām. 
184-188. 	karuneṭuṅ kuvaḷiyum āmpalum kamalamum - kariya neṭiya kuvaḷiyum alliyum tāmariyum, tiyalum kaṇavaṉum taṉittu uṟutuyaram - kaṇṇakiyum avaḷ kaṇavaṉ kōvala ṉum pirintu aṭiyum tuṉpattiṉi, aiyam iṉṟi aṟintaṉa pōla - aiyappāṭu ciṟitum iṉṟi uṇarntaṉa pōla, paṇnīr vaṇṭu parintu iṉintu ēṅki - vaṇṭukaḷ pāṭum paṇṇīr miyāl varunti ēkkamuṟṟu aḻutu, kaṇṇīr koṇṭu kāl uṟa naṭuṅka - kaṇṇīrikkoṇṭu kāl porunta naṭuṅka;
kuvaḷi mutaliyavaṟṟil vaṇṭukaḷ olittal ivarkkuṟu tuyaraṅ kaṇṭu ak kuvaḷi mutaliyaṉa aḻutalpōṉṟiruntaṉa eṉka. kaṇṇīr - kaṇṇiṉnīr, kaḷḷākiya nīr eṉavum, kāl uṟa naṭuṅka - kālkaḷ mika naṭuṅka, kāṟṟāl mika aciya eṉavum iru poruḷ paṭumāṟaṟika. 
189-190.	pōr uḻantu eṭutta ār eyil neṭuṅkoṭi - pakivar varuntap pōr tēytteṭutta ariya matiliṉkaṇ neṭiya koṭikaḷ, vāral eṉpaṉapōl maṟittuk ki kāṭṭa- im maturikkaṇ vārātoḻivā yeṉpaṉa pōlat tam kiyiṉ maṟittuk kāṭṭa ;
āreyil neṭuṅkoṭi mēlkāṟṟaṭikka acikiṉṟavi kōvalaṉi iṅku vārātē pō eṉṟu ki kāṭṭiṉāṟ pōṉ ṟaṉa ; taṟkuṟippēṟṟam.
1 "	īṇṭunī variṉu meṅka ḷeḻiluṭi yeḻili vaṇṇaṉ
pāṇṭavar taṅkaṭ kallāṟ paṭittuṇi yāka māṭṭāṉ 
mīṇṭupō keṉṟeṉ ṟanta viyaṉmatiṟ kuṭumi tōṟum
kāṇṭaku patāki yāṭi kikaḷāṟ ṟaṭuppa pōṉṟa"
eṉṉuñ ceyyuḷ ik karuttip piṉpaṟṟiyatātal kāṇka.
191-196.	puḷaṇi kaḻaṉiyum poḻilum porunti - paṟavi kaḷ aḻaku ceyyum vayalkaḷum cōlikaḷum poruntap peṟṟu, vucḷanīrp paṇṇiyum virinīr ēriyum - mikka nīriṉiyuṭiya paṇṇikaḷum paranta nīriṉiyuṭiya ērikaḷum, kāyk kulit teṅkum vāḻiyum kamukum - kuliyākap porun tiya kāykaḷiyuṭiya teṅkum kamukum vāḻiyum, vēyttiraḷ pantarum - tiraṇṭa mūṅkilāl iṭappaṭṭa pantalum ākiya ivi, viḷaṅkiya irukki - viḷaṅkiya iruppiṉiyuṭiya, aṟampuri māntar aṉṟic cērā - aṟattiṉiyē virumpum muṉivarkaḷ aṉṟip piṟar cērāta, puṟañciṟi mūtūr pukkaṉar purinteṉ-mūtūriṉ puṟañcērikkaṇ virumpip pukkār eṉka.
paṇṇi - tōṭṭamum, ōṭiyum. kāykkuli eṉpataṉi vāḻi, kamuku eṉpavaṟṟoṭuṅ kūṭṭuka. irukkiyiṉiyuṭiya puṟañciṟi eṉka. aṟampuri māntar cērnta irukkiyākalāṉ ivar purintu pukkār eṉka. puṟañcēri - itu kīḻttici vāyiṟku ayalatōr muṉivar iruppiṭam eṉpar aṭiyārkku nallār.
toḻutu pōki eytic cūḻpōkip pukkaṉar eṉka. 'aṟam puri mānta raṉṟic cērāp, puṟañciṟi mūtūr' eṉṟa karuttup piṉ 2aṭikkalak kātiyuḷ varutaluṅ kāṇka.
itu nilimaṇṭilavāciriyappā
puṟañcēriyiṟutta kāti muṟṟiṟṟu.

14. ūrkāṇ kāti 
(poḻilum kaḻaṉiyum puṭkaḷ eḻuntolikka ñāyiṟu kīḻt ticit tōṉṟiyatu. iṟivaṉ kōyil mutaliyavaṟṟil valam puric caṅkum kāli muracum olittaṉa. kōvalaṉ kavunti yaṭikaḷi vaṇaṅkit tāṉ uṟṟa iṭumpiyi urittu, yāṉ in nakar vaṇikarkku eṉatu niliyi uṇartti varukāṟum, ip pintoṭi numatu pātak kāppiṉaḷ' eṉṟu kūṟiṉaṉ; kūṟak, kavunti yaṭikaḷ ulakilē makkaḷeytum iṉpa tuṉpaṅkaḷiṉ kāraṇaṅkaḷi eṭut turittu, muṉṉarum tuṉpamuṟṟōr palar eṉpataṟku irāmaṉi yum naḷaṉiyum eṭuttuk kāṭṭi, nī avarkaḷpōlvāyu malli; maṉiviyuṭaṉ piriyā vāḻkki peṟṟaṉi; ākaliṉ varuntātu ēkip poruntumiṭam aṟintu varuka' eṉṟaṉar; eṉṟalum, kōvalaṉ matilaka varippiṟ ceṉṟu, kaṭikaḻi makaḷir kātalañ celvaruṭaṉ kāliyiṟ puṉal viḷiyāṭiyum, naṇpakaliṟ poḻil viḷiyāṭṭayarntum, eṟpaṭupoḻutil nilā muṟṟattiṟ cēkki mītiruntum, muṉpu tamakkiṉpam viḷitta kār mutaliya paruvaṅ kaḷiṉ varavi eṇṇi iṉpuṟum mutuvēṉiṟ kaṭināḷil aracaṉpāṟ ciṟappup peṟṟa poṟṟoṭi maṭantiyaruṭaṉ putu maṇam puṇarntu ceḻuṅkuṭic celvarum viyaṅ kāvalarum makiḻā niṟkum vītiyum, eṇṇeṇ kaliyu muṇarnta parattiyariṉ iru peruvītiyum, aracaṉum virumpum celvattiyuṭiya aṅkāṭi vītiyum, payaṉ mikka irattiṉakkaṭi vītiyum, poṟkaṭi vīti yum, aṟuvi vītiyum, kūla vītiyum, nālvēṟu teruvum, canti yum, catukkamum, maṉṟamum, kavaliyum, maṟukum tirintu kāvalaṉ pērūrik kaṇṭu makiḻntu puṟañcērikkaṇ mīṇṭaṉaṉ. (itil irattiṉak kaṭitteru kūṟumiṭattē 180-200 aṭikaḷil nava maṇikaḷiṉ ilakkaṇam kūṟappeṟṟuḷḷatu.) 
		puṟañciṟip poḻilum piṟaṅkunīrp paṇṇiyum
iṟaṅkukatirk kaḻaṉiyum puḷḷeḻun tārppap
pulari vikaṟip poykit tāmari
malarpoti aviḻtta ulakutoḻu maṇṭilam
5		vēntutali paṉippa ēntuvāṭ ceḻiyaṉ
ōṅkuyar kūṭal ūrtuyi leṭuppa
nutalviḻi nāṭṭat tiṟiyōṉ kōyilum
uvaṇac cēva luyarttōṉ niyamamum
mēḻivala ṉuyartta veḷḷi nakaramum
10		kōḻic cēvaṟ koṭiyōṉ kōṭṭamum
aṟattuṟi viḷaṅkiya aṟavōr paḷḷiyum
maṟattuṟi viḷaṅkiya maṉṉavaṉ kōyilum
vālveṇ caṅkoṭu vakipeṟ ṟōṅkiya
kāli muracaṅ kaṉikural iyampak
15		kōvalaṉ ceṉṟu koḷkiyi ṉirunta
kāvunti aiyiyik kitoḻu tētti
neṟiyiṉ nīṅkiyōr nīrmiyē ṉāki
naṟumalar mēṉi naṭuṅkutuya reyta
aṟiyāt tēyat tāriṭi yuḻantu
20		ciṟumi yuṟṟēṉ ceytavat tīryāṉ
toṉṉakar maruṅkiṉ maṉṉar piṉṉōrkku
eṉṉili yuṇartti yāṉvaruṅ kāṟum
pātak kāppiṉaḷ pintoṭi yākaliṉ
ēta muṇṭō aṭikaḷīṅ keṉṟalum
25		kavunti kūṟuṅ kātali taṉṉoṭu
tavantīr maruṅkiṉ taṉittuya ruḻantōy
maṟattuṟi nīṅkumiṉ valviṉi yūṭṭumeṉ
ṟaṟattuṟi mākkaḷ tiṟattiṟ cāṟṟi
nākkaṭip pāka vāyppaṟi yaṟiyiṉum
30		yāppaṟi mākkaḷ iyalpiṟ koḷḷār
tītuṭi vevviṉi yurutta kālip
pētimi kantāp perumpē tuṟuvar
oyyā viṉippayaṉ uṇṇuṅ kālik
kiyāṟu koḷḷār kaṟṟaṟi mākkaḷ
35		pirital tuṉpamum puṇartal tuṉpamum
uruvi lāḷaṉ oṟukkun tuṉpamum
purikuḻal mātarp puṇarntōrk kallatu
orutaṉi vāḻkki uravōrk killi
peṇṭirum uṇṭiyum iṉpameṉ ṟulakiṟ
40		koṇṭō ruṟūuṅ koḷḷāt tuṉpam
kaṇṭaṉa rākik kaṭavuḷar varinta
kāmañ cārpāk kātaliṉ uḻantāṅku
ēmañ cārā iṭumpi eytiṉar
iṉṟē yallāl iṟantōr palarāl
45		toṉṟu paṭavarūun toṉmit tātaliṉ
tāti ēvaliṉ mātuṭaṉ pōkik
kātali nīṅkak kaṭuntuya ruḻantōṉ
vēta mutalvaṟ payantō ṉeṉpatu
nīyaṟin tiliyō neṭumoḻi yaṉṟō
50		vallā ṭāyattu maṇṇara ciḻantu
melliyal taṉṉuṭaṉ veṅkā ṉaṭintōṉ
kātaliṟ pirintō ṉallaṉ kātali
tītoṭu paṭūuñ ciṟumiya ḷallaḷ
aṭavik kāṉakat tāyiḻi taṉṉi
55		iṭiyiruḷ yāmat tiṭṭu nīkkiyatu
valviṉi yaṉṟō maṭantitaṉ piḻiyeṉac
collalum uṇṭēṟ collā yōnī
aṉiyiyum alli āyiḻi taṉṉoṭu
piriyā vāḻkki peṟṟaṉi yaṉṟē
60		varuntā tēki maṉṉavaṉ kūṭal
poruntuḻi yaṟintu pōtīṅ keṉṟalum
iḷicūḻ miḷiyoṭu vaḷivuṭaṉ kiṭanta
ilaṅkunīrp parappiṉ valampuṇa rakaḻiyil
peruṅki yāṉi iṉaniri peyarum
65		curuṅki vīti maruṅkiṟ pōkik
kaṭimatil vāyil kāvaliṟ ciṟanta
aṭalvāḷ yavaṉark kayirātu pukkāṅku
āyiraṅ kaṇṇōṉ aruṅkalac ceppu
vāytiṟan taṉṉa matilaka varippil
70		kuṭakāṟ ṟeṟintu koṭinuṭaṅku maṟukiṉ
kaṭikaḻi makaḷir kātalañ celvaroṭu
varupuṉal viyi marutōṅku muṉṟuṟi
viripūn turutti veṇmaṇa laṭikari
ōṅkunīr māṭamoṭu nāvā yiyakkip
75		pūmpuṇi taḻīip puṉalāṭ ṭamarntu
taṇṇaṟu mulliyun tāḻnīrk kuvaḷiyum
kaṇṇaviḻ neytaluṅ katuppuṟa aṭicci
veṇpū malliki viriyaloṭu toṭarnta
taṇceṅ kaḻunīr tātuviri piṇiyal
80		koṟkiyam peruntuṟi muttoṭu pūṇṭu
tekkaṇa malayac ceḻuñcē ṟāṭip
poṟkoṭi mūtūrp poḻilāṭ ṭamarntāṅku
eṟpaṭu poḻutiṉ iḷanila muṉṟil
tāḻtaru kōlan takipā raṭṭa
85		vīḻpūñ cēkki mēliṉi tiruntāṅku
arattap pūmpaṭ ṭarimici yuṭīik
kuraṟṟalik kūntaṟ kuṭacam poruntic
ciṟumalic cilampiṟ ceṅkū tāḷamoṭu
naṟumalark kuṟiñci nāṇmalar vēyntu
90		kuṅkuma varuṇaṅ koṅkiyi ṉiḻittuc
ceṅkoṭu vēric ceḻumpūm piṇiyal
cinturac cuṇṇañ cērnta mēṉiyil
antukirk kōvi aṇiyoṭu pūṇṭu
malicciṟa karinta vaccira vēntaṟkuk
95		kalikeḻu kūṭaṟ cevvaṇi kāṭṭak
kārara cāḷaṉ vāṭiyoṭu varūum
kāla maṉṟiyum nūlōr ciṟappiṉ
mukiltōy māṭat takiltaru viṟakiṉ
maṭavaral makaḷir taṭavunerup pamarntu
100		naṟuñcān takalattu nampiyar tammoṭu
kuṟuṅkaṇ aṭikkum kūtirk kāliyum
vaḷamaṉi makaḷirum mintarum virumpi
iḷanilā muṉṟiliṉ iḷaveyil nukara
virikatir maṇṭilan teṟkērpu veṇmaḻi
105		aritil tōṉṟum accirak kāliyum
āṅka taṉṟiyum ōṅkirum parappiṉ
vaṅka īṭṭattut toṇṭiyō riṭṭa
akilun tukilum āramum vācamum
tokukarup pūramuñ cumantuṭaṉ vanta
110		koṇṭaloṭu pukuntu kōmakaṉ kūṭal
veṅkaṇ neṭuvēḷ vilviḻāk kāṇum
paṅkuṉi muyakkattup paṉiyara ciyāṇṭuḷaṉ
kōti mātavi koḻuṅkoṭi yeṭuppak
kāvum kāṉamum kaṭimala rēntat
115		teṉṉavaṉ potiyil teṉṟaloṭu pukuntu
maṉṉavaṉ kūṭal makiḻtuṇi taḻūum
iṉṉiḷa vēṉil yāṇṭuḷaṉ kolleṉṟu
uruvak koṭiyō ruṭipperuṅ koḻunaroṭu
paruva meṇṇum paṭartīr kālik
120		kaṉṟama rāyamoṭu kaḷiṟṟiṉam naṭuṅka
eṉṟūḻ niṉṟa kuṉṟukeḻu naṉṉāṭṭuk
kāṭutīp piṟappak kaṉiyeri pottik
kōṭiyoṭu pukuntu kūṭa lāṇṭa
vēṉil vēntaṉ vēṟṟuppulam paṭara
125		ōcaṉik kiṉṟa uṟuveyiṟ kaṭināḷ
viyamuñ civikiyum maṇikkāl amaḷiyum
uyyā ṉattiṉ uṟutuṇi makiḻcciyum
cāmarik kavariyun tamaṉiya aṭippiyum
kūrnuṉi vāḷuṅ kōmakaṉ koṭuppap
130		peṟṟa celvam piṟaḻā vāḻkkip
poṟṟoṭi maṭantiyar putumaṇam puṇarntu
cempoṉ vaḷḷattuc cilatiya rēntiya
antīn tēṟal māntiṉar mayaṅkip
poṟivari vaṇṭiṉam pulluvaḻi aṉṟiyum
135		naṟumalar māliyiṉ vaṟitiṭaṅ kaṭintāṅku
ilavitaḻc cevvāy iḷamut tarumpap
pulavik kālattup pōṟṟā turitta
kāviyaṅ kaṇṇār kaṭṭuri yeṭṭukkum
nāvoṭu navilā nakipaṭu kiḷaviyum
140		añceṅ kaḻunīr arumpaviḻt taṉṉa
ceṅkayal neṭuṅkaṭ ceḻuṅkaṭip pūcalum
koliviṟ puruvattuk koḻuṅkaṭi curuḷat
tilakac ciṟunutal arumpiya viyarum
cevvi pārkkuñ ceḻuṅkuṭic celvaroṭu
145		viyaṅ kāvalar makiḻtarum vītiyum
cuṭumaṇ ēṟā vaṭunīṅku ciṟappiṉ
muṭiyara coṭuṅkuṅ kaṭimaṉi vāḻkki
vēttiyal potuviyal eṉaviru tiṟattu
māttiri yaṟintu mayaṅkā marapiṉ
150		āṭalum variyum pāṇiyun tūkkum
kūṭiya kuyivak karuviyum uṇarntu
nālvaki marapiṉ aviṉayak kaḷattilum
ēḻvaki nilattiṉum eytiya virikkum
malipparuñ ciṟappiṉ talikkō lariviyum
155		vāram pāṭun tōriya maṭantiyum
talippāṭṭuk kūttiyum iṭippāṭṭuk kūttiyum
nālvēṟu vakiyiṉ nayattaku marapiṉ
eṭṭuk kaṭiniṟutta āyirat teṇkaḻañcu
muṭṭā vikal muṟimiyiṉ vaḻāat
160		tākkaṇaṅ kaṉiyār nōkkuvalip paṭṭāṅku
arumpeṟal aṟivum perumpiṟi tākat
tavattō rāyiṉun takimalar vaṇṭiṉ
nakippatam pārkkum iḷiyō rāyiṉum
kāma viruntiṉ maṭavō rāyiṉum
165		ēma vikal iṉṟuyil vatiyum
paṇṇuṅ kiḷiyum paḻitta tīñcol
eṇṇeṇ kaliyōr iruperu vītiyum
viyamum pāṇṭilum maṇittērk koṭuñciyum
meypuku kavacamum vīḻmaṇit tōṭṭiyum
170		ataḷpuṉi araṇamum ariyā yōkamum
vaḷitaru kuḻiyamum vālveṇ kavariyum
ēṉap paṭamum kiṭukiṉ paṭamum
kāṉap paṭamum kāḻūṉṟu kaṭikiyum
cempiṟ ceynavum kañcat toḻilavum
175		vampiṉ muṭinavum māliyiṟ puṉinavum
vētiṉat tuppavum kōṭukaṭi toḻilavum
pukiyavum cāntavum pūviṟ puṉinavum
vakiteri variyā vaḷantali mayaṅkiya
aracuviḻi tiruviṉ aṅkāṭi vītiyum
180		kāka pātamum kaḷaṅkamum vintuvum
ēkiyum nīṅki iyalpiṟ kuṉṟā
nūlavar noṭinta nuḻinuṇ kōṭi
nālvaki varuṇattu nalaṅkēḻ oḷiyavum
ēkiyum māliyum iruḷoṭu tuṟanta
185		pācār mēṉip pacuṅkatir oḷiyavum
patumamum nīlamum vintamum paṭitamum
vitimuṟi piḻiyā viḷaṅkiya cātiyum
pūca uruviṉ polanteḷit taṉiyavum
tītaṟu katiroḷit teṇmaṭ ṭuruvavum
190		iruḷteḷit taṉiyavum iruvē ṟuruvavum
orumit tōṟṟat tivēṟu vaṉappiṉ
ilaṅkukatir viṭūum nalaṅkeḻu maṇikaḷum
kāṟṟiṉum maṇṇiṉum kalliṉum nīriṉum
tōṟṟiya kuṟṟan tukaḷaṟat tuṇintavum
195		cantira kuruvē aṅkā rakaṉeṉa
vanta nīrmiya vaṭṭat tokutiyum
karuppat tuḷiyavum kalliṭi muṭaṅkalum
tirukku nīṅkiya ceṅkoṭi valliyum
vakiteri mākkaḷ tokipeṟ ṟōṅkip
200		pakiteṟal aṟiyāp payaṅkeḻu vītiyum
cāta rūpam kiḷicciṟi āṭakam
cāmpū natameṉa ōṅkiya koḷkiyiṉ
polanteri mākkaḷ kalaṅkaña roḻittāṅku
ilaṅkukoṭi yeṭukkum nalaṅkiḷar vītiyum
205		nūliṉum mayiriṉum nuḻinūṟ paṭṭiṉum
pālvaki teriyāp paṉṉū ṟaṭukkattu
naṟumaṭi ceṟinta aṟuvi vītiyum
niṟikkōl tulāttar paṟikkaṭ parāriyar
ampaṇa vaḷaviyar eṅkaṇun tiritarak
210		kālamaṉ ṟiyuṅ karuṅkaṟi mūṭiyoṭu
kūlaṅ kuvitta kūla vītiyum
pālvēṟu terinta nālvēṟu teruvum
antiyuñ catukkamum āvaṇa vītiyum
maṉṟamum kavaliyum maṟukum tirintu
215		vicumpakaṭu tirukiya veṅkatir nuḻiyāp
pacuṅkoṭip paṭākip pantar nīḻal
kāvalaṉ pērūr kaṇṭumakiḻ veytik
kōvalaṉ peyarntaṉaṉ koṭimatiṟ puṟatteṉ.
uri
1-2.		puṟañciṟip poḻilum - aṅṅaṉam pukka puṟañcēriyiṭattuḷḷa cōliyiṉ kaṇṇum, piṟaṅku nīrp paṇṇiyum - viḷaṅkukiṉṟa nīr poruntiya paṇṇi kaḷiṭattum, iṟaṅku katirk kaḻaṉiyum - vaḷinta katirkaḷiyuṭiya vayalkaḷilum, puḷ eḻuntu ārppa - paṟavikaḷ tuyileḻuntu oli ceyya ; paṇṇi - ōṭiyum tōṭṭamum. iṟaṅkutal - vaḷital ; tāḻtal eṉalum poruntum. 
piṟaṅkal - mikutalumām. 1"puḷḷaṇi kaḻaṉiyum .....veḷḷanīrp paṇṇiyum" eṉṟār muṉṉum. 
3-6. 		pulari vikaṟi - pularkiṉṟatākiya vikaṟip poḻutiṉ
kaṇ, poykit tāmari malar poti aviḻtta ulaku toḻu maṇṭilam - kuḷaṅkaḷiluḷḷa tāmarip pūkkaḷiṉ kaṭṭaviḻtta ulakiṉuḷḷār yāvarum vaṇaṅkum ñāyiṟu, vēntu tali paṉippa - pakiyaracarkaḷ tali naṭuṅkum vaṇṇam, ēntu vāṭ ceḻiyaṉ - vāḷiṉi ēntiya pāṇṭiyaṉatu, ōṅku uyar kūṭal ūr tuyil eṭuppa - vīrattāṉ mēmpaṭṭa vaṇmiyāṉ uyarnta maturi nakarattiṉit tuyiliṉiṉṟum eḻuppa;
potil - kuvital. ōṅku uyar - oru poruṭ paṉmoḻiyumām. kūṭal ūr - kūṭalākiya ūr ; āku peyar. eṭuppa - eḻuppa. 2'ūr tuyil eṭuppa' eṉṟār piṟarum. ārppa aviḻtta maṇṭilam ūr tuyil eṭuppa veṉka. 
7. 		nutalviḻi nāṭṭattu iṟiyōṉ kōyilum - neṟṟiyiṉiṭattu viḻitta kaṇṇiṉiyuṭiya iṟivaṉatu kōyilum;
viḻittal - īṇṭut tōṉṟutal. 3'nutalviḻi nāṭṭat tiṟiyōṉ' eṉap piṟarum kūṟuvar. viḻināṭṭam - viṉittoki. nutalil imiyā nāṭṭam eṉṟumām. 
8. 		uvaṇac cēval uyarttōṉ niyamamum - karuṭac cēvalik koṭiyāka uyartta tirumāliṉ kōyilum ; 
9. 		mēḻi valaṉ uyartta veḷḷi nakaramum - veṟṟi tarum kalappip paṭiyiṉi ēntiya palarāmaṉuṭiya kōyilum;
mēḻi - ākupeyarāṉ kalappiyi uṇarttiṟṟu. valaṉ mēḻi eṉka. veḷḷi - veṇṇiṟamuṭiya palarāmaṉ. valaṉ uyartta - valamāka ēntiya eṉalum ām.  
10. 		kōḻic cēvaṟ koṭiyōṉ kōṭṭamum - kōḻiyiṉ cēvalākiya koṭiyiyuṭiya murukaṉatu kōyilum; 
11. 		aṟattuṟi viḷaṅkiya aṟavōr paḷḷiyum - aṟaneṟik kaṇṇē niṉṟu viḷakkamuṟṟa muṉivarkaḷuṭiya paḷḷikaḷum;
iṉi, ivvaṭikku aṭiyārkku nallār viritturikkum poruḷ varumāṟu:- "aṟattuṟi viḷaṅkiya aṟavōr paḷḷiyum - aṟamum ataṉṟuṟikaḷum viḷaṅkuvataṟkuk kāraṇamākiya aṟavōrkaḷuṭiya iruppiṭaṅkaḷum ; 
īṇṭup paḷḷi eṉṟatu, avviṭaṅkaḷi. aṟattuṟi - aṟamum aṟattiṉ tuṟiyumeṉa ummit toki. aṟamāvatu iruvakittu; illaṟamum tuṟavaṟamum eṉa. avaṟṟuḷ illaṟa
meṉpatu kaṟpuṭi maṉiviyōṭu illiṉkaṇiruntu ceyyum aṟam. ataṉ ṟuṟiyāvaṉa: taṉṉi yoḻinta mūvarkkum, tuṟantārkkum, tuvvātavarkkum, iṟantārkkum, tēvarkkum, muṉivarkkum, viruntiṉarkkum, cuṟṟattārkkum, piṟarkkum tuṇiyātalum, vēḷvi ceytalum, cīlaṅkāttal mutaliyaṉavum, aruḷum aṉpum uṭiyaṉātalum, piṟavum.
iṉit tuṟavaṟamāvatu. nākam tōlurittāṟ pōla akappaṟṟum puṟappaṟṟum aṟṟu intiriya vacamaṟuttu muṟṟat tuṟattal.
ataṉ tuṟiyāvaṉa:- cariyi, kiriyi, yōkam, ñāṉam eṉpaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ, cariyi alakiṭal mutaliyaṉa ; kiriyi pūci mutaliyaṉa;
yōkam eṇvakiya; avi:- iyamam, niyamam, ācaṉam, vaḷinili, tokinili, poṟinili, niṉital, camāti, eṉpaṉa;
avaṟṟuḷ, "poykoli kaḷavē kāmam poruṇaci, ivvaki yintu maṭakkiya tiyamam" eṉavum, "peṟṟataṟ kuvattal piḻampunaṉi veṟuttal, kaṟpaṉa kaṟṟal kaḻikaṭun tūymi, pūcaṉip perumpaya mācāṟ kaḷittaloṭu, payaṉuṭi marapiṉi ṉiyamamintē" eṉavum, "niṟṟa liruttal kiṭattal naṭattaleṉ, ṟotta nāṉki ṉolkā nilimiyo, ṭiṉpam payakkuñ camaya mutaliya, vantamil ciṟappi ṉācaṉa mākum" eṉavum, "untiyoṭu puṇarnta viruvaki vaḷiyun, tanta miyakkan taṭuppatu vaḷinili" eṉavum, "poṟiyuṇar vellām pulattiṉ vaḻāmi, orunilippaṭuppatu tokinili yāmē" eṉavum, "maṉattiṉi yoruvaḻi niṟuppatu poṟinili" eṉavum, "niṟuttiya ammaṉa niliteriyāmaṟ, kuṟitta poruḷoṭu koḷuttuvatu niṉivē" eṉavum, "āṅṅaṉaṅ kuṟitta ammutaṟ poruḷoṭu, tāṉpiṟa ṉākāt takiyatu camāti" eṉavum varuvaṉavaṟṟāṉaṟika." 
12. 		maṟattuṟi viḷaṅkiya maṉṉavaṉ kōyilum - valimi aminta pōrttuṟikkaṇṇē cēṟalāṉ viḷakkamuṟṟa aracaṉatu kōyilum ākiya ivviṭaṅkaḷil;
iṉi, maṟattuṟi viḷaṅkutaṟkuk kāraṇamākiya maṉṉavaṉ eṉalum poruntum. maṟattuṟiyāvaṉa veṭci mutaliya tiṇikaḷum avaṟṟiṉ tuṟikaḷumām.
oru poruṭkuk kōyil, niyamam, nakaram, kōṭṭam, paḷḷi eṉap pala peyar vantatu ōr aṇiyākum. itaṉip pariyāyam eṉpar.
13-14. 	vāl veṇ caṅkōṭu - tūya veḷḷiya caṅkiṉōṭum, vakipeṟṟu ōṅkiya kāli muracam kaṉi kural iyampa-mūṉṟu vakippaṭṭu uyarnta ceṟinta kuraliṉiyuṭiya kāli muracam olippa;
vāl - tūymi. muracavakiyāvaṉa : koṭi muracu, veṟṟi muracu, niyāya muracu eṉpaṉavām. 
15-16. 	kōvalaṉ ceṉṟu koḷkiyiṉ irunta kāvunti aiyiyik ki toḻutu ētti - kōvalaṉ ceṉṟu tava voḻukkattil irunta kavuntiyaṭikaḷik kiyāl toḻutu nāvāṟ pōṟṟi;
koḷki - tavam ; āvatu tiyāṉam. ceṉṟu eṉpataṉāṟ kavuntiyaṭikaḷ vēṟiṭattiruntār eṉpatum koḷkiyiṉ irunta eṉpataṉāl avar tavaniliyil iruntār eṉpatum peṟappaṭum. iṉi, koḷkiyiṉ irunta eṉpataṟku ivarkaḷaṭiyum tuṉpam aṭiyātirunta eṉalum poruntum.
17-20. 	neṟiyiṉ nīṅkiyōr nīrmiyēṉ āki - nalloḻukka neṟiyiṉiṉṟum piṟaḻntōratu taṉmiyi uṭiyēṉāy, naṟumalar mēṉi naṭuṅku tuyar eyta - naṟiya malarpōlum mēṉiyiyuṭiya ivaḷ naṭuṅkutaṟkuk kāraṇamāya tuyarattiṉi aṭiya, aṟiyāt tēyattu ār iṭi uḻantu - muṉṉam uṇarāta nāṭṭiṉkaṇ ariya vaḻiyiṭattu alintu varunti, ciṟumi uṟṟēṉ ceytavattīr yāṉ - ceyta tavat tiṉiyuṭiyīr yāṉ iḻivuṟṟēṉ;
illaṟa neṟiyiṉīṅkik kaṇikiyar vāḻkkiyoṭu porun tiṉamiyāṉum, viḻukkuṭip piṟantum taṉ kaṟpuṭi maṉiviyōṭu nīṇeṟic ceṉṟamiyāṉum "neṟiyi ṉīṅkiyōr nīrmiyē ṉāki............ciṟumi yuṟṟēṉ" eṉṟāṉ eṉka. naṟumalar mēṉi - ākupeyar. ceytavattīr yāṉ nīrmiyēṉāki āriṭi yuḻantu ciṟumiyuṟṟēṉ eṉa māṟuka. 
21-24. 	tol nakar maruṅkiṉ maṉṉar piṉṉōrkku - paḻamiyāṉa in nakarattiṭattuḷḷa vaṇika mākkaḷukku, eṉ nili uṇartti yāṉ varuṅkāṟum - eṉatu nilimiyi aṟivittu yāṉ varuntuṇiyum, pātak kāppiṉaḷ pintoṭiyākaliṉ ētam uṇṭō aṭikaḷ īṅku eṉṟalum - aṭikāḷ ip paciya toṭiyiṉiyuṭiyāḷ num tiruvaṭikaḷākiya kāvali yuṭiyāḷākalāṉ ivviṭattu ivaḷukku ōr tīṅku uṇṭāmō eṉṟu kūṟalum ;
maṉṉar piṉṉōr - nāṟpāluḷḷē maṉṉarkkup piṉṉāka vuḷḷōr; vaṇikar. eṉṉili eṉṟatu muṉpu tāṉirunta nilimiyum ippōtu aṭinta nilimiyum eṉka. pātakkāppu - pātamākiya kāppu. pintoṭi - aṉmoḻittoki. muṉṉar, 1"aṭika ṇīrē yaruḷiti rāyiṉit, toṭivaḷit tōḷi tuyartīrttēṉ" eṉa ivaṉ kūṟiyatu periyōric cārntōrkku yātum tīṅku nērāteṉpataṉi uṭkoṇṭatākalāṉ, īṇṭum akkoḷkiyi uṭkoṇṭē ētam uṇṭō' eṉṟāṉ eṉka. 
25-26. 	kavunti kūṟum : kavuntiyaṭikaḷ kūṟuvār : kātali taṉṉoṭu tavan tīr maruṅkiṉ taṉittuyar uḻantōy - kātali yuṭiya maṉiviyōṭu aṟaviṉi oḻinta pakkattāṉē taṉittut tuyariṉi uḻantōy ;
tīrtal - oḻital. 1"tavantīr maruṅkiṟ ṟaṉittuya ruḻantēṉ" eṉṟār piṟarum. tavantīr maruṅkil eṉṟatu muṉpu nalviṉi ceyta nī ciṟitu tīviṉiyum ceytamiyāṉē eṉṟapaṭi. taṉit tuyar - oppaṟṟa tuyarumām. uḻattal - nukartal. 
27-32. 	maṟattuṟi nīṅkumiṉ - pāva neṟiyiṉiṉṟum vilaku miṉ, val viṉi ūṭṭum eṉṟu - nīṅkīrāyiṉ ap pāvacceyal taṉ payaṉākiya tuṉpattiṉi nukarvikkum eṉa, aṟattuṟi mākkaḷ - aṟattuṟiyil niṉṟa aṟavōr, tiṟattiṟ cāṟṟi nāk kaṭippu āka vāyppaṟi aṟiyiṉum - avaravarkku ēṟṟa vakiyāṉ nāvākiya kuṟuntaṭiyāṉ vāyākiya paṟiyi aṟintu kūṟiṉum, yāppu aṟi mākkaḷ iyalpiṟ koḷḷār - aṟiveṉṉum uṟutiyaṟṟa mākkaḷ ataṉi naṉmit taṉmiyiṉ ēṟṟukkoḷḷārāki, tītu uṭi vevviṉi urutta kāli - piṉṉart tīppayaṉiyuṭiya koṭiya viṉi yāṉatu tōṉṟit taṉ tuṉpap payaṉi ūṭṭuṅkālattu, pētimi kantuāp perum pētuṟuvar - tam aṟiyāmi kāraṇamāka mikavum mayakkamuṟuvar ;
tiṟam - naṉmi tīmi eṉṉum vaki eṉiṉum poruntum. cāṟṟi aṟiyiṉum eṉpavaṟṟuḷ vikuti pirittuk kūṭṭi aṟintu cāṟṟiṉum eṉa māṟuka. uruttal - tōṉṟip payaṉaḷittal; 2"ummi viṉivan turutta loḻiyātu" eṉa varutal kāṇka. 
33-34. 	oyyā viṉippayaṉ uṇṇuṅkāli - pōkkavoṇṇāta tīviṉiyiṉ payaṉākiya tuṉpattiṉi nukaruṅkālattu, ki yāṟu koḷḷār kaṟṟu aṟi mākkaḷ - aṟanūlkaḷik kaṟṟuṇarnta aṟiñar ceyalaṟaviṉik koḷḷār;
oyyāmi - pōkkavoṇṇāmi. tāmē muṉ ceytu koṇṭamiyāṉum ūṭṭātu kaḻiyāmiyāṉum iyintu nukartaṟpāla teṉak koḷḷuvareṉṟatām. iṉi, muṉṉar ‘valviṉi yūṭṭum' eṉak kūṟiyataṉi mēṟkōḷ kāṭṭi valiyuṟuttukiṉṟār.  
35-45.	pirital tuṉpamum - makaḷirip piritalāṉ varum tuṉpamum, puṇartal tuṉpamum - avarip puṇartal kāraṇamāka uṇṭām tuṉpamum, uruvilāḷaṉ oṟukkum tuṉpamum- iv virukālattum kāmaṉ oṟukkum tuṉpamum, purikuḻal mātarp puṇarntōrkku allatu - purinta kūntaliyuṭiya peṇṭirip puṇarntu mayaṅkiṉārkkallatu, oru taṉi vāḻkki uravōrkku illi - oppaṟṟa taṉitta vāḻkki yiṉiyuṭiya aṟiñariṭattu illi ; peṇṭirum uṇṭiyum iṉpam eṉṟu ulakiṟ koṇṭōr uṟūum koḷḷāt tuṉpam - ivvulakattu makaḷirum uṇavumē iṉpam tarat takka poruḷām eṉṟu koṇṭa aṟivilār aṭiyum iṭaṅ
koḷḷāt tuṉpattiṉi, kaṇṭaṉar ākik kaṭavuḷar varinta - uṇarntaṉarāy muṉivarkaḷ nīkkiya, kāmam cārpu ā - kāmam paṟṟukkōṭāka, kātaliṉ uḻantu - aṉpu koṇṭu varunti, āṅku ēmañ cārā iṭumpi eytiṉar - kari kāṇappaṭāta tuṉpattiṉi uṟṟōr, iṉṟē allāl iṟantōr palarāl - in nikaḻkālattuḷḷō rallāmalum muṟkālattuk kaḻintōrum palarāvar, toṉṟupaṭa varūum toṉmittu - in nikaḻcci aṭippaṟṟi varukiṉṟa paḻimiyiṉi uṭittu ;
puṇartaṟ ṟuṉpattiṉum piritaṟ ṟuṉpam peritu ākalāṉ piritaṟ ṟuṉpam muṟkūṟappaṭṭatu. uruvilāḷaṉ - anaṅkaṉ. ura vōr - muṉivar. 1"peṇṭiru muṇṭiyu miṉṟeṉiṉ mākkaṭ, kuṇṭō ñālat tuṟupayaṉ" eṉa varutalāṉ, peṇṭirum uṇṭi yumē iṉpameṉak koṇṭāru muḷareṉpataṟika. iṉpam taruvaṉa vaṟṟi iṉpameṉṟār. koḷḷāt tuṉpam - tammaḷavallāta tuṉpameṉalumām.
iṉi, aṭiyārkku nallār, peṇṭirum uṇṭiyum eṉpavaṟṟi ākupeyarāṉ iṉpamum poruḷumeṉakkoṇṭu ataṟkēṟpap pirital puṇartal eṉpavaṟṟi iraṇṭukkum ēṟṟip poruḷ kūṟic celvar ; avar uruvilāḷaṉ eṉpataṟkup poruḷuk
kēṟpa `vaṟumi' eṉṟurippar. ēmam - kāval ; kari. āl, aci.  
45-49.	ātaliṉ - ākiyāṉ, tāti ēvaliṉ - tantiyiṉ ēvalāl, mātuṭaṉ pōki - taṉ maṉiviyōṭum kāṉam ceṉṟu, kātali nīṅkak kaṭuntuyar uḻantōṉ - am maṉivi piritalāṉē mikka tuyarattiṉi nukarntōṉ, vēta mutalvaṟ payantōṉ eṉpatu - vētatti aruḷiya nāṉmukaṉip peṟṟa tirumāl eṉpataṉi, nī aṟintiliyō - nī aṟiyāyō, neṭu moḻi aṉṟō - atu yāvarum aṟiyum vaṇṇam parantu paṭṭa collaṉṟō ;
tāti - tayarataṉ. `nī aṟintiliyō' eṉṟatu aṟintu vittum iraṅkutal aṟivuṭimiyākātu eṉpatām. 

50-57. 	val āṭu āyattu - puṭkaraṉōṭu cūtāṭum tāyattāṉ, maṇ aracu iḻantu - nilattiṉiyum aracāṭciyiyum iḻantu, mel iyal taṉṉuṭaṉ veṅkāṉ aṭintōṉ - meṉmit taṉmiyuṭiya tamayantiyuṭaṉ vevviya kāṭṭi aṭintōṉākiya naḷaṉ, kātaliṟ pirintōṉ allaṉ - poruḷmītu koṇṭa aṉpu kāraṇamāka avaḷip pirintō ṉumallaṉ, kātali tītoṭu paṭūum ciṟumiyaḷ allaḷ - avaḷ kuṟṟattiṉkaṇ paṭukiṉṟa iḻiviṉi uṟuvāḷumallaḷ, aṭavik kāṉakattu āyiḻi taṉṉi - aṅṅaṉamākavum avaḷi aṭaviyākiya kāṭṭiṉiṭattu, iṭiyiruḷ yāmattu iṭṭu nīkkiyatu - iruḷ niṟinta naṭu yāmattiṉkaṇ uṟakkiṭip pōkaṭṭu nīṅkac ceytatu, valviṉi aṉṟō - avar muṉ ceyta tīviṉi aṉṟō, maṭantitaṉ piḻi eṉac collalum uṇṭēl collāyō nī - at tamayantiyiṉ piḻiyē kāraṇamām eṉṟu collutaṟku vēṟu uṇṭāyiṉ nī ataṉic colvāy;
āyam - tāyam. maṇṇum aracum eṉka. aṭintōṉ - viṉippeyar. kātaliṟ pirintō ṉallaṉ eṉpataṟkut taṉ viruppam kāraṇamākavum avaḷ viruppam kāraṇamākavum pirintō ṉallaṉ eṉṟum, kātaliṉiṉṟum, nīṅkiṉō ṉallaṉ eṉṟum urittalumām. tītu - piṟar neñcu pukutal.
tēvaruḷ mikkā ṉoruvaṉiyum, makkaḷuḷ mikkā ṉoruvaṉiyum muṟiyē eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyavāṟu kāṇka.  
58-59. 	aṉiyiyum alli - nī avarkaḷip pōṉṟāyumalli, āyiḻi taṉṉoṭu piriyā vāḻkki peṟṟaṉi aṉṟē - eṉṉiyeṉiṉ? nī niṉ maṉiviyiṉiṉṟum piriyāta vāḻkkiyiṉip peṟṟāyaṉṟē;
ummi - ciṟappu. tīviṉi kāraṇamākat tuyaruḻattaliṉ muṟkūṟiya iruvarōṭu nī oppāy eṉiṉum, nī avariṉum oru naṉmiyi uṭiyi ; aḵteṉṉi yeṉiṉ? maṉiviyip piriyātu vāḻtalām eṉṟu kūṟiṉāreṉka.  
60-61. 	varuntātu ēki maṉṉavaṉ kūṭal - im maṉṉavaṉatu kūṭaṟkaṇṇē aṟiyāt tēyattu āriṭi yuḻantataṉi niṉintu varuntātu ceṉṟu, poruntuḻi aṟintu pōtu īṅku eṉṟalum - viruntetir koḷvāriṭatti aṟintukoṇṭu īṅkup pōtuvāyāka eṉṟu collalum ;
muṉṉar, 1"maṉṉar piṉṉōrk keṉṉili yuṇartti yāṉ varuṅ kāṟum" eṉavum, piṉṉar. 2"taṅkula vāṇar.......kaṭi maṉip paṭuttuvar" eṉavum varuvaṉavaṟṟi uṭkoṇṭatu poruntuḻi yaṟinteṉṟatu eṉka. tīṅku poruntuḻi aṟintu pō eṉavum poruḷ koḷḷak kiṭantami kāṇka. 
62-65. 	iḷi cūḻ miḷiyoṭu - kaṭṭuvēli cūḻnta kāvaṟ kāṭṭoṭu porunti, vaḷivuṭaṉ kiṭanta ilaṅku nīrp parappiṉ valam puṇar akaḻiyil - vaḷivu paṭṭuk kiṭanta viḷaṅkukiṉṟa nīrp parappiṉiyuṭiya veṟṟi poruntiya akaḻikkaṇ, peruṅki yāṉi iṉaniri peyarum - periya kiyiṉi yuṭiya yāṉiyiṉ niritta iṉaṅkaḷ pōkku varavu ceytaṟku aminta, curuṅki vīti maruṅkiṟ pōki - curuṅkiyi uṭiya vītiyiṉiṭattik kaḻintu pōy;
iḷi - kāval araṇumām. curuṅki - pukuntu cellutali oruvaru maṟiyātapaṭi nilattiṉkīḻ maṟittup paṭutta vaḻi; itaṉik karantuṟi eṉavum, karantupaṭi eṉavum kūṟuvar.  
66-67. 	kaṭi matil vāyil kāvaliṟ ciṟanta - accatti viḷikkum matiliṉ vāyilik kāttaṟṟoḻilāṟ ciṟappuppeṟṟa, aṭal vāḷ yavaṉarkku ayirātu pukku - kollutaliyuṭiya vāḷiṉi ēntiya yavaṉarkku aiyamuṇṭākā vaṇṇam pukuntu;
kaṭi - accam; mikutiyumām. yavaṉar - tulukkar eṉpar aṭiyārkku nallār. ayirātu - putiyareṉa aiyuṟātapaṭi.
67. 	āṅku - avviṭattu ;  
68-69. 	āyiraṅ kaṇṇōṉ aruṅkalacceppu - āyiraṅ kaṇ kaḷiyuṭiya intiraṉatu peṟutaṟkariya aṇikalaṅkaḷip peyta paṇippēḻiyiṉ, vāytiṟantaṉṉa matilaka varippil- vāyit tiṟantu vittāṟpōṉṟa matiliṉuḷḷiṭatta tākiya an nakar ellikkaṇṇē;
kalacceppu - aṇikalappeṭṭi. akanakarc ciṟappuk kūṟutalāṉ, `matilaka varippu' eṉṟār.
70-75. 	kuṭakāṟṟu eṟintu - mēlkāṟṟu vicittu vīcuta lāṉ, koṭi nuṭaṅku maṟukiṉ - koṭikaḷ acikiṉṟa maṟu kiṉkaṇ uḷḷa, kaṭi kaḻimakaḷir - peṇṭirkkaminta elliyiṉik kaḻinta potu makaḷir, kātalam celvaroṭu - tam pāṟ kātal
koṇṭa celva iḷiñaruṭaṉ, varupuṉal viyi marutu ōṅku muṉtuṟi - iṭiyaṟātu nīroḻukum viyi yāṟṟiṉ ōṅkiya tirumarutattuṟi muṉṉar, viripūnturutti veṇmaṇal aṭikari - paranta polivupeṟṟa āṟṟiṭik kuṟiyiṉ veḷḷiya maṇaliyuṭiya aṭikarikkaṇ, ōṅku nīr māṭamoṭu nāvāy iyakki - uyarnta paḷḷiyōṭat tōṭu tōṇikaḷi ēṟic celuttiyum, pūm puṇi taḻīi - polivu peṟṟa teppattiṉit taḻuvi nīntiyum, puṉal āṭṭu amarntu - iṅṅaṉam nīrāṭutali virumpi;
āṭittiṅkaḷātaliṉ kuṭakāṟṟeṟinta teṉka. potu makaḷir varampiṉillātavarākalāṉ kaṭikaḻi makaḷir eṉappaṭṭaṉar. kaṭi - elli, varampu. iṉi, nāṇum maṭaṉum peṇmiyavāka lāṉ, ivaṟṟik kaḻintē kaṭikaḻiya vēṇṭutaliṉ kaṭikaḻi makaḷir eṉap potumakaḷirkkup peyar kūṟiṉār eṉalumām. potumakaḷirkkuk kātal poruḷiṉkaṇṇa tākalāṉ, kātalam celvar eṉpataṟkut tām kātalikkum celvattiṉiyuṭiya kāmukar eṉak kōṭalum amiyum. marutamaram ōṅkiyuḷḷamiyāl tirumarutan tuṟiyeṉap peyar peṟṟa nīrttuṟiyeṉka.
ittuṟi caṅkac ceyyuḷ palavaṟṟāṟ ciṟappikkappaṭuvatu;
1"tici tici tēṉārkkun tirumaruta muṉṟuṟi," 2"varupuṉal viyi vārmaṇa lakaṉṟuṟit, tirumaru tōṅkiya virimalark kāviṉ," 3"tīmpuṉal viyit tirumaruta muṉṟuṟiyāl eṉpaṉa kāṇka.
itu ciṟupoḻutāṟiṉum kālippoḻutu kaḻikkumāṟu kūṟiyatu. 
79-82. 	taṇ naṟu mulliyum - kuḷirnta naṟiya mulli malarum, tāḻnīrk kuvaḷiyum - āḻnta nīriṟ pūtta ceṅkuvaḷi malarum kaṇ aviḻ neytal - kaṇpōl malarnta neytal malarum, ākiya ivaṟṟi, katuppu uṟa aṭicci - kūntaliṟ poruntac cūṭi, veṇpū malliki viriyaloṭu toṭarnta - mallikiyiṉ veḷḷiya pūkkaḷālākiya māliyuṭaṉ iṇinta, taṇ ceṅkaḻunīrt tātu viri piṇiyal - kuḷirnta ceṅkaḻunīriṉ tātu virinta itaḻkaḷāṟ kaṭṭiya piṇiyali, koṟkiyam perun tuṟi muttoṭu pūṇṭu - koṟkit tuṟiyil uṇṭāya periya muttāṟ ceyta vaṭattuṭaṉ pūṇṭu, tekkaṇa malayac ceḻuñcēṟu āṭi - teṉṟicikkaṇṇatākiya potiyiliṟ piṟanta cantaṉattiṉ kuḻampi uṭal muḻutum pūci, poṟkoṭi mūtūrp poḻilāṭṭu amarntāṅku - aḻakiya koṭi kaḷiyuṭiya mūtūrkku ayalatākiya cōliyiṭattu viḷiyāṭum viḷiyāṭṭi virumpi ;
tāṇīrkkuvaḷi eṉpatu pāṭamāyiṉ, tāḷiyuṭiya nīrmi yatākiya kuvaḷi yeṉka. viriyal - māli; virinta pū eṉṟu koṇṭu ataṉuṭaṉ ceṅkaḻunīritaḻic cērttuk kaṭṭiya piṇiyal eṉṟumām. piṇiyal - mārpiliṭuvatu. tekkaṇam; vaṭacoṟṟiripu. āṅku; aci. aṭiccip pūṇṭu āṭi amarntu eṉka. 
itu naṇpakaṟ poḻutu kaḻikkumāṟu kūṟiyatu. 
83-85. 	ēṟpaṭu poḻutiṉ - ēṟpāṭākiya poḻutilē, iḷa nilā muṉṟil - iḷanilāviṉ payaṉi nukartaṟkuriya muṟṟattil, tāḻtarukōlam taki pārāṭṭa - tammaṉattiṟ ṟaṅkiya kōlattip puṉintu koḻunar pārāṭṭa, vīḻpūñ cēkkimēl iṉitu iruntāṅku - virumpappaṭum malaramaḷi mēl iṉimiyuṭaṉiruntu;
eṟpaṭupoḻutu - ñāyiṟu maṟiyum poḻutu. tāḻtaru kōlam taki pārāṭṭa eṉpataṟku muṉṉarp puṉaliṉum poḻiliṉum āṭi iḷitta kōlattit taṅkoḻunar pārāṭṭit tīrkka eṉṟurippārumuḷar. kātalañ celvar pārāṭṭa avaruṭaṉ irunteṉka. kōlattaki yeṉpatu melintu niṉṟatu. āṅku, aci.
iḵtu ēṟpāṭu kaḻikkumāṟu kūṟiyatu.
86-97. 	arattap pūmpaṭṭu arimici uṭīi - aṅṅaṉam cēkkimēliruntu pūttoḻiliyuṭiya cempaṭṭi ari yiṉmītē uṭutti, kural talik kūntal kuṭacam porunti - taliyiṭattuk kottākavuḷḷa kūntalil veṭpālip pūvi muṭittu, ciṟumalic cilampiṟ ceṅkūtāḷamoṭu naṟumalark kuṟiñci nāḷmalar vēyntu - ciṟumaliyeṉap peyariya maliyiṟ pūtta cennaṟuntāḷip pūvuṭaṉē naṟiya malariyuṭiya kuṟiñciyiṉ putiya pūvic cūṭi, kuṅkuma varuṇam koṅkiyiṉ iḻittu - kuṅkumam pōlum niṟattiyuṭiya ceñ cantaṉattik koṅkiyil eḻuti, ceṅkoṭu vēric ceḻum pūmpiṇiyal - ceṅkoṭu vēriyiṉ vaḷaviya pūvāṟ kaṭṭappaṭṭa piṇiyali, cinturac cuṇṇam cērnta mēṉiyil - civanta cuṇṇam appiya mārpilē, am tukirkkōvi aṇiyoṭu pūṇṭu - aḻakiya pavaḷa vaṭamākiya aṇiyuṭaṉ pūṇṭu, malic ciṟaku arinta vaccira vēntaṟku - malikaḷiṉ ciṟaki yarinta vaccirap paṭiyiṉiyuṭiya intiraṉukku, kali keḻu kūṭaṟ cevvaṇi kāṭṭa - āravāram poruntiya kūṭaliṭattē tam cevvaṇiyik 
kāṭṭumāṟu, kār aracāḷaṉ vāṭiyoṭu varūum kālam aṉṟiyum - kāri āḷum aracaṉ vāṭik kāṟṟōṭu varum kālamum, ak kāla maṉṟiyum;
ari - ākupeyarāṉ mēkaliyumām. kural - pūṅkottumām. kuṭacam - ceṅkuṭacam. nāṇmalar eṉap piṉ varutaliṉ, naṟumalark kuṟiñci eṉpatil mutaṟkēṟṟa aṭiyaṭuttatu. iḻittuc cērnta mēṉiyil piṇiyali aṇiyoṭu pūṇṭu eṉṟiyikka. cevvaṇi kāṭṭa eṉpataṟkuk kaṭi kaḻi makaḷir eḻu vāy; kuṭacam, kuṟiñci, koṭuvēri yeṉṉumivi kār kālattup pūvātalkoṇṭu kāraracāḷaṉi eḻuvāyākkuvar aṭiyārkku nallār; ceyte ṉeccaṅkaḷ muṭivu peṟāmiyiṉ atu poruḷaṉmiyōrka. kār - mukil; kāraracāḷaṉ - kār kālam; 1"icittalu muriya vēṟiṭat tāṉa" eṉpataṉāl uyartiṇiyāṟ kūṟiṉār; iv viti mēlvaruvaṉavaṟṟiṟkuṅ koḷka. cevvaṇi kāṭṭat tuṇiyāka varuṅ kāla meṉka. vaccira vēntaṉ mukilukkut talivaṉākaliṉ avaṟkuc cevvaṇi kāṭṭiṉareṉka. avar aramakaḷi roppā reṉpatumām. kūṭaṟ cevvaṇi ; vēṟu poruḷun tōṉṟa niṉṟatu. kālamum aḵtaṉṟiyum eṉap pirikka.     
97-101. 	nūlōr ciṟappiṉ - ciṟpa nūl vallōrāṟ ciṟappuṟac ceyyappaṭṭa, mukil tōy māṭattu - mēkam tavaḻumpaṭi uyarnta māṭaṅkaḷilē, akil taru viṟakiṉ - koṇṭuvanta akilākiya viṟakālē, maṭavaral makaḷir - maṭappam poruntutaliyuṭiya makaḷir, taṭavu neruppu amarntu - intaḷattil iṭṭa tīyiṉik kāytali virumpi, naṟuñcāntu akalattu nampiyar tammoṭu - naṟiya cāntu pūciya mārpiṉiyuṭiya mintarōṭu kūṭi, kuṟuṅkaṇ aṭikkum kūtirkkāliyum - kuṟiya kaṇkaḷiyuṭiya cāḷaraṅkaḷi aṭikkum kūtirākiya kālamum;
nampiyar - kātalañ celvar. kuṟuṅkaṇ; ākupeyar. taṭavu - intaḷam; tūpamuṭṭi. 
102-105. 	vaḷa maṉi makaḷirum mintarum - celva mikka maṉiyiṭattu makaḷirum mintarum, virumpi iḷa nilā muṉṟiliṉ iḷaveyil nukara - iḷanilāvi nukartaṟkuriya muṟṟatti liruntu iḷaveyili virumpi nukarumpaṭi, virikatir maṇṭilam teṟku ērpu - virinta katiriyuṭiya ñāyiṟṟu maṇṭilam teṟkē eḻuntu iyaṅkutalāṉē, veṇ maḻi aritil tōṉṟum accirakkāliyum - veṇmukil aritākat tōṉṟum muṉpaṉik kālamum evviṭattuḷḷaṉa;
vaḷamaṉi nilā muṉṟil eṉṟiyittalumām. iḷanilā - mālip poḻutiṉ nilā; cutinilā eṉa muṉṟiṟku aṭiyāk kalumām. teṟku - mituṉavīti ; vāṉilē kōṭkaḷ iyaṅkum neṟikaḷ mēṭavīti, iṭapavīti, mituṉavīti, eṉa mūṉṟākap pakukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa; patiṉoṉṟām paripāṭṭuḷ 1"ericaṭi yeḻil vēḻam taliyeṉak kīḻiruntu, teruviṭip paṭuttamūṉ ṟoṉpatiṟ ṟirukkiyuḷ" eṉa varutal kāṇka. ērpu - eḻutalāṉ eṉat tirikka. evviṭattuḷḷaṉa eṉṟu oru col varuvikka. 
106. 		āṅkatu aṉṟiyum - am muṉpaṉikkāla maṉṟiyum; 
106-112. 	ōṅku irumparappiṉ - mikap periya kaṭaliṉ kaṇuḷḷa, vaṅka īṭṭattu - nāvāyiṉ tiraḷālē, toṇṭiyōr iṭṭa - toṇṭi yeṉṉum patiyiluḷḷa aracar tiṟiyāka viṭṭa, akilum tukilum āramum vācamum toku karuppūramum cumantu uṭaṉvanta - akil paṭṭucantaṉam vācam karuppūram eṉṉum ivaṟṟiṉ maṇattiṉi oruṅku cumantu vanta, koṇṭaloṭu pukuntu kōmakaṉ kūṭal - kīḻ kāṟṟōṭu aracaṉatu kūṭaṟkaṭ pukuntu, veṅkaṇ neṭuvēḷ vil viḻāk kāṇum - kāmaṉatu koṭiya vil veṟṟi poruntiya viḻāviṉik kāṇum, paṅkuṉi muyakkattup paṉiyaracu yāṇṭu uḷaṉ - paṅkuṉit tiṅkaḷ īṟākap poruntiya piṉpaṉik kālamākiya aracaṉ evviṭattuḷḷāṉ;
parappu - paravi; kaṭal. toṇṭi - cōṇāṭṭuk kaṭaṟkarik kaṇṇatōr pati yeṉpatu koṇṭaloṭu pukuntu eṉpataṉāṟ peṟappaṭum; caṅkac ceyyuṭkaḷ palavaṟṟiṟ kūṟappaṭum cērar kaṭaṟṟuṟip paṭṭiṉamākiya toṇṭi vēṟu, itu vēṟu eṉpataṟika. toṇṭiyōr - cōḻa kulattōr. koṇṭal - kīḻk kāṟṟu. koṇṭaloṭu pukuntu kāṇum paṉiyaraceṉka. tukil - paṭṭuvarkkam; vācamūṭṭap peṟṟamiyāl ivaṟṟuṭaṉ ōtap paṭṭatu.
(aṭi. 	iṉi, toku eṉpataṉi iṟuti viḷakkākak koṇṭu poruḷurikka. urikkumāṟu;- akil; arumaṇavaṉ takkōli kiṭāravaṉ kārakil eṉṟu collappaṭṭa palavakittāya tokutiyum, tukil; kōcikam pītakam paccili arattam nuṇṭukil cuṇṇam vaṭakam pañcu iraṭṭu pāṭakam kōṅkalar kōpam cittirakkammi kuruti kariyal pāṭakam pariyaṭṭakkācu vētaṅkam puṅkark kāḻakam cilliki tūriyam paṅkam tattiyam vaṇṇaṭi kavaṟṟumaṭi nūlyāppu tirukku tēvāṅku poṉṉeḻuttu kuccari tēvakiri kāttūlam iṟañci veṇpotti cempotti paṇippotti yeṉṟu collappaṭṭa palavakittākiya tokutiyum, āram ; maliyāram tīmuraṉpacci kiḻāṉpacci pacciveṭṭi aricantaṉam; vēraccukkoṭi yeṉṟu collappaṭṭa palavakittākiya tokutiyum, vācam ; ampar eccam kattūri cavātu cāntu kuṅkumam paṉinīr puḻuku takkōlam nākappū ilavaṅkam cātikkāy vacuvāki niriyācam tilam eṉṟu collappaṭṭa palavakittākiya tokutiyum, karuppūram: maliccarakku kali aṭivucarakku mārpu iḷamārpu ārūrkkāl kiyoṭṭukkāl mārappaṟṟu varācāṉ kumaṭeṟivāṉ urukkurukku vāṟōcu cūṭaṉ cīṉaccūṭaṉ eṉṟu peyar kūṟappaṭṭa palavakittākiya tokutiyum eṉat tokuttum virittum poruḷurikka.) 
113-117. 	kōti mātavi koḻuṅkoṭi eṭuppa - māliyi yuṭiya kurukkatti aḻakiya koṭiyi yeṭukkavum, kāvum kāṉamum kaṭimalar ēnta - iḷamarakkāvum nantavaṉamum naṟiya malarkaḷi ēntavum, teṉṉavaṉ potiyil teṉṟa loṭu pukuntu maṉṉavaṉ kūṭal - pāṇṭiyaṉatu potiyiṉ malit teṉṟalōṭu am maṉṉavaṉatu kūṭaliṉkaṭ pukuntu, makiḻtuṇi taḻūum - tām virumpum tuṇikaḷit taḻuvuvikkum, iṉ iḷavēṉil yāṇṭuḷaṉkol eṉṟu - iṉiya iḷa vēṉileṉṉum aracaṉ evviṭattuḷḷāṉ eṉṟu;
kōti mātavi - mālipōlap pūkkum kurukkatti. koṭi yeṭuppa - koṭi vaḷarappeṟa; tukiṟkoṭi yēntivara eṉṉum poruḷum tōṉṟa niṉṟatu. kūṭaliṟ pukunteṉa māṟuka, 
taḻūum - taḻuvuvikkum ; piṟaviṉi. 
118-119. 	uruvak koṭiyōr - pūṅkoṭi pōlum uruviṉiyuṭiya makaḷir, uṭipperuṅ koḻunaroṭu - tammi yuṭiya periya koḻunarōṭiruntu, paruvam eṇṇum - ap paruvaṅkaḷiṉ varavi yeṇṇukiṉṟa, paṭar tīr kāli - varuttam nīṅkiya kālattē;
uruvak koṭiyōr - uruvilē yeḻutiya koṭiyiṉiyuṭayōr eṉṟumām ; ivar muṟkūṟiya kaṭikaḻi makaḷir. paruvam eṇṇum - muṉpu tamak kiṉpam viḷitta kār mutaliya paruvaṅkaḷ mīṭṭum varutali yeṇṇum. kaṭikaḻi makaḷir kātalañ celvaroṭu cēkkimēliruntu paruva meṇṇum mutuvēṉiṟ kālatteṉka. vēṉil nīṅkik kār toṭaṅkumpoḻutu aṇittātaliṉ 'paṭartīr kāli' eṉṟār.
120-125. 	kaṉṟu amar āyamoṭu kaḷiṟṟiṉam naṭuṅka - kaṉṟukaḷ virumpiya piṭiyiṉ kūṭṭattōṭu avaṟṟip purakkum kaḷiṟṟiṉamum naṭuṅkumpaṭi, eṉṟūḻ niṉṟa kuṉṟu keḻu naṉ ṉāṭṭu - veyil nilipeṟṟa nalla malicārnta nāṭṭiṉ, kāṭu tīppiṟappak kaṉi eri potti - kāṭumuḻutum tīyuṇṭāka muḻaṅkum aḻali mūṭṭi, kōṭiyoṭu pukuntu - mēl kāṟṟōṭu vantu pukuntu, kūṭal āṇṭa vēṉil vēntaṉ - kūṭali āṭci ceyta vēṉilākiya aracaṉ, vēṟṟuppulam paṭara ōcaṉikkiṉṟa - vēṟṟup pulaṅkaṭkuc cellumāṟu muyal kiṉṟa, uṟuveyil kaṭināḷ - mikka veyiliyuṭiya vēṉiliṉ kaṭi nāḷil;
eṉṟūḻ nīṭiya eṉpatum, pottiya eṉpatum arumpatavuri yāciriyar koṇṭa pāṭam. kaṉi - ceṟivumām. potti - mūṭṭi. ōcaṉittal - pōtaṟku oruppaṭṭu muyalutal; 1"uṭitiri muttañ cinta vōcaṉik kiṉṟa vaṉṉam" eṉpatu kāṇka. avviṭattiruntum akala veṉpār 'vēṟṟup pulampaṭara' eṉṟār; "ōrōr tēyaṅkaṭku ōroru kālam māṟi nikaḻtaliṉ vēṟṟup pulam paṭara veṉṟār" eṉṉum aṭiyārkku nallāruri tamiḻ kūṟu nallulakattiṟkup poruntuvataṉṟu. 
126-131.	viyamum-kūṭārap paṇṭiyum, civikiyum-pallakkum, maṇikkāl amaḷiyum, maṇikaḷiḻitta kālkaḷiyuṭiya cēkkiyum, uyyāṉattiṉ uṟutuṇi makiḻcciyum - nīrāvic cōliyilē cēvippāruṭaṉiruntu putumi kāṇṭalum, cāmarik kavariyum - cāmariyākiya kavariyum, tamaṉiya aṭippiyum - poṉṉāṟ ceyta veṟṟilip peṭṭiyum, kūr nuṉivāḷum - kūriya muṉiyiyuṭiya vāḷum, tam kōmakaṉ koṭuppa - tam aracaṉ koṭukka, peṟṟa celvam piṟaḻā vāḻkki - aṅṅaṉam peṟṟa variciyākiya celvam ekkālattum māṟāta. poṟṟoṭi maṭantiyar putumaṇam puṇarntu - poṉvaḷi yaṇinta makaḷir putiya maṇattiṉip porunti;
uyyāṉam - aracar viḷiyāṭum kāvaṟ cōli. uṟutuṇi makiḻcci eṉpataṟku uṟṟa tuṇivaṉākiya aracaṉōṭu mēvi mey toṭṭu viḷiyāṭu makiḻcci yeṉṟurippar aṭiyārkku nallār. cāmarik kavari - irupeyaroṭṭu. aṭippi - veṟṟilippi (peṭṭi) ; aṭi - veṟṟili. viyam mutaliyaṉa ēṟic cellutalum, uyyāṉattil viḷiyāṭutalum, cāmari vīcap peṟutalum, aṭippi piṭittu iṭap peṟutalum, vāḷ ēntap peṟutalum aracaṉpāl variciyākap peṟṟava reṉka. koḻunaroṭu putu maṇam puṇarnteṉṟu kūṭṭuka. putumaṇam puṇarnteṉpataṟku nāṭōṟum putiyārōṭu maṇam puṇarnteṉṟurippārumuḷar. 
132-133. 	cempoṉ vaḷḷattuc cilatiyar ēntiya - ēvaṟ peṇṭir poṉ vaḷḷattil ēntiya, am tīntēṟal māntiṉar mayaṅki - aḻakiya iṉiya kaḷḷiṉ teḷivip parukiṉarāyp piṉṉum mayaṅki;
puṇarcciyāṉuṇṭāya cōrviṉip pōkkik kaḷippu viḷit taṟkut tēṟaṉ māntiṉa reṉka. 
134-145. 	poṟivari vaṇṭiṉam pulluvaḻi aṉṟiyum - vari pāṭum poṟiyiyuṭiya vaṇṭiṉatti avi poruntu miṭattiṉaṉṟiyum, naṟumalar māliyiṉ vaṟitiṭam kaṭintāṅku - naṟiya pūmāliyālē avi poruntāta iṭattiṟ kaṭintu ilavu itaḻc cevvāy iḷamuttu arumpa - ilavitaḻ pō lum civanta vāyiṉ mītē iḷamuttup pōlum paṟkaḷ tōṉṟa muṟuvalittu, pulavik kālattup pōṟṟātu uritta- ūṭaṟ kālattup pātukāvātu kūṟiya, kāviyaṅ kaṇṇār kaṭṭuri - nīlōṟpalam pōṉṟa kaṇṇiṉiyuṭiyāratu pulavip poruḷ potinta uriyākiya, eṭṭukku nāvoṭu navilā nakipaṭu kiḷaviyum - eṇvaki yiṭattiṟ porunti nāvāl navilappaṭāta naki tōṉṟuṅ kiḷaviyum, am ceṅkaḻu nīr arumpu aviḻttaṉṉa - aḻakiya ceṅkaḻunīriṉ arumpi nekiḻttup pārttālotta, ceṅkayal neṭuṅkaṇ ceḻuṅ kaṭip pūcalum - civanta kayaliṉi yotta nīṇṭa kaṇṇiṉ kaṭiccivappāṟ ceyta pūcalum, koli viṟ puruvattuk koḻuṅkaṭi curuḷa - kolittoḻil puriyum villi yotta puruvattiṉ aḻakiya kōṭikaḷ uḷ vaḷiya, tilakac ciṟu nutal arumpiya viyarum - tilakamaṇinta ciṟiya neṟṟiyil arumpiya viyarum, cevvi pārkkum ceḻuṅkuṭic celva roṭu - tīruñ cevvi pārttu varuntum vaḷamikka kuṭip piṟanta celvarōṭē, viyam kāvalar makiḻtaru vītiyum - nilattiṉip purakkum aracarum virumpum vītiyum;
vaṇṭiṉam pullutal - itaḻil maṟiyum viḻiyip pūvitaḻil maṟiyum tamatiṉameṉak karutic cērtal. māli - puṇarcciyāṟ parinta māli. matuvuṇṭa mayakkattāl vaṇṭu moykkāta iṭat tiyum māliyāṟ kaṭintāreṉka. eṭṭu - neñcu mutalāya eṇ vakiyiṭam. kaṭṭuri yeṭukkunar eṉṟu pāṭaṅkoṇṭu, vārtti collappukukiṉṟavar eṉṟurippar arumpatavuri yāciriyar. kāviyaṅkaṇṇār uritta kaṭṭuriyākiya kiḷaviyum eṉak kūṭṭuka. kiḷaviyum pūcalum viyarum pulaviyā luṇṭāyavi. tīruñ cevviyeṉa oru col varuvitturikka, ceḻuṅkuṭic celvar - aracarallā ēṉiyōr. vaṇṭu mūcutalum māli koṇṭu mayakkattāṟ piṟitiṭaṅ kaṭitalum 1"orutti, kaṇaṅ
koṇṭavi mūcak kiyāṟṟāṉ pūṇṭa, maṇaṅkamaḻ kōti parivukoṇṭōcci" eṉavum, "orutti, yiṟanta kaḷiyāṉitaḻmaṟinta kaṇṇaḷ, paṟantavi mūcak kaṭivāḷ kaṭiyum, iṭantēṟṟāḷ cōrntaṉaḷ ki" eṉavum kalittokiyuṭ kūṟappaṭutal īṇṭaṟin tiṉ puṟaṟpālatu. 
146-147. 	cuṭumaṇ ēṟā - ceṅkal taliyil ēṟappaṭāta, vaṭu nīṅku ciṟappiṉ - kuṭippaḻi nīṅkiya ciṟappiṉiyuṭiya, muṭi aracu oṭuṅkum kaṭimaṉi vāḻkki - muṭicūṭiya aracarum piṟa raṟiyātu taṅkiyiruttaṟkēṟṟa viḷakkamuṟṟa maṉiyil vāḻ taliyuṭiya;
patiyilāriṟ kuṭikkuṟṟappaṭṭāri ēḻu ceṅkaṟ cumatti ūric cuṟṟivarac ceytu puṟattu viṭutal marapākaliṉ, aṅṅaṉam kuṟṟappaṭātava reṉpār "cuṭuma ṇēṟā vaṭunīṅku ciṟappiṉ" eṉṟār ; itaṉi, 1"maṟṟavaṉ ṟaṉṉāl maṇimē kalitaṉip, poṟṟērk koṇṭu pōtē ṉākiṟ, cuṭuma ṇēṟṟi yāṅkuñcūḻ pōki, vaṭu voṭu vāḻu maṭantiyar tammō, ṭaṉiyē ṉāki yaraṅkak kūttiyar maṉiyakam pukāa marapiṉaḷ" eṉa, maṇimēkaliyiṟ cittirāpati vañ ciṉaṅ kūṟutalāṉu maṟika; iṉi, ōṭuvēyātu poṟṟakaṭu vēynta maṉi yeṉṟalumām. muṭiyara coṭuṅkum eṉṟatu maṉiyiṉ perumi kūṟiyapaṭi. 
148-151.	vēttiyal potuviyal eṉa iru tiṟattu māt tiri aṟintu - vēttiyalum potuviyalumākiya iruvakik kūttiṉ iyalpiṉiyaṟintu, mayaṅkā marapiṉ āṭalum-avi mayaṅkāta muṟimiyāṉē āṭum āṭalum, variyum-pāṭalum, pāṇiyum - tāḷaṅkaḷum, tūkkum - it tāḷaṅkaḷiṉ vaḻi varum eḻuvakit tūkkukkaḷum, kūṭiya kuyiluvak karuviyum uṇarntu- ivaṟṟuṭaṉ kūṭiyicikkum kuyiluvak karuvikaḷum uṇarntu;
vēttiyal - aracarkkāṭuvatu ; potuviyal - ēṉōrkkāṭuvatu: ivi vacikkūttiṉ vakiyeṉpa. āṭal mutaliyavaṟṟiṉiyalpiṉi araṅkēṟṟu kātiyil "āṭalum pāṭalum pāṇiyun tūkkum, kūṭiya neṟiyiṉa koḷuttuṅ kāli" eṉa varuvataṉ uri mutaliyavaṟṟāṉaṟika. kuyiluvak karuvi - tōṟkaruvi tuḷik karuvi narappuk karuvi yeṉpaṉa ; mattaḷam taṇṇumi iṭakki calliki yeṉṉum uttamat tōṟkaruvi nāṉku meṉpārumuḷar. 
152-154. 	nālvaki marapiṉ aviṉayak kaḷattiṉum-nālvaki muṟimiyiṉiyuṭiya aviṉaya nilattiṉum, eḻuvaki nilattiṉum - kural mutalāya ēḻ nilattiṉum, eytiya virikkum poruntiya āṭal pāṭalkaḷip parappum, malipparuñ ciṟappiṉ talikkōl ariviyum - māṟupaṭuttaṟkariya ciṟappiṉiyuṭiya talikkōṟ paṭṭa meytiya ariviyum;
aviṉaya nilam nāṉkāvaṉa ; niṟṟal iyaṅkal iruttal kiṭattal eṉpaṉa. talikkōl iyalpiṉi 2araṅkēṟṟu kātiyāṉaṟika. 
155. 		vāram pāṭum tōriya maṭantiyum - vārappāṭṭiṉip pāṭum tōriya maṭantiyum ; 
tōriya maṭanti - āṭi mutirnta piṉpu pāṭaṉ makaḷāy āṭaṉ makaḷir kālukku oṟṟaṟuttup pāṭuṅkāl iṭattūṇ cērntiyalumavaḷ; 1"inneṟi vakiyā liṭattūṇ cērnta. toṉṉeṟi yiyaṟkit tōriya makaḷirum" eṉpataṉ uri kāṇka. 
156. 		talip pāṭṭuk kūttiyum - talippāṭṭip pāṭuṅ kūttiyum, iṭip pāṭṭuk kūttiyum - iṭippāṭṭip pāṭuṅ kūttiyum eṉṉum;
talippāṭṭu ukam eṉavum, iṭippāṭṭu oḷakam eṉavum paṭumeṉpar. 
157-160. 	nālvēṟu vakiyiṉ nayattaku marapiṉ - nālvaki yōṭuṅ kūṭi yāvarum virumpattakka muṟimiyiṉāl, eṭṭuk kaṭi niṟutta āyirattu eṇ kaḻañcu - āyiratteṭṭu eṉṉum eṇṇiṉiyuṭiya kaḻañciṉi, muṭṭā vikal muṟimiyiṉ vaḻā - nāṭōṟum muṭṭātu peṟum muṟimiyiṉiṉṟum vaḻuvāta, tākkaṇaṅku aṉiyār nōkku valippaṭṭu āṅku - tīṇṭi varuttum aṇaṅku pōlvāruṭiya kaṇṇākiya valiyilakappaṭṭu;
eṭṭiṉik kaṭiyil niṟutta āyiram - āyiratteṭṭu; āyirattu eṇ kaḻañcu ; āyiramākiya eṇṇiṉiyuṭiya kaḻañcu; āyirak kaḻañcu eṉpatūum pāṭam ; āyiratteṭṭuk kaḻañcu poṉ peṟutali, 2"vitimuṟik koḷkiyi ṉāyirat teṇ
kaḻañ corumuṟiyākap peṟṟaṉaḷ" eṉpataṉāṉum aṟika. āṅku, aci; paṭṭa appoḻutē yeṉṟumām. 
161-167. 	arumpeṟal aṟivum perum piṟitāka - peṟutaṟ kariya tamataṟivum keṭṭoḻiya, tavattōr āyiṉum - tava neṟiyil muyalvōrāyiṉum, takimalar vaṇṭiṉ - aḻakiya malartōṟuñ ceṉṟu avaṟṟiṉ tēṉip parukum vaṇṭu pōla, nakippatam pārkkum iḷiyōr āyiṉum - avar kāmak kuṟippu nikaḻa nakikkum nakiyiṉ cevvi pārttup putuvōrip puṇarum kāmukarāyiṉum, kāmaviruntiṉ maṭavōr āyiṉum - kāmaviṉpatti muṉpu nukarntaṟiyāta putiyarāyiṉum, ēma vikal iṉ tuyil vatiyum - nāṭōṟum puṇarcciyil mayaṅki iṉiya tuyililē kiṭakkum, paṇṇum kiḷiyum paḻitta tīñcol - paṇṇiṉiyum kiḷiyiṉiyum paḻitta iṉiya colliyuṭiya. eṇṇeṇ kaliyōr iru peru vītiyum - tamakku vakukkappaṭṭa aṟupattu nāṉku kalikaḷi valla patiyilāruṭiya iruvakippaṭṭa vītikaḷum;
perum piṟitātal - ceyalaṟṟoḻital. vaṇṭākiya uvamat tāl atu muṉ nukarnta malarit tuṟantu cellutal pōl iḷiyōr muṉpu nukarnta makaḷirit tuṟantu cellutaluṅ koḷka. ēmam - mayakkam, kiḷi - kiḷḷi. eṇṇeṇ kali - yāḻvāciṉi mutaliya aṟupattu nāṉku kalikaḷ ; nāṭakak kaṇikiyarkku ivi uriya veṉpatu, 1"eṇṇāṇ kiraṭṭi yiruṅkali payiṉṟa, paṇṇiyaṉ maṭantiyar payaṅkeḻu vīti" eṉap piṉṉarum kūṟappaṭukiṉ ṟatu; 2"yāḻmuta lāka aṟupat torunāṉ, kēriḷa makaḷirk kiyaṟkiyeṉ ṟeṇṇik, kaliyuṟa vakutta kāmak kēḷvi" eṉṟār piṟarum iruperuvīti - ciṟutaṉam peruntaṉam peṟuvār vīti yeṉpār.
iṉi, paṇṇum kiḷiyum paḻitta eṉpataṟkup paṇkaḷiyum avaṟṟiṉ tiṟaṅkaḷiyum paḻitta eṉṟalumām. 
168-179.	viyamum - kollāp paṇṭiyum, pāṇṭilum - iraṇṭuruḷiyuṭiya cakaṭamum, maṇit tērk koṭiñciyum - aḻakiya tēr moṭṭum, mey puku kavacamum - meypukutaṟ kiṭamākiya kavacamum, vīḻmaṇit tōṭṭiyum - virumpappaṭum maṇikaḷ patitta aṅkucamum, ataḷ puṉi araṇamum. - tōlāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa kittaḷamum, ariyā yōkamum - arippaṭṭi kiyum, vaḷitaru kuḻiyamum - vaḷitaṭiyum, vāl veṇkavariyum - mikaveḷḷiya cāmariyum, ēṉap paṭamum - paṉṟimukak kaṭakum, kiṭukiṉpaṭamum - ciṟukaṭakum, kāṉappaṭamum - kāṭeḻutiṉa kaṭakum, kāḻ ūṉṟu kaṭikiyum - kuttuk kōlkaḷum, cempiṟ ceynavum - cempāṟ ceytaṉavum, kañcat toḻi lavum - veṇkalattāṟ ceytaṉavum, vampiṉ muṭinavum - kayiṟṟāl muṭivaṉavum, māliyiṉ puṉinavum - kiṭiyāl māliyākap puṉivaṉavum, vētiṉat tuppavum - īrvāḷ mutaliya karuvikaḷum, kōṭu kaṭi toḻilavum - tantattik kaṭintu ceyta toḻili yuṭiyavikaḷum, pukiyavum - vācap pukikku uṟuppāyuḷḷaṉavum, cāntavum - mayirccāntukku uṟuppāyuḷḷaṉavum, pūviṟ puṉinavum - pūvāṟ puṉiyappaṭum māli kaḷum ākiya, vaki terivu aṟiyā - vēṟupāṭu teritaṟkariya, vaḷam talimayaṅkiya - iv vaḷaṅkaḷ kalantu kiṭakkiṉṟa, aracu viḻi tiruviṉ aṅkāṭi vītiyum - aracarum kāṇavirumpum celvattiyuṭiya aṅkāṭit teruvum ;
ariyā yōkam - piṇiyik keṭukkum maruntu eṉṟum, kuḻiyam - vācavuruṇṭi yeṉṟum, kiṭukiṉpaṭam - tōṟkaṭaku eṉṟum, kōṭukaṭitoḻil - āṉikkōṭu mutaliyavaṟṟik kaṭi yum toḻiṟkuriya karuvikaḷ eṉṟum kūṟuvārumuḷar. vālveṇ: oru poruṭ paṉmoḻi. ēṉappaṭam mutaliya mūṉṟum kēṭaka vakikaḷ. vampiṉ muṭina - kayiṟṟiṉāṟ putumiyuṟa muṭiyappaṭuvaṉa ; allikkayiṟu, kucikkayiṟu mutaliyaṉa. vētiṉam-īrvāḷ. tuppu - karuvi. pukiya - nērkaṭṭi centēṉ niriyācam paccili āram eṉṉum pukiyuṟuppukkaḷ, cānta - meyyiṟpūcum cāntiṉ uṟuppukkaḷumām.  
180-183. 	kākapātamum kaḷaṅkamum vintuvum ēkiyum nīṅki - kuṟṟam paṉṉiraṇṭaṉuḷ mikavum tīyaṉavākiya kākapātam kaḷaṅkam vintu irēki yeṉṉum nāṉkum nīṅki, iyalpiṟ kuṉṟā - kuṇaṅkaḷiṟ kuṉṟātaṉavāki, nūlavar noṭinta - nūlōrāṟ kūṟappaṭṭa, nuḻi nuṇkōṭi - mikka nuṇmiyuṭiya kōṭiyiṉiyum, nālvaki varuṇattu - nālvaki niṟattiṉiyum, nalam kēḻ oḷiyavum - naṉmi poruntiya oḷiyiyum uṭiya vayirac cātiyum ;
vintu- puḷḷi. ēki - irēki yeṉpataṉ citivu. nūlavar noṭinta eṉpataṉi ellāvaṟṟoṭuṅ kūṭṭuka. nuḻinuṇ - mikakkūrtta ; oru poruḷiru col. oḷi - intiraviṟ pōlum oḷiyeṉpar. kuṟṟam mutaliyavaṟṟip piṉvarum paḻiya nūṟpākkaḷāṉaṟika. "carimalaṅ kīṟṟuc cappaṭi piḷattal, tuḷikari vintu kāka pātam, iruttuk kōṭika ḷilātaṉa murital, tāri maḻuṅkal taṉṉōṭu, īrāṟum vayirat tiḻipeṉa moḻipa," "palaki yeṭṭuṅ kōṇa māṟum, ilakiya tāriyuñ cuttiyun tarācamum, aintuṅ kuṇameṉ ṟaṟintaṉar pulavar, intira cāpat tikaloḷi peṟiṉē," "kāka pātam nākaṅ kollum," "malampiri yātatu nilantaru kiḷikeṭum," "vintu cintiyiṟ cantā pantarum," "kīṟṟu varaliṉi yēṟṟavar māyvar."  
184-185.	ēkiyum māliyum iruḷoṭu tuṟanta - kīṟṟum tārumākiya kuṟṟaṅkaḷi iruḷeṉṉum kuṟṟattōṭu nīkkiṉa, pācār mēṉi - pacumi niṟinta meyyiyuṭiya, pacuṅ katir oḷiyavum - iḷaṅkatiroḷi paranta marakatac cātiyum :
184-185. 	ēki - irēki ; kīṟṟu. iruḷ - karukal. marakatak kuṟṟam eṭṭaṉuḷ ivi mūṉṟum mikka kuṟṟameṉka. kuṟṟam eṭṭiṉiyum, "karukutal veḷḷi kaṉmaṇal kīṟṟup, porivu tarāca miṟuku taṉ marakatat, teṇṇiya kuṟṟa miviyeṉa moḻipa" eṉpataṉāṉaṟika. iṉi, marakatattiṉ kuṇamum eṭṭeṉpa; avaṟṟi, "neyttalkiḷi mayiṟkaḻut tottalpim payiriṟ, pacuttal poṉmi taṉṉuṭaṉ pacuttal, patti pāytal poṉvaṇ ṭiṉ vayi, ṟottut teḷitalo ṭeṭṭuṅ kuṇamē" eṉpataṉāṉaṟika. 
186-187. 	patumamum nīlamum vintamum paṭitamum vitimuṟi piḻiyā viḷaṅkiya cātiyum - nūlkaḷil vititta muṟiyiṟ piṟaḻāmal viḷaṅkiya patumam nīlam vintam paṭitam eṉṉum nālvakic cāti māṇikka varukkamum;
vitimuṟi piḻiyā eṉavē, piṟappiṭam, varuṇam, peyar kuṇam, kuṟṟam, niṟam, vili, patti eṉṉumiviyum, piṟavum aṭaṅkiṉa; eṉṉi? "māṇikkattiyal vakukkuṅ kālic, camaṉoḷi cūḻnta vorunāṉ kiṭamum, nālvaki varuṇamum naviṉṟavip peyarum, paṉṉiru kuṇamum patiṉaṟu kuṟṟamum, irupat teṇvaki ilaṅkiya niṟamum, maruviya viliyum patti pāytalum, iviyeṉa moḻipa viyalpuṇarn tōrē" eṉṟārākaliṉ. ivaṟṟuḷ, patumam - patumarākam eṉavum cāturaṅkam eṉavum paṭum. nīlam - nīla kanti eṉavum cukanti eṉavum paṭum. vintam - kuruvintam eṉavum irattavintu eṉavum paṭum. paṭitam - kōvāṅku eṉappaṭum. ivaṟṟiṉ niṟaṅkaḷi, "tāmari kaḻunīr cātakap puṭkaṇ, kōpa miṉmiṉi koṭuṅkatir viḷakku, mātuḷip pūviti vaṉṉi yīrintum, ōtucā turaṅka voḷiyā kummē," tilaka mulōtti rañ cemparut tippūk, kavirmalar kuṉṟi muyaluti rammē, cinturaṅkukkiṟ kaṇṇeṉa veṭṭum, eṇṇiya kuruvinta maṉṉiya niṟamē," "kōkilakkaṇ cempañcu koymmalarp palācam, acōka pallava maṇimalark kuvaḷi, ilavat talarka ḷeṉṟāṟu kuṇamum, cukan tikkuc cāṟṟiya niṟaṉē," "kōvinaṟ ceṅkal kurāmalar mañcaḷeṉak, kūṟiya nāṉkuṅ kōvāṅku niṟaṉē" eṉpavaṟṟāṉaṟika.  
188. 		pūca uruviṉ polam teḷittaṉiyavum - pūcamīṉiṉ uruviṉiyuṭiya poṉṉik kaḷaṅkamaṟat teḷivittālotta puruṭarāka varukkamum;
pūciyuruviṟ polantēyttaṉiyavum eṉṟu pāṭaṅkoṇṭu, pūṉikkaṇ pōṉṟu poṉṉit tēyttāṟ pōṉṟavi puruṭarākam eṉpar arumpata vuriyāciyar.
189. 		tītu aṟu katir oḷit teṇmaṭṭu uruvavum - kuṟṟa maṟṟa paritiyiṉoḷiyum teḷinta tēṉṟuḷiyiṉ niṟamum uṭiya vayiṭūriya varukkamum ;
itaṉik kōmētakam eṉpar arumpata vuriyāciriyar.
190. 		iruḷ teḷittaṉiyavum - iruḷit teḷiyavittā lotta nīlamaṇi varukkamum;
"nīlat tiyalpu niṟukkuṅ kāli, nālvaki varuṇamum naṇṇumā karamum, kuṇampati ṉoṉṟum kuṟiyiru nāṉkum, aṇivōr ceyalu maṟintici ṉōrē" eṉpataṉāl, nīlattiṟkuc cāti nāṉkum, kuṇam patiṉoṉṟum, kuṟṟam eṭṭumeṉak koḷka.
190. 		iruvēṟu uruvavum - mañcaḷum civappum kalantā lotta kōmētaka varukkamum ;
iruvēṟuru viṉiyuṭiyaṉa vayiṭūriyam eṉpar arumpatavuri yāciriyar. 
191-192. 	orumit tōṟṟattu aivēṟu vaṉappiṉ - oṉṟu paṭṭa piṟappiṉiyum vēṟupaṭṭa aintu vaṉappiṉiyumuṭiya. ilaṅku katir viṭūum nalamkeḻu maṇikaḷum - viḷaṅkum oḷiviṭā niṉṟa naṉmi poruntiya māṇikkam puruṭarākam vayiṭūriyam nīlam kōmētakam eṉṉum maṇikaḷum ;
cātiyum aṉiyavum uruvamum aṉiyavum iruvēṟuruvamum ākiya aivēṟuvaṉappiṉ maṇikaḷum eṉka. 
193-196. 	kāṟṟiṉum maṇṇiṉum kalliṉum nīriṉum tōṟṟiya kuṟṟam tukaḷaṟat tuṇintavum - kāṟṟu maṇ kal nīr eṉpavaṟṟāluṇṭāya kuṟṟam ciṟitum iṉmiyālē teḷinta oḷiyuṭiyaṉavum, cantirakuruvē aṅkārakaṉ eṉa vanta nīrmiya vaṭṭat tokutiyum - veḷḷiyum cevvāyum pōla veṇṇīrmi cennīrmiyuṭiyaṉavum tiraṭciyuṭi yaṉavumākiya muttu varukkamum ;
kāṟṟēṟu maṇṇēṟu kallēṟu nīrniliyeṉpaṉa kuṟṟaṅkaḷ palavaṟṟuḷḷum mikkaṉa eṉavum, veḷḷi cevvāy nīrmi kuṇaṅkaḷ palavaṟṟuḷḷum mikkaṉa eṉavuṅ koḷka. cantirakuru - veḷḷi ; viyāḻaṉ eṉṟal purāṇattiṟkottatu. aṅkārakaṉ cevvāy. vaṭṭattokuti - āṇimutteṉpar arumpata vuriyāciriyar.
197-198.	karuppat tuḷiyavum - naṭuvē tuḷipaṭṭaṉavum, kalliṭi muṭaṅkalum - kalliṭukkiṟ pukku vaḷivuṟṟaṉavum, tirukkum nīṅkiya - tirukutaluṟṟaṉavum eṉṉum ik kuṟṟaṅkaḷ nīṅkiya, ceṅkoṭi valliyum -civanta koṭippavaḷa varukkamum;
kalluṭi muṭaṅkal eṉap pāṭaṅkoṇṭu, kalli uḷḷē yuṭiya vaḷivu eṉpar arumpata vuriyāciriyar. piṟavum kuṟṟa muḷavāyiṉum ivi mikka kuṟṟa meṉka. iṉi, kuṇam mikkuk kuṟṟaṅkaḷ nīṅkiyaṉa cinturamum īccaṅkāyum mucumucukkik
kaṉiyum tūtuvaḻutuṇam paḻamum pōṉṟa niṟamum uruṭciyum uṭiyaṉa veṉpar.
iṉi, muṟkūṟiyavaṟṟuḷ māṇikkattiṉ iyalpaṉittum, 1"katirniṟi" eṉṉum kallāṭac ceyyuḷil. "kuṭumic cēkarac camaṉoḷi cūḻnta, niṟitaru nāṉki ṉikaḻntaṉa kuṟiyum, kuruvin tañcu kantikō vāṅku, cāturaṅ kammeṉuñ cātika ṇāṉkum, tēkki ṉeruppiṟ cērkkiṉaṅ kiyil, tūkkiṉaṟ ṟakaṭṭiṟ cuṭar vāy veyilil, kucciyiṉ mattakak kuṟiyiṉō rattil, neyttup pārviyi ṉērntucivan tāṅku, otta naṟkuṇa muṭiyapaṉ ṉiraṇṭum, karukinoy tātal kāṟṟu vekuḷi, tirukaṉ muraṇē cemma ṇiṟukal, mattakak kuḻivu kāca miliccumi, veccam porivu pukital puṭāyam, cantineyp piliyeṉat takupati ṉāṟu, muntiya nūliṉ moḻintaṉa kuṟṟamum, cātakap puṭkaṇ ṭāmari kaḻunīr, kōpa miṉmiṉi koṭuṅkatir viḷakku, vaṉṉi mātuḷam pūviti yeṉṉap, paṉṉucā turaṅka voḷikkuṇam pattum, cempañ carattan tilaka mulōttiram, muyaliṉ cōri cinturaṅ kuṉṟi, kavirala reṉṉak kavarniṟa meṭṭum, kuruvin tattiṟ kuṟittaṉa niṟamum, acōkap pallava malaricem pañcu, kōkilak kaṇṇī ḷilavalar cem peṉat, tarucu kanti taṉṉiṟa māṟum, ceṅkal kurāmalar mañcaḷ kōvi, kuṅkuma mañciṟ kōvāṅku niṟamum, tiṭṭi yēṟu civanta vitāyam, okkal puṟṟāṅ kuruti toḻutiṉi, maṇikō kaṉakaṅkaṟpam pāṭi, māṅkica kanti vaḷarkāñ cuṇṭiyeṉ, ṟāṅkoru patiṉmūṉ ṟaṭinta kuṟṟamum, iviyeṉak kūṟiya niṟiyaruṭkaṭavuḷ" eṉak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷami kāṇka. iṉi, valaṉ eṉṉum avuṇaṉ vēḷvip pacuvāka intiraṉ ataṉic cekuttu vēḷvi ceyta poḻutu ataṉ kuruti mutaliyavaṟṟiṉiṉṟu māṇikkam mutaliya navamaṇikaḷ piṟantaṉaveṉṟu purāṇaṅ kūṟum;
1"	attaki yāviṉ cōri māṇikka māmpal muttam
pittivi ṭūya meṉpu vacciram pittam pacci
neyttaveṇ ṇiṇaṅkō mētan tacitukir neṭuṅka ṇīlam 
eyttavi puruṭa rāka mivinava maṇiyiṉ ṟōṟṟam"
eṉpatu kāṇka. maṟṟum navamaṇikaḷiṉ iyalpaṉittum tiru vālavā yuṭiyār tiruviḷiyāṭaṟ purāṇattu māṇikkam viṟṟa tiruviḷiyāṭalilum, parañcōti muṉivar tiruviḷiyāṭaṟ purāṇattu māṇikkam viṟṟa paṭalattilum virivākap kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa; āṇṭu kāṇka.
199-200. 	vaki teri mākkaḷ toki peṟṟu ōṅki - iv voṉpatu vakippaṭṭa maṇikaḷiṉ piṟappu mutal ciṟappīṟāya iyalpaṉittum teriyavalla vaṇikar tokutalāl uyarcciyuṟṟu, paki teṟal aṟiyāp payaṅkeḻu vītiyum - pakivar varuttutali yaṟiyāta payaṉ mikka irattiṉak kaṭit teruvum; 
201-204. 	cātarūpam kiḷicciṟi āṭakam cāmpūnatam eṉa ōṅkiya koḷkiyiṉ polam terimākkaḷ -cātarūpamum kiḷicciṟiyum āṭakamum cāmpūnatamum eṉṉum nāṉku cātiyāka ōṅkiya iyalpiṉiyuṭiya poṉṉiṉ vēṟṟumiyip pakuttaṟiyum poṉ vāṇikar, kalaṅku, añar oḻittu - koḷvōr evviṭattu eppoṉ uḷateṉṟu aiyuṟun tuṉpatti oḻippa, āṅku - avviṭaṅkaḷil, ilaṅku koṭi eṭukkum nalam kiḷar vītiyum - ivviṭattu ip poṉ uḷateṉa viḷakkak koṭi yeṭukkum naṉmi mikka poṟkaṭit teruvum;
oḻittu- oḻippaveṉat tirikka. nālvakip poṉṉuḷ iṟutik kaṇṇatu 2"poṉṉukkuc cāmpuṉatam" eṉa uyarttuk kūṟappaṭum. 
205-207.	nūliṉum mayiriṉum nuḻinūṟ paṭṭiṉum pāl vaki teriyā - nuṇṇiya parutti nūlāṉum elimayirāṉum paṭṭu nūlāṉum tattam pakuti tōṉṟa neyyappaṭṭu, paṉṉūṟu aṭukkattu naṟumaṭi ceṟinta aṟuvi vītiyum - ovvoṉṟu nūṟāka aṭukkappaṭṭa pala nūṟu aṭukkukaḷi yuṭiya naṟiya puṭavikaḷ neruṅkiyuḷḷa puṭavik kaṭit teruvum ;
nūṟpaṭṭu - paṭṭunūl. mēl, 1''paṭṭiṉu mayiriṉum, parutti nūliṉum, kaṭṭu nuṇviṉik kāruka rirukkiyum" eṉpuḻi urittami kāṇka. maṇamūṭṭutaliṉ, naṟumaṭi yeṉṟār. 
208-211. 	niṟikkōl tulāttar - niṟukkum tulākkōli yuṭiyarāyum, paṟikkaṇ parāriyar - pariya ariyiyum irumpāl vāy māṭṭākak kaṭṭiṉa kaṇṇiyumuṭiya paṟiyiṉiyuṭiyarāyum, ampaṇa aḷaviyar - aḷakkum marakkāliyuṭiyarāyum, eṅkaṇum tiritara - taraku ceyvār niṉṟuḻi nillātu evviṭattum tiriyumpaṭi, kālam aṉṟiyum - ekkālamum, karuṅkaṟi mūṭiyoṭu - periya miḷaku potiyuṭaṉē, kūlam kuvitta kūla vītiyum - kūlaṅkaḷum kuvitta kūlak kaṭitteruvum;
tulākkōl eṉavum, parārikkaṭ paṟi eṉavum māṟuka. paṟi - tali maṭṭamāka aḷakkum ōr mukattalaḷavik karuvi. am paṇam - marakkāl. mūṭiyoṭu eṉṉum uṭaṉikaḻcciyāl pākku mutaliya potikaḷ koḷḷalumām. kūlamāvaṉa; "nellup pullu varakutiṉi cāmi, iṟuṅku tōri yirākiyeṇ kūlam" eṉavum, "eḷḷuk koḷḷup payaṟuḻuntavari, kaṭali tuvari mocci yeṉṟāṅ, kuṭaṉivi mutirik kūlat tuṇavē" eṉavum ōtappaṭṭa īreṇ vakipporuḷkaḷ.  
212-218. 	pālvēṟu terinta nālvēṟu teruvum - pakuti vēṟu terinta antaṇar aracar vaṇikar vēḷāḷar eṉpār irukkum nāl vēṟākiya terukkaḷum, antiyum - muccantiyum, catukkamum - nāṟcantiyum, āvaṇa vītiyum - kōyiṟ kaṭit teruvum, maṉṟamum - maṉṟukaḷum, kavaliyum - pala neṟikaḷ kūṭiṉa muṭukkukaḷum, maṟukum - kuṟunterukkaḷum, tirintu - ulāvi, vicumpu akaṭu tirukiya veṅkatir nuḻiyā - vāṉiṉ naṭuvilē vemmi muṟukiyōṭum ñāyiṟṟiṉ katirkaḷ nuḻiyappaṭāta, pacuṅ koṭip paṭākip pantar nīḻal - putiya ciṟu koṭiyum peruṅkoṭiyumeṉṉum ivaṟṟiṉ pantar niḻalilē, kāvalaṉ pērūr kaṇṭu makiḻvu eyti - pāṇṭi maṉṉaṉatu periya nakariṉik kaṇṭu makiḻcciyuṟṟu, kōvalaṉ peyarntaṉaṉ koṭimatiṟ puṟatteṉ - kōvalaṉ koṭikaḷi yuṭiya matiṟpuṟattu mīṇṭu pōntāṉ eṉka.
kaṇṭu pantar nīḻaṟ ṟirintu makiḻveyti eṉak kūṭṭuka. makiḻcciyeytutalālē  tāṉ karutivanta poruntuḻiyaṟitali maṟantupeyarntaṉaṉ eṉka.
puḷḷeḻuntārppa maṇṭilam tuyileṭuppa iyampac ceṉṟu ēttic ciṟumiyuṟṟēṉ eṉṟalum, kavuntiyaṭikaḷ, aṉiyiyu malli ; piriyā vāḻkki peṟṟaṉiyaṉṟē ; varuntātēkip pōtīṅ keṉṟalum maruṅkiṟpōki ayirātu pukku makiḻtaru vītiyum iru peru vītiyum aṅkāṭi vītiyum payaṅkeḻu vītiyum nalaṅkiḷar vītiyum aṟuvi vītiyum kūlavītiyum nālvēṟu teruvum antiyum catukkamum āvaṇavītiyum maṉṟamum kavaliyum maṟukum ākiya ivviṭaṅkaḷil pantar nīḻalil tirintu kāvalaṉ pērūrik kōvalaṉ kaṇṭu makiḻveytip peyarntāṉ eṉa viṉimuṭikka.
itu nilimaṇṭila vāciriyappā.
ūrkāṇ kāti muṟṟiṟṟu.  

15. aṭikkalak kāti  
(puṟañcēriyiṟ pukka kōvalaṉ kavuntiyaṭikaṭku maturiyiṉ ciṟappiyum pāṇṭiyaṉ koṟṟattiyum kūṟumpoḻutu, talic ceṅkāṉattu maṟiyavaṉākiya māṭalaṉeṉpōṉ kumariyāṭi mīṇṭu varupavaṉ vaḻinaṭi varuttam nīṅkak kavuntiyirukkumiṭatti aṭintāṉ. kōvalaṉ avaṉik kaṇṭu vaṇaṅka, avaṉ kōvalaṉi nōkki, mātavi makaṭku maṇimēkali yeṉṟu peyariṭṭu vāḻttit tāṉam koṭukkumpoḻutu tāṉam peṟutaṟku vanta mutumaṟiyōṉi matayāṉi paṟṟa ataṉ kiyiṉiṉṟum avaṉi viṭuvittu, ataṉ kiyakattē pukkuk kōṭṭiṭiyoṭuṅkip piṭaril ēṟi ataṉi aṭakkiya karuṇi maṟavaṉē! tāṉ vaḷarttatum taṉ makaviṉ uyirik kāttatumākiya kīriyip piṟaḻa uṇarntu koṉṟa kuṟṟattiṟkākak kaṇavaṉāl tuṟakkappaṭṭa pārppaṉiyiṉ pāvam nīṅkat tāṉañceytu, kaṇavaṉi avaḷuṭaṉ kūṭṭi, avarkaḷ vāḻkkikku mikka celvattiyum koṭutta cellāc celvaṉē! pattiṉi orutti aṭāppaḻi yeytap poykkari kūṟic catukkap pūtattāṟ kollappaṭṭavaṉuṭiya tāyiṉ tuyar nīṅka avaṉ cuṟṟattārkkum kiḷikaṭkum poruḷīntu pallāṇṭu puranta illōr cemmalē! yāṉaṟiya nī immiyiṟ ceytaṉa vellām nalviṉiyākavum im māṇikkak koḻuntuṭaṉ `nī iṅṅaṉam pōntatu ummip payaṉō?' eṉa viṉava, kōvalaṉ tāṉ kaṇṭa tīkkaṉāvik kūṟi, ataṉ payaṉāya tuṉpam virivil uṇṭākumeṉṟurikka, maṟiyavaṉum kavuntiyum ivviṭam tuṟantōrkkē uriyatākaliṉ, nī maturiyiṟ pukuka' eṉṟu kūṟiṉar. appoḻutu aṅku vanta āyar mutumakaḷākiya mātiri kavuntiyaṭikaḷik kaṇṭu vaṇaṅkiṉāḷ. koṭumiyillāta vāḻkkiyiyuṭiya kōvalar kuṭiyiṉ mutumakaḷum cevviyaḷumākiya ivaḷiṭattuk kaṇṇakiyi iruttutal kuṟṟamiṉṟeṉa eṇṇi, kaṇṇakiyiṉ uyarviyum kaṟpiṉ ciṟappiyuṅ kūṟi, tavattiṉaratu aṭikkalattip pātukāttalāl eytum perumpayaṉukku ōr varalāṟṟiyum kāṭṭi, avaḷi mātaripāl oppuvikka, avaḷ kavuntiyi ētti naṅkiyuṭaṉ taṉ maṉiyi aṭintāḷ.)   
		nilantaru tiruviṉ niḻalvāy nēmi
kaṭampūṇ ṭuruṭṭuṅ kuriyar peruñcīrk
kōliṉ cemmiyum kuṭiyiṉ taṇmiyum
vēliṉ koṟṟamum viḷaṅkiya koḷkip
5		patiyeḻu vaṟiyāp paṇpumēm paṭṭa
maturi mūtūr mānakar kaṇṭāṅku
aṟantaru neñciṉ aṟavōr palkiya
puṟañciṟi mūtūrp poḻiliṭam pukuntu
tītutīr maturiyum teṉṉavaṉ koṟṟamum
10		mātavat tāṭṭikkuk kōvalaṉ kūṟuḻit
tāḻnīr vēlit talicceṅ kāṉattu
nāṉmaṟi muṟṟiya nalampuri koḷki
māmaṟi mutalvaṉ māṭalaṉ eṉpōṉ
mātava muṉivaṉ malivalaṅ koṇṭu
15		kumariyam peruntuṟi koḷkiyiṟ paṭintu
tamarmutaṟ peyarvōṉ tāḻpoḻi lāṅkaṇ 
vakuntucel varuttattu vāṉṟuyar nīṅkak
kavunti iṭavayiṟ pukuntōṉ taṉṉik
kōvalaṉ ceṉṟu cēvaṭi vaṇaṅka
20		nāva lantaṇaṉ tāṉaviṉ ṟurippōṉ
vēntuṟu ciṟappiṉ viḻuccī reytiya
māntaḷir mēṉi mātavi maṭanti
pālvāyk kuḻavi payantaṉa ḷeṭuttu
vālā minnāḷ nīṅkiya piṉṉar
25		māmutu kaṇikiyar mātavi makaṭku
nāma nalluri nāṭṭutu meṉṟu
tāmiṉ puṟūun takimoḻi kēṭṭāṅku
iṭiyiruḷ yāmat teṟi tirip peruṅkaṭal
uṭikalap paṭṭa eṅkōṉ muṉṉāḷ
30		puṇṇiya tāṉam purintō ṉākaliṉ
naṇṇuvaḻi iṉṟi nāḷcila nīnta
intiraṉ ēvaliṉ īṅku vāḻvēṉ
vantēṉ añcal maṇimē kaliyāṉ
uṉperun tāṉat tuṟuti yoḻiyātu
35		tuṉpa nīṅkit tuyarkkaṭa loḻikeṉa
viñciyiṟ peyarttu viḻuman tīrtta
eṅkula teyvap peyarīṅ kiṭukeṉa
aṇimē kaliyār āyiraṅ kaṇikiyar
maṇimē kaliyeṉa vāḻttiya ñāṉṟu
40		maṅkala maṭanti mātavi taṉṉoṭu
cempoṉ māri ceṅkiyiṟ poḻiya
ñāṉa naṉṉeṟi nalvaram pāyōṉ
tāṉaṅ koḷḷun takimiyiṉ varuvōṉ
taḷarnta naṭiyil taṇṭukā lūṉṟi
45		vaḷinta yākki maṟiyōṉ ṟaṉṉip
pākukaḻin tiyāṅkaṇum paṟipaṭa varūum
vēka yāṉi vemmiyiṟ kikkoḷa
oyyeṉat teḻittāṅ kuyarpiṟap pāḷaṉik
kiyakat toḻittataṉ kiyakam pukkup
50		poyporu muṭaṅkuki veṇkōṭ ṭaṭaṅki
miyiruṅ kuṉṟiṉ viñciyaṉ ēyppap
piṭarttali iruntu peruñciṉam piṟaḻāk
kaṭakkaḷi ṟaṭakkiya karuṇi maṟava
piḷḷi nakulam perumpiṟi tāka
55		eḷḷiya maṉiyōḷ iṉintupiṉ cella
vaṭaticip peyaru māmaṟi yāḷaṉ
kaṭava taṉṟuniṉ kittūṇ vāḻkki
vaṭamoḻi vācakañ ceytanal lēṭu
kaṭaṉaṟi māntar kinnī koṭukkeṉap
60		pīṭikit teruviṟ peruṅkuṭi vāṇikar
māṭa maṟukiṉ maṉitoṟu maṟukik
karumak kaḻipalaṅ koṇmi ṉōveṉum
arumaṟi yāṭṭiyi aṇukak kūuy
yātunī yuṟṟa iṭarī teṉṉeṉa
65		mātartā ṉuṟṟa vāṉtuyar ceppi
ipporu ḷeḻutiya itaḻitu vāṅkik
kipporuḷ tanteṉ kaṭuntuyar kaḷikeṉa
añcal uṉṟaṉ aruntuyar kaḷikēṉ
neñcuṟu tuyaram nīṅkuka eṉṟāṅku
70	ōttuṭi antaṇar urinūṟ kiṭakkiyil
tīttiṟam purintōḷ ceytuyar nīṅkat
tāṉañ ceytavaḷ taṉṟuyar nīkkik
kāṉam pōṉa kaṇavaṉik kūṭṭi
olkāc celvat tuṟuporuḷ koṭuttu
75		nalvaḻip paṭutta cellāc celva
pattiṉi yorutti paṭiṟṟuri eyta
maṟṟavaḷ kaṇavaṟku vaṟiyōṉ oruvaṉ
aṟiyāk karipoyt taṟintuṇum pūtattuk
kaṟikeḻu pācattuk kiyakap paṭalum
80		paṭṭōṉ ṟavvi paṭutuyar kaṇṭu
kaṭṭiya pācattuk kaṭituceṉ ṟeyti
eṉṉuyir koṇṭīṅ kivaṉuyir tāveṉa
naṉṉeṭum pūtam nalkā tāki
naraka ṉuyirkku nalluyir koṇṭu
85		parakati yiḻakkum paṇpīṅ killi
oḻikaniṉ karutteṉa uyirmuṉ puṭippa
aḻitaru muḷḷat tavaḷoṭum pōntavaṉ
cuṟṟat tōrkkum toṭarpuṟu kiḷikaṭkum
paṟṟiya kiḷiñariṉ pacippiṇi yaṟuttup
90		pallāṇṭu puranta illōr cemmal
immic ceytaṉa yāṉaṟi nalviṉi
ummip payaṉkol orutaṉi yuḻantit
tiruttaku māmaṇik koḻuntuṭaṉ pōntatu
viruttakō pāla nīyeṉa viṉavak
95		kōvalaṉ kūṟumōr kuṟumakaṉ taṉṉāl
kāval vēntaṉ kaṭinakar taṉṉil
nāṟiṅ kūntal naṭuṅkutuya reytak
kūṟikōṭ paṭṭuk kōṭṭumā ūravum
aṇittaku purikuḻa lāyiḻi taṉṉoṭum
100		piṇippaṟut tōrtam peṟṟi yeytavum
māmalar vāḷi vaṟunilat teṟintu
kāmak kaṭavuḷ kiyaṟ ṟēṅka
aṇitikaḻ pōti aṟavōṉ ṟaṉmuṉ
maṇimē kaliyi mātavi yaḷippavum
105		naṉavu pōla naḷḷiruḷ yāmattuk
kaṉavu kaṇṭēṉ kaṭitīṅ kuṟumeṉa
aṟattuṟi mākkaṭ kalla tintap
puṟacciṟi yirukki poruntā tākaliṉ
aricar piṉṉōr akanakar maruṅkiṉiṉ
110	uriyiṟ koḷvariṅ koḻikaniṉ iruppuk
kātali taṉṉoṭu katircel vataṉmuṉ
māṭa maturi mānakar pukukeṉa
mātavat tāṭṭiyum māmaṟi mutalvaṉum
kōvalaṉ ṟaṉakkuk kūṟuṅ kāli
115		aṟampuri neñciṉ aṟavōr palkiya
puṟañciṟi mūtūrp pūṅkaṇ iyakkikkup
pāṉmaṭi koṭuttupa paṇpiṟ peyarvōḷ
āyar mutumakaḷ mātari eṉpōḷ
kāvunti aiyiyik kaṇṭaṭi toḻalum
120		ākāt tōmpi āppayaṉ aḷikkum
kōvalar vāḻkkiyōr koṭumpā ṭilli
tītilaḷ mutumakaḷ cevviyaḷ aḷiyaḷ
mātari taṉṉuṭaṉ maṭantiyi iruttutaṟku
ētam iṉṟeṉa eṇṇiṉa ḷāki
125		mātari kēḷim maṭantitaṉ kaṇavaṉ
tātiyik kēṭkil taṉkula vāṇar
arumporuḷ peṟunariṉ viruntetir koṇṭu
karuntaṭaṅ kaṇṇiyoṭu kaṭimaṉip paṭuttuvar
uṭipperuñ celvar maṉippuku maḷavum
130	iṭikkula maṭantik kaṭikkalan tantēṉ
maṅkala maṭantiyi naṉṉī rāṭṭic
ceṅkayal neṭuṅkaṇ añcaṉan tīṭṭit
tēmeṉ kūntaṟ ciṉmalar peytu
tūmaṭi uṭīit tollōr ciṟappiṉ
135		āyamum kāvalum āyiḻi taṉakkut
tāyum nīyē yākit tāṅkiṅku
eṉṉoṭu pōnta iḷaṅkoṭi naṅkitaṉ
vaṇṇac cīṟaṭi maṇmakaḷ aṟintilaḷ
kaṭuṅkatir vemmiyiṟ kātalaṉ ṟaṉakku
140		naṭuṅkutuya reyti nāppulara vāṭit
taṉtuyar kāṇāt takicāl pūṅkoṭi
iṉtuṇi makaḷirk kiṉṟi yamiyāk
kaṟpuk kaṭampūṇṭa itteyva mallatu
poṟpuṭit teyvam yāṅkaṇ ṭilamāl
145		vāṉam poyyātu vaḷampiḻip paṟiyātu
nīṇila vēntar koṟṟam citiyātu
pattiṉip peṇṭir iruntanā ṭeṉṉum
attaku nalluri aṟiyā  yōnī
tavattōr aṭikkalam tāṉciṟi tāyiṉum
150		mikappē riṉpam tarumatu kēḷāy
kāvirip paṭappip paṭṭiṉan taṉṉuḷ
pūviri piṇṭip potunīṅku tiruniḻal
ulaka nōṉpikaḷ oruṅkuṭaṉ iṭṭa
ilakoḷic cilātala mēlirun taruḷit
155	tarumañ cāṟṟuñ cāraṇar tammuṉ
tiruvil iṭṭut tikaḻtaru mēṉiyaṉ
tāraṉ māliyaṉ tamaṉiyap pūṇiṉaṉ
pārōr kāṇāp palartoḻu paṭimiyaṉ
karuviraṟ kuraṅkiṉ kiyoru pākattup
160		peruviṟal vāṉavaṉ vantuniṉ ṟōṉic
cāvaka rellām cāraṇart toḻutīṅku
yātivaṉ varaveṉa iṟiyōṉ kūṟum
eṭṭi cāyalaṉ iruntōṉ ṟaṉatu
paṭṭiṉi nōṉpikaḷ palarpuku maṉiyilōr
165		mātava mutalvaṉi maṉipperuṅ kiḻatti
ētam nīṅka etirkoḷ amayattu
ūrcciṟu kuraṅkoṉ ṟotuṅkiuḷ pukkup
pāṟpaṭu mātavaṉ pātam porunti
uṇṭoḻi miccilum ukutta nīrum
170		taṇṭā vēṭkiyiṉ tāṉciṟi tarunti
	etirmukam nōkkiya iṉpac cevviyi
atirāk koḷki aṟivaṉum nayantuniṉ
makkaḷiṉ ōmpu maṉikkiḻat tīyeṉa
mikkōṉ kūṟiya meymmoḻi ōmpik
175		kātaṟ kuraṅku kaṭinā ḷeytavum
tāṉañ ceyvuḻi ataṟkoru kūṟu
tītaṟu keṉṟē ceytaṉa ḷātaliṉ
mattima naṉṉāṭṭu vāraṇan taṉṉuḷ
uttara kuttaṟ korumaka ṉāki
180		uruviṉum tiruviṉum uṇarviṉun tōṉṟip
peruviṟal tāṉam palavuñ ceytāṅku
eṇṇāl āṇṭiṉ iṟantapiṟ pāṭu
viṇṇōr vaṭivam peṟṟaṉa ṉātaliṉ
peṟṟa celvap perumpaya ṉellām
185		taṟkāt taḷittōḷ tāṉac ciṟappeṉap
paṇṭip piṟappiṟ kuraṅkiṉ ciṟuki 
koṇṭoru pākattuk koḷkiyiṟ puṇarnta
cāyalaṉ maṉivi tāṉan taṉṉāl
āyiṉaṉ ivvaṭivu aṟimi ṉōveṉac
190		cāvakark kellām cāṟṟiṉaṉ kāṭṭat
tēva kumaraṉ tōṉṟiṉaṉ eṉṟalum
cāraṇar kūṟiya takicāl naṉmoḻi
āraṇaṅ kāka aṟantalip paṭṭōr
aṉṟap patiyuḷ aruntava mākkaḷum
195		taṉteṟal vāḻkkic cāvaka mākkaḷum
iṭṭa tāṉat teṭṭiyum maṉiviyum
muṭṭā iṉpattu muṭivula keytiṉar
kēṭṭaṉi yāyiṉit tōṭṭār kuḻaliyoṭu
nīṭṭit tirātu nīpō keṉṟē
200		kavunti kūṟa uvantaṉa ḷētti
vaḷariḷa vaṉamuli vāṅkamip paṇittōḷ
muḷiyiḷa veṇpal mutukkuṟi naṅkiyoṭu
ceṉṟa ñāyiṟṟuc celcuṭar amayattuk
kaṉṟutēr āviṉ kaṉikural iyampa
205	maṟittōḷ naviyattu uṟikkā vāḷaroṭu 
ceṟivaḷi āycciyar cilarpuṟañ cūḻa
miḷiyuṅ kiṭaṅkum vaḷiviṟ poṟiyum
karuvira lūkamum kallumiḻ kavaṇum
parivuṟu venneyum pākaṭu kuḻiciyum
210		kāypoṉ uliyum kalliṭu kūṭiyum 
tūṇṭilum toṭakkum āṇṭali aṭuppum
kaviyum kaḻuvum putiyum puḻiyum
aiyavit tulāmum kipeyar ūciyum
ceṉṟeṟi ciralum paṉṟiyum paṇiyum
215		eḻuvuñ cīppum muḻuviṟaṟ kaṇiyamum
kōlum kuntamum vēlum piṟavum
ñāyilum ciṟantu nāṭkoṭi nuṭaṅkum
vāyil kaḻintutaṉ maṉipuk kaṉaḷāl
kōvalar maṭanti koḷkiyiṟ puṇarnteṉ.
uri
1-8. 		nilam taru tiruviṉ niḻal vāy nēmi - nilattiṟkup pala celvattiṉiyum tarukiṉṟa aruḷ vāynta āṇiyi, kaṭam pūṇṭu uruṭṭum kuriyar - muṟimiyi mēṟkoṇṭu celuttum pāṇṭiyarkaḷuṭiya, perum cīrk kōliṉ cemmiyum - periya ciṟappiṉiyuṭiya ceṅkōlum, kuṭiyiṉ taṇmiyum - kuṭiyiṉatu taṇmiyum, vēliṉ koṟṟamum - vēliṉuṭiya veṟṟiyum, viḷaṅkiya koḷki - viḷaṅkutaṟku iṭamākiya kōṭpāṭṭiṉiyuṭiya, pati eḻuvu aṟiyāp paṇpu mēmpaṭṭa maturi mūtūr mā nakar kaṇṭu-āṇṭu vāḻvōr avviṭattiṉiṉṟum peyartali aṟiyāta muṟimi mēmpaṭṭa paḻiya ūrākiya maturi mā nakarattiṉik kaṇṭu, āṅku aṟantaru neñciṉ aṟavōr palkiya puṟañciṟi mūtūrp poḻil iṭam pukuntu - avviṭattu aṟattiṉip piṟarkkaṟivuṟuttum uḷḷattiṉiyuṭiya muṉivarkaḷ niṟinta puṟamatiṟkaṇ uḷḷa mūtūrc cōliyiṭattup pukuntu ;
maṉṉaṉ kuṭikaḷiṭattu aruḷuṭiyaṉ āyavaḻi nilattup pal vaḷamum perukumākaliṉ ''nilamtaru tiruviṉ niḻal" eṉṟār ; niḻal - aruḷ. māṟṟāratu nilattit tarum veṟṟiyākiya celvam eṉṟum, oḷi poruntiya nēmi yeṉṟum urittalumām. pati eḻuvaṟiyāmi āṇṭup palvaḷamum perukalāṉ eṉka. pati eṉpatu ākupeyarāy āṇṭuḷḷārik kuṟittu niṉṟateṉalum poruntum. 
9-10.	tītu tīr maturiyum teṉṉavaṉ koṟṟamum - kuṟṟam nīṅkiya maturiyiṉ ciṟappiṉiyum pāṇṭiyaṉatu koṟṟattiyum, mātavattāṭṭikkuk kōvalaṉ kūṟuḻi - kavuntiyaṭikaḷukkuk kōvalaṉ kūṟiya kāli ;
tītu - paci, piṇi, paki; im mūṉṟum aruḷvāytta kōliṉ cemmiyāṉum vēliṉ koṟṟattāṉum ilavāyiṉa eṉka. 
11-13. 	tāḻ nīr vēlit talicceṅkāṉattu - tāḻnta nīri vēliyākavuṭiya talic ceṅkāṭu eṉṉum ūriṭattuḷḷa, nāṉmaṟi muṟṟiya nalampuri koḷki-nāṉku vētaṅkaḷiyum muṟṟa uṇarnta naṉmiyi virumpiya kōṭpāṭṭiṉiyuṭiya, māmaṟi mutalvaṉ māṭalaṉ eṉpōṉ - maṟiyavar talivaṉākiya māṭalaṉ eṉappaṭuvōṉ;
tāḻtal - āḻamātal; taṅkutal eṉṟumām. tāḻnīr - kaṭal; kiṭaṅkumām. 
14-19.	mātava muṉivaṉ mali valaṅkoṇṭu-mikka tavattiṉi
yuṭiya akattiya muṉivaṉuṭiya potiya maliyi valaṅ
koṇṭu, kumariyam peruntuṟi koḷkiyiṟ paṭintu - kumariyiṉ periya tuṟikkaṇ muṟippaṭi nīrāṭi, tamarmutaṟ peyarvōṉ - tam kiḷiñar irukkumiṭattiṟku mīṇṭu
varuvōṉ, tāḻ poḻil āṅkaṇ vakuntu cel varuttattu vāṉ tuyar nīṅkak kavunti iṭavayiṉ pukuntōṉ taṉṉi - vaḻic ceṉṟa varuttattāṉ uṇṭāya mikka tuyaram oḻiya niḻal paṭinta cōliyuḷḷa avviṭattuk kavuntiyaṭikaḷirunta paḷḷiyiṭattup pukkavaṉi, kōvalaṉ ceṉṟu cēvaṭi vaṇaṅka - kōvalaṉ ceṉṟu cēvaṭikkaṇ toḻa;
kumari - yāṟu, kaṭalumām; 1''toṭiyōḷ puvam" eṉṟārākaliṉ. mutal - iṭam. vakuntu - vaḻi. iṭavayiṉ - iṭattil. tuyar nīṅkap poḻilāṅkaṇ iṭavayiṟ pukuntōṉ eṉka. pukuntāṉ ; pukuntavaṉi eṉa aṟutturikka. 
nā val antaṇaṉ tāṉ naviṉṟu urippōṉ - nāvil valla am māṭalaṉ avaṉi viṉavit tāṉ urikkiṉṟavaṉ ;
20.		nāval eṉpataṉip pirikkātu, nāvalan tīvu eṉṟum, veṟṟi eṉṟum kūṟutalumām. 2''nāva lantaṇa rarumaṟip poruḷē" eṉpatu kāṇka. tāṉaviṉṟu - viṉaviyeṉṉum poruṭṭu ; nīr iṅṅaṉam vanta kāraṇam yāteṉa viṉavi
yeṉka. 
21-27.	vēntu uṟu ciṟappiṉ viḻuccīr eytiya - aracaṉ aḷitta mikka talivariciyāl ciṟanta pukaḻiṉip peṟṟa, māntaḷir mēṉi mātavi maṭanti - māntaḷir pōlum mēṉiyiyuṭiya mātaviyākiya maṭanti, pāl vāyk kuḻavi payantaṉaḷ eṭuttu- naṟpakuti vāyttaliyuṭiya peṇ kuḻantiyiṉip peṟṟeṭuttu, vālāmi nāḷ nīṅkiya piṉṉar - tūymiyiṉmiyākiya nāṭkaḷ kaḻinta piṉṉar, mā mutu kaṇikiyar - āṇṭil mutirnta kaṇiki makaḷir, mātavi makaṭku nāma nal uri nāṭṭutum eṉṟu - mātaviyiṉ putalvikku nalla pukaḻ aminta peyari iṭuvōm eṉṟu, tām iṉpu uṟūum taki moḻi kēṭṭu āṅku - tāṅkaḷ makiḻcciyuṟutaṟkuk kāraṇamākiya takuti aminta colliṉikkūṟak kēṭṭu appoḻutu;
