‘vēntuṟu ciṟappiṉ viḻuccīr eytiya' eṉṟatu aracaṉāl talikkōl peṟṟu āyirattu eṇkaḻañcu poṉ peṟṟataṉi. perumpulavōr oru nikaḻcciyiṉik kūṟuṅkāl, an nikaḻcciyiṉ mēl viḷiviṉiyum āṇṭē pulappaṭuttal iyalpākalāṉ, `pāl vāykkuḻavi' eṉṟār; pāl - pakuti ; nallūḻ; pālvāy eṉpatu kuḻavikku iyaṟkiyaṭi eṉṟalumām. vālāmi - puṉiṟu tīrāmi. mutukaṇikiyar - cittirāpati mutaliyōr. muṉṉark kuḻavi eṉṟa potumi nīṅka makaṭku eṉṟār. nalluri nāmam eṉka. kēṭṭāṉ kōvalaṉ.  
28-37. 	(muṉnāḷ) iṭi iruḷ yāmattu eṟitirip peruṅ kaṭal - muṉṉāḷil iruḷ niṟinta naṭuyāmattil ali mōtum periya kaṭaliṉiṭattu, uṭikalappaṭṭa eṅkōṉ- marakkalam uṭiyappaṭṭa em muṉṉōṉ, muṉnāḷ puṇṇiya tāṉam purintōṉ ākaliṉ - muṟpiṟappil aṟattiṉiyum tāṉattiṉiyum ceytōṉākalāṉ, naṇṇuvaḻi iṉṟi nāḷ cila nīnta kariyik kiṭṭum iṭamiṉṟiyē ciṉṉāṭkaḷ nīntic cella, intiraṉ ēvaliṉ īṅku vāḻvēṉ vantēṉ añcal maṇimēkali yāṉ - ōr teyvam tōṉṟi, yāṉ maṇimēkali eṉappaṭuvēṉ it tīviṉuḷḷāri acurar naliyātiruttaṟ poruṭṭu intiraṉ ēvalāl īṅku vāḻuvēṉ av vintiraṉ ēvalāṉ niṉ tuyar oḻikka ivaṇ vantēṉ añcaṟka, uṉ perun tāṉattu uṟuti oḻiyātu - niṉatu periya tāṉattiṉ payaṉ niṉṉiviṭṭu nīṅkātu, tuṉpam nīṅkit tuyarkkaṭal oḻikeṉa - ākalāṉ kaṭalil nīntiya ittuṉ pattiṉiṉṟum nīṅkit tuyarkkaṭali oḻivāyākaveṉṟu kūṟi, viñciyil peyarttu viḻumam tīrtta - mantirattiṉāl kariyi mīṇṭum aṭiyac ceytu tuṉpam oḻitta, em kulateyvappeyar īṅku iṭukeṉa - ematu kulateyvattiṉ peyari iṅku nīvir iṭumiṉ eṉṟu nī colla;
yāmam - iravu; 1'yāmamum pakalu maṟiyā miyāl' eṉṟār piṟarum. muṉṉāḷ eṉpataṉi uṭikalappaṭṭa eṉpataṉoṭum, puṇṇiyatāṉam eṉpataṉoṭum kūṭṭuka. puṇṇiyatāṉam - puṇṇiyamum tāṉamum. naṇṇuvaḻi iṉṟi eṉpataṟkut taṉṉi aṇukum iṭiyūṟu ōriṭattumiṉṟi eṉalumām. nāḷ cila nīnta-ēḻu nāṭkaḷ nīnta ; (maṇi 29 : 18-9. vari kāṇka.) intiraṉ ēvaliṉ vāḻvēṉ intiraṉ ēvaliṉ vantēṉ eṉka. uṟuti - payaṉ. peyarttu - oru tiṭariṟ peyarttu eṉavu murippar.
vāḻvēṉ vantēṉ añcal uṟuti oḻiyātu nīṅki oḻikeṉap peyarttut tīrtta kulateyvam eṉka. 
38-41. 	aṇimēkaliyār āyiraṅ kaṇikiyar - aḻaku ceyyum mēkaliyi aṇinta āyiraṅ kaṇikiyar kūṭi, maṇimēkali eṉa vāḻttiya ñāṉṟu - maṇimēkali eṉap peyar iṭṭu ak kuḻaviyi vāḻttiya aṟṟi nāḷ, maṅkala maṭanti mātavi taṉṉōṭu - aḻakiṉi yuṭiya maṭantiyākiya mātaviyōṭu, cempoṉ māri ceṅkiyil poḻiya - civanta poṉṉākiya maḻiyiṉic civanta kiyālē nī poḻiya;
āyiram - eṇṇiṉ mikuti kuṟippatu. vāḻttiya - aracaṉiyum nakariṉiyum vāḻttiya eṉṟumām. 
42-47. 	ñāṉa nalneṟi nal varampu āyōṉ - vīṭu cēṟaṟku nalla neṟiyākiya ñāṉattiṟkuc ciṟanta elliyāyuḷḷōṉ, tāṉam koḷḷum takimiyiṉ varuvōṉ taḷarntanaṭiyil taṇṭukāl ūṉṟi - tāṉattip peṟum takutiyōṭu taḷarnta naṭiyuṭaṉ ūṉṟukōliyē kālāka ūṉṟi varuvōṉākiya, vaḷinta yākki maṟiyōṉ taṉṉi - kūṉiya uṭampiṉi yuṭiya antaṇaṉi, pāku kaḻintu yāṅkaṇum paṟi paṭa varūum vēka yāṉi - pākar ceyalik kaṭantu evviṭattum paṟi koṭṭa varukiṉṟa matavēkattiṉiyuṭiya yāṉi, vemmi yil kikkoḷa - ciṉattāl paṟṟikkoṇṭatāka ;
2"taḷarnta naṭiyiṟ ṟaṇṭukāl ūṉṟi, vaḷinta yāk kiyōr maṟiyōṉ" eṉpar maṇimēkaliyiṉum, pāku - pākar. kaḻintu eṉṟatu avarkku aṭaṅkāmi kūṟiṟṟu. paṟipaṭutal makkaḷ aṟintu kāttaṟku.
 poḻiyak koḷḷum takimiyiṉ varuvōṉākiya maṟi yōṉ taṉṉi yāṉi kikkoḷa eṉka. 
48-53.	oy eṉat teḻittu āṅku uyarpiṟappāḷaṉi - appoḻutu virivil urappi am maṟiyōṉi, ki akattu oḻittu - yāṉiyiṉ kiyiṉiṉṟum viṭuvittu, ki akam puk kup poy poru muṭaṅku ki veṇkōṭṭu aṭaṅki - tāṉ av viyāṉiyiṉ puri poruntiya kiyiṉiṭattup pukuntu pōr ceyyum vaḷitaliyuṭiya veḷḷiya kōṭṭiṉkaṇ aṭaṅki, mi iruṅ kuṉṟiṉ viñciyaṉ ēyppap piṭarttali iruntu - kariya periya kuṉṟiṉkaṇ irunta vittiyātaraṉip pōla ataṉ piṭariṉiṭattiruntu, peruñciṉam piṟaḻāk kaṭakkaḷiṟu aṭakkiya karuṇi maṟava - mikka ciṉam nīṅkāta kaḷiṟṟiṉ matattiṉi yaṭakkiya aruḷvīraṉē;
oy - yāṉiyip pākar viyum āriya moḻiyumām. poyk kiyakam eṉavum poruveṇkōṭu eṉavum iyikka. iṉi, poyporu muṭaṅkuki eṉpataṟkup puri poruntiya vaḷinta ki eṉpatum poruntum. piṟaḻtal - nīṅkutal ; 1"moykoḷap piṟaḻntu muttār maruppiṭik kuḷittu" eṉṟār piṟarum. kaṭak kaḷiṟu aṭakkiya - matattiyuṭiya yāṉiyi aṭakkiya eṉalumām. maṟiyōṟ keytum tuyariṉip poṟāmiyāṉum, taṉṉuyirk kañcātu pāyntu aṭakkutalāṉum karuṇi maṟavaṉ eṉṟāṉ. 
54-75. 	piḷḷi nakulam perum piṟitu āka - tam piḷḷiyik kātta kīri taṉ maṉivi aṭittami kāraṇamāka iṟantamiyāṉ, eḷḷiya maṉiyōḷ iṉintupiṉ cella vaṭaticip peyarum mā maṟiyāḷaṉ - vaṭakkaṇ kaṅkiyāṭac cellum maṟiyōṉ ak kōṟal kāraṇamāka ikaḻntu otukkiya maṉivi varuntit taṉ piṉṉē varalāṉ, kaṭavatu aṉṟu niṉ kittu ūṇ vāḻkki - niṉatu kiyiṭattatākiya uṇavi uṇṭu vāḻum vāḻkki iṉi muṟimiyuṭiyatu aṉṟu, vaṭamoḻi vācakam ceyta nal ēṭu - ākalāṉ vaṭamoḻi vācakam eḻutiya nalla ivvēṭṭiṉi, kaṭaṉ aṟi māntar ki nī koṭukkeṉa - uṇarattaku poruḷi yuṇarum makkaḷ kiyakattu nī koṭukkaveṉac collip pōka, pīṭikit teruviṉ peruṅkuṭi vāṇikar māṭamaṟukiṉ maṉi toṟum maṟuki - kaṭivītiyiṉum peruṅkuṭi vaṇikar vāḻum māṭaṅkaḷ niṟinta maṟukilum ēṉiyōr illaṅkaḷilum cuḻaṉṟu tirintu, karumak kaḻipalam koṇmiṉō eṉum aru maṟiyāṭṭiyi - pāvattiṉip pōkkum payaṉik koḷḷuṅ kaḷ eṉṟu kūṟic cellum pārppaṉiyi, aṇukak kūy - aṇmiyil varum vaṇṇam aḻittu, yātu nī uṟṟa iṭar ītu eṉ eṉa - nī yaṭinta tuṉpam yātu ik kiyiṉkaṇ ēṭu yātu eṉṟu kēṭpa, mātar tāṉ uṟṟa vāṉ tuyar ceppi - am mātu tāṉ aṭinta mikka tuṉpattiṉik kūṟi, ip poruḷ eḻutiya itaḻ itu vāṅki - ic ceyyuḷ eḻutiya iv vēṭ ṭiṉip peṟṟu, kipporuḷ tantu eṉ kaṭuntuyar kaḷi keṉa - kipporuḷik koṭuttu eṉatu koṭiya tuyarat tiṉi oḻippāyāka veṉṟu kūṟa, añcal uṉṟaṉ aruntuyar kaḷikēṉ - añcaṟka uṉatu poṟuttaṟkariya tuṉpattiṉi yāṉ pōkkuvēṉ, neñcuṟu tuyaram nīṅkuka eṉṟu - uṉ uḷḷattup poruntum iṭumpiyi nīkkuka eṉṟu colli, āṅku ōttuṭi antaṇar urinūl kiṭakkiyiṉ - appoḻutē vētattiṉiyuṭiya muṉivarkaḷ kūṟiya aṟanūṉ muṟiyāṉē, tīttiṟam purintōḷ ceytuyar nīṅka - kolit toḻil purintavaḷākiya ap pārppaṉi ceyta pāvam oḻiyum vaṇṇam, tāṉam ceytu avaḷtaṉ tuyar nīkki - tāṉattic ceytu avaḷuṭiya tuṉpattiṉip pōkki, kāṉam pōṉa kaṇavaṉik kūṭṭi - kāṭṭu neṟikkaṇ ceṉṟa avaḷ kaṇavaṉiyum cērttu, olkāc celvattu uṟuporuḷ koṭuttu - taḷarāta celvamuṭimiyāṉ mikka poruḷiṉit tantu, nalvaḻippaṭutta cellāc celva - naṉṉeṟip paṭuttiya toliyāta celvamuṭiyāṉē;
piḷḷi nakulam - kīrikkuṭṭi. `piḷḷi nakulam perumpiṟi tāka' eṉṟatu pārppaṉiyiṉ piḷḷiyik kāttirunta kīri avaṇ pām poṉṟu vara ataṉik koṉṟu utiram paṭinta vāyuṭaṉē etirvanta taṉik kaṇṭa ap pārppaṉi ak kīri taṉ kuḻantiyik koṉṟa teṉak karuti ataṉik koṉṟaṉaḷ eṉṉuṅ kati kuṟittu niṉṟatu. perumpiṟitu - iṟattal. kittūṇ vāḻkki - oḻukkattiṉ uṇṭu vāḻum vāḻkki eṉalumām. vācakam - ceyyuḷ : atā vatu :-"aparīkṣya nakarttavyaṅ karttavyam suparīkṣitam, pac cātpavati santāpam prāhmaṇī nakulamyatā" eṉpatākum, kaṭa ṉaṟimāntar eṉṟatu makkaṭ piṟappiṉ muṟimiyi uṇarum makkaḷ eṉṟavāṟu. koṭukkeṉa eṉpataṉ piṉṉarp pōkaveṉa oru colvaruvikka. teruviṉum maṟukiṉum uḷḷa maṉi eṉalum poruntum. karumak kaḻipalaṅ koṇmiṉ eṉṟatu kolip pāvam oḻiyap pirinta kaṇavaṉ varum vaṇṇam tāṉam ceytaṟkup poruḷ aḷittali ēṟṟuk koṇmiṉ eṉṟavāṟu. `ip poruḷ' eṉṟatu poruṭkiṭamākiya ceyyuḷi. ōttuṭi antaṇar - tarumācaṉattōr eṉka. ‘tuyar nīṅka' eṉṟatu tuyarattiṟkuk kāraṇamāya pāvam nīṅka eṉṟapaṭi. 
76-90. 	pattiṉi orutti paṭiṟṟu uri eyta - ōr kaṟpuṭi makaḷ poyp paḻiyiṉi eytutaṟku, maṟṟu avaḷ kaṇavaṟku vaṟiyōṉ oruvaṉ aṟiyāk kari poyttu - avaḷuṭiya kaṇavaṉukku aṟivilāṉ oruvaṉ kaṇṭu teḷiyāta cāṉṟu kūṟip poyttalāṉ, aṟintu uṇum pūtattuk kaṟikeḻu pācattuk ki akappaṭalum - tava maṟintu oḻukuvōr mutaliya aṟuvakiyōriyum puṭittuṇṇum pūtattiṉuṭiya kariya pācattiṉiṭattu akappaṭa, paṭṭōṉ tavvi paṭutuyar kaṇṭu - aṅṅaṉam akappaṭṭōṉuṭiya tāy paṭum tuṉpattiṉi nōkki, kaṭṭiya pācattuk kaṭitu ceṉṟu eyti - avaṉik kaṭṭiya pācattiṉuḷ tāṉ virintu ceṉṟu aṭintu, eṉ uyirkoṇṭu īṅku ivaṉ uyir tā eṉa - ippoḻutu eṉṉuṭiya uyiri nī peṟṟukkoṇṭu ivaṉuṭiya uyirit tantiṭuvāy eṉṟu kēṭpa, nal neṭum pūtam nalkātu āki - nalla periya pūtam avaṉuyirik koṭātāy, narakaṉ uyirkku nal uyir koṇṭu parakati iḻakkum paṇpu īṅku illi - kīḻōṉuṭiya uyiriṉ poruṭṭu nalla uyiriṉik koṇṭu mēlāṉa niliyi iḻakkun taṉmi eṉṉiṭattu illi, oḻika niṉ karuttu eṉa uyir muṉ puṭippa - ātalāl niṉ eṇṇattiṉi oḻippāyāka eṉṟu kūṟi avaṉit taṉ etirē puṭittu uṇṇa, aḻitarum uḷḷattu avaḷoṭum pōntu - varuntiya uḷḷattiṉiyuṭiya avaḷoṭum mīṇṭu ceṉṟu, avaṉ cuṟṟattōrkkum toṭarpu uṟu kiḷikaṭkum - avaṉuṭiya cuṟṟattārkkum toṭarpu koṇṭa kiḷiñarkkum, paṟṟiya kiḷiñaril pacippiṇi aṟuttu-aṉpāṟ piṇitta niṉatu cuṟṟattārip pōlak karutip paciyākiya nōyik keṭuttu, pal āṇṭu puranta illōr cemmal - pala yāṇṭukaḷ kātta vaṟiyōr talivaṉē;
paṭiṟu - poy, vaṟiyāṉ - poruḷilāṉ eṉak koṇṭu poruḷ peṟukikkākap poykkari kūṟiṉāṉ eṉṟalum ami yum. kaṇṭu teḷinta kūṟalē uṇmikkariyākalāṉ, aṟiyāk kari poykkariyāyiṟṟu. nallōrkkut tīṅku ceyyāmiyāṉ, `naṉṉeṭum pūtam' eṉappaṭṭatu. tavamaṟin toḻukuvōr mutaliyōrip pūtam puṭittuṇṇutali, 1"tavamaṟin toḻu kum.........kippaṭuvōr" eṉa varutalāṉaṟika. uyir - uṭal ; ākupeyar. illōr cemmal - illaṟattōr talivaṉ eṉa lumām. narakaṉ - narakamaṭitaṟkuriyāṉ ; pāvi.
kōvalaṉ vaṇaṅka vaṇaṅkiyōṉik karuṇimaṟava, cellāc celva, illōr cemmal eṉa nāvalantaṇaṉ pukaḻntaṉaṉ eṉka. 
91-94. 	virutta kōpāla - aṟivāl mutirnta kōpālaṉē, nī immic ceytaṉa yāṉ aṟi nalviṉi - yāṉ aṟiya ip piṟappiṉkaṇ nī ceytaṉa yāvum nalviṉiyē yākavum, ummip payaṉkol oru taṉi uḻantu it tiruttaku māmaṇik koḻuntuṭaṉ pōntatu - oppaṟṟa taṉimiyāṉ varunti it tiruviṉi otta māṇikkat taḷiruṭaṉ ivaṇ pukuntatu muṟpiṟappiṟ ceyta tīviṉiyiṉ payaṉēyō, eṉa viṉava - eṉṟu māṭalaṉ kēṭka ;
orutaṉi uḻattal - ucāviṉṟi varuntutal. māmaṇi - muḻu māṇikkam. kaṇṇakiyiṉ iḷami karutik koḻuntu eṉṟār. viruttaṉ - ñāṉa viruttaṉ eṉal marapu. kōpāla - kōvala. kol - aiyam.
kōpāla nī immic ceytaṉa nalviṉi uḻantu pōntatu ummip payaṉkol eṉa māṟuka.  
95-106. 	kōvalaṉ kūṟum - kōvalaṉ kūṟuvāṉ : ōr kuṟu makaṉ taṉṉāl - oru kīḻōṉāl, kāval vēntaṉ kaṭinakar taṉṉil - purattalil valla pāṇṭiya maṉṉaṉuṭiya kāvaliyuṭiya im maturi nakarattiṉkaṇ, nāṟu aiṅ kūntal naṭuṅku tuyar eyta - maṇanāṟum aintu pakutiyāka muṭikkappaṭum kūntali yuṭiya ivaḷ kaṇṭār naṭuṅkattakka tuṉpattiṉi aṭiya, kūṟi kōḷpaṭṭuk kōṭṭu mā ūravum - uṭutta āṭi piṟarāṟ koḷḷappaṭṭu yāṉ paṉṟimītu ēṟic celuttavum, aṇittaku purikuḻal āyiḻi taṉṉoṭum - aḻaku takka kaṭi kuḻaṉṟa kūntaliṉiyum ārāynta iḻiyiṉiyum uṭiya ivaḷōṭu, piṇippu aṟuttōrtam peṟṟi eytavum - paṟṟiṉi yaṟutta cāṉṟōr peṟum pēṟṟiṉi yāṉ peṟavum, mā malar vāḷi vaṟunilattu eṟintu - malar ampukaḷi veṟunilattil vīci, kāmak kaṭavuḷ kiyaṟṟu ēṅka - maṉmataṉ ceyalaṟṟu ēkkaṅ koḷḷum vaṇṇam, aṇitikaḻ pōti aṟavōṉ taṉmuṉ - aḻaku viḷaṅkum pōtikkaṇ aṟavōṉākiya puttaṉiṭattu, maṇimēkaliyi mātavi aḷippavum - maṇimēkaliyi mātavi koṭukkavum, naṉavu pōla naḷ iruḷ yāmattu - naṉaviṉiṟ pōlac ceṟinta iruḷiṉiyuṭiya kaṭi yāmattiṉkaṇ, kaṉavu kaṇṭēṉ - kaṉāk kaṇṭēṉ, kaṭitu īṅku uṟum eṉa - āka lāṉ pollāṅku oṉṟu ippoḻutē vantu cērum eṉṟu colla;
kuṟumi - kīḻmi yeṉṉumporuṭṭu. kāval vēntaṉ eṉa vum kaṭinakar eṉavum kūṟiyatu tuyareytalākāviṭattilē tuya reyta eṉṉum poruḷ tōṉṟa. kōṭṭu mā - erumikkaṭāvu mām. piṇippaṟuttōr peṟṟiyāvatu tuṟakkamaṭital. naṉavu - viḻippu. naḷiyeṉṉum uriccol īṟu tirintatu. kaṭitu uṟum - kaṭiyāmattuk kaṉavātaliṉ kaṭitil uṟum eṉṉalumām. kaṭi tīṅku eṉap pirittu mikka tīṅku, accattiyuṭiya tīṅku eṉavumām.
paṉṟiyiyum erumikkaṭāviyum ūrtal kāṇiṉ tīṅkuṇ ṭām eṉpatum kaṭiyāmattiṟ kāṇiṉ virivil uṟum eṉpatum,
1"	kaḷiṟumēl koḷḷavum 
kaṉavi ṉariyaṉa kāṇā"
2"collat takumukaṭ ṭoṭṭakam veṭṭun tuṇimaruppār 
illat terumi kaḻutika ḷeṉṟivi yēṟiniṉṟē
mellat tariyi liḻivataṉ muṉṉam viḻittiṭumēl 
kollat talivaru māṟṟaruñ cīṟṟattuk kūṟṟuvaṉē"
3"paṭittamuṟ cāmamō rāṇṭiṟ palikkum pakariraṇṭē 
kiṭittapiṟ cāma mikutiṅka ḷeṭṭiṟ kiṭikku meṉṟum
iṭippaṭṭa cāmamōr mūṉṟiṉiṟ ṟiṅkaḷor mūṉṟeṉpavāl 
kaṭippaṭṭa cāmamu nāḷpattu ḷēpalaṅ kipeṟumē"
eṉpavaṟṟāṉ aṟiyappaṭum. 
107-114. 	aṟattuṟi mākkaṭku allatu - tuṟavaṟattuṟi yiṉkaṇ niṟku muṉivarkaḷukkallatu, intap puṟacciṟi irukki poruntātu ākaliṉ - iv veyiṟpuṟattup paḷḷi yil irukkum irukki ovvātu ākalāṉum, aricar piṉ ṉōr akanakar maruṅkiṉ niṉ uriyiṟ koḷvar - iv vaka nakariṭattu uḷḷa vaṇikar niṉatu pukaḻāl niṉṉi aṟintu etirēṟṟukkoḷvar ākalāṉum, iṅku oḻika niṉ iruppu - ivviṭattu niṉatu irukkiyi oḻivāyāka, kātali taṉṉoṭu katir celvataṉmuṉ māṭa maturi mā nakar pukukeṉa - ñāyiṟu mēṟṟici ceṉṟu vīḻvataṉ muṉṉar niṉ maṉivi yōṭu maṭāṅkaḷiyuṭiya maturi nakarattil pukukaveṉṟu, mātavattāṭṭiyum māmaṟi mutalvaṉum kōvalaṉ taṉakkuk kūṟuṅkāli - kavuntiyaṭikaḷum maṟiyōṉākiya māṭalaṉum kōvalaṉukkuk kūṟumpōtu;
aṟattuṟi - aṟattiṉkaṇ uṟiyum eṉalumām. puṟacciṟi irukki - eyiṟpuṟattup paḷḷi. aricar piṉṉōr - nālvakik kulattu aracarkkuppiṉ eṇṇappaṭupavarākiya vaṇikar. akanakar maruṅkiṉ aricar piṉṉōr eṉka. kōvalaṉ 1'maṇtēytta pukaḻiṉāṉ' ākalāṉ `niṉ uriyiṟ koḷvar' eṉṟār. iṉi, niṉ uriyiṟ koḷvar eṉpataṟku, mācāttuvāṉ makaṉ eṉṉum pukaḻāṟ koḷvar eṉalumām. 
115-119. 	aṟampuri neñciṉ aṟavōr palkiya - aṟatti virumpiya uḷḷattiṉiyuṭiya muṉivarkaḷ niṟinta, puṟañ ciṟi mūtūrp pūṅkaṇ iyakkikku - eyiṟpuṟattu mūtūrk kaṇ eḻuntaruḷiya pūppōlum kaṇkaḷiyuṭiya iyakkikku, pālmaṭi koṭuttup paṇpiṟ peyarvōḷ - muṟiyāṉē pāṟcōṟu paṭittu mīḷvōḷākiya, āyar mutumakaḷ mātari eṉpōḷ - iṭiyar kuṭip piṟanta mutiyōḷ mātari eṉappaṭupavaḷ, kavuntiyiyiyik kaṇṭu aṭi toḻalum - kavuntiyaṭi
kaḷik kaṇṭu avaruṭiya aṭikaḷi vaṇaṅkutalum;
aṟavōr palkiya mūtūrkkaṇ iyakki eṉka. iyakki - oru peṇ teyvam; pāṇṭi nāṭṭil icakkiyeṉa vaḻaṅkum; āriyāṅkaṉi yeṉavum kūṟuvar; āriyāṅkaṉi - kaṇavaṉirukkum poḻutē tuṟavu pūṇṭa tavappeṇ. paṇpiṟ pāl maṭi koṭuttu eṉa māṟuka. iṉi, puṟañciṟi mūtūrkkaṇ kāvuntiyiyi eṉavum iyippar. 
120-124.	ā kāttu ōmpi āp payaṉ aḷikkum - pacukkaḷip piṇi mutaliyavaṟṟiṉiṉṟum kāppāṟṟip pul nīr mutaliya aḷittup pēṇi ap pacuviṉ payaṉi yāvarkkum koṭukkiṉṟa, kōvalar vāḻkki ōr koṭumpāṭu illi - iṭiyarkaḷuṭiya vāḻkkiyil oru ciṟitum tīmi illi, tītu ilaḷ - ākaliṉ kuṟṟamillātavaḷ, mutumakaḷ cevviyaḷ aḷiyaḷ - mēlum ivaḷ mutiyōḷum uṭkōṭṭa millātavaḷum taṇṇaḷiyuṭiyāḷumāvaḷ, mātari taṉṉuṭaṉ maṭantiyi iruttutaṟku ētam iṉṟu eṉa eṇṇiṉaḷ āki - ākalāṉ im mātari yiṭattuk kaṇṇakiyi vippataṉāl kuṟṟam oṉṟum iṉṟām eṉak karutiyavaḷāy;
piṇi mutaliya uṇṭāyavaḻi avaṟṟi nīkkutalik kāttu eṉavum, pul aruttal mutaliyavaṟṟi ōmpi eṉavum kūṟiṉār. āp payaṉ - pāl mutaliyaṉa. aḷiyaḷ - aḷikkattakkāḷ eṉalum poruntum koṭumpāṭu - koṭumi. 
125-130. 	mātari kēḷ - mātariyē kēṭpāyāka, immaṭanti taṉ kaṇavaṉ tātiyik kēṭkiṉ taṉ kulavāṇar - ip peṇṇiṉ kaṇavaṉuṭiya tantiyiṉ peyarik kēṭpārāyiṉ avaṉ kulattup piṟanta in nakarattu vāḻvōr, arum poruḷ peṟunariṉ viruntu etir koṇṭu - peṟutaṟkariya celvattiṉip peṟṟār pōṉṟu tamatu viruntiṉarāka etir koṇṭu aḻittu, karuntaṭaṅ kaṇṇiyoṭu kaṭimaṉip paṭuttuvar - ik kariya periya kaṇkaḷiyuṭiyāḷoṭu tamatu kāvaliyuṭiya illattiṉkaṇ vittuk koḷvar, uṭip peruñ celva maṉippukum aḷavum - aṅṅaṉam ap peruñ celva muṭiyār illattiṉkaṇ pukum variyum, iṭikkula maṭantikku aṭikkalam tantēṉ - iṭiyarkulap peṇṇākiya niṉakku ivaḷi aṭikkalamākak koṭuttēṉ; 
tātiyi - tantiyiṉ peyari ; eṉṟatu mācāttuvāṉ makaṉ ivaṉ eṉpataṉik kēṭkiṉ eṉṟavāṟu. uṭipperuñ celvar - peruñ celvamuṭiyār; uṭiya periya celvar eṉalumām. 1"uṭip peruñ celvarum" eṉṟār piṟarum. iṭikkula maṭanti - muṉṉiliyiṟ paṭarkki vanta vaḻuvamiti. 
131-136.	maṅkala maṭantiyi nal nīr āṭṭi - aḻakiya ik kaṇṇakiyit tūya nīrāṟ kuḷippāṭṭi, ceṅkayal neṭuṅkaṇ añcaṉam tīṭṭi - civanta kayal pōlum neṭiya kaṇ
kaḷil mi eḻuti, tē meṉ kūntal cil malar peytu - tēṉ poruntiya melliya kūntaṟkaṇ cilavākiya malarkaḷic cūṭi, tū maṭi uṭīi - tūya puṭiviyi uṭuttu, tollōr ciṟappiṉ āyamum kāvalum āyiḻi taṉakkut tāyum nīyē ākit tāṅku - ārāynta kalaṉ aṇinta ivaḷukku muṉṉōr kūṟiya ciṟappiṉi yuṭiya tōḻimārum kāvaṟ peṇṭum naṟṟāyum nīyēyākik kāppāṟṟuvāyāka ;
maṅkala naṉṉīr eṉa iyippiṉu mamiyum. tollōr ciṟappiṉ āyam - paḻiyōrākac ciṟappittuk kūṟappaṭṭa āyam eṉalumām. kāval - cevilit tāyar.
maṭantiyi āṭṭit tīṭṭip peytu uṭīit tāṅku eṉka.  
136-138. 	iṅku eṉṉoṭu pōnta iḷaṅkoṭi naṅki taṉ vaṇṇac cīṟaṭi maṇmakaḷ aṟintilaḷ - ivviṭattu eṉṉōṭu kūṭi vanta iḷaṅkoṭi pōlum ik kaṇṇakiyiṉuṭiya aḻakiya ciṟiya aṭikaḷi muṉṉar nilamakaḷum kaṇṭaṟiyāḷ.
aṭiyi maṇ makaḷ aṟintilaḷ eṉṟatu ivaḷ akam viṭṭup puṟampōkāmiyāṉ eṉka, iṉi, ivaḷuṭiya aṭiyiṉ meṉmiyi uṇarntu ataṟkēṟpa maṇmakaḷ tāṉum meṉmiyi aṭintāḷilaḷ eṉavum, kālkaḷ koppuḷaṅkoṇṭu nilattiṟ pāvāmiyiṉ avaṟṟik kaṇṭilaḷ eṉavum poruḷ koḷḷak kiṭantamiyum kāṇka. 
139-148. 	kaṭuṅ katir vemmiyiṉ kātalaṉ taṉakku - ñāyiṟṟiṉ koṭiya vemmiyiṉāl tuṉpamuṟṟa taṉ kaṇavaṉ poruṭṭu, naṭuṅku tuyar eyti nāp pulara vāṭi - kaṇṭār naṭuṅkattakka tuyaratti aṭintu nāvum pulara vāṭṭamuṟṟu, taṉ tuyar kāṇāt taki cāl pūṅkoṭi - taṉatu vaḻi naṭit tuṉpattiṉic ciṟitum uṇarāta takuti mikka pūṅkoṭipōl vāḷākiya, iṉ tuṇi makaḷirkku iṉṟiyamiyā - tam kaṇavarkku iṉiya tuṇiyākap poruntiya peṇkaḷukku iṉṟiyamiyāta, kaṟpuk kaṭam pūṇṭa it teyvam allatu - kaṟpākiya kaṭaṉi mēṟkoṇṭa it teyvamē yallātu, poṟpu uṭit teyvam yām kaṇṭilamāl - vēṟu poli
viṉiyuṭiya teyvam oṉṟiṉi yām kāṇēm, vāṉam poyyātu vaḷam piḻippu aṟiyātu - paruva maḻi peytaliṉum tavaṟātu nila vaḷamum piḻiyātu, nīḷ nila vēntar koṟṟam citi yātu - periya nilap parappiṉi āḷum maṉṉaratu veṟṟiyum aḻivuṟātu, pattiṉip peṇṭir irunta nāṭu - kaṟpuṭi makaḷir vāḻum nāṭṭiṉkaṇ, eṉṉum at taku nal uri aṟiyāyō nī - eṉṟu periyōr kūṟum at takuti vāynta nalla moḻiyi nī uṇarāyō ;
vemmiyiṉāl tuṉpuṟṟa eṉa oru col varuvikka. kātalaṉ tuyarkaṇṭu taṉ tuyar kāṇāḷāyiṉāḷ, kaṟpuk kaṭam pūṇṭōḷākalāṉ ; eṉṉi? 1"ōṅkal veṟpa, orunāḷ viḻuma muṟiṉum vaḻināḷ, vāḻkuva ḷalla ḷeṉṟōḻi" eṉavum kūṟuvarākalāṉ. pūṅkoṭiyākiya teyvam kaṟpuk kaṭam pūṇṭa teyvam eṉka. kaṟpuk kaṭam pūṇṭa pūṅkoṭiyākiya teyvam eṉiṉum amiyum. irunta nāṭṭiṉkaṇ poyyātu aṟiyātu citiyātu eṉka. iṉi, nāṭu poyyātu, aṟiyātu, citiyātu eṉa muṭittalumām. 
149-150.	tavattōr aṭikkalam tāṉ ciṟitu āyiṉum - tavamuṭiyōr tarum aṭikkalap poruḷik kākkum kāval eḷitāyiṉum, mikap pēr iṉpam tarum atu kēḷāy - mikap periya iṉpattiṉi aḷikkum ataṉi nī'a6 kēṭpāyāka ;
aṭikkalam - aṭikkalap poruḷip pēṇal. ciṟitu - īṇṭu eṇmip poruṭṭu. 
151-155.	kāvirip paṭappip paṭṭiṉam taṉṉuḷ - tōṭṭak kūṟukaḷiyuṭiya kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉattuḷ, pū viri piṇṭip potu nīṅku tiru niḻal - malar virinta acōkiṉ potumi nīṅkiya aḻakiya niḻaṟkaṇṇē, ulaka nōṉpikaḷ oruṅkuṭaṉ iṭṭa - cāvakar pallōr oṉṟu cērntu iṭṭa, ilaku oḷic cilātalammēl iruntaruḷi - viḷaṅkukiṉṟa oḷiyiṉiyuṭiya cilāvaṭṭattiṉmēl eḻuntaruḷi, tarumam cāṟṟum cāraṇar tammuṉ - aṟavuri kūṟukiṉṟa cāraṇar muṉpāka ;
paṭappik kāvirippaṭṭiṉam eṉka. 2"kāvirippaṭṭiṉam kaṭalkoḷum" eṉṟār piṟarum. iṉi, kāviriyit tōṭṭak kūṟṟiṟku elliyākavuṭiya paṭṭiṉam eṉṟalum poruntum. niḻaliṉ potumi nīṅkalāvatu ēṉiyavaṟṟiṉ niḻal cāyavum tāṉ oṉṟē cāyātu niṟṟalām. 3"ulaka nōṉpika ḷoruṅkuṭaṉiṭṭa, vilakoḷic cilātalam" eṉṟār muṉṉum. ulaka nōṉpi kaḷ - illaṟattiliruntu virataṅ kāppōr.  
156-160.	tiruvil iṭṭut tikaḻtaru mēṉiyaṉ - vāṉa villip pōṉṟu oḷiviṭṭu viḷaṅkukiṉṟa mēṉiyi yuṭiyaṉāy, tāraṉ - pūmāliyi uṭiyaṉāy, māliyaṉ - maṇi māliyi uṭiyaṉāy, tamaṉiyap pūṇiṉaṉ poṉṉāṟ ceyta aṇi kalaṅkaḷi aṇintavaṉāy, pārōr kāṇāp palar toḻu paṭimiyaṉ - ivvulaka makkaḷ kāṇavoṇṇāta tēvar palarum vaṇaṅkum teyva vaṭivattiṉi yuṭiyavaṉākiya, karuviral kuraṅkiṉ ki oru pākattu - oru pākattuk ki kariyaviraliyuṭiya kuraṅkiṉ kiyākavuṭiya, peruviṟal vāṉavaṉ vantu niṉṟōṉi - periya veṟṟiyiṉi yuṭiya tēvaṉoruvaṉ āṅkaṇ vantu niṉṟavaṉi ;
tiruvil - vāṉavil. vāṉavil iṉṉa kālattu ivvāṟu tōṉṟum eṉpatu aṟiyappaṭātavāṟu pōlat tēvaṉuṭiya varavum aṟiyappaṭāmiyāṉ "tiruvi liṭṭut tikaḻtaru mēṉiyaṉ" eṉṟār eṉalumām ; eṉṉi? 1"vāṉiṭu villiṉ varavaṟiyā vāymiyāṉ" eṉṟār piṟarumākalāṉ. kāṇā - muṉ kaṇṭaṟiyāta eṉṟalumām.
2"tāraṉ māliyaṉ tamaṉiyap pūṇiṉaṉ, pārōr kāṇāp palartoḻu paṭimiyaṉ" eṉṟār maṇimēkaliyiṉum. tāraṉ māliyaṉ eṉpataṉit tāramāliyaṉ eṉap pāṭamōti, mantāra māliyiyuṭiyavaṉ eṉiṉum poruntum, paṭimi - teyva vaṭivu. viṟal - veṟṟi ; āvatu tēvaṉāyatu. niṉṟōṉ - viṉippeyar. 
161-162. 	cāvakar ellām cāraṇart toḻutu - ulaka nōṉpikaḷ yāvarum cāraṇari vaṇaṅki, īṅku yātu ivaṉ varavu eṉa - ivviṭattu it tēvaṉuṭiya varuki yātaṉik karutiyatākum eṉṟu kēṭka, iṟiyōṉ kūṟum - cāraṇar talivaṉ kūṟuvāṉāyiṉaṉ, (kūṟupavaṉ) ;
muṉṉarc cāraṇar eṉap paṉmi kūṟip piṉṉar iṟiyōṉ eṉa orumi kūṟiyatu avarkaḷuḷ talivaṉē makkaḷukku aṟivuṟuttuvaṉa aṟivuṟuttuvāṉākaliṉ eṉka. muṉṉarum, 3"tarumañ cāṟṟuñ cāraṇar tōṉṟa............cinti viḷakkiṟ ṟerintō ṉāyiṉum" eṉak kūṟiṉami kāṇka. 
163-191.	eṭṭi cāyalaṉ iruntōṉ taṉatu -eṭṭippaṭṭat tiṉiyuṭiya illaṟattiruntōṉākiya cāyalaṉ eṉṟa peyari yuṭiyāṉatu, paṭṭiṉi nōṉpikaḷ palar pukum maṉiyil - paṭṭiṉi viṭṭuṇṇum viratikaḷ palarum vantu cērkiṉṟa illattiṉkaṇ, ōr mā tava mutalvaṉi - periya tavattāṉ mēmpaṭṭa oruvaṉi, maṉip peruṅ kiḻatti - illaṟattiṟkut tuṇiyākiya ac cāyalaṉuṭiya maṉivi, ētam nīṅka etir koḷ amayattu - tam tīviṉi oḻiya etirkoṇṭu aḻitta camayattu.
ūrc ciṟu kuraṅku oṉṟu otuṅki uḷpukku - avvūrk kaṇuḷḷa ciṟiyatōr kuraṅku accattāṉ otuṅki am maṉiyiṉuḷḷē nuḻintu, pāṟpaṭu mātavaṉ pātam porunti - aruḷaṟattiṉ pāṟ paṭṭa am mātavaṉuṭiya tiruvaṭikaḷi vaṇaṅki, uṇṭu oḻi miccilum ukutta nīrum - avaṉ uṇṭu oḻitta cōṟṟiṉiyum ūṟṟiya nīriyum, taṇṭā vēṭkiyiṉ tāṉ ciṟitu arunti - nīṅkāta viruppiṉāl ciṟitaḷavu uṇṭu paci oḻintu. etir mukam nōkkiya iṉpac cevviyi - iṉpattōṭu etirāka iruntu taṉ mukatti nōkkiya taṉmiyi, atirāk koḷki aṟivaṉum nayantu - naṭukkamillāk kōṭ pāṭṭiṉiyuṭiya mātavaṉum virumpi, niṉ makkaḷiṉ ōmpu maṉikkiḻattī eṉa - illaṟattiṟku uriyavaḷē ik kuraṅkiṉi niṉ makkaḷi ōmputal pōlak kāppāyāka eṉṟurikka,
mikkōṉ kūṟiya meymmoḻi ōmpi - am mēlōṉ kūṟiya uṇmi moḻiyiṉik kāttu, kātaṟ kuraṅku kaṭi nāḷ eytavum - aṉpu niṟinta ak kuraṅku iṟanta piṉṉarum, tāṉam ceyvuḻi - tāṉam ceyyumpōtu, ataṟku oru kūṟu tītu aṟukeṉṟē ceytaṉaḷ ātaliṉ - ak kuraṅkiṟku oru pakutit tāṉatti ataṉ tīya piṟavi oḻikaveṉṟu karutic ceytu vantāḷ ākalāṉ, mattima nal nāṭṭu vāraṇam taṉṉuḷ - nalla matti matēcattuḷḷa vāraṇavāci eṉṉum nakarattu, uttara kutaṟku oru makaṉ āki - uttara kuttaṉ eṉṉum oruvaṉukku oppaṟṟa putalvaṉāy, uruviṉum tiruviṉum uṇarviṉum tōṉṟi - aḻakiṉāṉum celvattāṉum aṟivāṉum mēmpaṭṭu viḷaṅki, peruviṟal tāṉam palavum ceytu- periya veṉṟiyōṭu tāṉaṅkaḷ palavaṟṟiyum ceytu, āṅku eṇṇāl āṇṭiṉ iṟanta piṟpāṭu - ap piṟaviyilē muppattiraṇṭāvatu āṇṭil iṟanta piṉṉar, viṇṇōr vaṭivam peṟṟaṉaṉ - tēvar vaṭivattiṉi aṭintaṉaṉ,
ātaliṉ - ākiyāl, peṟṟa celvap perumpayaṉ ellām - aṅṅaṉamāya celvattiṉip peṟṟa periya payaṉ yāvum, taṟkāttu aḷittōḷ tāṉac ciṟappu eṉa - taṉṉik kāttu aḷiceytavaḷ ceyta tāṉattiṉ mikutiyāṉ ām eṉṟu uṭkoṇṭu, paṇṭip piṟappiṟ kuraṅkiṉ ciṟuki koṇṭu oru pākattu - muṉṉip piṟaviyil poruntiya karuṅ kuraṅkiṉ ki vaṭivāṉa ciṟiya kiyi oru pākattē koṇṭu, koḷkiyiṟ puṇarnta cāyalaṉ maṉivi tāṉan taṉṉāl āyiṉaṉ iv vaṭivu - tāṉam ceyyum kōṭpāṭṭilē kūṭiya cāyalaṉuṭiya maṉivi muṉṉāḷiṟ ceyta tāṉattiṉ payaṉāl iv vaṭivattip peṟṟēṉ, aṟimiṉō eṉa - nīvir itaṉi aṟiyuṅkaḷ eṉṟu, cāvakarkku ellām cāṟṟiṉaṉ kāṭṭa - ulaka nōṉpikaḷ yāvarkkum kāṭṭi aṟivuṟutta, tēva kumaraṉ tōṉṟiṉaṉ eṉalum - it teyva kumāraṉ iṅṅaṉam tōṉṟiṉāṉ eṉṟu kūṟavum ;
eṭṭi - vaṇikarkku aracaraḷikkum ciṟappuppeyar. irun tōṉ eṉṟatu ik kūṟiya iṭattiruntōṉ eṉalumām. iruntōṉ taṉatu maṉi palar puku maṉi eṉka. pāṟpaṭu mātavaṉ eṉpataṟkut taṉpāṟpaṭṭa tavattiyuṭiyāṉ. eṉalum poruntum. miccil - eccil, eccil uṇavu. taṇṭā vēṭki-paci eṉpa. mukam nōkkiya cevvi, meymmoḻiyi ōmputalāvatu avar moḻintataṟkup puṟampāka naṭavātu ataṟku aṭaṅki ak kuraṅkiṉip pōṟṟalām. iṉi, meymmoḻi ōmpi eṉpataṟku meym moḻiyiṉ paṭiyē ak kuraṅkiṉik kāttu eṉṟalum poruntum. 'makkaḷiṉ ōmpu' eṉṟataṉāl tāṉam ceyvuḻi ataṟkuriya oru pakutiyic ceytaṉaḷ eṉka. tītu - tīviṉiyumām. kaṭināḷ - iṟappu. uttaraṉ kavippaṉ eṉap pāṭamōtik kavippaṉ eṉṉum vaṇikaṉukku uttaraṉ eṉṉum peyarōṭu oru makaṉāki eṉavum, kavippaṉ - pacu maṟiyat tēṭiṉa poruḷuṭiyavaṉ eṉavum urippārumuḷar. makkaḷiṭattu aḻakum aṟivum ākkamum mūṉṟum oruṅkē tōṉṟutal aritākalāṉ, "uruviṉum tiruviṉum uṇarviṉum tōṉṟi" eṉṟār. tāṉam pala ceytaṟkup palaruṭiya uḷḷam oruppaṭāvākalāṉ aṅṅaṉamiṉṟi oruppaṭṭuc ceyyum tāṉattiṉip "peruviṟaṟ ṟāṉam" eṉṟār. uṭiyāṉ toḻil uṭimi mēl ēṟṟappaṭṭatu. piṟpāṭu - piṉṉar. 1"peṇṇaṇi kōlam peyarnta piṟpāṭu" eṉṟār muṉṉum. tāṉac ciṟappu - tāṉattāṟ peṟṟa ciṟappu eṉṟalum amiyum. 
192-197. 	cāraṇar kūṟiya takicāl naṉmoḻi - cāraṇar moḻinta takuti yaminta nalla aṟavuriyi, aṉṟu - an nāḷil, ār aṇaṅku āka aṟantalippaṭṭōr - maṟi moḻiyākak koṇṭu aṟattuvaḻi niṉṟōrākiya, ap patiyuḷ aruntava mākkaḷum - an nakarattukkaṇ ariya tavamuṭiyōrum. taṉteṟal vāḻkkic cāvaka mākkaḷum - taṉakkeṉa vāḻāta vāḻkkiyiṉiyuṭiya ulaka nōṉpikaḷum, iṭṭa tāṉattu eṭṭiyum maṉiviyum - tāṉam ceyta cāyalaṉum avaṉ maṉiviyum, muṭṭā iṉpattu muṭivulaku eytiṉar - kuṟivilā iṉpattiṉiyuṭiya vīṭṭulakattiṉi aṭintaṉar;
āraṇaṅkāka eṉpataṟkut teyvat taṉmiyōṭu kūṭiyatāka eṉalumām . taṉ teṟal vāḻkki - tāṉ eṉṉun taṉmiyik keṭutta vāḻkki ; taṉakkeṉavāḻātu piṟarkkeṉa vāḻum vāḻkki,  iṭṭa tāṉam - kuraṅkiṉ poruṭṭuc ceyta tāṉam. muṭṭā iṉpam - taṭiyilā iṉpam, niṟinta iṉpam. vīṭṭulakiṉum muṭivāṉa vēṟu ulakam iṉmiyāṉ avvulaku muṭivulaku eṉappaṭṭatu. itu tāṉattiṉ ciṟappiṟku mēṟkōḷākum.  
198-200. 	kēṭṭaṉi āyiṉ - tāṉattiṉ ciṟappiṉi uṇarntu ataṉi uṭaṉpaṭṭāyāyiṉ it tōṭṭār kuḻali yoṭu - intat tokuti koṇṭa kūntali yuṭiyāḷoṭu, nīṭṭittu irātu - tāḻkkātu, nī pōkeṉṟē kavunti kūṟa - nī celvāyāka eṉṟu kavuntiyaṭikaḷ colla, uvantaṉaḷ ētti - tāṉ peṟṟa aṭikkalattiṉ poruṭṭu makiḻntu kavuntiyaṭikaḷip pōṟṟi; 
tōṭār eṉaṟpālatu tōṭṭār eṉa vikāramāyiṟṟu. tōṭu - malar eṉiṉum amiyum.
201-206. 	vaḷar iḷa vaṉamuli - vaḷarkiṉṟa iḷami poruntiya aḻakiya muliyiṉiyum, vāṅku ami paṇittōḷ - mūṅkiliṉ aḻakik kavarum periya tōḷiṉiyum, muḷi iḷa veṇpal - iḷiya nāṇal muḷi pōlum veḷḷiya paṟkaḷiyum, mutukkuṟi naṅkiyoṭu - pēraṟiviṉiyu muṭiya kaṇṇakiyōṭu, ceṉṟa ñāyiṟṟuc celcuṭar amiyattu - mēl kaṭaliṟ ceṉṟu cērnta ñāyiṟṟiṉ oḷi ceṉṟu oṭuṅkum poḻutil, kaṉṟu tēr āviṉ kaṉi kural iyampa - kaṉṟiṉi niṉintu varum pacukkaḷiṉ muḻaṅkum kural olippa, maṟittōḷ naviyattu uṟikkāvāḷaroṭu - āṭṭuk kuṭṭiyiyum kōṭariyiyum cumanta tōḷilē uṟiyik kāviya iṭiyarkaḷōṭu, ceṟivaḷi āycciyar cilar puṟañ cūḻa - niṟinta vaḷiyali aṇinta iṭicciyar cilar ivaḷ putiyaḷāy iruttaliṉ puṟattē cūḻntuvarā niṟpa;
vāṅku ami - vaḷinta mūṅkilumām. muḷi - nāṇal muḷi. mutukkuṟi - pēraṟivu. celcuṭar - cuṭar cel eṉka. maṟi naviyattōḷ eṉa māṟuka. kāvutal - tōḷiṟ cumattal; uṟi kaṭṭiṉa kāviyuṭiya eṉalumām. ivaḷ aḻaku kaṇṭu puṟattē cūḻntār eṉṟalum poruntum. 
207-217. 	miḷiyum - kāvaṟkāṭum, kiṭaṅkum - akaḻiyum, vaḷiviṟ poṟiyum - vaḷintu tāṉē eyyum iyantira villum, karuviral ūkamum - kariyaviraliṉiyuṭiya karuṅkuraṅku pōṉṟa poṟiyum, kal umiḻ kavaṇum - kalliṉi vīcukiṉṟa kavaṇum, parivu uṟu venneyum - cērntārit tuṉpuṟuttukiṉṟa vemmi mikka neyyum, pāku aṭu kuḻiciyum - cempiṉi urukkukiṉṟa kuḻicikaḷum, kāypoṉ uliyum - irumpu kāyntu urukaṟku vitta ulikaḷum, kal iṭu kūṭiyum - kal niṟiya iṭap peṟṟa kūṭikaḷum, tūṇṭilum - tūṇṭil vaṭivākap paṇṇiya karuvikaḷum, toṭakkum - kaḻuttiṟ pūṭṭi muṟukkum caṅkiliyum, āṇtali aṭuppum - āṇṭalip puḷ vaṭivākac ceyta aṭuppukaḷum, kaviyum - akaḻiyiṉiṉṟu ēṟiṉ taḷḷukiṉṟa iruppuk kavikaḷum, kaḻuvum - kaḻuk kōlum, putiyum - ampuk kaṭṭukaḷum, puḻiyum - ēvaṟi kaḷum, aiyavit tulāmum - taṉṉi neruṅkiṉār taliyi nerukkit tirukum maraṅkaḷum, ki peyar ūciyum - matiliṉ taliyip paṟṟuvār kiyi naṭuṅkacceyyum ūcip poṟikaḷum, ceṉṟu eṟi ciralum - pakivarmēṟ ceṉṟu tākkum ciccilip poṟiyum, paṉṟiyum - matiṟkaṇ ēṟiṉārik kōṭṭāṟ kiḻikkum paṉṟip poṟiyum, paṇiyum - mūṅkil vaṭivākap paṇṇi aṭittaṟku vitta poṟikaḷum, eḻuvum cīppum - kataviṟku valiyāka uḷvāyiṟpaṭiyilē nilattilē vīḻa viṭumaraṅkaḷum, muḻu viṟaṟ kaṇiyamum - mikka valiporuntiya kaṇiya maraṅkaḷum, kōlum -eṟikōlum, kuntamum - ciṟukavaḷamum, vēlum - īṭṭi mutaliyaṉavum, ñāyilum - kuruvit talikaḷum, piṟavum - matiṟkuriya ēṉiya poṟi mutaliyaṉavum, ciṟantu - mikuntu;
venney - kotikka vitta ney, pāku aṭu kuḻici - cāṇakaṅ karittuk kāykiṟa miṭā eṉavum cempurukku eṉavum kūṟupa. venney mutaliya mūṉṟum matilip paṟṟuvārmītu iṟittaṟ kamintaṉa. kalliṭu kūṭi eṉpataṟku iṭaṅkaṇip poṟikkuk kalliṭṭu vikkum kūṭi eṉavum, toṭakku eṉapataṟkuk kayiṟṟut toṭakku eṉavum. āṇṭali aṭuppu eṉpataṟku āṇtalip puḷ vaṭivākac ceyyappaṭṭa poṟi nirikaḷ eṉavum kūṟuvar. aiyavittulām taliyi nerukkit tirukum eṉpataṉi, 1"viṟpoṟikaḷ .........maraniliyē" eṉṉum cintāmaṇic ceyyuḷiṉ nacciṉārkkiṉiyar uriyāṉuṇarka. iṉi, itaṟku, kataviṟkuk kāvalākap puṟavāyililē kaṟkavi toṭaṅkit tūkkappaṭum maram eṉavum, paṟṟākki tūkkip pōkaṭṭa viṭṭameṉavum, cāṇampuk kūṭu eṉavum, ciṟṟampukaḷ vittu eyyum iyanctiram eṉavum poruḷ kūṟupa. ki peyar ūci - kiyik keṭukkum. ūci; kiyi appuṟappaṭuttum ūci; nirikkaḻu eṉalum poruntum. katavu tiṟakkuṅkālattu mēlē eḻuppukiyāl eḻuvuñ cīppum eṉṟār. kaṇiyam - cīppu akappaṭak kuṟukkē pōṭappaṭum maramumām. ñāyil - ēp puḻikku naṭuvāy eytu maṟiyum cūṭṭu eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar. (cīvaka, 105. ceyyuḷ uri nōkkuka). piṟa eṉṟatu kaḷiṟṟuppoṟi, pulippoṟi mutaliyaṉavām. 
217-219.	nāḷ koṭi nuṭaṅkum - nāḷtōṟum pakivari veṉṟu veṉṟu uyarttiya koṭikaḷ aciyum, vāyil kaḻintu - matil vāyilik kaṭantu, taṉ maṉi pukkaṉaḷāl kōvalar maṭanti koḷkiyiṟ puṇarnteṉ - iṭiyar kulattāḷākiya mātari aṭikkalam peṟṟa koḷkiyoṭu poruntit taṉ maṉikkaṇ pukkāḷ ;
nāḷ koṭi - nalla nāḷil eṭutta koṭiyumām. āl-aci. ciṟantu nuṭaṅkum vāyil eṉka.
mā nakar kaṇṭu poḻiliṭam pukuntu kōvalaṉ kūṟuḻi māṭalaṉ eṉpōṉ pukuntōṉṟaṉṉik kōvalaṉ vaṇaṅka, antaṇaṉ urippōṉ karuṇi maṟava, cellāc celva, illōr cemmal, ummip payaṉkol pōntatu nīyeṉa viṉava, kōvalaṉ kaṉavu kaṇṭēṉ kaṭitīṅkuṟum eṉa, iṅku oḻika niṉ iruppu, maturi mā nakar pukuka eṉa mātavattāṭṭiyum māmaṟi mutalvaṉum kūṟuṅkāli, mātari eṉpōḷ aiyiyik kaṇṭu aṭi toḻalum, eṇṇiṉaḷāki, aṭikkalam tantēṉ maṭantiyit tāyum nīyē ākit tāṅku, aṭikkalam ciṟitāyiṉum pēriṉpam tarum, atu kēḷāy, paṭṭiṉantaṉṉuḷ cāraṇar tammuṉ kuraṅkiṉ kiyorupākattu vāṉavaṉ vantu niṉṟōṉi yātu ivaṉ varavu eṉa, iṟiyōṉ tēvakumaraṉ tōṉṟiṉaṉ eṉṟalum, aṟantalippaṭṭōr muṭivulakeytiṉar ; kēṭṭaṉiyāyiṉ nī pōkeṉṟu kavunti kūṟa, naṅkiyoṭu kōvalar maṭanti koḷkiyiṟ puṇarntu maṉipukkaṉaḷ eṉa muṭikka.
itu nilimaṇṭila āciriyappā.
aṭikkalak kāti muṟṟiṟṟu.

16. kolikkaḷak kāti  
(mātari kaṇṇakiyiyum kōvalaṉiyum putiya maṉi yoṉṟil iruttit taṉ makaḷ aiyiyik kaṇṇakikkut tuṇiyāka vittu aṭicilākkutaṟku vēṇṭum poruḷkaḷi aḷikka, kaṇṇaki naṉku camittuk kaṇavaṉi muṟippaṭi uṇpittu avaṟku veṟṟili pākku aḷittu niṉṟaṉaḷc. appoḻutu kōvalaṉ kaṇṇakiyi arukaṇittu 'nī'a6 vevviya kāṭṭilē pōntataṟku eṉ tāy tantiyar eṉṉa tuṉpamuṟṟārkaḷō' eṉṟu kūṟi, tāṉ muṉ neṟi tavaṟi naṭantamikku iraṅki, `īṅku eṉṉoṭu pōntu eṉ tuyar kaḷinta poṉṉē! koṭiyē! puṉipūṅ kōtāy! nāṇiṉ pāvāy! nīṇila viḷakkē! kaṟpiṉ koḻuntē! poṟ piṉ celvi! nāṉ niṉ cīṟaṭic cilampiṉ oṉṟu koṇṭupōy viṟṟu varuvēṉ; mayaṅkātiru' eṉat tēṟṟi, aritiṉ nīṅkic celvāṉāyiṉaṉ. celpavaṉ pīṭikit teruvilē poṟ kollar nūṟṟuvar piṉvara muṉvanta oru poṟkollaṉik kaṇṭu, viṟpataṟkut tāṉ koṇarnta cilampiṉik kāṭṭa, ap poṟkollaṉ aracaṉ maṉiviyiṉ cilampoṉṟik kavarntavaṉātalāl taṉatu kaḷavu veḷippaṭu muṉ ic cilampāl taṉmītu uṇṭākum aiyattit tavirkkalāmeṉat tuṇintu `kōpperuntēvi aṇitaṟkēṟṟa ic cilampiṉi nāṉ aracaṉuk kaṟivittu varuṅkāṟum ivviṭattiruppīr' eṉat taṉ maṉiyiṉ pakkattiluḷḷa kōyilil iruttic ceṉṟaṉaṉ. ceṉṟavaṉ, taṉ tēviyiṉ ūṭal taṇittaṟ poruṭṭu avaḷ kōyili nōkkic ceṉṟukoṇ ṭirunta pāṇṭiyaṉ neṭuñceḻiyaṉik kaṇṭu, `kōyilil irunta cilampiṉit tiruṭiya kaḷvaṉ ac cilampuṭaṉ aṭiyēṉ kuṭilil vantuḷāṉ' eṉṟu kūṟa, aracaṉ, `avaṉik koṉṟu ac cilampiṉik koṇarvīr; eṉak kāvalāḷark kurittaṉaṉ. poṟ kollaṉ makiḻntu ak kāvalāḷaruṭaṉ ceṉṟu kōvalaṉi aṇuki, `aracaṉ ēvalāṟ cilampu kāṇa vantōr ivar' eṉak kūṟi, ac cilampiṉik kāṭṭuvittu, ‘mukakkuṟi mutaliyavaṟṟāl ivaṉ kaḷvaṉallaṉ' eṉṟu kūṟiyavarkaḷi ikaḻnturittu, kaḷavu nūl kūṟum ētukkaḷi yellām eṭuttuk kāṭṭi avaṉik kaḷvaṉeṉṟu vaṟpuṟuttiṉaṉ ; appoḻutu aṟivaṟṟa taṟukaṇaṉoruvaṉ taṉ ki vāḷāṟ kōvalaṉi veṭṭi vīḻttiṉaṉ. (itil, kaṇṇaki kōvalaṉi uṇpittatum, kōvalaṉ kūṟṟukku maṟu moḻi kūṟiyatum avaḷatu uyarnta paṇpukaḷi veḷippaṭuttukiṉṟaṉa.) 
		arumpeṟaṟ pāviyi aṭikkalam peṟṟa
irumpē ruvakiyiṉ iṭikkula  maṭanti
aḷivili yuṇaviṉ āycciyar tammoṭu
miḷicūḻ kōvalar irukki yaṉṟip
5		pūva lūṭṭiya puṉimāṇ pantark
kāvaṟ ciṟṟiṟ kaṭimaṉip paṭuttuc
ceṟivaḷi yāycciyar cilaruṭaṉ kūṭi
naṟumalark kōtiyi nāṇī rāṭṭik
kūṭal makaḷir kōlaṅ koḷḷum
10		āṭakap pimpū ṇaruvili yaḻippac
ceyyāk kōlamoṭu vantīrk keṉmakaḷ
aiyi kāṇī raṭittoḻi lāṭṭi
poṉṉiṟ potintēṉ puṉipūṅ kōti
eṉṉuṭaṉ naṅkiyīṅ kirukkeṉat toḻutu
15		mātavat tāṭṭi vaḻittuyar nīkki
ēta millā iṭantalip paṭuttiṉaḷ
nōtaka vuṇṭō nummaka ṉārkkiṉic
cāvaka nōṉpika ḷaṭika ḷātaliṉ
nāttūṇ naṅkiyoṭu nāḷvaḻip paṭūum
20		aṭici lākkutaṟ kamintanaṟ kalaṅkaḷ
neṭiyā taḷimiṉ nīreṉak kūṟa
iṭikkula maṭantiyar iyalpiṟ kuṉṟā
maṭikkalan taṉṉoṭu māṇpuṭi marapiṟ
kōḷip pākaṟ koḻuṅkaṉit tiraḷkāy
25		vāḷvarik koṭuṅkāy mātuḷam pacuṅkāy
māviṉ kaṉiyoṭu vāḻit tīṅkaṉi
cāli yarici tampāṟ payaṉoṭu
kōlvaḷi mātē koḷkeṉak koṭuppa
melviral civappap palvēṟu pacuṅkāy
30		koṭuvāyk kuyattu viṭuvāy ceyyat
tirumukam viyarttatu ceṅkaṇ cēntaṉa
karipuṟa aṭṭil kaṇṭaṉaḷ peyara
viyeri mūṭṭiya aiyi taṉṉoṭu
kiyaṟi maṭimiyiṟ kātalaṟ kākkit
35		tālap pulliṉ vālveṇ tōṭṭuk
kivaṉ makaṭūuk kaviṉpeṟap puṉinta
ceyviṉit taviciṟ celvaṉ iruntapiṉ
kaṭimala raṅkiyiṟ kātala ṉaṭinīr
cuṭumaṇ maṇṭiyil toḻutaṉaḷ māṟṟi
40		maṇṇaka maṭantiyi mayakkoḻip paṉaḷpōl
taṇṇīr teḷittut taṉkiyāl taṭavik
kumari vāḻiyiṉ kuruttakam virittīṅku
amuta muṇka aṭika ḷīṅkeṉa
aracar piṉṉōrk karumaṟi maruṅkiṉ
45		uriya vellām orumuṟi kaḻittāṅku
āyar pāṭiyiṉ acōtipeṟ ṟeṭutta
pūvip putumalar vaṇṇaṉ kollō
nallamu tuṇṇum nampi yīṅkup
palvaḷit tōḷiyum paṇṭunaṅ kulattut
50		toḻuṉi yāṟṟiṉuḷ tūmaṇi vaṇṇaṉi
viḻuman tīrtta viḷakkuk kolleṉa
aiyiyun tavviyum vimmita meytik
kaṇkoḷā namakkivar kāṭci yīṅkeṉa
uṇṭiṉi tirunta uyarpē rāḷaṟku
55		ammeṉ tiriyalō ṭaṭikkā yītta
miyī rōtiyi varukeṉap poruntik
kallatar attam kaṭakka yāvatum
valluna kollō maṭantimel laṭiyeṉa
vemmuṉi yaruñcuram pōntataṟ kiraṅki
60		emmutu kuravar eṉṉuṟ ṟaṉarkol
māyaṅ kollō valviṉi kollō
yāṉuḷaṅ kalaṅki yāvatum aṟiyēṉ
varumoḻi yāḷaroṭu vampap parattaroṭu
kuṟumoḻik kōṭṭi neṭunaki pukkup
65		poccāp puṇṭu poruḷuri yāḷar
naccukkoṉ ṟēṟkum naṉṉeṟi yuṇṭō
irumutu kurava rēvalum piḻittēṉ
ciṟumutuk kuṟivikkuc ciṟumiyuñ ceytēṉ
vaḻuveṉum pārēṉ mānakar maruṅkīṇṭu
70		eḻukeṉa eḻuntāy eṉcey taṉiyeṉa
aṟavōrk kaḷittalum antaṇa rōmpalum
tuṟavōrk ketirtalum tollōr ciṟappiṉ
viruntetir kōṭalum iḻanta eṉṉinum
perumaka ṭaṉṉoṭum perumpeyart talittāḷ
75		maṉperuñ ciṟappiṉ mānitik kiḻavaṉ
munti nillā muṉivikan taṉaṉā
aṟpuḷañ ciṟantāṅ karuṇmoḻi yaḷii
eṟpā rāṭṭa yāṉakat toḷitta
nōyum tuṉpamum noṭivatu pōlumeṉ
80		vāyal muṟuvaṟkavar uḷḷakam varuntap
pōṟṟā voḻukkam purintīr yāvatum
māṟṟā uḷḷa vāḻkkiyē ṉātaliṉ
ēṟṟeḻun taṉaṉyāṉ eṉṟavaḷ kūṟak
kuṭimutaṟ cuṟṟamum kuṟṟiḷi yōrum
85		aṭiyōr pāṅkum āyamum nīṅki
nāṇamum maṭaṉum nallō rēttum
pēṇiya kaṟpum peruntuṇi yāka
eṉṉoṭu pōntīṅ keṉṟuyar kaḷinta
poṉṉē koṭiyē puṉipūṅ kōtāy
90		nāṇiṉ pāvāy nīṇila viḷakkē
kaṟpiṉ koḻuntē poṟpiṉ celvi
cīṟaṭic cilampi ṉoṉṟukoṇ ṭiyāṉpōy
māṟi varuvaṉ mayaṅkā toḻikeṉak
karuṅkayal neṭuṅkaṭ kātali taṉṉi
95		oruṅkuṭaṉ taḻīi uḻiyō rillā
orutaṉi kaṇṭutaṉ uḷḷakam vetumpi
varupaṉi karanta kaṇṇa ṉākip
pallāṉ kōvala rillam nīṅki
vallā naṭiyiṉ maṟukiṟ celvōṉ
100		imilē ṟetirnta tiḻukkeṉa aṟiyāṉ
taṉkulam aṟiyun takutiyaṉ ṟātaliṉ
tāteru maṉṟan tāṉuṭaṉ kaḻintu
mātar vīti maṟukiṭi naṭantu
pīṭikit teruviṟ peyarvōṉ āṅkaṇ
105		kaṇṇuḷ viṉiñar kiviṉi muṟṟiya
nuṇviṉik kollar nūṟṟuvar piṉvara
meyppi pukku vilaṅkunaṭic celaviṉ
kikkōṟ kollaṉik kaṇṭaṉa ṉākit
teṉṉavaṉ peyaroṭu ciṟappup peṟṟa
110		poṉviṉik kollaṉ ivaṉeṉap poruntik
kāvalaṉ ṟēvik kāvatōr kāṟkaṇi
nīvili yiṭutaṟ kāti yōveṉa
aṭiyēṉ aṟiyē ṉāyiṉum vēntar
muṭimutaṟ kalaṉkaḷ camippēṉ yāṉeṉak
115		kūṟṟat tūtaṉ kitoḻu tēttap
pōṟṟaruñ cilampiṉ potivā yaviḻttaṉaṉ
mattaka maṇiyoṭu vayiraṅ kaṭṭiya
pattik kēvaṇap pacumpoṟ kuṭiccūṟ
cittirac cilampiṉ ceyviṉi yellām
120		poyttoḻiṟ kollaṉ purintuṭaṉ nōkkik
kōpperun tēvik kallati iccilampu
yāppuṟa villi yeṉamuṉ pōntu
viṟalmiku vēntaṟku viḷampiyāṉ varaveṉ
ciṟukuṭi laṅkaṇ irumiṉ nīreṉak
125		kōvalaṉ ceṉṟak kuṟumaka ṉirukkiyōr
tēva kōṭṭac ciṟiyakam pukkapiṉ
karantiyāṉ koṇṭa kālaṇi īṅkup
parantu veḷippaṭā muṉṉam maṉṉaṟkup
pulampeyar putuvaṉiṟ pōkkuvaṉ yāṉeṉak
130		kalaṅkā vuḷḷam karantaṉaṉ celvōṉ
kūṭaṉ makaḷir āṭal tōṟṟamum
pāṭaṟ pakutiyum paṇṇiṉ payaṅkaḷum
kāvala ṉuḷḷam kavarntaṉa eṉṟutaṉ
ūṭa luḷḷam uḷkaran toḷittut
135		talinōy varuttan taṉmē liṭṭuk
kulamutal tēvi kūṭā tēka
mantirac cuṟṟam nīṅki maṉṉavaṉ
cintari neṭuṅkaṭ cilatiyar tammoṭu
kōpperun tēvi kōyil nōkkik
140		kāppuṭi vāyiṟ kaṭikāṇ akaviyiṉ
vīḻntaṉaḷ kiṭantu tāḻntupala ēttik
kaṉṉaka miṉṟiyum kavikkō liṉṟiyum
tuṉṉiya mantiran tuṇiyeṉak koṇṭu
vāyi lāḷari mayakkutuyi luṟuttuk
145		kōyiṟ cilampu koṇṭa kaḷvaṉ
kalleṉ pērūrk kāvalark karanteṉ
cillic ciṟukuṭi lakattirun tōṉeṉa
viṉiviḷi kāla mātaliṉ yāvatum
ciṉiyalar vēmpaṉ tērā ṉāki
150		ūrkāp pāḷarik kūvi īṅkeṉ
tāḻpūṅ kōti taṉkāṟ cilampu
kaṉṟiya kaḷvaṉ kiya tākil
koṉṟac cilampu koṇarka īṅkeṉak
kāvalaṉ ēvak karuntoḻiṟ kollaṉum
155		ēva luḷḷat teṇṇiyatu muṭitteṉat
tīviṉi mutirvalic ceṉṟupaṭ ṭirunta
kōvalaṉ ṟaṉṉik kuṟukiṉa ṉāki
valampaṭu tāṉi maṉṉavaṉ ēvac
cilampu kāṇiya vantōr ivareṉac
160		ceyviṉic cilampiṉ ceyti yellām
poyviṉik kollaṉ purintuṭaṉ kāṭṭa
ilakkaṇa muṟimiyiṉ iruntōṉ īṅkivaṉ
kolippaṭu makaṉalaṉ eṉṟu kūṟum
aruntiṟaṉ mākkaḷi akanakit turittuk
165		karuntoḻiṟ kollaṉ kāṭṭiṉa ṉurippōṉ
mantiram teyvam maruntē nimittam
tantiram iṭaṉē kālam karuviyeṉṟu
eṭṭuṭa ṉaṉṟē iḻukkuṭi marapiṟ
kaṭṭuṇ mākkaḷ tuṇiyeṉat tirivatu
170		maruntiṟ paṭṭī rāyiṉ yāvarum
perumpeyar maṉṉaṉiṟ perunavip paṭṭīr
mantira nāviṭi vaḻuttuva rāyiṉ
intira kumarariṉ yāṅkāṇ kuvamō
teyvat tōṟṟam teḷikuva rāyiṉ
175		kiyakat tuṟuporuḷ kāṭṭiyum peyarkuvar
maruntiṉ naṅkaṇ mayakkuva rāyiṉ
iruntōm peyarum iṭaṉumā ruṇṭō
nimittam vāyttiṭi ṉalla tiyāvatum
pukaṟkilā arumporuḷ vantukip pukutiṉum
180		tantira karaṇam eṇṇuva rāyiṉ
intiraṉ mārpat tāramum eytuvar
ivviṭam ipporuḷ kōṭaṟ kiṭameṉiṉ
avviṭat tavari yārkāṇ kiṟpār
kālaṅ karuti avarporuḷ kiyuṟiṉ
185		mēlō rāyiṉum vilakkalu muṇṭō
karuvi koṇṭavar arumporuḷ kiyuṟiṉ
irunila maruṅkiṉ yārkāṇ kiṟpār
iravē pakalē eṉṟiraṇ ṭilli
karaviṭaṅ kēṭpiṉōr pukaliṭa milli
190		tūtarkō lattu vāyiliṉ iruntu 
mātarkō lattu valliruṭ pukku
viḷakku niḻalil tuḷakkilaṉ ceṉṟāṅku
iḷaṅkō vēntaṉ tuḷaṅkoḷi āram
veyiliṭu vayirattu miṉṉiṉ vāṅkat
195		tuyilkaṇ viḻittōṉ tāḷiṟ kāṇāṉ
uṭivāḷ uruva uṟiki vāṅki
eṟitoṟuñ ceṟitta iyalpiṟ kāṟṟāṉ
malliṟ kāṇa maṇittūṇ kāṭṭik
kalviyiṟ peyarnta kaḷvaṉ ṟaṉṉik
200		kaṇṭō ruḷareṉiṟ kāṭṭum īṅkivark
kuṇṭō vulakat toppō reṉṟak
karuntoḻiṟ kollaṉ colla āṅkōr
tiruntuvēṟ ṟaṭakki iḷiyōṉ kūṟum
nilaṉakaḻ uḷiyaṉ nīlat tāṉiyaṉ
205		kalaṉnaci vēṭkiyiṟ kaṭumpuli pōṉṟu
māri naṭunāḷ valliruḷ mayakkattu
ūrmaṭi kaṅkul oruvaṉ tōṉṟak
kivāḷ uruveṉ kivāḷ vāṅka
evvāy maruṅkiṉum yāṉavaṟ kaṇṭilēṉ
210		aritivar ceyti alikkum vēntaṉum
uriyatoṉ ṟurimiṉ uṟupaṭi yīreṉak
kallāk kaḷimaka ṉoruvaṉ kiyil
veḷvāḷ eṟintaṉaṉ vilaṅkū ṭaṟuttatu
puṇṇumiḻ kuruti poḻintuṭaṉ parappa
215		maṇṇaka maṭanti vāṉṟuyar kūrak
kāvalaṉ ceṅkōl vaḷiiya vīḻntaṉaṉ
kōvalaṉ paṇṭi ūḻviṉi yurutteṉ.
	nērici veṇpā
		naṇṇum iruviṉiyum naṇṇumiṉkaḷ nallaṟamē
kaṇṇaki taṉkēḷvaṉ kāraṇattāṉ - maṇṇil
vaḷiyāta ceṅkōl vaḷintatē paṇṭi
viḷivāki vanta viṉi.
uri
1-2. 		arum peṟaṟ pāviyi aṭikkalam peṟṟa - peṟutaṟku ariya pāvi pōlvāḷāya kaṇṇakiyi aṭikkala mākap peṟṟa, irum pēr uvakiyiṉ iṭikkula maṭanti - mikka periya makiḻcciyiṉiyuṭiya iṭikkulattut tōṉṟiya mātari;
arum peṟal - peṟalarum eṉa māṟuka. maṭanti - īṇṭup paruvaṅ kuṟiyātu peṇpāli uṇartti niṉṟatu. tāṉ aṭikkalaṅkākkap peṟṟamiyāl irumpēruvaki eytiṉāḷ eṉka. 
3-6. 		aḷi vili uṇaviṉ āycciyar tammoṭu - mōriṉi viṟṟup peṟum poruḷāl uṇṭu vāḻum vāḻkkiyiṉiyuṭiya iṭicciyarōṭu, miḷi cūḻ kōvalar irukki aṉṟi - kaṭṭu vēli cūḻnta iṭiyarkaḷatu irukkikkaṇ aṉṟi, pūval ūṭṭiya puṉi māṇ pantark kāvaṟ ciṟṟil kaṭi maṉip paṭuttu - aḻaku māṭcimippaṭṭa pantariṉiyum kāvaliṉiyumuṭiya cemmaṇ pūciya ciṟu vīṭākiya viḷakka maminta maṉikkaṇ cērttu; 
miḷi kōvalarirukkikkum, kāval maṉikkum koḷka, pūval- cemmaṇ; 1"ilpūvalūṭṭi" eṉṟār piṟarum. kaṭi-viḷakkam. 
7-8. 		ceṟi viḷi āycciyar cilaruṭaṉ kūṭi - ceṟinta vaḷi yiṉi aṇinta iṭicciyar cilaruṭaṉ cērntu, naṟumalark kōtiyi nāḷ nīr āṭṭi - maṇamuḷḷa malar māliyi aṇinta kaṇṇakiyip putu nīrāṉē kuḷippāṭṭi ;
	 nāḷ nīr - putiya nīr.  
9-14. 	kūṭal makaḷir kōlaṅ koḷḷum - kūṭal nakariṭattu makaḷir aḻakiṉ poruṭṭuk koḷḷukiṉṟa, āṭakap pim pūṇ aruvili aḻippa - poṉṉāṟ ceyta paciya pūṇiṉ ariya uyarcciyik keṭuttaṟku, ceyyāk kōlamoṭu vantīrkku - iyaṟki yaḻakuṭaṉ vanta numakku, eṉ makaḷ aiyikāṇīr aṭittoḻilāṭṭi - eṉakku makaḷāya aiyi kuṟṟēval makaḷāvāḷ, poṉṉiṟ potintēṉ - poṉṉip pōl aritākap pōṟṟuvēṉ, puṉi pūṅ kōti eṉṉuṭaṉ naṅki īṅku irukkeṉat toḻutu - aḻakiya pūṅkōtiyiṉi yuṭiya naṅkāy eṉṉuṭaṉ ivviṭattiruppā yākaveṉa vaṇaṅki ; 
kūṭal makaḷir eṉṟāḷ, tāṉaṟiyumūr atuvākalāṉ. pūṇviliyi aḻittalāvatu, ivaḷatu iyaṟki aḻaku kaṇṭār pūṇiṉ nalaṉi ikaḻtalām. ceyyāk kōlam - puṉiyā aḻaku; āvatu iyaṟki aḻaku. iṉi, kūṭal makaḷir koḷḷukiṉṟa ariya viliyi yuṭiya āṭakap pimpūṇāṟ ceyta kōlam aḻittaṟku eṉavumurippa. naṅki - viḷi. naṅki - makaṉ maṉiviyik kuṟikkum muṟip peyareṉpar aṭiyārkku nallār.
15-17. 	mātavattāṭṭi vaḻittuyar nīkki - kavuntiyaṭikaḷ vaḻic cēṟaṟkaṇ uḷavām tuṉpaṅkaḷi vilakkik kāttu, ētam illā iṭantalippaṭuttiṉaḷ - kuṟṟam ciṟitumillā viṭattuk kūṭṭiṉāḷ, nōtakavu uṇṭō num makaṉārkku iṉi- ākalāṉ num kaṇavaṉukku iṉi ivviṭattu uḷak kavali yuṟutal uṇṭō eṉṟu colli ;
talippaṭuttal - kūṭṭutal. nōvu takavu, nōtakavu āyiṟṟu eṉpa. makaṉ - kaṇavaṉ; 1"niṉakkivaṉ makaṉāyt tōṉṟiya tūum" eṉṟār maṇimēkaliyiṉum. uṇṭō - o, etirmaṟi. eṉṟu colli eṉa varuvitturikka. 
18-21. cāvaka nōṉpikaḷ aṭikaḷ ātaliṉ - aṭikaḷ cāvaka nōṉpuṭiyarākalāṉ, nāttūṇ naṅkiyoṭu nāḷ vaḻippaṭūum aṭicil ākkutaṟku - nāttūṇākiya aiyiyoṭu pakaṟpoḻutē uṇṇum uṇaviṉic camippataṟku, aminta naṟkalaṅkaḷ neṭiyātu aḷimiṉ nīr eṉak kūṟa - poruntiya nalla kalaṅkaḷi nīṭṭiyātu nīvir aḷimiṉ eṉṟu colla ;
cāvakanōṉpi - tuṟavātu virataṅkāppōr. aṭikaḷ eṉṟatu īṇṭuk kōvalaṉi. nāttūṇ - kaṇavaṉuṭaṉ piṟantāḷ. nāttūṇākiya naṅki. iṉi, nāttūṇum naṅkiyum eṉalumām ; īṇṭu nāttūṇ - aiyi ; naṅki - kaṇṇaki, nāḷvaḻippaṭūu maṭicil - kālattil ākkum aṭicil eṉalumamiyum. naṟkalam - putukkalam. aminta naṟkalam eṉṟatu tāṅkaḷ āḷum kalamaṉṟi mēṉmakkaḷāḷum kalattiṉi. neṭittal - nīṭittal
iṭikkulamaṭanti āycciyar tammoṭu maṉippaṭuttuk kūṭi nīrāṭṭit toḻutu  aḷimiṉ eṉak kūṟa eṉka.
22-28.	iṭikkula maṭantiyar - āyar kulattu makaḷir. iyalpiṟ kuṉṟā maṭikkalan taṉṉoṭu - mātari kūṟiya taṉmi yiṟ kuṟiyāta camittaṟ kuriya kalaṅkaḷuṭaṉē, māṇpuṭi marapiṟkōḷip pākaṟ koḻuṅ kaṉit tiraḷkāy - māṭcimi yuṭiyōr koṭukkum taṉmi pōlap pūvātu kāykkum palāviṉuṭiya koḻuviya tiraṇṭa mutirnta kāyum, vāḷvarikkoṭuṅkāy - vaḷinta varikaḷiyuṭiya veḷḷarik
kāyum, mātuḷam pacuṅkāy - kommaṭṭi mātuḷiyiṉ iḷaṅkāyum, māviṉ kaṉiyoṭu vāḻit tīṅkaṉi - māmpaḻamum iṉiya vāḻippaḻamum, cāli arici - cennel ariciyum ākiya ivaṟṟi, tampāṟ payaṉoṭu - tamkulattiṟkuriya pāluṭaṉum neyyuṭaṉum, kōl vaḷi mātē koḷkeṉak koṭuppa - tiraṇṭa vaḷiyali yaṇinta mātē koḷvāyāka veṉṟu collik koṭukka ;
periyōr collāmalē ceytal pōlat tāṉ pūvātē kāyttaliṉ, māṇpuṭi marapiṟ kōḷippākal eṉappaṭṭatu. 1"collāma lēperiyar collic ciṟiyarceyvar, colliyuñ ceyyār kayavarē - nalla, kulāmāli vēṟkaṇṇāy kūṟuvami nāṭiṟ, palāmāvip pātiriyip pār" eṉpatu aṟiyaṟpālatu. kōḷi - pūvātu kāykkum maram. pākal - palā. kōḷippākal : veḷippaṭi. kaṉikkāy - kaṉikku āṉa kāy, mutirnta kāy : itaṉi ikkālattuc ceṅkāy eṉpa. vāl varik koṭuṅkāy eṉap pāṭaṅ koṇṭu, veḷvarikkāy eṉpar arumpata uri yāciriyar. kommaṭṭi mātuḷaṅkāy puḷitta kaṟi ākkutaṟkuc ciṟantatu eṉpar. pāṟpayaṉ - pālākiya payaṉ eṉalumamiyum. 
29-34. 	mel viral cēppap palvēṟu pacuṅkāy koṭuvāyk kuyattu viṭuvāy ceyya - aṅṅaṉam avar koṭutta palavēṟu vakippaṭṭa paciya kāykaḷit taṉatu melliya viralkaḷ civakkum vaṇṇamvaḷinta vāyiṉiyuṭiya arivāḷāl aritalic ceyya, tirumukam viyarttatu - aḻakiya mukam viyarttatu, ceṅkaṇ cēntaṉa - cevvari poruntiya kaṇkaḷ civantaṉa, karipuṟa aṭṭil kaṇṭaṉaḷ peyara - karinta iṭattiyuṭiya aṭṭilik kaṇṭaṉaḷāy mukam māṟi? vi erimūṭṭiya aiyi taṉṉoṭu - vikkōlāl tī mūṭṭiya aiyiyuṭaṉ ki aṟimaṭimiyiṟ kātalaṟku ākki - taṉ ki valla aḷavālē kaṇavaṉukkuc camittu;
koṭuvāykkuyam - vaḷinta vāyiṉiyuṭiya arivāḷ. 1"kūṉikkuyam" eṉṟār piṟarum. viṭuvāy ceytal - arital. mukam viyarttatu pacuṅkāy arintamiyāṉum, ceṅkaṇ cēntami aṭṭil kaṇṭamiyāṉum eṉka. kaṇṭaṉaḷ; muṟṟeccam. tī mūṭṭuvār mutalil vikkōlāl mūṭṭuvar. maṭimi - aṭicil aṭutaṟṟaṉmi.
35-43. 	tālap pulliṉ vālveṇ tōṭṭu - paṉiyākiya pulliṉatu mika veḷḷiya ōliyiṉāl, kival makaṭūu kaviṉ peṟap puṉinta - kittoḻil valla makaḷ aḻaku peṟac ceyta, cey viṉit tavicil celvaṉ iruntapiṉ - toḻiṟ pāṭaminta taviciṉkaṇ kōvalaṉ amarnta piṉṉar, kaṭi malar aṅkiyiṟ kātalaṉ aṭinīr cuṭumaṇ maṇṭiyil toḻutaṉaḷ māṟṟi - cuṭṭa maṇkalaṅ koṇṭu kātalaṉ aṭikaḷik kaḻuviya nīri viḷakkamaminta malar pōlum akaṅkiyāl vaṇaṅki māṟṟi, maṇṇaka maṭantiyi mayakku oḻip paṉaḷpōl - nilamaṭantiyiṉatu mayakkatti oḻippavaḷ pōla, taṇṇīr teḷittut taṉ kiyāl taṭavi - taṉatu kiyiṉāl kuḷirnta nīrit teḷittu meḻuki, kumari vāḻiyiṉ kuruttu akam virittu īṅku amutam uṇka aṭikaḷ īṅku eṉa - īṉāta vāḻiyiṉ kuruttiṉi virittu ataṉkaṇ uṇaviṉip paṭittu aṭikāḷ ivviṭattu uṇṭaruḷka eṉṟu colla;
paṉi puṟakkāḻttu ākalāṉ pul eṉappaṭṭatu. tavicu - taṭukku eṉappaṭum. akam+ki - aṅki. aṭinīr - aṭiyik kaḻuviyanīr. cuṭumaṇ maṇṭi eṉṟa kuṟippu ivaḷ muṉpellām poṉ, veḷḷi mutaliyavaṟṟāṉāya kalaṅkaḷi vaḻaṅkiṉāḷ eṉpatuṇartti niṉṟatu. toḻutaṉaḷ - muṟṟeccam. maṇṇaka maṭantikku mayakkam ivarkku mēl varum tīṅku nōkki uṇṭāyatu eṉka. iṉi mayakkam - aṉantal eṉavumām. kumari vāḻi - īṉāta tali vāḻi. īṅku iraṇṭaṉuḷ oṉṟu aci.
ākki, iruntapiṉ māṟṟit teḷittut taṭavi virittu amutam uṇkaveṉa eṉka. 
44-53. 	aracar piṉṉōrkku arumaṟi maruṅkiṉ uriyavel lām orumuṟi kaḻittu - ariya maṟiyiṉiṭattu aracar piṉṉōrāya vaṇikarkku uriyaṉavākak kūṟiya paliyiṭal mutaliya vaṟṟi oru muṟiyāṟ kaḻittu, āṅku āyar pāṭiyiṉ acōti peṟṟu eṭutta pūvip putumalar vaṇṇaṉ kollō nal amutu uṇṇum nampi īṅku - im maturikkaṇ āyar pāṭiyil nalla amutiṉi yuṇṇum nampiyākiya kōvalaṉ am maturikkaṇ āyarpāṭiyil acōti eṉ pāḷ peṟṟeṭutta anta nallamutamuṇṇum putiya kāyām pūppōlum niṟattiṉiyuṭiya kaṇṇaṉōtāṉ, palvaḷit tōḷiyum - ivaṉ tuyar tīrtta ip palavākiya vaḷiyali aṇinta tōḷiṉiyuṭiyāḷum, paṇṭu nam kulattut toḻuṉi yāṟṟiṉuḷ tūmaṇivaṇṇaṉi viḻumam tīrtta viḷakkukkol eṉa - muṉṉar nam kulattut tōṉṟiya tūya nīlamaṇi pōlum niṟamuṭiya māyōṉik kāḷinti yāṟṟiṉkaṇ tuyar tīrtta nappiṉṉi eṉṉum viḷakkōtāṉ eṉṟu, aiyiyum tavviyum vimmitam eyti- aiyiyum avaḷ tāy mātari yum viyap puṟṟu, kaṇ koḷā namakku ivar kāṭci īṅku eṉa - ivaruṭiya aḻaku nam kaṇkaḷiṉ aṭaṅkāveṉṟu pukaḻntu kūṟa;
aracar piṉṉōr - vaṇikar, taṉṉūriṟ ceyvaṉa vellām ceyyap peṟāmiyāṉ orumuṟi kaḻittu eṉṟār.
īṅku nallamutuṇṇum nampi āṅku acōti peṟṟeṭutta malar vaṇṇaṉ kollō eṉa māṟuka. naṅkulattut tūmaṇi vaṇṇaṉ eṉka. 
54-56. 	uṇṭu iṉitu irunta uyar pērāḷaṟku - iṉitē uṇavuṇṭu taṅkiya uyarnta perumiyuṭiyōṉākiya kōvalaṉukku, am meṉ tiriyalōṭu aṭikkāy ītta - aḻakiya melliya veṟṟiliyōṭu pākkiṉiyum tanta, miyīr ōtiyi varukeṉap porunti - kariya kuḷirnta kūntaliyuṭi yāḷi varukaveṉṟu arukaṇittu ;
orumuṟi kaḻittu uṇṭiṉitirunta pērāḷaṉ eṉa muṭikka. tiriyal - veṟṟilic curuḷ. ōti - ākupeyar. 
57-62. 	kallatar attam kaṭakka yāvatum valluna kollō maṭanti mellaṭi eṉa - maṭantiyiṉ melliya aṭikaḷ parukkik kaṟkaḷ niṟinta neṟiyākiya aruvaḻiyik kaṭappa
taṟkuc ciṟitāvatu vaṉmiyiyuṭiyaṉavō eṉṟu niṉintu, vemmuṉi aruñcuram pōntataṟku iraṅki - vemmiyiyuṭiya maṟavar āṟalikkum muṉikaḷiyuṭiya ariya curattu neṟiyiṟ pōntamikku irakkaṅkoṇṭu, em mutu kuravar eṉ uṟṟaṉar kol - emmuṭiya iru mutukuravarum en niliyi aṭintaṉarō, māyaṅ kollō - yāṉ iṅṅaṉa muṟṟatu kaṉavō, valviṉi kollō - naṉavāyiṉ muṉceyta tīviṉiyiṉ payaṉō, yāṉ uḷam kalaṅki yāvatum aṟiyēṉ - ippoḻutu eṉṉuḷḷaṅ kalakkamuṟalāṉ yāṉ ivaṟṟiṉoṉṟiṉiyum uṇarkiṉṟilēṉ ;
muṉi - maṟavar iruppukkaḷ; kuṟumpukaḷ. yāvatum vallunakollō eṉṟatu vallā eṉṟavāṟu. eṉṉuṟṟaṉar kol - eṉṉa tuṉpamuṟṟārō; iṟantaṉarō. māyam - kaṉavu. yāvatum - yātum; ciṟitum. 
63-70.	vaṟumoḻiyāḷaroṭu vampap parattaroṭu - payaṉilacolvārōṭum putiya parattamiyi yuṭiyārōṭuṅ kūṭi, kuṟumoḻik kōṭṭi neṭu naki pukku - ciṟu coṟ collum iḻintōr kūṭṭattiṉkaṇ mikka cirippukku uṭpaṭṭu, poccāppu uṇṭu - maṟaviyiṟ porunti, poruḷ uriyāḷar naccuk koṉṟēṟku - poruḷ potinta uri yiṉiyuṭiya periyōr virumpukiṉṟa nalloḻukkattiṉik keṭutta eṉakku, naṉṉeṟi uṇṭō - iṉit tīk katiyaṉṟi naṟkati uṇṭāmō, irumutu kuravar ēvalum piḻittēṉ - tāytantiyarkku ēval ceytaliṉum vaḻuviṉēṉ, ciṟumutuk kuṟivikkuc ciṟumiyum ceytēṉ - ciṟu paruvattil periya aṟiviṉiyuṭiyaḷāya niṉakkum tīmi ceytēṉ, vaḻu veṉum pārēṉ - iṅṅaṉam nam nakar viṭṭu iṅku varutal kuṟṟamuṭittām eṉpataṉic ciṟitum nōkkēṉāy, mā nakar maruṅku īṇṭu eḻukeṉa eḻuntāy - namatu periya nakarat tiṭattiṉiṉṟum ivviṭattu eḻuka veṉṟu yāṉ kūṟa uṭaṉē oruppaṭṭu eḻuntaṉi, eṉ ceytaṉi eṉa - eṉṉa ariya kāriyañ ceytaṉiyeṉṟu kōvalaṉ iraṅkik kūṟa;
vaṟumoḻi - poruḷil moḻi, vampap parattar - putiya kāma nukarcci virumpuṅ kāmukar. kōṭṭi - kūṭṭam poccāppu - maṟati. naccu - naccappaṭum poruḷ. kollal - īṇṭu mēṟ koḷḷāmi. ēval piḻittal - tāy tantiyarkkuc ceyvaṉa tavirtal; iṉi, mātaviyiṉ naṭpi avarkaḷ oḻittiṭuka eṉṉavum oḻiyāmiyumām. eṉum - ciṟitum. īṇṭu eṉṟatu maturiyi. ittuṇiyum taṉatu oḻukkattiṉi veṟuttuk kūṟi iv varaviṟku nīyum uṭaṉ
paṭṭaṉiyē eṉa iraṅkik kūṟiṉāṉ. 
71-83. 	aṟavōrkku aḷittalum - cāvakarkkuk koṭuttalum, antaṇar ōmpalum - pārppaṉarip pēṇutalum, tuṟavōrkku etirtalum - tuṟavikaḷi etirkkoḷḷutalum, tollōr ciṟappiṉ viruntu etirkōṭalum iḻanta eṉṉi - mēliyōr uyarttuk kūṟum ciṟappiṉiyuṭiya viruntiṉari etirkoḷḷutalumāya ivaṟṟi iḻanta eṉṉi, num perumakaḷ taṉṉoṭum - numatu tāyōṭum, perum peyart talittāḷ maṉperuñ ciṟappiṉ mānitik kiḻavaṉ - periya pukaḻiṉiyum taliyāya muyaṟciyiṉiyum maṉṉaratu perumi mikka ciṟappiṉiyum uṭiya mācāttuvāṉ kaṇṭu, munti nillā muṉivu ikantaṉaṉ ā - nīr eṉ muṉpu nillāmiyāṟ ṟōṉṟum veṟuppiṉi nīṅkiṉēṉāka, aṟpu uḷam ciṟantu aruḷmoḻi aḷii eṉ pārāṭṭa - ataṉi uṇarnta avarkaḷ uḷḷattu mikkut tōṉṟum aṉpōṭu aruḷ niṟinta moḻikaḷik kalantu eṉṉip pārāṭṭa, yāṉ akattu oḷitta nōyum tuṉpamum noṭivatu pōlum - nāṉ eṉṉuḷḷattu maṟitta maṉakkavaliyiyum meyvaruttattiṉiyum kūṟuvatu pōṉṟa, eṉ vāy al muṟuvaṟku avar uḷḷakam varunta - eṉatu uṇmiyallāp puṉcirippiṟku avarkaḷ uḷḷam varuntum vaṇṇam, pōṟṟā oḻukkam purintīr - nīr periyōr veṟukkum tīya voḻukkattiṉi virumpiṉīr ākavum, yāvatum māṟṟā uḷḷa vāḻkkiyēṉ ātaliṉ - ummuṭiya collic ciṟitum māṟṟa niṉiyāta uḷḷattu vāḻkkiyi uṭiyēṉākalāṉ, ēṟṟu eḻuntaṉaṉ yāṉ eṉṟu avaḷ kūṟa - nāṉ nīr kūṟiyataṉi uṭaṉpaṭṭu eḻuntēṉ eṉṟu kaṇṇaki kūṟa; 
tāḷ - muyaṟci. maṉperum ciṟappu - aracaṉāṟ peṟum periya ciṟappu. ikattal - nīṅkutal. nīr nillā muṉivu eṉa oru col varuvikka. noṭital - collutal. vāy - uṇmi.  
84-93. 	kuṭimutaṟ cuṟṟamum - kuṭikkaṇ mutaṟ cuṟṟamāy tāy tanti mutaliyōriyum, kuṟṟiḷiyōrum - kuṟṟēval puriyum makaḷiriyum, aṭiyōr pāṅkum - aṭiyār pakutiyiyum, āyamum nīṅki - cēvikkum tōḻimāriyum viṭṭu vilaki, nāṇamum maṭaṉum nallōr ēttum pēṇiya kaṟpum peruntuṇiyāka - nāṇiṉiyum maṭaṉiyum nallōrkaḷatu pōṟṟutaliyum virumpiya kaṟpiṉiyum perumi mikka tuṇiyākak koṇṭu, eṉṉoṭu pōntu īṅku eṉ tuyar kaḷinta - ivviṭattu eṉṉōṭu vantu eṉ tuṉpattiṉik keṭutta, poṉṉē koṭiyē puṉi pūṅ kōtāy - poṉṉiyoppāy koṭipōlvāy aḻakiya malarmāliyi aṉiyāy, nāṇiṉpāvāy nīḷ nila viḷakkē-nāṇiṉiyuṭiya pāviyi nikarppāy periya ivvulakiṟku viḷakkamāka amintāy, kaṟpiṉ koḻuntē poṟpiṉ celvi - kaṟpukkuk koḻuntu pōlvāy aḻakiṉ celviyē, cīṟaṭic cilampiṉ oṉṟu koṇṭu yāṉ pōy māṟi varuvaṉ mayaṅkātu oḻikeṉa - niṉ ciṟiya aṭikku aṇiyākiya cilampukaḷuḷ oṉṟaṉi yāṉ koṇṭu ceṉṟu viṟṟu varuvēṉ ; varuntuṇiyum nī mayaṅkā tiruppāyākaveṉṟu colli :
aṭiyōr pakutiyarāvār - cevilittāy mutaliya aivar : āvār, āṭṭuvāḷ, ūṭṭuvāḷ, ōluṟuttuvāḷ, noṭi payiṟṟuvāḷ, kittāy eṉappaṭuvār. iṉi itaṟkuc cēvikkum peyar eṉavum kūṟupa.
maṭaṉ - koḷuttak koṇṭu koṇṭatu viṭāmi. pēṇiya kaṟpu eṉṟatu kaṟputtāṉ ivaḷi virumpi vantaṭintatu eṉṟa paṭi. nīṇila viḷakku eṉpataṉi uvami yākki nilattiṉ viḷakkākiya ñāyiṟṟiṉiyum tiṅkaḷiṉiyum viḷakkattāl oppāy eṉalum amiyum. yāṉ pōy māṟi varuvaṉ eṉṟatu mēl nikaḻum nikaḻcciyik kuṟippiṟ pulappaṭuttum moḻiyākavuḷatu 
94-99. 	karuṅkayal neṭuṅkaṇ kātali taṉṉi - kariya kayal pōlum neṭiya kaṇkaḷiyuṭiya taṉ kātaliyi, oruṅkuṭaṉ taḻīi - meymmuḻutum taḻuvi, uḻiyōr illāta oru taṉi kaṇṭu taṉ uḷḷakam vetumpi - avaḷarukilirukkum tōḻi mutaliyōr oruvarum illāta avaḷatu taṉimiyi nōkkit taṉ uḷḷam kotittalāṉ, varu paṉi karanta kaṇṇaṉ āki - niṟinta nīri maṟitta kaṇkaḷiyuṭiyaṉāy, pal āṉ kōvalar illam nīṅki - pala pacukkaḷiyum erumikaḷiyum uṭiya iṭiyar vīṭṭiṉi viṭṭu nīṅki, vallā naṭiyiṉ maṟukiṟ celvōṉ - taḷarvuṟṟa naṭiyōṭu av vītiyiṟ celkiṉṟavaṉ;
uḻiyōrillā oru taṉi kaṇṭu eṉṟatu muṉṉar ivaḷ āyattār palarōṭu kūṭiyirukkum nilimiyi nōkkik kūṟiyatākak koḷka. iṉi, īṇṭu āycciyarellām kuraviyāṭap pōyiṉamiyāṉ āṇṭu oruvaru millāmi nōkkik kūṟiyatāk kalumamiyum. kaṇṇīrik karantāṉ, kātali atu kāṇiṉ varuntumeṉṟu koṇṭu. vallā naṭi - māṭṭāta naṭi. 
100-101. 	imil ēṟu etirntatu. iḻukku eṉa aṟiyāṉ taṉ kulam aṟiyum takuti aṉṟu ātaliṉ - murippiṉiyuṭiya āṉēṟu taṉṉi etirntu pāya vantataṉit tīya nimittameṉṟu aṟintilaṉ; atu taṉatu kulam uṇarum takutiyiṉi uṭit taṉṟu ākalāṉ;
etirtal - etirppaṭalumām. takutiyaṉṟu ākaliṉ aṟiyāṉ eṉka. aṟiyāṉ - muṟṟeccam.
102-104. 	tātu eru maṉṟam tāṉ uṭaṉ kaḻintu - tātākiya eruviṉiyuṭiya maṉṟamellāṅ kaḻintu, mātar vīti maṟukiṭi naṭantu - taḷip peṇṭukaḷ teruviṉ naṭuvē naṭantu ceṉṟu, pīṭikit teruviṟ peyarvōṉ āṅkaṇ - kaṭivītiyiṟ celkiṉṟavaṉ avviṭattē;
tāterumaṉṟam - erukkaḷ tukaḷākik kiṭappatum, marattaṭiyu mākiya iṭam. taḷippeṇkaḷ - kōyiṟpaṇi ceyyum makaḷir. vīti maṟuku - oruporuṭ paṉmoḻi. 
105-112. 	kaṇṇuḷ viṉiñar - urukkut taṭṭārum, kiviṉi muṟṟiya nuṇviṉik kollar nūṟṟuvar piṉvara - kittoḻilil tērnta nuṭpa viṉittiṟamuṭiya paṇit taṭṭārum nūṟu peyar taṉ piṉṉē vara, meyppi pukku - caṭṭi aṇintu, vilaṅku naṭiccelaviṉ - vilaki naṭakkum celaviṉiyuṭiya, kikkōl - kiyiṉkaṇ koṭiṟṟiṉiyuṭiya, kollaṉik kaṇṭaṉaṉ āki - poṟkollaṉik kaṇṭu, teṉṉavaṉ peyaroṭu ciṟappup peṟṟa poṉviṉik kollaṉ ivaṉ eṉap porunti - pāṇṭiyaṉ peyarōṭu varici peṟṟa poṟṟoḻilil tērnta kollaṉāvāṉ ivaṉ eṉak karuti avaṉi aṇmi, kāvalaṉ tēvikku āvatōr kāṟku aṇi nī viliyiṭutaṟku ātiyō eṉa - aracaṉ peruntēvikku aṇiyalāvatoru kāṟcilampiṉi nī vili matippiṭutaṟku valliyō eṉṟu kēṭpa;
meyppi - caṭṭi. vilaṅku naṭi - uyarntōrik kāṇiṉ vilaki naṭattal. kikkōl - koṭiṟu. teṉṉavaṉ peyaroṭu ciṟappup peṟṟa kollaṉ eṉak kaṇṭāṉ, nūṟṟuvar piṉvaralāṉ. kāṟku aṇi - kālil aṇiyum cilampu. ātiyō - āvāyō; taṉ cilampiṉ aruvili karuti ātiyō veṉṟāṉ. piṉvarac cellutaliyuṭiya kollaṉ eṉka.
oḻikeṉat taḻīik kaṇṭu vetumpik kaṇṇaṉāki nīṅkic celvōṉ aṟiyāṉ kaḻintu naṭantu peyarvōṉ kaṇṭaṉaṉākip porunti ātiyōveṉa eṉka.
iṉip poṟkollaṉ kūṟuvāṉ :
113-116.	aṭiyēṉ aṟiyēṉ āyiṉum - aṭiyēṉ makaḷir kālaṇiyiṉ vilimatittaṟku aṟiyēṉ eṉiṉum, vēntar muṭi mutaṟ kalaṉkaḷ camippēṉ yāṉ eṉa - aracarkaḷukku muṭi mutaliya aṇikalaṉkaḷi naṉku camippēṉ yāṉ eṉṟu colli, kūṟṟat tūtaṉ ki toḻutu ētta - kūṟṟuvaṉiṉ tūtaṉāka vanta poṟkollaṉ kiyāṉ vaṇaṅkip pukaḻa, pōṟṟarum cilampiṉ potivāy aviḻttaṉaṉ - pukaḻtaṟkariya cilampiṉip potinta potiyiṉ kaṭṭiṉik kōvalaṉ aviḻttaṉaṉāka; 
camittal - naṉku amittal. potivāy - kaṭṭuvāy.
117-120. 	mattaka maṇiyoṭu vayiram kaṭṭiya pattikkēvaṇam - taliyāya māṇikkattōṭu vayiramum aḻuttiya patti paṭṭa kēvaṇattiyuṭiya, pacumpoṉ kuṭic cūl cittirac cilampiṉ ceyviṉi ellām - paciya poṉṉāṟ ceyta puṭi paṭṭu uṭkaruviyuṭiya cittirat toḻilaminta cilampiṉatu toḻil nuṇukkamellām, poyttoḻiṟ kollaṉ purintuṭaṉ nōkki-poy kūṟum toḻiliṉiyuṭiya poṟ kollaṉ viliyi nōkkātu kōvalaṉ mītu kuṟṟaṅ kūṟutaṟkut tāṉ kavarnta cilampōṭu okkum paṭiyi nōkki;
patti - varici. kēvaṇam - kallaḻuttuṅ kuḻi. kuṭiccūl - puṭipaṭutalumām; kuṭaccūl eṉap pāṭaṅ koḷḷalumām; 1"kuṭaccūl, añcilam poṭukki" eṉpatu kāṇka. kuṟṟamilāṉ mēṟ kuṟṟaṅ kūṟutaṟku iṭuvanti kūṟutal eṉpa. 
121-126.	kōpperuntēvikku allati - aracaṉuṭiya mātēvikku allātu, ic cilampu yāppuṟavu illi eṉa muṉ pōntu - ic cilampu piṟarkkup poruntumāṟu illi eṉṟu kūṟi muṉṉiṉṟum pōy, viṟal miku vēntaṟku viḷampi yāṉ vara - veṟṟi mikka aracaṉukku yāṉ itaṉi aṟivittu varuntuṇiyum, eṉ ciṟu kuṭil aṅkaṇ irumiṉ nīr eṉa - eṉatu ciṟiya kuṭilākiya avviṭattē nīr iruppīrāka eṉṟu colla, kōvalaṉ ceṉṟu ak kuṟumakaṉ irukki yōr tēva kōṭṭac ciṟiyakam pukkapiṉ - kōvalaṉum pōy ak kīḻōṉuṭiya irukkikku ayalatākiyatōr tēvakōṭṭattiṉ matilukkuḷ pukunta piṉ;
araca pattiṉiyit tēviyeṉṟal marapu ; peruntēvi - paṭṭattu maṉivi. allati - ai iṭiccol. ciṟukuṭilaṅkaṇ - ciṟu kuṭilukku arukāṉa avviṭam eṉalumām. kuṟu makaṉ - kīḻōṉ. ciṟi - matil.  
127-130. 	karantu yāṉ koṇṭa kāl aṇi - yāṉ vañcittuk koṇṭa cilampu, īṅkup parantu veḷippaṭā muṉṉam maṉṉaṟku - eṉṉiṭattatē yeṉṟu yāvarum aṟiya aracaṉukku veḷippaṭutaṟku muṉṉarē, pulampeyar putuvaṉiṟ pōkkuvaṉ yāṉ eṉa - vēṟu nāṭṭiṉiṉṟum pōnta ip putiyōṉāl eṉ kaḷḷattiṉi yāṉ maṟippēṉeṉṟu, kalaṅkā uḷḷam karantaṉaṉ celvōṉ - tuṇinta uḷḷat tiṉik karantu celkiṉṟavaṉ;
putuvaṉiṟ pōkkutal - avaṉ vāyilākat taṉ vañcatti maṟittal. pōkkutal - maṟittal.
131-141.	kūṭal makaḷir āṭal tōṟṟamum - kūṭaliṭattu nāṭaka makaḷiruṭiya āṭaliṟ ṟōṉṟum tōṟṟamum, pāṭaṟ pakutiyum - avvāṭaluk kēṟṟa pāṭaliṉ vēṟupāṭum, paṇṇiṉ payaṅkaḷum - yāḻiciyiṉ payaṉkaḷum, kāvalaṉ uḷḷam kavarntaṉa eṉṟu - aracaṉuṭiya neñciṉi īrttaṉaveṉṟu karuti, taṉ ūṭal uḷḷam uḷ karantu oḷittu - taṉatu ūṭu taliyuṭiya uḷḷatti uḷḷē maṟittu oṭukki, tali nōy varuttam taṉmēl iṭṭu - talinōyākiya varuttattit taṉ mēl ēṟaṭṭuk koṇṭu, kulamutal tēvi kūṭātu ēka - uyar kuṭip piṟappiṉiyuṭiya peruntēvi taṉṉoṭu mēvātē taṉatu kōyiṟkaṇ antappuram ceṉṟuviṭalāṉ, mantirac cuṟṟam nīṅki maṉṉavaṉ - aracaṉ kāma mikkavaṉāy amiccar tiraḷiṉi viṭṭu nīṅki, cintu ari neṭuṅkaṇ cilatiyar tammoṭu - ari citaṟiya neṭiya kaṇkaḷiyuṭiya paṇippeṇṭiruṭaṉē, kōpperuntēvi kōyil nōkki - kōpperuntēviyiṉuṭiya kōyili nōkkic celvōṉi, kāppu uṭi vāyiṟ kaṭikāṇ akaviyiṉ - kāvaliyuṭiya vāyiṟ kaṭiyiṭattuk kāṇumuṉpē, vīḻntaṉaṉ kiṭantu tāḻntu pala ētti - vaṇaṅkik kīḻē viḻuntu kiṭantu palavāka ētti ;
āṭaṟṟōṟṟam eṉpataṟku iraṭṭuṟa moḻitalāl āṭaliṭattut tōṉṟum mukat tōṟṟamum āṭal vēṟupāṭum eṉṟurittalumām. talinōy varuttam - varuttattic ceyyum talinōy eṉalumām. nōkkic celvōṉi eṉa oru col varuvit turikka. kaṭikāṇ akavi eṉpataṟku, nōkkic ceṉṟu kaṭiyik kāṇumuṉ eṉak kūṟik kaṭikāṇutali aracaṉ toḻilākkiṉumamiyum. celvōṉ vīḻntu kiṭantu tāḻntu ētti eṉka.  
142-147.	kaṉṉakam iṉṟiyum kavikkōl iṉṟiyum - kaṉṉakkōlum koṭiṟṟuk kōlum iṉṟākavum, tuṉṉiya mantiram tuṇiyeṉak koṇṭu - taṉ uḷḷattup poruntiya mantirattiṉiyē kaḷaviṟkut tuṇiyākak koṇṭu, vāyilāḷari mayakku tuyil uṟuttu - vāyil kāppōri mayakkat tiṉi uṇṭupaṇṇum uṟakkattiṉkaṇ poruntuvittu, kōyiṟ cilampu koṇṭa kaḷvaṉ - kōyiṟkaṇ irunta cilampiṉik kavarntukoṇṭa kaḷvaṉ, kalleṉ pērūrk kāvalark karantu - oliyiṉiyuṭiya periya ūrkkuk kāvalarāṉōr kaṇkaḷi maṟittu, eṉ cillic ciṟukuṭil akattu iruntōṉ eṉa - eṉṉuṭiya iḻinta ciṟiya kuṭiliṉkaṇ vantirukkiṉṟāṉ eṉṟu kūṟa;
kaṉṉakam - akaḻuṅ karuvi. kavikkōl - iṭṭiki mutaliyavaṟṟip paṟikkuṅ karuvi. mantiram - tuyiluṟukkum mantiram. muṉṉāḷ kōyiṟ cilampu taṉṉiṭattilliyeṉṟu cātittāṉākaliṉ, īṇṭuk 'kōyiṟ cilampu koṇṭa kaḷvaṉ' eṉṟāṉ. cilli - iḻivu. 
148-153. 	viṉi viḷi kālam ātaliṉ - viṉi tōṉṟip payaṉit tarum kālam eytiṟṟākalāṉ, yāvatum ciṉi alar vēmpaṉ tērāṉ āki - kompukaḷiyuṭiya vēmpiṉ malar māliyic cūṭiya pāṇṭiyaṉ ciṟitum ārāytalilaṉāy, ūr kāppāḷarik kūvi - ūr kāvalari aḻittu, īṅku eṉ tāḻ pūṅkōti taṉ kāṟ cilampu - eṉatu tāḻnta pūṅkōtiyi yuṭiyāḷatu kāliṉkaṇ aṇiyum cilampu, kaṉṟiya kaḷvaṉ kiyatu ākiṉ - aṭippaṭṭa ak kaḷvaṉuṭiya kiyakattatāyiṉ, koṉṟu ac cilampu koṇarka īṅku eṉa - ak kaḷvaṉik koṉṟu ac cilampiṉi iṅkuk koṇṭu varukaveṉṟu colla;
alar vēmpaṉ - vēmpalarōṉ ; alar ākupeyarāṉ māliyi yuṇarttiṟṟu. tāḻtal - nīṭal. kaṉṟiya kaḷvaṉ - kaḷavil taḻumpiṉavaṉ. tērntu muṟiceytaṟ kuriya kāvalaṉ tērātu iṅṅaṉam kūṟiyatu viṉiyiṉ payaṉeṉpār `viṉi viḷi kālamātaliṉ' eṉṟār. muṟi ceyyum aracaṉ ārāyavēṇṭiya neṟiyoṉṟiyum kikkoṇṭila ṉeṉpār `yāvatum tērāṉāki' eṉṟār. eṉiṉum eṉ kōtiyiṉ kāṟcilampu kaḷvaṉ kiyatākiṉ koṉṟu koṇarka veṉṟamiyāṉ am maṉṉaṉiṭattuk koṭuṅkōṉmi yiṉmi uṇarappaṭum. kollal - varuttutalumām. taṉ tēviyiṉ ūṭal tīrkkum maruntāy aḵtu utavu meṉṉuṅ karuttāl koṇarka īṅku eṉṟāṉeṉka. avaṉik kolla accilampōṭu īṅkuk koṇarkaveṉak karutiyavaṉ, viṉi viḷi kālamātaliṉ koṉṟu ac cilampu koṇarka veṉṟāṉ eṉpar aṭiyārkku nallār. 
154-157. 	kāvalaṉ ēvak karuntoḻiṟ kollaṉum-aṅṅaṉam aracaṉ ūr kāppāḷarit taṉṉōṭu ēvutalāṉē kolit toḻiliyuṭiya kollaṉum, ēval uḷḷattu eṇṇiyatu muṭittu eṉa - ēvappaṭṭa uḷḷattōṭē yāṉ karutiyataṉi muṭittēṉ eṉṟu karuti, tīviṉi mutirvalic ceṉṟupaṭṭu irunta - tīyaviṉiyākiya cūḻnta valikkaṇṇē ceṉṟu akap paṭṭirunta, kōvalaṉ taṉṉik kuṟukiṉaṉ āki - kōvalaṉi aṇukiyavaṉāy ;
karuntoḻil - kolittoḻil. ēval uḷḷam - ēviya uḷḷam; turanta uḷḷam. muṭittu - muṭittēṉ ; virivu paṟṟi etir kālam iṟanta kālamākak kūṟappaṭṭatu. iṉi muṭintatu eṉaṟ pālatu muṭittu eṉa vikāramāyiṟṟu eṉavuṅ kūṟupa. mutirtal - cūḻtal. tī viṉi mutirvali eṉpataṉi mutir tīviṉi vali eṉa māṟṟi, muṟṟiya tīviṉiyākiya vali eṉalumamiyum. kuṟukutal - aṇukutal.  
158-161. 	valam paṭu tāṉi maṉṉavaṉ ēva - veṉṟi kāṇum cēṉiyiṉiyuṭiya aracaṉ ēvutalāṉē, cilampu kāṇiya vantōr ivar eṉa - ivarkaḷ cilampiṉik kāṇpataṟku vantaṉar ; ataṉik kāṭṭumiṉ eṉac colli, ceyviṉic cilampiṉ ceyti ellām poyviṉik kollaṉ purintu uṭaṉ kāṭṭa - poyttoḻil cārnta poṟkollaṉ avari vēṟāka aḻittut toḻilaminta cilampiṉ arumiyellām virumpic collik kōyiṟ cilampuṭaṉ iḵtu okkun taṉmiyiyum uṭaṉ kāṭṭa ;
kāṇiya, ceyyiyaveṉṉum viṉiyeccam, kāṭṭumiṉ, colleccam. ceyti - toḻil nuṭpam; arumi. uṭaṉ kāṭṭa - kōyiṟcilampuṭaṉ okkun taṉmiyik kāṭṭa ; eṉpataṉāl avarkaḷi vēṟāka aḻittuc ceṉṟu kūṟiṉami peṟappaṭum. 
162-165. 	ilakkaṇa muṟimiyiṉ iruntōṉ īṅku ivaṉ - ivviṭattiruntōṉākiya ivaṉ meyppoṟiyāṉum irukkiṉṟa muṟimiyāṉum, kolippaṭu makaṉ alaṉ eṉṟu kūṟum - koli ceytaṟkuriya kaḷvaṉ allaṉ eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟa, aruntiṟal mākkaḷi aka nakittu urittu - ariya tiṟaliyuṭiya vēṟkārari eḷḷal naki nakittu ikaḻntu kūṟi, karuntoḻiṟ kollaṉ kāṭṭiṉaṉ urippōṉ - kolit toḻili virumpiya kollaṉ kaḷavu nūliluḷḷa ētukkaḷik kāṭṭi urippavaṉ:
ilakkaṇa muṟimi - ilakkaṇamum muṟimiyum ; ummit toki. aka naki - ikaḻcci naki. iṉi, aka naka eṉṟu pāṭaṅ koṇṭu vēṟkārar uḷam piriyamāmpaṭi eṉavum kūṟupa.
166-169.	mantiram teyvam maruntē nimittam - mantiramum teyvamum maruntum nimittamum, tantiram iṭaṉē kālam karuvi eṉṟu eṭṭuṭaṉ aṉṟē - tantiramum iṭaṉum kālamum karuviyum eṉappaṭukiṉṟa eṭṭaṉiyumaṉṟō, iḻukku uṭi marapiṟ kaṭṭuṇ mākkaḷ tuṇi eṉat tirivatu- kuṟṟam poruntiya muṟimiyiṉiyuṭiya kaḷavu kaṇṭu uṇṇum vāḻkkiyiṉiyuṭiya kaḷvar tuṇiyākak koṇṭu tirivatu;
maruntē iṭaṉē ivaṟṟu ēkāram eṇ; 1"eṇṇē kāram iṭiyiṭṭuk koḷiṉum, eṇṇuk kuṟittiyalu meṉmaṉār pulavar" eṉpavākalāṉ ēṉiyavaṟṟeṇṇēkāram tokkaṉa. iḻukkuṭi marapiṟ kaṭṭuṇmākkaḷ eṭṭuṭaṉaṉṟē tuṇiyeṉat tirivatu eṉa māṟuka. uṭaṉ : muṟṟummip poruṭṭu.  
170-171. 	maruntiṟ paṭṭīr āyiṉ yāvarum perum peyar maṉṉaṉiṟ peru navip paṭṭīr - ivaṉ maruntiṉkaṇ akap paṭṭīrāyiṉ nīvir yāvirum periya pukaḻiyuṭiya aracaṉāl kaṭuntaṇṭattiṉi aṭintīr ;
maruntiṟ paṭṭīrāyiṉ eṉṟatu, muṉṉar ilakkaṇamuṟimiyiṉ kolippaṭumakaṉalaṉ eṉak kūṟiyataṉi uṭkoṇṭatu. navi - tuṉpam ; taṇṭam. navip paṭṭīr; tuṇivu paṟṟi iṟanta kālattāṟ kūṟiṉāṉ.
iṉi, mantira mutaliya eṭṭaṉ viḷivu kūṟuvāṉ:- 
172-173. 	mantiram nāviṭi vaḻuttuvar āyiṉ - kaḷvar mantirattiṉi nāviṉiṭattu uccarippārāyiṉ, intira kumarariṉ yām kāṇkuvamō - tēva kumārarip pōla avariyum nām kāṇamāṭṭōm;
intirar - tēvar; 2"intirar amuta miyiva tāyiṉum" eṉṟār piṟarum. intira kumarar - tēvakumārar ; tēvar. ōkāram etir maṟi 
174-175.	teyvat tōṟṟam teḷikuvar āyiṉ - avar tam teyvattiṉ vaṭiviṉi uḷḷattē teḷintu niṉippārāyiṉ, kiyakattu uṟu poruḷ kāṭṭiyum peyarkuvar - tam kiyilakappaṭṭa mikka poruḷiṉi namakkuk kāṭṭiyum tappuvār;
176-177.	tōṟṟam - vaṭivu. 
maruntiṉ namkaṇ mayakkuvar āyiṉ - avar maruntiṉālē nammiṭattu mayakkañ ceyvārāyiṉ, iruntōm peyarum iṭaṉumār uṇṭō - irunta nām eḻuntu pōkalām iṭamum uṇṭāmō; mār, aci. uṇṭō - illi; ō, etirmaṟi.  
178-179.	nimittam vāyttiṭiṉ allatu - naṉṉimittam vāykkap peṟṟā lallatu, yāvatum pukaṟkilar arumporuḷ vantu kip pukutiṉum - peṟutaṟkariya mikka poruḷ tāṉē vantu kiṭippiṉum eḷiyaviṭattum pukār ;
yāvatum - ettuṇi eḷiyaviṭattum ; yāvatum pukaṟkilar - ciṟitum virumpār eṉṟumām. 
180-181.	tantira karaṇam eṇṇuvar āyiṉ - kaḷavu nūliṟ collu kiṉṟa toḻilkaḷi eṇṇic ceyvārāyiṉ, intiraṉ mārpattu āramum eytuvar - intiraṉ mārpilaṇinta ārattiṉiyum aṭikuvar ;
	tantiram - nūl, kaḷavunūl, karaṇam - toḻil. eṇṇutal - āyntu ceytal.
182-183.	ivviṭam ip poruḷ kōṭaṟku iṭam eṉiṉ - ip poruḷiṉi yām koḷḷutaṟku ivviṭamē ēṟṟa iṭamākum eṉṟu eṇṇuvārāyiṉ, avviṭattu avari yār kāṇkiṟpār - avviṭattu avarik kāṇa vallār yār ; 
184-185.	kālam karuti avar poruḷ kiyuṟiṉ - avar kaḷavu koḷḷuṅ kālam ituvē eṉṟu karutip poruḷik kippaṟṟuvārāyiṉ, mēlōr āyiṉum vilakkalum uṇṭō - tēva rāyiṉum ataṉi vilakkutal kūṭumō; 
mēlōr - tēvar. 
186-187.	karuvi koṇṭu avar arum poruḷ kiyuṟiṉ - avar kaṉṉakam mutaliya karuvikaḷik koṇṭu peṟutaṟkariya poruḷkaḷik kippaṟṟiṉāl, irunilam maruṅkiṉ yār kāṇkiṟpār - ip periya vulakattu avarik kāṇa vallār yār? ākalāṉ;
ākalāṉ eṉa oru col varuvikka. 
188-189. 	iravē pakalē eṉṟu iraṇṭu illi - avarkku iravum pakalum eṉṉum iraṇṭu pakuti illi, karavu iṭam kēṭpiṉ ōr pukal iṭam illi - avar kaḷavu ceyyum iṭattik kēṭpiṉ nām ōṭi oḷikkalām iṭam vēṟu illi;
ellāk kālamum, ellā iṭamum avar kaḷavu ceytaṟkup poruntiyaṉavē eṉṟavāṟāyiṟṟu.
iṅṅaṉam kaḷavu nūṟṟuṟi kūṟiyōṉ, kaḷvaruṭiya vaṉmiyi avarkkuṇarttuvāṉ poyk kari oṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟāṉ;  
190-202. 	tūtar kōlattu vāyiliṉ iruntu - muṉṉoru nāḷil oru kaḷvaṉ tūtaruṭiya uruvattuṭaṉ pakaṟ kālattu aracaṉ vāyiliṉkaṇiruntu, mātar kōlattu val iruḷ pukku - mikka iruḷiyuṭiya irākkālattup peṇṇurukkoṇṭu uḷḷē nuḻintu, viḷakku niḻalil tuḷakkilaṉ ceṉṟu viḷakkiṉ niḻalilē paḷḷiyaṟiyiṉuḷ naṭukkamiṉṟip pukku, āṅku iḷaṅkō vēntaṉ tuḷaṅkoḷi āram veyiliṭu vayirattu miṉṉiṉ vāṅka - avviṭattu in neṭuñceḻiyaṉukku iḷaṅkōvākiya vēntaṉ tuyilkiṉṟavaṉuṭiya veyiliṭukiṉṟa vayirattiyuṭiya aciyum oḷi poruntiya āratti virivil vāṅkiṉāṉāka, tuyil kaṇviḻittōṉ tōḷiṟ kāṇāṉ uṭivāḷ uruva - tūkkattiṉiṉṟum eḻunta av viḷavaracaṉ ārattit tōḷiṟ kāṇāṉāyt taṉ uṭivāḷi uruva, uṟi ki vāṅki eṟitoṟum ceṟitta iyalpiṟku āṟṟāṉ - ataṉ uṟiyit taṉ kiyiṟ paṟṟit tāṉ kuttuntōṟum vāḷilē uṟiyic ceṟitta at taṉmikku āṟṟāṉāy, malliṟ kāṇa - maṟpōrāṉ avaṉ valiyik kāṇa virumpiya aḷavilē, maṇittūṇ kāṭṭik kalviyiṟ peyarnta kaḷvaṉ taṉṉi - avviṭattu niṉṟatōr maṇit tūṇit tāṉākak kāṭṭit taṉ kaḷavu nūṟpayiṟciyiṉāl maṟinta kaḷvaṉi, kaṇṭōr uḷar eṉil kāṭṭum - pārttōruḷarāyiṉ avarkaḷik kāṭṭumiṉ, īṅku ivarkku uṇṭō ulakattu oppōr eṉṟu ak karuntoḻiṟ kollaṉ colla - kāṭṭuvārilar ākalāṉ ik kaḷvarkku oppāvār iv vulakattu oruvaruṇṭō eṉa ak kolit toḻiliṉiyuṭiya poṟkollaṉ kūṟa ;
val iruḷ - mikka iruḷ. valliruṭpukku eṉṟataṉāl, vāyiliṉiruntami pakaṟ kālamāyiṟṟu. tuḷakkam - naṭukkam, accam. miṉṉiṉ vāṅka - miṉṉaliṉ vaḻiyē vāṅka eṉalumām. miṉ - oḷi.
vēntaṉ viḻittōṉ kāṇāṉ uruva vāṅkic ceṟitta iyalpiṟku āṟṟāṉ kāṇak kāṭṭip peyarnta kaḷvaṉ eṉka.  
202-203. 	āṅku ōr tiruntu vēl taṭakki iḷiyōṉ kūṟum - appoḻutu tiruntiya vēlēntiya periya kiyiṉiyuṭiya ōr iḷiñaṉ kūṟuvāṉ ; kūṟuvati yāteṉiṉ :-
204-211.	nilaṉ akaḻ uḷiyaṉ nīlat tāṉiyaṉ - muṉṉar nilattiṉi akaḻum uḷiyi yuṭiyaṉāy nīla niṟam poruntiya kacciṉiyuṭiyavaṉāy, kalaṉ naci vēṭkiyiṟ kaṭumpuli pōṉṟu - kalaṉkaḷi mika virumpiya viruppat tāṉē paciyāl tāṉ uṇṭaṟku vilaṅkiṉit tēṭi yaliyum puliyip pōṉṟu, māri naṭunāḷ val iruḷ mayakkattu ūr maṭikaṅkul oruvaṉ tōṉṟa - maḻikkālattu iṭiyāmamākiya mikka iruḷ kalanta ūrārellām uṟaṅkiya irāk kālattē oru kaḷvaṉ vantu tōṉṟa, ki vāḷ uruva eṉ ki vāḷ vāṅka - yāṉ eṉ kivāḷiṉi uṟiyi ṉiṉṟum uruviṉēṉāka avaṉ eṉatu ak kivāḷiṉip paṟṟa, ev vāy maruṅkiṉum yāṉ avaṟ kaṇṭilēṉ - ataṉ piṉṉar avaṉi yāṉ evviṭattum kāṇakillēṉ, aritu ivar ceyti - ākalāṉ ivaruṭiya ceyki yāvarālum aṟitaṟkaritu, alikkum vēntaṉum - ivaṉi nām kollātu viṭiṉ nammi aracaṉ varuttuvāṉ, uriyatu oṉṟu urimiṉ uṟu paṭiyīr eṉa - ākalāṉ mikka paṭikkalattiṉi uṭiyīr iṉic ceytaṟkuriyatu oṉṟaṉi ārāyntu kūṟumiṉ eṉṟu colla ;
1"niṟaṅkavarpu puṉinta nīlak kacciṉar, meṉṉū lēṇip paṉmāṇ cuṟṟiṉar, nilaṉaka ḻuḷiyar kalaṉaciik koṭkuṅ, kaṇmā ṟāṭava roṭukka moṟṟi" eṉṟār piṟarum. kōvalaṉiyum kaḷvaṉākak koṇṭu 'ivar ceyki ' eṉṟāṉ.
uḷiyaṉ tāṉiyaṉ pulipōṉṟu oruvaṉ tōṉṟa eṉka. 
212-217. 	kallāk kaḷimakaṉ oruvaṉ kiyil veḷvāḷ eṟintaṉaṉ - kalviyiṉmiyāṟ koliyañcāṉāya kaḷimakaṉ oruvaṉ taṉ kiyiluḷḷa veḷḷiya vāḷālē veṭṭiṉāṉ, vilaṅku ūṭu aṟuttatu - av veṭṭāṉatu kuṟukkākat tuṇittatu, puṇ umiḻ kuruti poḻintu uṭaṉ parappa - tuṇipaṭṭa puṇṇiṉ vaḻiyē koppuḷikkiṉṟa utiram kutittu eṅkum parakka, maṇṇaka maṭanti vāṉ tuyar kūra - nila maṭanti mikka tuyarattiṉi aṭiya, kāvalaṉ ceṅkōl vaḷiiya - ak kāvalaṉuṭiya ceṅkōl vaḷiyum vaṇṇam, vīḻntaṉaṉ kōvalaṉ paṇṭi ūḻviṉi urutteṉ - muṉṉit tīviṉi mutirtalāṉ kōvalaṉ veṭṭuṇṭu vīḻntaṉaṉ eṉka.
veḷvāḷ - pakivarōṭu porutu kuruti paṭiyāta vāḷ. maṇṇaka maṭanti tuyar kūrntatu ivaṉ veṭṭuṇṭamikkum ataṉāṟ kaṇṇaki tuyaruṟutaṟku meṉka. muṉṉarum, 1"maṇṇaka maṭantiyi mayakkoḻip paṉaḷpōl" eṉṟār. iṉi, maṇṇaka maṭanti - maturi yeṉak koṇṭu mēl taṉakku āvatuṇarntu tuyar kūra eṉṟurittalumām.
itu nilimaṇṭila vāciriyappā. 
veṇpāvuri
"naṇṇu miruviṉiyu ..... vanta viṉi." paṇṭi viḷivāki vanta viṉi - pāṇṭiyaṉ muṉ ceyta tīṅkiṉ payaṉāki vanta tīviṉiyāl, maṇṇil vaḷiyāta ceṅkōl - iv vulakattu vaḷi yāta avaṉuṭiya ceṅkōl, kaṇṇaki taṉ kēḷvaṉ kāraṇat tāṉ vaḷintatu - kaṇṇakiyiṉ kaṇavaṉāya kōvalaṉ muṉṉili yāka vaḷivuṟṟatu, naṇṇum iruviṉiyum - ākalāṉ, nīvir ceyta iru viṉip payaṉum nummi vantu poruntum eṉpataṉi aṟintu, naṇṇumiṉkaḷ nallaṟamē - ulakattīr nalviṉiyic ceyyuṅkaḷ.
pāṇṭiyaṉ kōl vaḷitaṟku paḻaviṉi kāraṇamum, kōvalaṉ vāyilu meṉṟār.
kolikkaḷak kāti muṟṟiṟṟu. 

17. āycciyar kuravi      
 (āyar cēriyil palavaki uṟpātaṅkaḷ nikaḻntaṉa. mātari taṉ makaḷ aiyiyi nōkki, muṉpu āyarpāṭiyil kaṇṇaṉum palarāmaṉum piṉṉiyuṭaṉ āṭiya kuraviyi nām ippoḻutu kaṟavi kaṉṟu tuyar nīṅkukaveṉa āṭuvēm eṉak kūṟi, eḻuvar kaṉṉiyari niṟutti ēḻicikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷākiya kural mutaliya vaṟṟi avarkaṭkup peyarkaḷāka iṭṭu, avaruḷ kuralākiyavaḷik kaṇṇaṉ eṉṟum, iḷiyākiyavaḷip palarāmaṉ eṉṟum, tuttam ākiyavaḷip piṉṉi eṉṟum, ēṉi narampukaḷākiyōri maṟṟi nālvar eṉṟum paṭittukkōṭ peyariṭa, avarkaḷ kaṟkaṭakak ki kōttu niṉṟu māyōṉip pāṭik kuravi yāṭiṉar. (itilē, muṉṉilip paravalum paṭarkkip paravalumākat tirumālip pāṭiya pāṭṭukkaḷ mikavum arumiyāṉavi.)      
		kayaleḻutiya imayaneṟṟiyiṉ
ayaleḻutiya puliyumvillum
nāvalantaṇ poḻiṉmaṉṉar

5		māli veṇkuṭip pāṇṭiyaṉ kōyilil
kāli muracaṅ kaṉikura liyampu mākaliṉ

10		iṭimutumakaḷ vantutōṉṟumaṉ;
urippāṭṭu maṭi
1		kuṭappāl uṟiyā kuviyimil ēṟṟiṉ
maṭakkaṇīr cōrum varuvatoṉ ṟuṇṭu;
2		uṟunaṟu veṇṇey urukā urukum;
maṟiteṟit tāṭā varuvatoṉ ṟuṇṭu;
3		nāṉmuli yāyam naṭuṅkupu niṉṟiraṅkum
	māṉmaṇi vīḻum varuvatoṉ ṟuṇṭu;
karuppam
kuṭattuppā luṟiyāmiyum kuviyimi lēṟṟiṉ maṭak kaṇṇīr cōrtalum uṟiyil veṇṇe yurukāmiyum maṟimuṭaṅkiyāṭāmiyum māṉmaṇi nilattaṟṟu vīḻtalum varuvatōr tuṉpamuṇṭeṉa makaḷi nōkki maṉamayaṅkātē maṇṇiṉmātark
kaṇiyākiya kaṇṇakiyuntāṉkāṇa āyarpāṭiyil erumaṉṟattu māyavaṉuṭaṉ tammuṉ āṭiya vālacariti nāṭakaṅkaḷil vēl neṭuṅkaṇ piññiyō ṭāṭiya kuravi yāṭutum yāmeṉṟāḷ kaṟavi kaṉṟu tuyar nīṅkukaveṉavē;
koḷu
1		kāri kataṉañcāṉ pāyntāṉik kāmuṟumiv
vēri malarkkōti yāḷ;
cuṭṭu
2		neṟṟic cekili yaṭarttāṟ kuriyavip
poṟṟoṭi mātarāḷ tōḷ;
3		mallal maḻaviṭi yūrntāṟ kuriyaḷim
mulliyam pūṅkuḻal tāṉ;
4		nuṇpoṟi veḷḷi yaṭarttāṟkē yākumip
peṇkoṭi mātartaṉ tōḷ;
5		poṟpoṟi veḷḷi yaṭarttāṟkē yākumin
naṟkoṭi meṉmuli tāṉ;

koṉṟiyam pūṅkuḻa lāḷ;

pūvip putumala rāḷ;
eṭuttuk kāṭṭu
		āṅku,
toḻuviṭi ēṟu kuṟittu vaḷarttār
eḻuvariḷaṅ kōti yār
eṉṟutaṉ makaḷinōkkit
toṉṟupaṭu muṟiyāl niṟutti
iṭimutu makaḷivarkkup
paṭittukkōṭ peyariṭuvāḷ
kuṭamutal iṭamuṟi yākkural tuttam
kikkiḷi uḻiiḷi viḷari tārameṉa
viritaru pūṅkuḻal vēṇṭiya peyarē;
māyavaṉ eṉṟāḷ kurali viṟalveḷḷi
āyavaṉ eṉṟāḷ iḷitaṉṉi - āyamakaḷ
piṉṉiyām eṉṟāḷōr tuttatti maṟṟiyār
muṉṉiyām eṉṟāḷ muṟi;
māyavaṉ cīruḷār piññiyun tāramum
vālveḷḷi cīrār uḻiyum viḷariyum
kikkiḷi piññi iṭattāḷ valattuḷāḷ
muttikku nalviḷari tāṉ;
avaruḷ,
vaṇṭuḻāy māliyi māyavaṉ mēliṭṭut
taṇṭāk kuravitāṉ uḷpaṭuvāḷ - koṇṭacīr
viyam aḷantāṉṟaṉ mārpiṉ tirunōkkāp
peyvaḷik kiyāḷnam piṉṉitā ṉāmeṉṟē
aiyeṉṟā ḷāyar makaḷ;
kūttuḷ paṭutal
		avartām,
cennili maṇṭilattāṟ kaṟkaṭakak kikōottu
anniliyē yāṭaṟcī rāyntuḷār - muṉṉik
kuraṟkoṭi taṉkiḷiyi nōkkip parappuṟṟa
kollip puṉattuk kuruntocittāṟ pāṭutum
mullittīm pāṇiyeṉ ṟāḷ;
eṉāk,
kuraṉmanta māka iḷicama ṉāka
varaṉmuṟiyē tuttam valiyā -  uraṉilā
mantam viḷari piṭippāḷ avaḷ naṭpiṉ
piṉṟiyip pāṭṭeṭup pāḷ;
pāṭṭu
1		kaṉṟu kuṇilāk kaṉiyutirtta māyavaṉ
	iṉṟunam āṉuḷ varumēl avaṉvāyil
	koṉṟiyan tīṅkuḻal kēḷāmō tōḻī;
2		pāmpu kayiṟāk kaṭalkaṭinta māyavaṉ
	īṅkunam āṉuḷ varumēl avaṉvāyil
	āmpalan tīṅkuḻal kēḷāmō tōḻī;
3		kolliyañ cāraṟ kuruntocitta māyavaṉ
ellinam āṉuḷ varumēl avaṉ vāyil
mulliyan tīṅkuḻal kēḷāmō tōḻī;
toḻuṉit tuṟivaṉō ṭāṭiya piṉṉi
aṇiniṟam pāṭukēm yām;
1		iṟumeṉ cāyal nuṭaṅka nuṭaṅki
	aṟuvi yoḷittāṉ vaṭiveṉ kōyām
	aṟuvi yoḷittāṉ ayara ayarum
īṟumeṉ cāyal mukameṉ kōyām;
2		vañcañ ceytāṉ toḻuṉip puṉaluḷ
neñcaṅ kavarntāḷ niṟiyeṉ kōyām
neñcaṅ kavarntāḷ niṟiyum vaḷiyum
vañcañ ceytāṉ vaṭiveṉ kōyām;
3		tiyal kaliyum vaḷiyum iḻantē
kiyi loḷittāḷ mukameṉ kōyām
kiyi loḷittāḷ mukaṅkaṇ ṭaḻuṅki
miya luḻantāṉ vaṭiveṉ kōyām;
oṉṟaṉpakuti
1		katirtikiri yāṉmaṟitta kaṭalvaṇṇaṉ iṭattuḷāḷ
matipuriyu naṟumēṉit tammuṉōṉ valattuḷāḷ
potiyaviḻ malarkkūntaṟ piññicīr puṟaṅkāppār
mutumaṟitēr nārataṉār muntimuṟi narampuḷarvār;
2		mayilerut tuṟaḻmēṉi māyavaṉ valattuḷāḷ
payilitaḻ malarmēṉit tammuṉōṉ iṭattuḷāḷ
kayileruttam kōṭṭiyanam piṉṉicīr puṟaṅkāppār
kuyiluvaruḷ nārataṉār koḷipuṇarcīr narampuḷarvār;
āṭunarp pukaḻtal
		māyavaṉṟam muṉṉiṉoṭum varivaḷikkip piṉṉiyoṭum
kōvalartañ ciṟumiyarkaḷ kuḻaṟkōti puṟañcōra
āyvaḷiccīrk kaṭipeyarttiṭ ṭacōtiyār toḻutē tēttat
tāterumaṉ ṟattāṭuṅ kuraviyō takavuṭittē;
ellānām,
puḷḷūr kaṭavuḷip pōṟṟutum pōṟṟutum
uḷvarip pāṇiyoṉ ṟuṟṟu;
uḷvari vāḻttu
1		kōvā maliyāram kōtta kaṭalāram
tēvarkōṉ pūṇāram teṉṉarkōṉ mārpiṉavē
tēvarkōṉ pūṇāram pūṇṭāṉ ceḻuntuvarik
kōkula mēyttuk kuruntocittā ṉeṉparāl;
2		poṉṉimayakkōṭṭup pulipoṟittu maṇṇāṇṭāṉ
maṉṉaṉ vaḷavaṉ matiṟpukār vāḻvēntaṉ
maṉṉaṉ vaḷavaṉ matiṟpukār vāḻvēntaṉ
poṉṉan tikirip porupaṭiyā ṉeṉparāl;
3		munnīri ṉuḷpukku mūvāk kaṭampeṟintāṉ
	maṉṉarkōc cēraṉ vaḷavañci vāḻvēntaṉ
maṉṉarkōc cēraṉ vaḷavañci vāḻvēntaṉ
kaṉṉaviltō ḷōccik kaṭalkaṭintā ṉeṉparāl;
muṉṉilip paraval
1		vaṭavariyi mattākki vācukiyi nāṇākkik
kaṭalvaṇṇaṉ paṇṭorunāḷ kaṭalvayiṟu kalakkiṉiyē
kalakkiyaki acōtiyār kaṭikayiṟṟāṟ kaṭṭuṇki
malarkkamala untiyāy māyamō maruṭkittē;
2		aṟuporuḷ ivaṉeṉṟē amararkaṇan toḻutētta
uṟupaciyoṉ ṟiṉṟiyē ulakaṭiya uṇṭaṉiyē
uṇṭavāy kaḷaviṉāṉ uṟiveṇṇe yuṇṭavāy
vaṇṭuḻāy māliyāy māyamō maruṭkittē;
3		tiraṇṭamarar toḻutēttum tirumālniṉ ceṅkamala
iraṇṭaṭiyāṉ mūvulakum iruḷtīra naṭantaṉiyē
naṭanta aṭi pañcavarkkut tūtāka naṭantaaṭi
maṭaṅkalāy māṟaṭṭāy māyamō maruṭkittē;
paṭarkkip paraval
1		mūvulakum īraṭiyāṉ muṟinirampā vakimuṭiyat
tāviyacē vaṭicēppat tampiyoṭuṅ kāṉpōntu
cōvaraṇum pōrmaṭiyat tollilaṅki kaṭṭaḻitta
cēvakaṉcīr kēḷāta ceviyeṉṉa ceviyē
tirumālcīr kēḷāta ceviyeṉṉa ceviyē;
2		periyavaṉi māyavaṉip pērulaka mellām
virikamala untiyuṭi viṇṇavaṉik kaṇṇum
tiruvaṭiyuṅ kiyum tiruvāyum ceyya
kariyavaṉik kāṇāta kaṇṇeṉṉa kaṇṉē;
kaṇṇimittuk kāṇpār tam kaṇṇeṉṉa kaṇṇē
3		maṭantāḻu neñcattuk kañcaṉār vañcam
kaṭantāṉi nūṟṟuvarpāl nāṟṟiciyum pōṟṟap
paṭarntā raṇamuḻaṅkap pañcavarkkut tūtu
naṭantāṉi ēttāta nāveṉṉa nāvē
nāraya ṇāveṉṉā nāveṉṉa nāvē;
eṉṟiyām....
kōtta kuraviyuḷ ēttiya teyvanam
āttalip paṭṭa tuyartīrkka vēttar
maruḷa vikal vikal māṟaṭṭu
veṟṟi viḷippatu maṉṉō koṟṟattu
iṭippaṭi vāṉavaṉ muṭittali yuṭitta
toṭittōṭ ṭeṉṉavaṉ kaṭippiku muracē.
uri
1-10. 		kayal eḻutiya imaya neṟṟiyiṉ - imayattucciyil tāṉ eḻutiya kayalmīṉiṟku, ayal eḻutiya puliyum villum - pakkattē eḻutiya puliyiṉiyum villiṉiyumuṭiya cōḻaṉum cēraṉum, nāvalan taṇ poḻil maṉṉar - nāvalantīviluḷḷa ēṉiya aracarkaḷum, ēval kēṭpap pār aracu āṇṭa - taṉ ēval kēṭṭu oḻuka nilavulaka muḻutiyum aracāṇṭa, māli veṇkuṭip pāṇṭiyaṉ kōyiliṉ - mutta māli poruntiya veṇkoṟṟak kuṭiyiṉiyuṭiya pāṇṭiyaṉatu kōyiṟkaṇṇē, kāli muracam kaṉi kural iyampum - paḷḷi yeḻucci muracu mikka kuralōṭu muḻaṅkum, ākaliṉ neymmuṟi namakku iṉṟu ām eṉṟu - ātalāṉ namakku iṉṟu kōyiṟku neyyaḷakkum muṟiyām eṉa eṇṇi, aiyi taṉ makaḷik kūyk kaṭikayiṟum mattum koṇṭu iṭimutumakaḷ vantu tōṉṟumaṉ - iṭikkulattu mutiyaḷākiya mātari aiyiyākiya taṉ makaḷi aḻittuk kaṭikayiṟṟiṉiyum mattiṉiyum eṭuttukkoṇṭu tayirt tāḻiyiṭattu vantu tōṉṟiṉāḷ; 
kayal mutaliyaṉa ilacciṉi. imayaneṟṟiyiṉ eḻutiya kayal ayal eḻutiya puliyum villumeṉak kūṭṭuka. kayali vaṇaṅkutaṟku ataṉ ayalil puliyum villum eḻutappaṭṭaṉavaṉṟi oppāka eḻutappaṭṭila veṉka. eṉṉi? iv vāciriyar avvakkāṇ ṭattu avvannāṭṭu maṉṉari uyarttuk kūṟutal muṟiyākalāṉ eṉka. puliyum villum eṉṟatu, avaṟṟiṉiyuṭiya maṉṉari. maṉṉarum eṉṉum ummi tokkatu. iyampum, muṟṟu. maṉ, aci.  
urippāṭṭu maṭi - uriyākiya pāṭṭi iṭiyē maṭuttatu.
1. 	"kuṭappāl uṟiyā................oṉṟuṇṭu" 
kuṭappāl uṟiyā - nām uṟiyiṭṭa tāḻikaḷiṟ pālum tōyntila, kuvi imil ēṟṟiṉ maṭakkaṇīr cōrum - tiraṇṭa murippiṉi yuṭiya āṉēṟṟiṉ aḻakiya kaṇkaḷiṉiṉṟum nīr ukum ; varuvatu oṉṟu uṇṭu - ātalāṉ namakku varuvatōr tīṅku uṇṭu ; uṟital - tōytal. kuvital - tiraḷutal. maṭam - īṇṭu aḻaku. 
2.	"uṟinaṟu veṇṇey.........oṉṟuṇṭu" 
uṟinaṟu veṇṇey urukā - uṟikkaṇ vitta mutal nāḷi veṇṇey uruka vittaṉa urukukiṉṟila, urukum maṟi teṟittu āṭā - āṭṭuk kuṭṭikaḷum tuḷḷi viḷiyāṭāmaṟ kuḻintu kiṭakkum; varuvatu oṉṟu uṇṭu - ātalāṉ namakku varuvatōr tīṅku uṇṭu ;
 urukutal - oṉṟu nekiḻtal, maṟṟoṉṟu kuḻintukiṭattal. iṉi. urukum eṉṟa pāṭattiṉi uruki eṉakkoṇṭu uruki āṭā eṉavum muṭippa. uruki - melintu.
3. 	"nāṉmuli.........oṉṟuṇṭu" 
nāṉ muli āyam naṭuṅkupu niṉṟu iraṅkum - nāṉku mulikaḷiyuṭiya pacu niri mey naṭuṅki niṉṟu araṟṟum, māṉ maṇi vīḻum - ap pacukkaḷiṉ kaḻattiṟkaṭṭiya maṇikaḷum aṟṟu nilattil viḻum ; varuvatu oṉṟu uṇṭu - ākalāṉ namakku varum tīṅku oṉṟu uṇṭu;
āyam - āṉiri. naṭuṅkupu - naṭuṅki. māl maṇi - periya maṇi yeṉṟumām.  
karuppam - ivi kēṭṭiṟkuk karuppam ; mēlē kūṟuvaṉa vaṟṟiṟkuk kāraṇam eṉṟumām.
"kuṭattuppāl uṟiyāmiyum.....nīṅkukaveṉavē"
tāḻikkaṇ pāl uṟiyāmiyāṉum, tiraṇṭa murippiṉi yuṭiya āṉēṟṟiṉ kaṇkaḷiliruntu nīr ukutalāṉum , uṟik kaṇ veṇṇey urukāmiyāṉum, āṭṭu maṟikaḷ tuḷḷi viḷiyāṭāmiyāṉum, pacukkaḷiṉ kaḻuttu maṇikaḷ aṟṟu nilattu vīḻtalāṉum varuvatōr tuṉpam uṇṭeṉṟu colli, makaḷi nōkki,  `uḷḷaṅ kavalaṟka ; in nilattu mātarkku aṇiyāka viḷaṅkum kaṇṇakiyum kāṇum vaṇṇam muṉṉar āyarpāṭiyil erumaṉṟattiṉkaṇ kaṇṇaṉ tam muṉākiya palarāmaṉuṭaṉ viḷiyāṭiya pāla carita nāṭakaṅkaḷuḷ vēl pōlum neṭiya kaṇkaḷiyuṭiya nappiṉṉiyōṭāṭiya kuravik kūttiṉi, pacukkaḷum kaṉṟukaḷum tuṉpam oḻi vaṉavāka veṉṟu kūṟi yām āṭuvōm' eṉṟāḷ;
uṟiyāmiyum mutaliya eṇṇummikaḷil mūṉṟaṉurupu viritturikka. māyavaṉuṭaṉ eṉṉum mūṉṟaṉ collurupiṉit tammuṉ eṉpataṉōṭu iyikka. kaṇṇakiyum kāṇa āṭutum eṉṟāḷeṉka. tāṉ, aci. kuraviyāvatu eḻuvarēṉum. oṉ patiṉmarēṉum kikōttāṭuṅ kūttu. 
koḷu - kuravik kūttiṟkuk karuttu.
1. 	"kārikataṉ.........kōtiyāḷ" 
kāri kataṉ añcāṉ pāyntāṉik kāmuṟum iv vēri malark kōtiyāḷ - ik kāri eruttiṉ cīṟṟattiṉi añcāṉāyp pāyntu taḻuviyōṉi it tēṉiṟinta malarmāliyiyuṭiyāḷ virumpuvāḷ;
kāri - karumiyuṭiyatu. kataṉ - cīṟṟam. kōtiyāḷ kāmuṟum eṉka.
cuṭṭu - itu mutaṟcuṭṭu. 
2. 	"neṟṟic cekili..........mātarāṭōḷ" 
neṟṟic cekili aṭarttāṟku uriya ip poṉ toṭi mātarāḷ tōḷ - neṟṟikkaṇ civanta cuṭṭiyiṉiyuṭiya ēṟṟiṉi vali tolit tāṉukkē ip poṉṉālāya vaḷiyiṉiyaṇinta mātaruṭiya tōḷkaḷ uriyaṉavākum; 
cekil - civappu; ākupeyar.  
3.	"mallal.........pūṅkuḻaṟāṉ" 
mallal maḻaviṭi ūrntāṟku uriyaḷ im mulliyam pūṅkuḻal tāṉ - vali mikka iv viḷiya ēṟṟiṉi eṟic celuttiṉāṉukkē im mulli malariṉic cūṭiya aḻakiya kūntaluṭiyāḷ uriyaḷāvāḷ;
mallal - vaḷam eṉiṉum amiyum. tāṉ, aci. 
4.	"nuṇpoṟi ..... tōḷ" 
nuṇpoṟi veḷḷi aṭart tāṟkē ākum ip peṇ koṭi mātartaṉ tōḷ - nuṇṇiya puḷḷikaḷiyuṭiya iv veḷḷēṟṟiṉi valitolit tāṉukkē ik koṭipōṉṟa peṇṇiṉ kātalikkappaṭum tōḷkaḷ uriyaṉavām;
veḷḷi - ākupeyar. poṟi veḷḷi - maṟi eṉalumākum. mātar - kātal. peṇ koṭiyākiya mātar eṉṟalumām. taṉ, cāriyi.
5. 	"poṟpoṟi ......... meṉmulitāṉ" 
poṟpoṟi veḷḷi aṭarttāṟkē ākum in naṟkoṭi meṉmuli tāṉ - aḻakiya im maṟiyēṟṟiṉi aṭakkiyōṉukkē iv vaḻakiya koṭipōṉṟāḷuṭiya melliya mulikaḷ uriyaṉa vākum; tāṉ, aci. 
6. 	"veṉṟi.........pūṅkuḻalāḷ" 
veṉṟi maḻaviṭi ūrn tāṟku uriyaḷ ik koṉṟiyam pūṅkuḻalāḷ - veṉṟi yiṉiyuṭiya ivviḷiya ēṟṟiṉi aṭakkic celuttiṉāṉukku ik koṉṟip paḻam pōlum polivu peṟṟa kūntaliṉi yuṭiyāḷ uriyaḷāvāḷ;
1"koṉṟip paḻakkuḻaṟ kōtiyar" eṉṟār piṟarum. 
7.	 "tū niṟa veḷḷi ......... malarāḷ" 
tū niṟa veḷḷi aṭart tāṟku uriyaḷ ip pūvip putumalarāḷ - tūya veḷḷi niṟa muṭiya iv vēṟṟiṉ cīṟṟattiṉik keṭuttavaṉukku ik kāyām pūp pōlum niṟattiṉiyuṭiyāḷ maṉivi āvāḷ ;
-_?ī72"kāri kataṉañcāṉ pāynta potuvaṟkē" 3"nuṇpoṟi veḷḷik kataṉañcāṉ pāynta potuvaṉ" 4"cemmaṟu veḷḷiyum" 5"pāṉiṟa vaṇṇaṉpōṟ paḻitīrnta veḷḷiyum" eṉṟaṟṟoṭakkattaṉavāka mullik kaliyil varum eruttu vēṟupāṭu kaḷum ēṟukōḷ iyalpum īṇṭu aṟiyaṟpālaṉa.
eṭuttukkāṭṭu  ivvēṟu koṇṭāṉukku ivaḷ uriyaṉ eṉṟal. iṉi, iṅṅaṉam cuṭṭik kāṭṭutal eṉalumām.
āṅku - appaṭiyē
	toḻuviṭi .................................... kōtiyār
 toḻuviṭi ēṟu kuṟittu vaḷarttār eḻuvar iḷaṅkōti yār -  iṅṅaṉamākat toḻuviṭat tuṇṭākiya ēḻuvaki ēṟṟiṉi ik kōtiyiyuṭiya ēḻu kaṉṉiyar taṉ maṇaṅkuṟittu vaḷarttaṉar.
kuṟittu vaḷarttal - itaṉi aṭakkiyōṉiyē maṇappēm eṉṟu karuti vaḷarttal.
